MUKTABODHA INDOLOGICAL RESEARCH INSTITUTE and INSTITUT FRANCAIS DE PONDICHERY


Catalog number : I00022
Uniform title: siddhāntasārāvali with the commentary of anantaśambhu
Author : trilocanaśivācārya
Author : anantaśambhu
Manuscript : IFP manuscript 30687
Description:
Siddhāntasārāvali with the commentary of Anantaśambhu. The Siddhāntasārāvali authored by Trilocanaśivācārya in 12th-century Tamil Nadu, is a cornerstone in the realm of Śaiva philosophy. It offers a comprehensive overview of the intricate doctrines of the Śaiva Siddhānta tradition. Anantaśambhu's exposition (vyākhyā) written possibly around the 14th century quotes from a wide-range of sources and is structured into four sections, or Pādas—Kriyā, Caryā, Yōga, and Jñāna—mirroring the foundational texts of Śaiva-mūlāgama-s.  The incomplete bundle comprises 160 folios, measuring 42 cm in length and 4 cm in width. It provides invaluable insights into the nuanced philosophical discourse of Śaiva thought, with verse distribution as follows: Jñānapāda (27 verses), Kriyāpāda (90 verses out of 118), Yōgapāda (8 verses), and Caryāpāda (41 verses). This bundle is incomplete and some of the folios are not ordered in the right sequence.
Notes:
This e text was produced under an agreement between the IFP (Institut Francais de Pondichery) and The Muktabodha Indological Research Institute, India.

The e-text was prepared by the staff of the IFP, Sambandhan Anandavardhana (Research Associate) and Arul Nandi (Research Assistant)  under the supervision of Dr. Deviprasad Mishra (Project Co-ordinator).

 ...-yāṇāntyāgaḥ nādanādāntayoḥ sadāśivatyāgena dvayostyāgaḥ śaktivyāpinī samanānāmadhipatiranāhataśivaḥ 
tatyāgena raktyāditrayāṇāṃ tyāga..... (l2)
 pakṣe vyomaśabdena śaktyāditrasthānamucyate| prāsādamantrasya sūkṣmoccāreṇa sadāśivatyāgaḥ 
atisūkṣmoccāreṇānāhataśivatyāgaḥ tathā coktaṃ bhagavatā jña.... (l3)
...na śivācāryeṇa pūjāstotre śrīmanmūlamanūtthanādaśikha(va)yā brahmādisadāśivasthānatyāga gatikramordhvavimalaṃ 
vyomāntasaṃsthāyate iti|
 
tathā (l4)
svacchande - akāraśca ukāraśca makāro bindureva ca ardhacandro nirodhī ca nādo nādānta eva ca| śaktiśca 
vyāpini caiva samanaikādaśasmṛtāḥ| u.... (l5)
..nmā tu tatoti(di)taṃ tadatītaṃ nirāmayamiti| jyotiṣmatāṃ jyotīṃṣi eṣāṃ santi(ntvi)ti jyotiṣmantaḥ teṣāṃ 
jyātiṣṭhatāṃ pūrvoktānā(nāṃ) hataśivasadāśiveśvara.... (l6)
.. drācyutabrahmādināṃ dyotakaṃ sarvatra prakāśamānaṃ paramaśivajyotiṣaiva eṣāṃ jyotīṃṣi vidyanta ityarthaḥ tathāgamaḥ| 
sarveṣāṃ tejasāṃ cai..... (l7)
...nidhiśca bhagavāñchivaḥ sarva-prakāśakatvācca tejo jyotiriti smṛtaḥ iti| 

tathā śrutirapi - dūraṃ gamaṃ jyotiṣā jyotirekaṃ tanme manaḥ śivasaṅkalpamastviti śivākhyaṃ śivanāmadheyaṃ (l8)
 tajjyotiḥ sarvatra saṃpūrṇatvena prakāśamānaṃ uktaṃ ca| yadindhanāgnisaṃyogāt somārkamaṇijaṃ ca yat tejastasmāt 
susaṃpūrṇaṃ prakāśaṃ (p14a, l1)
 jyotiruttamamitidhiyā prapadye| sohaṃbhāvanātmikayā buddhyā paraśive svātmānaṃ vilīnaṃ bhāvayāmītyarthaḥ| nanu| 
buddhistāvadantaḥkaraṇamadhye ekanāmajātyādi(l2)
sahitasavikalpakajñānamantaḥkaraṇasādhyaṃ nirvikalpakaśivajñānasya vasturūpamātrasya grahaṇaṃ 
nāntaḥkaraṇasādhaayamantaḥkaraṇasya pāśatvāt| uktaṃ ca| (l3)
 antaḥkaraṇavṛttiyyāt bodhākhyā sā maheśvaraṃ na prakāśayituṃ śaktā pāśavānnigalādivaditi kathaṃ dhiyā prapadye 
ityuktamityāśaṅkāyāṃ pariha....raḥ (l4)
 dīkṣitāḥ nirmalāḥ sādhatu varāḥ pūrvoktavanmūlamanūtthanādādikrameṇa svacitprakāśikayā buddhyā niṣkalaṃ śivaṃ 
paśyanti prāsādamantrā na .... (l5)
...ṣṭhānasāmarthyena uktaṃ śivārkaśaktidīdhityā samarthīkṛtaciddṛśā śivaṃ śaktyādibhiḥ sārdhaṃ 
paśyatyātmāganāvṛtiriti| kaḥ śāntaḥ sarvakṛdasya yaḥ sakala... (l6)
citsvānyaprakāśo ca lome yo brahmaparopyanādiratulaḥ sūkṣmo mahān cidghanaḥ vyāpī muktasakhaḥ sa 
śaktiramalopyudyogavān brahmagaḥ sā(l7)
dākhyaḥ sakalādhipo dhvanisite'nantaśca rudro site| idānīṃ sṛṣṭyādikāraṇārthaṃ namo mātrasaṃyuktasya 
paraśaktyātītaparaśivasya paraśaktyā saṃyuktasadāśivādi(l8)
saṃjñāntaramastītyāha| śānta iti śāntaḥ rāga-dveṣādirahitaḥ tatkāraṇasya malāderasaṃbhavāt sarvakṛt samastaṃ 
karotīti sarvakṛt svatantratayā sarvakakṣitaḥ .... (p14b, l1)
 avyayaḥ vyatho nāśaḥ mahāpralayeṣu vyaktānāṃ śaktyātmanāvasthānaṃ paripūrṇatvena tadra.... (l2)
tvādavyayaḥ sakalavit sakalaṃ samastaṃ vettatijñānaśaktyā jānāditi sakalavit sarvajña ityarthaḥ svānyaḥ sva 
evānyo yasya saḥ svānyaḥ eka evetyarthaḥ prakāśaḥ sarvatra (l3)
 prakāśaḥ sarvatra prakāśamānaḥ yadvā svaścānyaśca svānyau tayoḥ prakāśaṃ svānyaprakāśaḥ svānyau prakāśayatīti vā 
svānyaprakāśaḥ svaprakāśaḥ| (l4)
 rama prakāśa iti yāvat| yathā divākaro lokānāṃ tamaḥ paṭalamutsāryātmaprakāśako bhūtvā 
svānyasūryakāntaśilādīnāṃ tejotpādakatvena (l5)
 paraprakāśako bhavati tadvat śiddhādhvavāsīnāmātmanāmāvaraṇabhūtamamalamapasārya svaśaktikiraṇātmakatvenādhiṣṭhāya 
svaprakāśako (l6)
 bhūtvā svasāmarthyakalpitānantādīnāmapi śivatejotpādakatvena paraprakāśakaśca bhavatītyarthaḥ acalaḥ calanarahitaḥ 
kāryakaraṇe kulālādi... (l7)
...svadehaspandanādirāhityāt saṅkalpamātreṇa sakalakaraṇāccalanarahita ityarthaḥ ameyaḥ meyarahitaḥ 
pratyakṣapramāṇādijñānenāparicchedyaḥ vāṅ(l8)
....sayoḥ panthānamatīta ityarthaḥ brahmaparaḥ vedāntaśāstrādipratipāditabrahmaṇaḥ paraḥ śreṣṭhaḥ brahmavidāpnoti paramiti 
śrutervidāmānatvāditi bhāvaḥ (p15a, l1)
 .....di svabhāvasiddhaḥ atulaḥ sādṛśyarahitaḥ sūkṣmaḥ sūkṣmarūpī mahān maha(hat)svarūpī aroraṇīyanmahato 
mahīyānityarthaḥ cidghanaḥ cicchabdena jñānakriyāśaktita-(l2)
....ghanaṃ dehasvarūpaṃ yasya cidghanaḥ baindavaśarīrādivajjaḍaśarīrarahita ityarthaḥ| vyāpī bindvāderavyāpakatvāt 
svavyāpakāntararahitatvācca vyāpaka(l3)
śi....duktaṃ ratnatraye vyāpako bhuvanādināmabhivyāptaḥ sabindunā| binduḥ śaktyā śivenaiṣā nānyena vyāpyate śiva 
iti muktasakhaḥ (l4)
 muktānāmātmanāṃ sakhā muktasakhaḥ mokṣakārītyarthaḥ sa śaktiḥ parāśaktyā yuktaḥ amalaḥ anādimalarahitaḥ 
apiśabdena nirmalasya sṛṣṭyādikaraṇamayukta(l5)
mityāśaṅkya mahā-saṃsārāt pūrvamārjitakarmaṇāmātmānāṃ tatkarmabhogāya sṛṣṭyādikaraṇaṃ yuktameveti jñāpanārthamapi 
śabdaḥ kṛta ityava(l6)
gantavyaḥ udyogavān sṛṣṭyādivyāpāravān brahmagaḥ brahmāṇi mūrtitatvabhūtapiṇḍasakalā brahmāṇi gacchatīti brahmagaḥ 
mūrtyādibrahmasvarūpamityarthaḥ (l7)
 tathā śuddhākhye mūrtibrahmatayā pūrvaṃ tatvabrahmadvitīyakaṃ bhūtabrahma tri(tṛ)tīyakaṃ syāt piṇḍabrahmacaturthakaṃ pañcamaṃ tu 
kalābrahma pañca-brahmasamīrita iti sākalyādipañca(l8)
praṇavo mūrtibrahma śaddhatale śuddhamūla-tatvādyātmanāmadheyāḥ pañcaprāsādāḥ tatvabrahma lavarayahakārakāḥ 
bhūtabrahmaśivabījajāta-sadyojātādipañcapiṇḍabrahmīśāḥ (p15b, l1)
 sarvavidyānāmityādikalābrahma īśānamūrdhnāyetyādinā vyomavyāpipadamadhyapaṭhitaṃ padabrahma vyomavyāpibhedena 
padabrahmāṇi bahuprakārāṇi brahmatvāt sarvatra (l2)
 vyāpakatvāt brahmasadāśivītanam| taduktaṃ bṛhatvāt brahmaṇatvācca brahma sadāśivītanuriti sādākhyaḥ 
sadāśivanāmadheyaḥ  site dhvani śuddhādhvani sakalādhipaḥ sakalānāmanantādī(l3)
nāmadhipaḥ svatantratayā kartā bhavatīti śeṣaḥ anantaḥ ananteśvaraḥ rudraśca śrīkaṇṭharudraḥ asite aśuddhādhvani ubhau 
adhipau bhavataḥ śuddhāśuddhaviṣaye anantaḥ aśuddhā(l4)
dhvaviṣaye śrīkaṇṭha ityarthaḥ paraśivāt sadāśivaḥ sadāśivādananteśaḥ anantāt śrīkaṇṭha iti paramparyamavagantavyaṃ 
tathoktaṃ śrīmatā jñānaśivena| (l5)
 agrāhi kila īśvaraḥ paraśivastasmāt sa deśaḥ śivo nādātmā sakalākalaḥ prabhurato'nanteśabhaṭṭārakaḥ 
tasmādaṇḍajaviṣṇuvāsavavidhi śrīkaṇṭha(l6)
rudrastadā tantrāṇāmavatārako munivaro durvāsanāmā gururiti||2||

parānapekṣaṃ śivasaṃjñamasya rūpaṃ parāpekṣamapi (l7)
śrutijñāḥ| śaktyākhyamāhustadabhinnarūpaḥ sa dharmīśaḥ sa ca dharmarūpaḥ| paraśivasadāśivayordharmadharmasvarūpayogena 
bhāvasvarūpa-mutpannamityāha(l8)
...parānapekṣamiti paraśivādanāhataśivaḥ anāhataśivāt sadāśivaḥ ata eva anāhataśivasāpekṣameva na tu 
paraśivāpekṣamityuktaṃ madhye vyavadhā(p16a, l1)
 nāditi bhāvaḥ parāpekṣaṃ paramapekṣata iti parāpekṣamapi śabdasamuccayārthaḥ paraśivānāhataśivayo vyomavyāpikatveneti 
niṣkalatayā aikyameva na (l2)
 bheda iti parāpekṣa-mityuktaṃ evaṃ vidhaṃ sadāśivasvarūpaṃ śrutijñāḥ āgamajñā na sahitācāryāḥ 
śaktākhyuparaśaktyotpannamityāhuḥ uktavantaḥ paraśaktiparaśivayoḥ (l3)
 samavedatayā tvabhedaṃ manya-mānairācāryaiḥ śaktyākhyamiti pratipāditamityarthaḥ| tadabhinnarūpaḥ tasmāt 
paraśivādabhinnarūpaḥ aikyarūpaḥ tasmāt kāraṇāditi (l4)
 vyākhyeyam īśa| sadāśivaḥ sadharmaḥ samāno dharmo yasya saḥ sadharmaḥ ākāśe vidyudrūpamiva 
sarvaprakāśamānamutpattivināśaśūnyaṃ caitanyā(l5)
diguṇayuktaṃ jyotirūpaṃ dharmaḥ sa ca paramaśivaḥ dharmarūpaḥ dharmo'-syāstīti dharmiṇo rūpaṃ yasya sa dharmarūpaḥ 
sadāśivaparaśivayoḥ dharma (l6)
 dharmarūpatvena sadāśiva| paraśivasya mūrtyākāreṇa sthitaḥ paraśivastu sadāśivasyāntastharūpeṇa sthitaḥ 
tayoryogeṇa yadrūpaṃ (l7)
 tadrūpameva bhāvatvāt sādākhyamiti kathyate| sarveṣāṃ dhyāna-pūjārthaṃ niṣkalaṃ sakalaṃ bhavet||3||

śaktiścittanurīśvarasya karaṇaṃ jñeyeṣu kārye ... (p16b, [10th folio from the beginning of the 
subject] l1)
 tadbhedaiḥ sopahitā prayāti guṇato bhedaṃ kriyā jñānataḥ nityaṃ tat sakalānyakartani hitāvartmyaśuddhe site kṛtyaṃ 
saiva karoti patyanugatā vāmā daśa.....(l2)
 itthaṃ paramaśivasadāśivayorddharmisvarūpayo-ryogenopacārabhūtabhāvākaraṃ darśayitvādhikārāvasthāṃ prāpptasya sadāśivasya 
jagatkārya..... (l3)
 svaśaktyātmakaṃ karaṇamastītyāha| śaktiriti cittanuḥ sadāśivasyāpi niṣkalatayā dehendriyādirahitasya 
jagatkartṛtvaṃ na ghaṭata iti svasāmarthyena sa.... (l4)
tīti sūcanārthaṃ śakterapi māyābindvoriva jaḍadharmarūpapariṇāmatvaṃ nāstīti jñāpanārthaṃ cittanurityuktaṃ 
māyābindvorupādānakā[ra]ṇatvāt (l5)
 samavāyikāraṇaṃ vinā jagadutpatyabhāvāt| uktaṃ ca bhojadevena kartā vinā na kāryaṃ na 
tathaivopādānakāraṇābhyāmiti (l6)
 ciccha...na jñānakriyāsvarūpaṃ caitanyaṃ gṛhyate cideva tanuḥ śarīrākṛtiryasyāḥ sā cittanuḥ śaktiḥ parāśaktiḥ 
īśvarasya sadāśivasya ...jñayeṣu (l7)
 jñātuṃ yogyeṣu viṣayeṣu kāryeṣu kartuṃ yogyeṣu vastuṣu ca karaṇaṃ sādhakamataṃ prakṛṣṭhopakārakaṃ jñānaśaktyā jñeyaṃ 
viṣayajñānāti(di)kriyā(l8)
śaktyā kāryaṃ vastu karotītyarthaḥ| jñānaṃ tāvadvividhaṃ savikalpakaṃ nirvikalpakaṃ ceti tatra savikalpakaṃ śivasya 
bhogādhikāraviṣayaṃ nirvikalpakaṃ layaviṣayaṃ savikalpaṃ (p17a, l1)
 nāmajātyādiyojanātmakaṃ nirvikalpakaṃ vastumātrāvagāhi|

tathoktaṃ pauṣkare -  vikalpayogāyogābhyāṃ tacca dvividhamiṣyate| vastusvarūpamātrasya grahaṇaṃ (l2)
 nirvikalpakam| tatraiva layaviṣayasya śkto'yaṃ śaktayo yasmādudyogādi-kriyā cyutāḥ śiva evāvatiṣṭhante 
niṣkalaśca sa eva tu upasaṃhṛtakāryātma.... (l3)
 ...durvyavasthitaḥ tadā layāhvayaṃ tatvaṃ śivatatvaṃ tadeva hi| tatraiva savikalpakasya nāmajātyādisaṃbandhasahitaṃ 
savikalpakam etaccendriyasāpekṣaṃ (l4)
 nirapekṣaṃ tathaiva ca| karmendriyānapekṣaṃ ca sarvadhā(thā) tyaktabandhayā| cicchaktyā nikhilārtheṣu yoga-svābhāviko 
mata iti| kriyāpi dvividhā (l5)
 saṅkalparūpākaraṇarūpā ceti tatra paraśivasya muktātmanāṃ ca ādyā dvitīyā bandhātmanām| 

taduktaṃ pauṣkare – na hi saṅkalpamātreṇa kulālaiḥ kriyate ghaṭāḥ (l6)
 śivasaṅkalpamātreṇa bindukṣobhakaraḥ sadā adhikāre kriyodraktā kāryeṣvadhikṛtā yataḥ kāryaṃ ca karaṇīyaṃ ca 
guṇasaṅkalpasaṃsmṛtiḥ..... (l7)
pāra ityuktā vibhostadviṣayīkṛtiḥ tasmāt jñātṛtvakartṛtve jñānarūpe iti sthita iti tasmādviśvasya 
guṇasmaraṇasaṅkalpa eva kriyā śivasya sā pañcadhā ...... (l8)
dena sā śaktiḥ tadbhedaiḥ teṣāṃ jñeyakāryādīnāṃ bhedaiḥ nānāprakāraiḥ saha upahitā prāptā sati guṇataḥ apradhānataḥ 
kriyā jñānataḥ kriyā ..... (p17b, l1)
ktyādita atra jñānādita ityādiśabdo lupto draṣṭavyaḥ ādiśabde ...cchāśaktivāmādiśaktayo gṛhyante bhedaṃ 
nānāprakāraṃ yātigacchati| ya....gne chakti......(l2)
lohakāṣṭhalavaṇodanādi viṣayopādhibhedena drāvikā dāhyakā sphāṭikā pācikābhidhānamupaiti tathā śivaśaktirekāpi 
yāti| śivaśaktirūpābhi... (l3)
 mṛgendre| karaṇaṃ ca na śaktyanyat śaktirnācetanā citaḥ| viṣayā niyamādekaṃ bodhe kṛtye ca tattathā| asyārthaḥ 
śaktyanyakaraṇaṃ na śaktyā.... (l4)
tam| sādhakatamaṃ śivasya nāsti samaveta-śaktireveti tātparyaṃ citaḥ śaktiracetanā na caitanyasvarūpetyarthaḥ 
viṣayāniyamāt kāryāne....yathā (l5)
 bodhe jñāne tathā kṛtye ca kārye ca tatkaraṇamekameva tat tasmāt kāraṇāt śaktikaraṇasvarūpādityarthaḥ 
vātādiśaktyā(l6)
tmikā vāmādinavaśaktisvarūpā ādiśabdena jyeṣṭhādayo grāhyante patyanugatapateḥ paramaśivasyānugatā samavetā saiva 
parāśaktiḥ si.... (l7)
 śuddhe vartmanyadhvani nityaṃ sārvakālaṃ kṛtyaṃ pañcakṛtyaṃ karoti| sadāśivādideva(ve)ṣu manonmanyādisvarūpeṇa 
parāśaktirbhā(rhā)ryārthaṃ yātīti jñāpanārthaṃ patisama(l8)
vatetyuktam|

tathā kāmike – mūrtiḥ sadāśivī yatra kalpyate tatra kalpitā| tadā manonmanīṃ saṃjñāṃ labhate vipra prabhavāḥ 
evakārastu kriyā kuṇḍalinyoḥ paryāyanāmadheyā (p18a, l1)
 kṛtvā kriyeti kuṇḍalinīśaktireva nānyeti vicāryamāṇānāṃ ko vāñcidācāryāṇā...sya bahiṣkaraṇārthaṃ kṛta 
ityavagantavyaḥ tathoktamācāryaiḥ| kecidācakṣate (l2)
 binduḥ samavetā śive tataḥ dṛkśaktivat kriyāśaktiriyaṃ kuṇḍalinī parā dve śakti samavāyinyau śive 
jñānakriyātmike| tatra cādyāhvayā sañcit kriyā kuṇḍalinī parā| jñānaśaktyā vijānāti (l3)
 kriyāyā kurute jagat| kriyā hi phaladā puṃsāṃ naṃ jñānaṃ syāt phalapradam| na tābhyāṃ virahastasasya te ca na 
staḥ śivaṃ vineti kṣīrasya dadhyākāreṇa kuṇḍinyāḥ (l4)
 pariṇāmatvena samayā yaḥ śivena ghaṭata iti kṛtvā kriyāśakteḥ kuṇḍinyā saha paryāyanāmadheyo na saṃbhavatīti 
manyamānairācāryaiḥ kriyāśaktikaṃ ... (l5)
tiyā mantrāṇāmutpattiḥ śivasya parigrahavartiniḥ kriyātmiketi pratipāditaḥ na kuṇḍalinīśaktiḥ taduktaṃ 
mantrayonirmahāmāyā yā parigrahavarti.......... (l6)
ttirātrānnāyayā sarve'pi pudgalāḥ seyaṃ kriyātmikā śaktiraiśvarī sarvadoditeti| ayaṃ prapañcastrilocanaśivācāryairuktaḥ 
....... (l7)
tmani tatvau samanekabhuvanāvalim| vidadhadvividhāna(nā)lpatanu-bhogendriyādikaṃ bindurākhyāyate yuktyā pariṇāmā 
pradhānavat pariṇāmi ca .... (l8)
 tadavśyaṃ jaḍātmakaṃ yathā kṣīraṃ jaḍatve'sya samavāyaḥ kathaṃ śive| sahitā-dātmyasaṃbandhāt jaḍena kaḍimāvahaḥ 
śivasyāmupamākhaṇḍacidghanaikasvarūpiṇastvevaṃ manyate mohā tu (p18b, l1)
 jaḍadhī jaḍamīśvaram| na tasya yuktiḥ śāstraṃ vā nāma nāsti ca devatā| sa varjanīyo vidvadbhiḥ 
sarvāstikabahiṣkṛta iti ata eveyaṃ kriyāśaktiḥ śuddhādhvavartinā mama kṛta mantre …. (l2)
ṇāṃ vijñānakevalānāṃ bhogasadbhāvena malamāyākarmarūpā sati vartate| kathamadhikāranibandhanamalalakṣaṇena paśutvena 
vyavasthitā paśuṣu vartate| nānāvidha(l3)
bhogabhuvaneṣu paśūn prerayati| ataḥ karmakāryamiyameva karoti śuddhādhvavāsināṃ tāratamyabhogavaicitryanibandhanaṃ 
malaparipāka..... (l4)
karmavivakṣitaṃ na tu dharmādharmaṃ svarūpaṃ tasya duḥkhasahitasukhahetutvenātrāsaṃbhavatvāt 
sukhaikahetubhūtadharmādharmavilakṣaṇamanyadeva karmāṅgī(l5)
kartavyaṃ tacca kalpanālāghavāt malaparipākākhyameveti vaktuṃ yuktaṃ māyeti śuddhādhvani śrūyamāṇasya śuddhadehendriyādeḥ 
śuddhopādānabhūto (l6)
 bindureva sādhyate tamimaṃ binduṃ parameśvaraḥ sākṣāt svasamavetayā cicchaktyā kṣobhayati sā ca cicchaktirekāpi 
trividhopādhibhedena laya-(l7)
bhogādhikārakāraṇādityucyate| aupādhikabhedayā tayā śivo layabhogādhikāravāniti vidha ucyate| yathākramaṃ 
śivaḥ sadāśivodhīśaśceti saṃjñaśca bhava(l8)
ti| triprakāra eva śivasya vyāpāraḥ adhikāro bhogo layaśceti tatrādhikriyate śivenetyadhikārasṛṣṭisthityātmako 
vyāpāraḥ bhogaśca bhuṅkṣyate pā(l19a, l1)
lyata iti vā saṃhārottara-bhāvijagadupādānādipālanātmako vyāpāraḥ ātmanāṃ yathānuguṇabhogabhojanadānaṃ vā 
tirobhāva ityarthaḥ| layastu layanaṃ ... (l2)
ṣṭopasaṃhāralakṣaṇo vyāpāraḥ sa ca dvividhaḥ punarutpatyātmako punarutpatyātmakaśceti tatra punarutpatyātmakastu 
kāryasyopādanakāraṇe śaktiśeṣa(l3)
tā punarutpatyātmakastu śaktipātavināśitaprasavadharmiṇāṃ kāryakāraṇādibandhanāṃ śakterupasaṃhāreṇa layo mokṣalakṣaṇaḥ 
saṃhāra(l4)
svarūpānugraha iti yāvat yadvā bindau kṣobhayogyatāmāpanne sati kṣobhāyodyuktamātreṇa devasya bhogaḥ yadā 
bindurudyogadaśāmatikramya (l5)
 kāryātmanā pariṇamate tadā devasyādhikāraḥ tatkāryasya hetubhūtatvāt yadā bindurbhogādhikāradaśāṃ vidhūya 
sakalasvakāryajālaṃ garbhīkṛtya (l6)
 vartate| tadā śivasya layaḥ tannimittatvāt| yadvātikrāntabindukaḥ kevalacidrūpaḥ śiva eva layasaṃjñāṃ labhate| 
ayamevābhiprāyaḥ layabhogādhi(l7)
kāraṇāṃ vyāpāro bindugarbhata ityuktaḥ tena bindunā saha cicchaktirupacārāt śivaina vāpi layabhogādhikāriṇī 
bhavati tathā tayāsaha (l8)
 śivaḥ śaktiṃ vihāya nāpravartata iti niyamāt kartṛkaraṇalakṣaṇasaṃbandhāt śivo'pi layabhogādhikāravān 
vyapadṛśyate| yathā dāhakriyā......... (p19b, l1)
 prajvalato vahniḥ dāhakaḥ tatprayoktā devadattaśca dāhaka ityucyate tadvat śivo layasaṃjñāṃ labhata ityarthaḥ evameva 
trilocanaśivairuktaṃ tatra kṣubdho bhavet| (l2)
 bhogo bindāvānandarūpiṇi| kṣubdhe'dhikāro devasya layo'tikrāntabindukaḥ| e kaivalakhu(vu)cicchaktiḥ śivasya 
samavāyini| trivodhopādhisaṃbhedāllayabhogādhi(l3)
kāriṇi tathaiva tayābhisaṃbaddhādeko devastridhā bhavet| śivaḥ sadāśivo'dhīśo laya-bhogādhikāravāniti| 
vāmādiśaktyātmikā saiva (l4)
parāśaktiḥ kṛtyaṃ karotītyuktam etena vāmādiśaktīnāṃ pṛthivyādyaṣṭamūrtyākāreṇa śuddhādhvaviṣaye pṛthivyādikāryaṃ 
kurvatīti dyotitaṃ bhavati| tadyathā| (l5)
 vāmā pṛthivīmadhijyeṣṭhā jalarūpiṇī raudryagnyākārā kālī vāyavyākārā kalavikaraṇyākāśarūpiṇī candrākārā 
balavikaraṇī arkākārā (l6)
 balapramathanī ātmākārā sarvabhūtadamanī manonmanī parāśaktirūpā etābhiḥ śaktibhiḥ pṛthivyādikāryaṃ 
kurvatītyarthaḥ| tathā coktaṃ kṣmādimūrtitadī(l7)
śānāṃ vāmādyāśaktayastatāḥ vāmaśabdena saundaryamuktaṃ pañcaguṇānvitaṃ saṃpūrṇaguṇavatvena jalādīnāmapekṣayā jalādīnāṃ 
catustridvyekaguṇatvā.... (l8)
rṇitā| vāmākārā-pātthivi(pṛthvi)tatvabhūtā vāmātmikā yathā sṛṣṭipra......vāmā sā śarvaḥ kṣitimūrtipaḥ sa eva 
vāmadevastacchaktirvāmeti gīyate| (p20a, l1)
 jyeṣṭhā jalamayī tatra svityupādānarūpiṇī| vivastadīśaḥ sa jyeṣṭhā jyeṣṭhātaḥ śa......tā| agneśca 
rudrarūpatvādraudrāgneyī tadīśvaraḥ paśupatiḥ sa vai rudro raudrī tacchaktiriṣyate| ....ṇastu (l2)
 kālarūpatvāt kālī śaktistu vāyavī vāyumūrtipa īśānaḥ sa kāla iti viśrutaḥ kālī tacchaktirākāśamayī 
kalavikaraṇī yataḥ kalā avayavāsteṣāṃ śunyā vikaraṇīyata (l3)
 vyomno vayavaśūnyatvāt sā tadvikaraṇī yathā| vigatakṣepaṇaṃ tena kalābhāvastu sa strītaḥ bhīmo gaganamūrtiśaḥ 
kalavīkaraṇa īritaḥ tacchaktikalavikaraṇacāndra ca (l4)
 balapūrvikā vikaraṇī ca balaḥ śaktikāraṇaṃ vai viśeṣataḥ| sudhāṃśo ro vidhīśatvānmahādevastadīśvaraḥ sa vai 
balavikaraṇastadīśi balapūrvikā| vikaraṇyaka| mayī (l5)
 śaktibala-pramathanī yataḥ arkasya caṇḍaraśmitvādrudrā vai arkamūrtipaḥ sa vai balapramathanaḥ pāśadrāvakam īśvaraḥ 
balapramathanī tasya sarvabhūtadamanyathā|| (l6)
 ātmīyapuṇyapāpau bhogena tama ityataḥ ātmā vai sarvabhūtāni yajamānastu sa smṛtaḥ sā sarvabhūtadamanī śaktirevaṃ 
prakīrtitā| śaivī manonmanīśaktiḥ (l7)
 samapāpaṃ paśūṃ yataḥ paraṃ nirvyāsakaraṇaṃ śivatvāya nayatyaṇum| manonmanīti sā proktā tadantasthaḥ paraḥ śiva 
iti asmin śuddhādhvani vartinaḥ sākṣāt parameśvarā nigraha(l8)
yogyā vijñānakevalāḥ pakvāpakvamalabhedena dvividhā bhavanti tatra pakvamalānantadayoṣṭau apakvamalāstu 
saptakoṭimahāmantrā vidyānāmadheyāḥ (p20b, l1)
 etān parameśvaraḥ śuddhādhvaśuddhatatvakaraṇabhuvanādisaṃbandhena sisṛkṣyatayā śuddhabhogapradānena sthāpanīyatayā tadvighaṭanena 
saṃhāryatayā sarvadhā tanmūlanenānugrā(l2)
hya tayā karaṇabhūtayā cicchaktyā yuktaḥ pañcakṛtyakaro bhavatītyarthaḥ evaṃ śuddhādhvaviṣayabhūtakṛtyaṃ darśayitvā 
cāśuddhādhvaviṣaye kathaṃ bhūtādhikṛtyaṃ ka...rātītyārhasaka(l3)
leti kalābhiḥ karmādibhiḥ ṣoḍaśakalābhiḥ savarṇatā sakalakarmabuddhitrayaṃ manohaṅkāravidyāvākkṛtyaśaktayaḥ 
ṣoḍaśakalāḥ puṇyapāpa(l4)
bhedena karma dvividhaṃ rājasatāsamasātvikabhedena buddhistridhā| tathā manohaṅkārau jñānakarmabhedenendriyadvividhaṃ 
vidyāvākśaktibhiḥ saha (l5)
 kalā ṣoḍaśakalā saṅkhyā uktaṃ ca karaṇasthena bhedena svacityāpi ca durdaśa – vākśaktibhedau dvau ceti puṃskalāḥ 
ṣoḍaśāsmṛtāḥ iti athavā karmabuddhima(l6)
pādanena kṛtyaṃ karotītyarthaḥ puryaṣṭakaṃ nāma pratipuruṣaniyamena sṛṣṭikālādārabhya mahāpralayāntaṃ mokṣāntaṃ vā 
vidyante| pṛthivītatva-mārabhya sakalāntaṃ triṃśattatvātmako (l7)
sādhāraṇarūpasūkṣmadehaḥ tathoktaṃ tatvasaṅgrahe| vasudhādyastatvagaṇaḥ pratipuṃniyatasalānto'yamiti ṣoḍaśakalāmadhye 
sthitena manaśśabdena puṃso bhogakriyāyāmantara(l8)
ṅgāṇi kalākālaniyatiḥ (p21a, l1)
 rāgaprakṛtiguṇākhyāni ṣaṭtatvānyucyante| buddhikarmaśabdābhyāṃ tadadhyavasitatayā grāhyāṇyākāśādīni pañcabhūtāni 
tatkāraṇāni śabdādīni pañcatanmātrāṇyucyante tathoktaṃ (l2)
 bhojadevena pralayākaleṣu yeṣāṃ pakve malakarmaṇi vrajantyanye puryaṣṭakadehayutāḥ yoniṣu nikhilāsu karmavaśāt iti 
atra yoniśabdena bhuvanānyucyante na tu mātrayoniḥ (l3)
 sthānāni anyakartṛri(ra)hitā anye ca te kartārāścānyakartārasteṣvanyakartṛṣu ananteśvaraśrīkaṇṭha-viṣṇubrahmādiṣu 
nihitā sthitā sati aśuddhevātmani aśuddhādhvaviṣa(l4)
ye pañcakṛtyaṃ karoti śuddhādhvaviṣaye patisamavetā aśuddhā-dhvaviṣaye anantādidvāreṇa sthitā cicchaktireva pañcakṛtyaṃ 
karotītyarthaḥ||4|| (l5.)

dehānyaḥ samalaḥ paśuḥ sahajacinnaikaḥ prabodhākalaḥ seśaśca pralayākalaḥ kṛtimalī ṛkkarmamāyī vibhuḥ sūkṣmāṅgī 
sakalolpavit svakṛtabhuk kartā ca nityaḥ prabhurde(l6)
hi bhuktakṛtiḥ krameṇa sucirāccharveṇa mukto bhavet| itthaṃ patipadārthabhedamuktvā idānīṃ paśupadārthabhedaṃ 
vaktumupakramate| dehānya iti dehādi(l7)
bhyo'nyo dehānyaḥ dehendriyādi-vyatiriktaḥ sahajacitsvābhāvikacaitanyasvarūpaḥ caitanyaṃ dṛkkriyārūpaṃ 
tadastyātmani sarvadetyuktabhāk tathāpi (l8)
 sa malaḥ anādimalabaddhaḥ malaśaktyā niruddha-citsvarūpītyarthaḥ tathā coktaṃ śivatvamātmānaḥ śuddhirvidyamānamapi 
sphuṭaṃ nātmalābhaṃ labhet puṃsāṃ malāttadavarodhakā(p21b, l1)
....ranantaraṃ mokṣaprado bhavati kalāyoge'pi no'vaśyāḥ kalānāṃ paśusaṅghavadityuktatvāt| etebhyo'nyetyata(taḥ) 
pakvakarmamalamiśrādhvavāsino saṅkhyāta......(l2)
...meya mokṣapradaḥ parameśvaraḥ sakalāstu paripakvakarmamalamāyābrahmāṇḍābhyantare sthitatadbhuvanavāsinaḥ eteṣāṃ paripakvaṃ 
tu karmasāmyataḥ śaktipātānusāreṇa (l3)
 saṃsāre dveṣaḥ muktau śraddhā śive bhaktiḥ guruśi(śu)śru(śrū)ṣādayo yeṣāṃ jāyante paripakvāsta eva 
etaccintanarāhityamevāparipakvānāṃ paripakvānā..... (l4)
 parameśvaro gurumūrtimāsthāya paripakvamalāditāratamyato mantreśvarādipadaṃ datvā mokṣaprado bhavati||5||

nihāro nādi...komarajasaṃ ... (l5)
 bhavastāmrakākālikeva svādhikārāntanāśipratipuruṣadragā vārakānekaśaktiḥ pāśyaḥ(śyā) śodhyaśca bodhyaḥ sa tu 
bhavati yato bhoktṛtā(vyā)pāratantryakarmā(l6)
deryoga etatkṛtimanukalitā rādhayitri śa(sa)śaktiḥ| atha malādipāśapadārthabhedamāha| nīhāra iti nīhāro malaḥ 
malaparyāya-nāmadheyeṣū(l7)
ktatvāt| 

tathoktaṃ mṛgendre – paśutva-paśunīhāra-mṛtyu-mūrchā-malāñjanaiḥ avidyā pratiruglānipāpamūlakṣayādibhiriti| 
anādisvābhāvikaḥ kāraṇā(l8)
ntarasyābhāvādanāditvaṃ siddhaṃ kārmyamāyīyabandhanavadāgantukaṃ na(bha)vatītyarthaḥ ata ekaḥ jaḍabhāve satyapi 
nityatvādanyathānekatvāt ghaṭādivadanityaṃ syāt manujasahabhavaḥ (l9)
 manujānāmātmanāṃ sahabhavaḥ sahotpannaḥ atra sahabhavaśabdenātmā yadā jātastadā malo jāta iti nāśaṅkanīyam 
ātmanaḥ paramārthato janmābhāvāt sahabhavaśabdenātra (p22a, l1)
 tenātmanānādi-siddhenāntarabhūto malo vastutvena kathyate| cakṣuṣaḥ paṭalādivat vastubhūta eva malasyāvastutve 
śaśaviṣāṇādivadabhāvaprasaṅgāt| sarveṣāṃ mūlanivṛttihetu(l2)
bhūtānāṃ dīkṣājñānādināmāna...kyaprasaṅgāt anāditvādīn dṛṣṭāntena dṛḍhīkaroti tāmrakākālike veti tāmre 
vidyamānā kālimātvanādirekāsahajātadvaṃ(tvaṃ) malasyāpyanādi(l3)
siddham| tāmrasya malinatvādisiddhau kāraṇābhāvatvāt anādibhāvatvādi siddham|

tathoktaṃ kiraṇe – anādimalasaṃbandhānmalinatvamaṇau sthitam anādimalamuktatvā(l4)
nnirmalatvaṃ śive sthitam| īdṛgrūpaṃ sthitaṃ tābhyāṃ śuddhāśuddhaṃ yathārthataḥ viśuddhasphaṭikaḥ tasmāt tasmāttāmraṃ 
sakālikaṃ yathāsminnimittaṃ hi tāvāneva śivātmanoriti (l5)
 tāmrakālikādṛṣṭāntena yathā tāmrasyāvārikā kālikā rasaśaktyā viddhā nivṛttā bhavati| tathātmāvārako malaḥ 
śivaśaktyā nivṛtto bhavatītyarthaḥ yathā taṇḍu(l6)
lasya tuṣāṅkurotpattau nimittaṃ tathā malopyātmanaḥ śarīrādyutpattau nimittam ātmā vyāpakaḥ tadācchādakatvāt 
samalopyatyantaṃ vyāpkaḥ ata eva nitya(l7)
bhūtamalātmanādātva(tma)yoranādyā vārakatvalakṣaṇasaṃbandhasadbhāvāt kadācidapi viśleṣo na saṃghaṭata iti 
svābhāvikādeva nirmokṣaprasaṅge... (l8)
 jāta iti viśantyāha svādhikārāntanāśipratipuruṣadṛgā vārakānekaśaktiriti svasyādhikāraḥ malādhikāra 
ityarthaḥ ātmacaitanyachādakatvasva(l9)
.....dhikāraḥ tasyānte avasāne malasya dravyatvāccakṣuṣaḥ paṭalāderiva pākaḥ saṃbhavatīti paripākāvasthāyāmiti 
yāvat tasminnāśe yāḥ samastāḥ svādhikārāntānā.... (p22b, l1)
lasyaikarūpatvāt nāmātmanāṃ madhye ekātmanaḥ paripākāvasthāyāḥ śivaśaktyāṃ nivṛtyātmano malaḥ sarvātmabhyo 
nivṛtyata iti kṛtvaikātmano mokṣaprasaṅgaḥ sarvātma(l2)
nāṃ mokṣaprasaṅgo jāta ityāśaṅkyāha pratipuruṣadṛgā vārakānekaśaktiriti pratipuruṣāṇāṃ pratyātmanāṃ dṛgā vārakāḥ 
anekaśaktayaḥ bahalasāmarthyā yasya sta(ta)tho(l3)
ktaḥ nityasyāprerakasya malasyānityāḥ pratipuruṣasthāḥ jñānācchādakāḥ anekāḥ śaktayo'trānveṣṭavyaḥ anyathā 
ekatve satyekasmā-dātmanopasṛte male (l4)
 sarveṣāṃ tulyakālamokṣaṃ syāt yato mokṣe yaugapadyaṃ na saṃbhavati tasmāt kāraṇārthamalasya 
śaktibahalatvamabhyupagantavyaṃ vyāpakasya malasya (l5)
 nityatvakābhyupagamādyathāgnerdāhikāśaktistambhanādimantreṇāśu nirudhyate| tadvadīśvarānugrāhakabhūtayā śaktyā 
malaśaktiṃ nirudhyata iti kṛtvopacāreṇa malaṃ (l6)
 puruṣādviśliṣṭa iti kathyata ityarthaḥ|

tathoktaṃ kiraṇe – vibhorapi malasyāsya tacchakteḥ kriyate vadhaḥ upāyācchaktisaṃrodhaḥ kathañcit kriyate male| 
(l7)
 yathāgnerdāhikāśaktirmantreṇāśu nirudhyate tadvattacchakti-saṃrodhādviśiṣṭa iti kathyate| iti śivaśaktyāpi 
svābhāvikamalaparipākāvasthāyāmeva (l8)
 malaśaktiṃ nirvartyate yathā taṇḍulācchādakastuṣādiḥ paripākāvasthāyāmeva nirvartyate anyathā na nivartyate 
tadvadityarthaḥ yathā taṇḍulasya paripākāvasthā yanmusalā(l9)
dighātena taṇḍulasyāpi kṣayaḥ tathā malaparipākāvasthāyāṃ nirvartyamāne sati cātmano vikāritvaprasaṅgāt iti 
nāśaṃ(p23a, l1)
kanīyam|

tathoktaṃ kiraṇe – saujā kālikā tāmre tatkṣayānna ca tatkṣayaḥ yadvat tāmre kṣayastadvat puruṣasya malakṣaya iti 
tāmrakālikāyāṃ paripākāvasthārasa(l2)
saṃyogakāla eva jāyata iti mantavyam ata eva paripākāvasthāyāṃ malaśaktikṣayaḥ spha(sphu)ṭīkṛtaḥ|

tathoktaṃ mṛgendre – tadekaṃ sarvabhūtānāmanādinibiḍamahatpratyātmasthasvakālāntā(l3)
pāśaśaktisamūhavaditi athātmano malasadbhāvena pāśyādivṛttibhedamāha| yato malasadbhāvāt sa tu paśuḥ 
pāśādanapetaḥ pāśyaḥ malādinā prerya (l4)
 ityarthaḥ ata eva śodhyaḥ śodhanabhūtaviḍadhvasvarūpayā dīkṣayā śodhituṃ yogya ātmano mānasādyantaḥ 
karaṇavirahe'pi malāvṛtatvāt śodhya ityarthaḥ| (l5)
 śodhyatvānantaraṃ bodhyaḥ śāstrātmanā karaṇena sarvajñatvādiyuktaśivajñānena bodhayituṃ yogyaṃ sahajasarvadoṣavināśe'pi 
ātmanaḥ sammātreṇa (l6)
 vidyamānatvāt sarvajñatvādiguṇanāśābhāvāt bodhya ityarthaḥ| bhavatīti sarvatra saṃbandhaḥ yato malāt sa paśuḥ 
pāśyo bhavati śodhyaśca bhavati (l7)
 kramāt pāśyādivṛttīnāṃ malanivṛttau nivṛttiḥ siddhā|

tathā coktaṃ kiraṇe – tatsvabhāvāt paśuḥ pāśyaḥ śodhyo bodhyo matastviha| pāśyādivṛttayo (l8)
 yāsu tasya bhedā vyavasthitāḥ male sati bhavantyetā bhoktṛtvaṃ ca na kevalamiti karmādisaṃbandhepyātmano 
malasadbhāvādeva bhoktṛtvaṃ pāratantryaṃ ca saṃbhavatīti (l9)
 saṃbandhaḥ nanvātmanaḥ karmaṇeva bhoktṛtvaṃ vidyate bhoge'pi rāgādihetuḥ kuto malenara saṃbandhaḥ atrāyaṃ parihāraḥ 
yathā loke puruṣasya ca (p23b, l1)
 sandṛṣṭvāyaṃ puruṣaścauryayukta iti rigalādinā badhyate tathātmāpi malayukta iti rāgādinā badhyate anyathā kuto 
rāgādinā saṃbaddhaḥ ata eva malasya (l2)
 sākṣannimittatvaṃ bhoktṛtāyāṃ proktaṃ pāraṃparyeṇa māyābandhaḥ dehotpattihetuḥ karmabandhaḥ sukhaduḥkhātmakabhogahetuḥ|

tathā coktaṃ kiraṇe – ca.... (l3)
 hi bījamāvekṣya yathā nigalabandhanaṃ tathā paśutvamāvekṣya rāgatatvaṃ pravartate paśutvena hi bhoktṛtvaṃ māyābandhastanau 
sthitaḥ sukhaduḥkhātmako (l4)
 bhogaḥ karmaṇaḥ saṃsthitaḥ paśoḥ nānyathāsya vinirdiṣṭaṃ bhogabhoktṛtvabandhanamiti malityakādevātmanaḥ 
karmādibhojane pāratantryaṃ siddhaṃ malaśaktayaḥ (l5)
 kimātmānaṃ svātantreṇa bandhayantyapasaranti veti tathā na bhavatītyāha| etat kṛtimati etasya malasya kṛtiṃ 
kāryaṃ bandhanāpasaraṇabhūtam anukali(l6)
tā anukaraṇabhūtā svayameva bandhayitri apasaraṇayitrīti bhāvaḥ rodhayitri tirodhānabhūtām īśaśaktiḥ 
śivaśaktirastīti śeṣaḥ atrāpi (l7)
 śivaśakti svayaṃ kartuṃ na bhavati śivasyaiva kartṛtvādata eva īśaśakti-rityuktaṃ śaktiḥ karaṇaṃ śivaḥ karteti 
prasiddhaṃ iyameva śivaśaktiryadā prasannā (l8)
 bhavati tadānugrāhiketyucyate|

tathā coktaṃ mṛgendre – tāsāṃ maheśvarīśaktiḥ sarvānugrāhikā śivā| dharmānuvartanādeva pāśa ityupacaryate| 
tāsā(p24a, l1)
 miti malaśaktīnāṃ madhyata ityadhyāhāryaṃ tatra malaprakaraṇe uktatvāt pariṇāmayati tāśca rodhāntaṃ kārkaci 
kviṣāyadonmīlanamādhatte tadānugrāhikocyate tā iti (l2)
 malaśaktiḥ kaḥ itīśvaraḥ sa evārkaḥ tasya cikliṣā jñānatejasāśritena tirodhānaśaktirevopacāreṇa pāśasvarūpeti 
yāvat||6||

dehādyairyogahetu(l3)
rvividhamapi ca nuścitra(nu citra)bhogaprasūtirnāśotpatyāpyanādipratinaraniyataṃ sūkṣmabhāvadṛṣṭam| vādhgikāyārthasādhyaṃ 
phalajananadharaṃ karma yamādi(l4)
yogyaṃ svāpe māyāptapākaṃ sthitimati sahitaṃ bhogadaṃ syāt kalāntam| itthaṃ 
vijñānakalātmaviṣayamalarodhākhyapāśaḥ yamuktvedānīṃ pralayau (l5)
 kalasakalātmaviṣayabhūtaṃ karma pāśalakṣaṇamāha| dehādyairiti ādiśabdena indriyārthau gṛhyatetyetenāyamarthaḥ sampannaḥ 
dehena sthūlasūkṣma(l6)
rūpaśarīreṇendriyaiḥ mānasādijñānendriyakarmendriyairarthairindriyārtha-bhūtaśabdādiviṣayairyogahetuḥ yogasya cidātmanāṃ 
dehendriyā(l7)
rthasaṃbandhasya hetuḥ kāraṇabhūtaḥ cidātmanāṃ dehādisaṃbandhaḥ pratyakṣeṇa dṛśyate| ata eva dehasaṃbandhaḥ kuto bhavati 
karmaṇe veti prasādhya (l8)
 dehādisaṃbandhe karmaiva kāraṇamityanumīyate anyathā dehādisaṃbandho na bhavedityarthaḥ itthaṃ kāraṇamuktvā tadviśeṣaṃ 
darśayitumāha| vividhamityādinā vividhaṃ dehakriyārthairātmanaṃ saṃbandheṣu nānāprakārabhūtaṃ prāṇināṃ nānāvidhāni 
śarīrāṇi ma(i)ndriyāṇi vāṣayāśca dṛśyante tatra śarīrāṇi tāvadaivā(p24b, l1)
surakinnaragandharvanarapiśācapaśvādisurakinnaragandharvanarapiśācapaśvāditiryagehāḥ nareṣu brāhmaṇakṣatriyavaiśyā(ya)śu(śū)drādi 
indriyāṇi tāvat gajānāṃ parāvṛttiji(jī)vatvaṃ sarpāṇāṃ (l2)
 cakṣuśravaṇatvam ulūkānāṃ divāndhakatvaṃ mārjālānāṃ rātrāvatiprakāśakatvaṃ narāṇāṃ rātrau kiñcit prakāśatvam|

tathoktaṃ kāmike - yathā vilakṣaṇaṃ ca cakṣukaṃ payanyabilāla(l3)
yoḥ iti anyatrāpi divāndhāḥ prāṇinaḥ kicidā(da)tra bandhāstathāpare| kiciddivā tathā rātrau prāṇina-
stulyadṛṣṭaya iti viṣayāstāvadyathā| cātakānāṃ (l4)
 meghapaṭalanipatatajjalakaṇopajīvatvaṃ haṃsamadhukarāṇāṃ kamalakiñjalkarasāsvādanaṃ paśvādīnāṃ tṛṇādyāhāratā 
devānāmamṛtānnatā na(l5)
rāṇāṃ vrīhyādyannatā api ca kiñcetyarthaḥ nuriti jātau ṣaṣṭhyekavacanaṃ nṛśabdenātmāno gṛhyante teṣāṃ citrabhogaprasūtiḥ 
nānāvidhabho(l6)
gotpattisthānabhūtaṃ yathā devānāṃ nānāvidhabhogakāraṇasvargādisthānaṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ bhūlokādisthānaṃ tatrāpi 
keṣāñcinnirapatitvaṃ (l7)
 keṣāñciddravyapatitvaṃ keṣāñcit daridratvaṃ nāśopatyā sahitamiti śeṣaḥ| pūrvoktadehendriyādināśajananābhyāṃ sahitam 
apiśabdena (l8)
 citrabhogādināṃ yadākadācidapi nāśotpattimatvaṃ dyotitam| ata eva pratinaraniyataṃ pratinarāṇāṃ pratipuruṣāṇāṃ 
niyataṃ karmānukūlajātyāyurbhogādi (p25a, l1)
niyamena yuktam atra tattadbhuvananivāsināṃ tattadbhuvanatāratamyato nānāprakārān nāśarūpān pratyātmaniyatān 
janmāntarabhāvinaśca dehendriyārthān pradātuṃ (l2)
 samartha ityarthaḥ anādimalapāśavat svābhāvikaṃ nanu karmabhogakaraṇanimittaṃ bhogastu sukha-duḥkhādirūpaḥ 
tannibandhanaṃ karma tasmādeva dehotpattau satyāṃ tadanantaraṃ karmā(l3)
rjanaṃ bhavati ata eva prathamasṛṣṭikāle śarīrādisaṃbandhaḥ kutaścit kāraṇādiṣyate| karmaṇā dehādisaṃbandho neṣyate| 
tasmāt kāraṇādeva karmaṇo'(l4)
nāditvaprasaṅgo na yukta iti vicāraḥ atrāyaṃ parihāraḥ prathamasṛṣṭikāle'pyātmānāṃ nānāvidhadehasaṃbandhakatvāt 
anāditvam anyathā nānāvidhadeha(l5)
saṃbandhaḥ kutaḥ samāyātaḥ|

tathoktaṃ kiraṇe – yadetat karma deveśa(śaḥ) proktaṃ bhoganibandhanaṃ karmārjanantanau satyāṃ sṛṣṭikāle tanuḥ kutaḥ 
yathānādi(l6)
rmalastasya karmāpyevamanādikaṃ yadyanādi na saṃsiddhaṃ vaicitryaṃ kena hetuneti kiraṇavṛttau bhagavatā rāmakaṇṭhena 
puruṣāṇāṃ hi sarvathā (l7)
 śarīrabhogavaicitryānyathānupapatyā karmaṇaḥ tatvasṛṣṭikāle'pi paśupakṣimṛgasarisṛpasthāvama- 
(ra)manuṣyādivaicitryaśuteḥ mahāpralaye'pi karma(l8)
siddhitaḥ pravāhā(ha)nādikarmaśarīrabandhaḥ siddha(dhya)tīti vyākhyātam ata eva bījāṅkuranyāyavaśāt 
karmaṇo'nāditvaṃ siddhameva sūkṣmabhāvāt kriyata iti karma (p25b, l1)
vyāpārajanakatvādātmani saṃhārarūpeṇa sthitasūkṣmarūpatvāddṛṣṭamityute| 

uktaṃ ca mṛgendre – karmavyāpārajanyatvāddṛṣṭaṃ sūkṣmabhāva iti yathā loke puruṣasya bhuvana(l2)
mātreṇa nirṇayiprāptiḥ vṛkṣamūlopasarpaṇamātreṇopariśākhādipatanenātmahāniḥ dharmādharmarūpeṇotpannametat phaladvayaṃ tasya 
dṛṣṭamityucyate| ata (l3)
 evādṛṣṭasvarūpaṃ vāgdhikāyārthasādhyaṃ vāk ca dhīśca kāryaścārthaścaitaiḥ sāthayituṃ yogyaṃ dhīśabdena mano buddhi(ddhiḥ) 
gṛhyate| arthaśabdena dhanadhā(l4)
nyādhanadhānyādayaḥ śivaviṣayastotrapāṭhādikaṃ vāgrūpaṃ śivadhyānaṃ manorūpaṃ śivayajanādikaṃ kāyarūpaṃ 
śivadhāmādinirmāṇamartharūpaṃ (l5)
 dhanena kriyamātvāt etaiḥ sādhanaiḥ sādhyamityarthaḥ phalajananadharaṃ phālājananaṃ ca dhārakaṃ ca bhogyaṃ cātmanāṃ 
karmaphalabhogāyaiva (l6)
 dehendriyabhuvanādīnāṃ ca janakaṃ niyatakālamātreṇaiva teṣāṃ dhārakaphalapradānadvāreṇa bhogyaṃ ca uktaṃ gṛgendre| janakaṃ 
dhārakaṃ (l7)
 bhogyamādhyātmāditrasādhanamiti dharmādidha.....| dharmasvarūpam ādiśabdena sahakāritvaṃ gṛhyate| 
acetanasyeśādyapekṣakatvāditi bhāvaḥ| (l8.)
 
tathā mṛgendre – īśavidyādyapekṣatvāt sahakāri taducyata iti karmākarmanāmadheyaḥ pāśaḥ svāpe sarvabhūtasaṃhārakāle 
māyāptapākaḥ māyayā prāptapariṇāmaṃ (l9)
 sthitimatisahitaṃ sthitibudhyā yuktaṃ māyāyāṃ saṃskārarūpatayā vartata ityarthaḥ māyāyāṃ saṃskārarūpeṇa 
vidyamānatvādeva bhuktyanantarakṣiyata iti mantra.... (p26a, l1)
 tathoktaṃ mṛgendre – māyāyāṃ vartate cāntenāsaktaṃ kṣayameti ceti anyatrāpi avaśyamanubhoktavyaṃ kṛtaṃ karma 
śubhāśubhaṃ nāśubhaṃ kṣīyat karma kalpakoṭiśatairapi karma sāmyakā(l2)
le'pi śivatvaṃ vyaktiḥ tathoktaṃ kiraṇe| same karmaṇi sañjāte kālāntaravaśāttataḥ| tīvraśakti-nipātena guruṇā 
dīkṣito yadā śivatvavyaktisaṃpūrṇaḥ saṃsārina punaḥ sadeti kalāntaṃ (l3)
 bhogadam – syat(syāt) pṛthvītatvamārabhya kalātatvaparyantaṃ tannivāsīnāmātmanāṃ sukhaduḥkhādibhogapradaṃ syāt| 
māyākhyaṃ citraśaktyā parigatamalaṃ kaṣṭavatmaika(l4)
bījaṃ nityaṃ karmāntaro dhī svakṛtinivasanaṃ tatkṛtervyākaṃ ca| kāryācitvaprakāśādacidaśivakaraṃ mohakaṃ svāpakāle 
sarvātmā vā sa evaṃ (l5)
 paramapi ca sitaṃ kāraṇaṃ bodhakaṃ tat| 

atha sakalātmaviṣayabhūtamāyāpāśalakṣaṇamāha – māyākhyamiti māyānāmadheyaṃ citraśaktāneka(l6)
sāmarthyena parihataṃ samavetaṃ akalaṃ sūkṣmarūpaṃ kaṣṭavatmaikabījaṃ kaṣṭavatmanaḥ aśuddhādhvanaḥ kalāditatvasthitabhuvanasya 
bhuvanajaśarīrasya cobhayātma(l7)
naḥ sarvasya jagataḥ ekabījamukhyopādānakāraṇam upādānakāraṇaṃ vinā jagadutpatyabhāvāt ekapadena 
māyākhyamekameveti pratipāditaṃ loke ghaṭapaṭādivat (l8)
 kāraṇajanyamanekamacena(tana)mutpattimatvena sahitaṃ dṛṣṭam idaṃ māyākhyamacetanamapi kāraṇajanyopattimatvābhāvena 
sthitaṃ tasmādekacetanasyānekatvādyu(p26b, l1)
pagamenānityatvaṃ siddhamityekameveti bhāvaḥ nanu māyākhyasyānekatvamaṅgīkriyatāṃ kimekātmakatvopādānena yathā 
loke upādānabhūtebhyo bahubhya..... (l2)
 saḥ paṭasyaikasyotpattirdṛṣṭā tathā sarvajagannirmāṇamanekasmāt kāraṇāt kriyatām atrāyaṃ parihāraḥ| tatra 
paṭepyupādānakāraṇaṃ tatvātmakamekameva ekasmā(l3)
deva kārpāsākhyadravyādutpannatvādevamekasmādeva māyākhyāt jagadutpattiḥ siddhā citraśaktimatvādeva 
citrabhivanādiracanā siddhā|

tathoktaṃ mṛgendre| (l4)
 paṭastantugaṇādyaṣṭaḥ sarvamekamanekatāḥ tadapyanekasmādevaṃ bījāt prajāyata iti nityaṃ mahāsaṃhāre'pi 
śivavadātmavanmāyāyāvasthānāt yadvakṣyati (l5)
 ...nte paśavaḥ suśaktiramalaḥ śambhuśca māyādvayamiti yadyanityā māyā tarhi jagadutpattirna syāt mahāsaṃhāre māyā 
nāśaścettarhi punaḥ sargārambhakāle (l6)
 kasmādutpādānājjagadutpadyeta tasmāt māyāyāṃ nityatvaṃ nu upādānābhāvena jagadabhāva iti māyāyāṃ nityatvaṃ 
nāṅagīkāryaṃ sṛṣṭeḥ karteśvaraḥ (l7)
sārgādau svasāmarthyādevopādānaṃ sṛjatīti naitadyuktaḥ upādānakāraṇe'pi upādānaṃ vinā netpattiriti upādānāntaraṃ 
draṣṭavyaṃ tasyāpyanityatvena (l8)
 sthāpāti syāt ata eva māyāyā nityatvaṃ saṃhārakāle śaktirūpeṇa sthitatvāt|  (p27a, l1)
 karmāntarodhikarmāntaṃ karmaṇāmadhikārāntaṃ ruṇaddhi badhvātīti karmāntaro'dhikarmaṇaḥ phaladātṛtvamevādhikāraḥ yathā 
kṛdādyupādānasya sahakāribhūta cakrādyapekṣakatvaṃ tadvanmāyāyā (l2)
 upādānatvena sahakāribhūtakarmāpekṣatvaṃ siddham| 

tathā mṛgendre - tadekamaśivaṃ bījaṃ jagataścitraśaktimat sahakāryadhikārāntasaṃrodhivyāpyanaśvaramiti svakṛtinivasanaṃ 
svakṛtau svakārye (l3)
 nivasatīti svakṛtinivasanaṃ māyāyāḥ kāryaṃ tāvadvividhaṃ bāhyamābhyantaraṃ ceti tatra sṛṣṭikāle 
kālāditatvasthitabhuvanādivyaktirūpeṇa vidyamānaṃ bāhyamābhyantaraṃ (l4)
 saṃhārakāle māyāgarbhe sākṣmākāreṇa vidyamānam eva dvivadhakāryeṇa vartata ityarthaḥ| 

uktaṃ ca mṛgendre – tadā dha.....ṇi kāryāṇi śaktirūpāṇi (l5)
 saṃsṛtau vikṛtau vyakti-rūpāṇi vyāpriyante'rthasiddhayaḥ iti tatkṛte svakāryasya vyāpakaṃ ca vyāpakābhāve 
samastabhuvanādirūpeṇa samantāt sthitanānāvidhakāryasyā(l6)
nutpattiḥ syādato māyāyā vyāpakatvamavaśyamaṅgīkartavyaṃ caitanyātmanaḥ sakāśādeva cidāmacidāmāvirbhavatito 
bhāvatvamityahitvaṃ pakṣaṃ (l7)
 niṣedhayituṃ māyāyāḥ caitanyābhāva evetyāha| kāryeti kāryācitvaprakāśāt kāryeṣu kalādikāryeṣu 
acitvaprakāśāt tanukaraṇabhuvanādirūpeṇa (l8)
 acetanatvaprakāśanāt acit acaitanyameva māyāyā yathā loke mṛdādyacetanopādānena (p27b, l1)
 acetanabhūtaghaṭādikāryaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ tadvanmāyākāryabhūtabhuvanā-dyacitvadarśanena bhuvanādyupādānabhūtamāyāyā acaitanyaṃ 
siddhameva|

uktaṃ ca mṛgendre – māyālakṣaṇapaṭale| (l2)
 tadacetanamevaṃ sya(syā)t kāryasyācitvadarśanāditi nanu caitanyādacaitanyaṃ jñāyeta tatra kiṃ niyamaḥ 
atatsavabhāvāt tatsvabhāvo-tpattistat svabhāvādatatsvabhāvādatatsvabhāvasyopatti(l3)
riti sarvaṃ sarvasmādutpadyata kāraṇābhāvādeva vahnivijñāpakasadhūmajñānena jalānumānaṃ kathaṃ na kriyate ata evānya-
liṅgādanyajñānaṃ kadācidapi (l4)
 na saṃbhavatīti caitanyādacaitanyasyotpattirna siddhā| uktaṃ ca yaiṣāṃ ciddharmakāddhetoracidapyupajāyate| teṣāṃ dhūmena 
liṅgena jalaṃ kiṃ (l5)
 nānumīyata iti| aśivakaraṃ śivajñānavyatiriktatvādevājñānakaram ata eva mohakaṃ ca| nanu māyāpi svato 
bandhakatvābhāvāt| kalādidvāreṇa (l6)
 bandhayitrī ca jñānakriyā prakāśikā cetyuktaṃ satyaṃ tat kathame māsikā māyotpannasya jñānādeḥ kiñcit 
jñātvādevānātmādau (l7)
 ātmādibhāvanājanakatvena mohakāramityarthaḥ| yathā mahāndhakārayukto dīpaḥ prakāśamāno'pi nīlotpalādau 
raktotpalādiviparītajñānaprakāśatvāvedavāndhakāra(l8)
yukto dīpo mohaproktaḥ tathā māyotpannajñānaṃ mohakameveti siddhaṃ svāpakāle saṃhārakāle sarvātmā vāsaḥ sarvātmānāṃ 
sakalapralayākalavijñā(p28a, l1)
nakalānāṃ sthityupāyasthārammā(mbhā)yā lakṣaṇamuktvā bindvākhyāyā mahāmāyāyā lakṣaṇamāha| evamityādinā evaṃ 
pūrvoktaprakārasvarūpaṃ paramabindvākhyaṃ api (l2)
 ce......lakṣaṇavat bindvyākhyasya viśeṣalakṣaṇamastīti dyotitam| tadviśeṣalakṣaṇamāha| sitaṃ śuddhādhvarūpaṃ kāraṇaṃ 
śuddhādhvopāda(dā)nakāraṇaṃ bodhakaṃ samastaviṣaya(l3)
jñāpakādīnāṃ prakāśakatvāt śabdopādānadvāreṇa śivādiśuddhavastudarśanena bodhakasvarūpaṃ māyāvanna mohakaṃ taditi 
sarvāgameṣvanena (l4)
 prakāreṇa prasiddhamityarthaḥ idaṃ bindvākhyaṃ māyālakṣaṇavaccitraśaktyā yuktam akalaṃ nityaṃ 
malādhikārāntaro'dhivāhyābhyantarakāryadvaya-yuktam atra tu (l5)
 bāhyakāryaṃ śaktyādiśuddhavidyātatvasthitabhuvanādisvarūpam ābhyantarakāryaṃ tu saṃhāre bindvātmakaśivatatve 
sūkṣmarūpeṇa bhavanādyavasthānaṃ (l6)
 svapariṇatirūpābhiḥ śāntyatītādipañcakalābhiḥ śuddhādhvarūpasarvajagato vyāpakaṃ kāryācitvaprakāśādacitsvarūpaṃ 
svāpakāle mantrāṇāṃ (l7)
 mantreśvarāṇāmanantādināmā vāsasthānamiti sarvaṃ samūhanīyam||8||

udyogācchaktitatvaṃ prasarati ca vibhostatkalāḍyu(ḍhyu)(l8)
si...kṣo bindoḥ prāk dṛkriyābhyāṃ sadṛśamadhikṛtādyat sade(nde)śākhyatatvam ādhikhye kye neśatatvaṃ 
manupatisahitaṃ tatkriyā śaktiyogāt jñānākhyā śaktiyoga... (p28b, l1)
manuniva...mukhaiḥ śuddhavidyākhyatatvam| itthamamalādipāśabandhajñānakalādīnāmātmanāṃ mantraṇāmantreśvarāṇāṃ ca 
bhogamokṣārthaṃ paraśivasṛṣṭiśivādiṣaṭtraṃ(triṃ)śata(tta)tva(l2)
mastītyāha| udyogāditi yadā bindurbhogādhikāradaśāyāṃ vidhūya sakalasvakāryajālaṃ svakasmin garbhīkṛtya vartate 
tadātikrāntabindukaḥ kevalacidrūpaśiva eva layasvarūpāṃ śivatatva(l3)
saṃjñāṃ labhate tadā samavetacicchaktirapyudāsīnasvarūpā bhavati śivasya kāryāsaktatvābhāvāt bindoracetanatvādeva 
tadadhiṣṭhānabhūtaiḥ kaiva śivasamavā(l4)
yinīśaktiḥ layasaṃjñāṃ labhete tayāpi saṃmaddhādeva eko devo layasaṃjñako bhavati yadā devaḥ kāryādhikṛto bhavati 
tadā śaktiḥ kāryādhikṛtā bhavati yadā (l5)
 sṛṣṭyādikarma kartumicchā bhavati tadā sṛṣṭyādikarmakāriṇī śivaśaktirevopādānabindvadhiṣṭhānabhūtā sati svakāryaṃ 
karotīti sisṛkṣorvibhorityuktaṃ (l6)
 

tathoktaṃ tatvaprakāśe – asminnilīya nikhilā icchādyāḥ śaktaya(yaḥ) svakaṃ kṛtyaṃ kurvanti tena tadidaṃ 
sarvānugrāhakaṃ prāhuriti sisṛkṣoḥ sraṣṭumicchorvibhoḥ para(l7)
meśvarasyodyogāt vyāpārāt bindoḥ śivatvasaṃjñābhūtaprathamopādānabhūtāt| 

tathoktaṃ pauṣkare – upasaṃhṛtakāryātmā yadā bindurvyavasthitaḥ tadā layāhva(l8)
yatatvaṃ śivatatvaṃ tadeva veti prākprathamataḥ kalāḍhyaṃ kalayā śāntikalayā āḍhyaṃ vyāptaṃ bhuvanaiśca samṛddham uktaṃ 
ca kalāvasthāni tatvastho bhuvanavraja iti (p29a, l1)
 tat prasiddhaṃ śaktitatvaṃ śaktyākhyatatvaṃ prasarati jāyateti saṃjñakaṃ prathamamupādānabhūtāt bindoḥ pariṇāmāt 
śaktitatvaṃ jātamityarthaḥ| caśabdena śāntikalāmastakasthaṃ śivatatva(l2)
saṃjñaṃ śānta(ntya)tītaṃ bhuvanamucyate| uktaṃ ca prakṛtitatvādayo binduḥ kṣodhyate nākhilātmanā tatra yo 
kṣubdhabhogo'sya śāntyatītakalā tu sā śāntyatītādibhuvanātmā yaḥ (l3)
 pariṇāmaḥ sabaindavaḥ śivatatvaṃ tadevoktamiti śaktitatvavyavadhānena sadāśivāditatvamāha| drakriyābhyāmityādinā 
drakriyābhyāṃ jñānakriyābhyāṃ sadṛśaṃ sāmyam adhikṛtāt (l4)
 sraṣṭutvenādhikāraṃ prāptāt parameśvarāt| yadupādānaṃ tayoktasya bindo jñānakriyāsāmyarūpaṃ dvitīyaṃ pariṇāmitvaṃ tat 
sadeśākhyatatvaṃ (l5)
 sadāśivanāmadheyaṃ tatvajātamityarthaḥ atra sadāśivāditatvaṃ dviprakāraṃ sraṣṭurūpaṃ sṛjyarūpaṃ ceti prathamata(taḥ) 
svayameva sadāśivāditatvasaṃjñāmanubhūyamānatve... (l6)
 sthitau śaktiśivāveva tadā sraṣṭurūpamityucyate| sṛjyarūpaṃ tu ṣaṭtriṃśaṃ tatvamadhyavihitabhuvanādiyuktatātvikarūpaṃ 
tathā hi tatra sṛṣṭisvarūpaṃ sṛjyamānasadāśivādi(l7)
tatvaviṣayavyāpārabhedopādhibheditaśivāsthātmakaṃ bhogādhikārarūpaṃ sadāśivāditatvam| 

tathā mataṅge - layabhogādhikārākhyastritatvokta(l8)
nidarśanāt padārthaḥ patināmāsau prathamaḥ parikīrtita iti asyāpi sraṣṭurūpasya sadāśivāditatvasyopādānāpekṣā 
nāsti śaktivyāpārabhedopādhikalpitabhedatvena (p29b, l1)
 paramārthato bhedābhāvāttaduktaṃ tatvaprakāśe| tatvaṃ vastuta ekaṃ śivasaṃjñaṃ citraśaktiśatakhacitaṃ śaktivyāpṛtibhedāt 
tasyaite kalpitā bhedā iti sṛjyarūpasadāśivāditatve ........ (l2)
tānāmaṇūnāṃ bhogabhūmitāmāpannaṃ sraṣṭupratiyogirūpaṃ karma vidyate| tatrai...dyupādānāpekṣā tatraupādhikasadāśivā-
dyavasthātrayaviśiṣṭaḥ śivaḥ kartā tacchaktireva karaṇa.... (l3)
 tato vibhaktā binduśaktirevopādānam ayamabhiprāyaḥ sadāśivāditatvatātvikaviṣayasisraṣṭāviśiṣṭasya bhagavata-
stannirmāṇārthaṃ vyāpāro bhavati (l4)
 ca jñānakriyāśakyoḥ samavibhāgarūpaḥ tadvyāpārabhedopādhitaḥ sa parameśvaro bhogatatvaṃ sadāśivatatvamiti 
vyapadiśyate| uktaṃ ca jñānakriyākhya (l5)
śaktyorapakarṣotkarṣayorabhāvena yaḥ prasaraḥ taṃ prāhuḥ sadāśivākhyaṃ budhāḥ tatvamiti tadvyāpārabhedopādhibhedito 
bhogasaṃjñakaḥ (l6)
 parameśvaraḥ sadāśivatatvatātvikāni binduśaktyā karoti tacca karaṇaṃ jñānakriyonmeṣalakṣaṇavyāpāramātrāt bhavati 
kriyāśaktiyogāt| kriyāśakti(l7)
saṃbandhaviśiṣṭāt parameśvarāt manupatisahitam ananteśvarādibhirupetaṃ tadīśatatvamīśvarākhyaṃ tatvamādhikyena 
kriyāśaktyutkarṣeṇa sādākhyādhastanaprasareṇa jātam (l8)
 atrāpi yadā sṛṣṭyādikarmaṇi kriyoddṛkto bhavati tadā tadvyāpāropādhiviśiṣṭaḥ śiva 
evādhikāratatvamīśvaratatvamiti ca vyapadiśyate| (p30a, l1)
 uktaṃ cānyat bhavati yatra śaktijñānākhyoddṛktatāṃ kriyā bhajate| īśvaratatvaṃ tadiha proktaṃ sarvārthakartā sadā iti 
punastadvyāpāropādhikārasaṃjñakaḥ parameśvaraḥ| (l2)
 īśvaratatvatātvikāni binduśaktyā karoti tacca karaṇaṃ kriyāśaktītyunmeṣalakṣaṇavyāpārādeva bhavati| sṛṣṭasyāsya 
sadāśivāditatva-tātvikasya tadvartino'ṇusaṃghātasya (l3)
 ca sthityādivyāpāreṇa pālanaṃ karotītyāha| tacca pālanamudriktajñānaṃ jñānākhyāśakti yogāt| 
jñānaśaktisaṃbandhaviśiṣṭāt parameśvarāt manuriva (l4)
 ha mukhaiḥ saptakoṭimahāmantrapramukhaiḥ sahitaṃ śuddhavidyākhyatatvajātam atrāpyarthasiddho hi niṣpannakāryasya 
tadrakṣaṇādau jñānotkarṣastadvyāpāro(l5)
pādhikaḥ śivaḥ sthūlādhikāra-saṃjñatatvaṃ vidyātatvamiti vyapadiśyate| uktaṃ cānyat bhavati 
kartṛśaktijñānākhyodrekamaśnute yatra tatvaṃ vidyākhyamiti punarudrikta(l6)
jñānavyāpāropādhikasthūlādhikārasaṃjñakaḥ śivo vidyātatvatātvikāni binduśaktyā karoti itthaṃ sraṣṭurūpasya 
aupādhika... (l7)
gāyikārātmakasya śivāvasthābhedasya sadāśivāditatvasya bhuvanādyāśrayatvābhāvādata eva sṛjyamāneṣu pāramārthikeṣu 
(l8)
 sadāśivādisaṃjñiteṣu bhogabhūmiṣu tatveṣu vartamānānāṃ vijñānakevalānāṃ vidyāvidyeśvarāṇāṃ 
vicitrabhuvanādinisvabhāvasundarī rāmāḥ api (p30b, l1)
 ... rimlānān bhogān karaṇāṅgāni ca parameśvaraḥ svasamavetayā jñānakriyāśaktyā vidadhādi taduktaṃ śivāgame| 
tatveṣu teṣu vijñānakevalānāṃ ..... (l2)
 bhuvanāni vicitrāṇi svabhāva-lalitāḥ priyāḥ bhogānapyuparimlānaṅgāni karaṇāni ca vidadhāti śivaḥ 
śakterananyāyāḥ kriyātma iti| kaṭamāya....... (l3)
 yogāt prasarati ca kalākālatatvaṃ niyatyā vidyā rāgaḥ kālātaḥ prakṛtisakhamito rudrayuk gauṇamasmāt| 
buddhiścātopyahaṃ kṛtrividhaguṇayu...... (l4)
yindriyairmānasaṃ prākkarmārthāṇyeva mātrā svaguṇagaṇabhidāḥ pañcabhūtāni tābhyaḥ| itthaṃ śuddhāni pañcatatvānyuktvā 
śuddhāni māyā(l5)
kalāditatvānyāha| māyāta iti| māyātaḥ aśuddhatatvopādānakāraṇabhūtāṃ māyātatvādanantayogādaśuddhatatvakartṛtvena 
vihitānanteśvarasya (l6)
 saṃbandhādanantanādho(do)'syātmanāṃ karmāṇi pralaye pakvāni budhvā svaśaktyadhiṣṭhitābhiranantaśaktibhirmāyāṃ 
vikṣobhitāṃ ca kṛtvā kalāditi (l7)
 tvaprasavābhimukhiṃ karoti tathoktaṃ vāmādiśaktibhiryuktaḥ śivecchāvidhi coditaḥ vidyārājādhirājeśo māyāṃ 
vikṣobhya mantrarāṭ| kalāmutpādayāmāseti (l8)
 evaṃ māyāṃ ca vikṣobhya tadadhiṣṭhānenaiva pralayākala-sakalātmanāṃ bhinnāni sūkṣmasthūlaśarīrāṇi bhuvanādīni ca 
(p31a, l1)
 sṛjati kalā kalātatvaṃ kālatatvaṃ niyatyāniyatyākhyatatvena saha prasati jāyate| etat tritayatatvatvaṃ māyāta 
utpannamityarthaḥ| 

tathā mṛgendre - tadanugrāhakaṃ tatvaṃ kalākhyaṃ taijasaṃ haraḥ māyā(l2)
....bhya kurute pravṛtyaṅgaṃ paraṃ hi taditi| 

tathā tatvaprakāśe - nānāvidhaśaktimayī sā jayati kālatatvamevādau niyatiṃ niyamanarūpā māyātaḥ sāpyanantaraṃ 
bhavati| kalātaḥ (l3)
 kalātatvā vidyā rāgaḥ vidyātatvaṃ rājatatva-jātamananteśvaraḥ kalāṃ ca svaśaktyākṣobhyaprasavābhimukhīṃ kṛtvā 
vidyātatvaṃ rāgatatvamajījanat tathā coktaṃ (l4)
 tadarthaṃ kṣobhayitveśaḥ kalāmevājani prabhāḥ| tatvaṃ vidyāsakhyamasṛjat karaṇaṃ paramātmana iti anyatrāpi 
kalātatvādrāgavidye dve tatve saṃbabhūva guruti itthaṃ (l5)
 māyotpannasya pañcatatvasya jananāntaraṃ gauṇāditatvānāmupādāna-kāraṇabhūtaṃ prakṛtitatvaṃ kalātatvājjātamityāha| 
prakṛtiriti itaḥ kalātatvāt (l6)
 prakṛtitatvaṃ jātam| 

tathā mṛgendre - tataḥ prādhānikaṃ tatvaṃ kalātatvādajījanat saptagrandhinidānasya yattat gauṇasya kāraṇam iti 
saptagrandhinidānasyeti (l7)
 taduktaṃ bhagavatā nārāyaṇakaṇṭhena yat pradhānaṃ mahadahaṅkārayestanmātrāpañcakasyetyevaṃ saptānāṃ granthīnāṃ 
kāryayonināmiti vyākhyātaṃ māyot(l8)
pannaṃ prakṛtyāditatvasyāpūrakaṃ bhauvanādhvanirudrasaṃśrayaṃ puruṣākhyatatvamastītyāha| prakṛti-sakhaṃ prakṛtitatvasya 
sahāyabhūtaṃ (p31b, l1)
 puruṣākhyatatvena kāryasahitasya prakṛtitatvasyeṣṭatvāt prakṛtipuruṣāviti prasiddheḥ aca eva prakṛtisakhamityuktaṃ 
rudrayuk rudraiḥ sahitaṃ puruṣatatvaṃ māyotpannam| (l2.)

tathā mṛgendre - puṃstatvaṃ tata evābhūt puṃpratyayanibandhanam āpūrakaṃ pradhānāderbhauvane rudrāṃśrayamiti| tata evaṃ 
māyātatvādityarthaḥ| nanu puruṣasya cetanatvādeva śivavadacaitanya(l3)
rūpeṣu tatveṣvadhvatayā gaṇanaṃ na saṃbhavati ata eva tatvamadhye adhvatayā pāṭho na yukta iti tat kathaṃ prakṛtisakhaṃ 
rudrayugityuktaṃ satyaṃ kalādipañcatatvena (l4)
 bandhātmā bhoktṛtvena puruṣa-tatvanirdeśaṃ yāti paramārthataḥ tasyādhvatvaṃ bhuvanādhāratvaṃ ca na vidyate ata eva 
tadbhuvanānāṃ rāgatatve sthiti(l5)
rityuktamāgameṣu tathoktaṃ vāmadevo'tha bhīmaścāpyugraśca bhavasaṃjñakaḥ sarvaiśānaikavīrau ca pracaṇḍaśceśvaraḥ pumān| umā 
bhartāsya jo'nanta (l6)
 ekaścaiva śivaḥ tataḥ rāgatatve sthitā hyete rudrāstīvrabalotkaṭāḥ| tatraiva puruṣo jñeyaḥ pradhānagrahapālakaḥ iti 
ata eva puruṣatatvasya (l7)
 nirvāṇadīkṣāyāṃ prakṛtitatvaśudhyanantaraṃ puṃstvamalaśudhyarthaṃ prakṛterupari rāgatatvādhaḥ pāṭha iti kaściddoṣo nāsti 
asmāt prakṛtitatvāt gauṇaṃ satvarajastamo (l8)
 guṇaṃ rūpaguṇatatvaṃ jñātam| 

tathā tatvaprakāśe - avyaktāt guṇatatvaṃ prakhyāvyāpāraniyamarūpamiha satvarajastamo'pi sukhaduḥkhamoha ityapi 
ceti (p32a, l1)
 ataḥ trividharūpāt guṇa-tatvāt siddhiśca satvarajastamorūpaṃ buddhitatvaṃ jātam| uktaṃ ca bhavati guṇatrayato 
dhīrviṣayādyavasāyarūpiṇī ceha| sāpi trividhā guṇataḥ prāktanakarmā(l2)
nusāreṇeti ato buddhitatvāttrivadhaguṇayutā satvarajastamorūpairguṇaiḥ saṃyuktā satvādi-guṇānāṃ yathākramaṃ 
taijasavaikārikabhūtāditi nāmabhiśca saṃyuktāhaṃkṛt (l3)
 ahaṅkāratatvaṃ jātamuktaṃ ca| sātvīka-rājasa-tāmasabhedena sañjāyate punastrividhaḥ sa ca taijasa-vaikārika-bhūtādikā 
nāmabhiḥ samucchvasatīti prākprathamaṃ prākchabda (l4)
taijasādidyotakaḥ ata eva taijasāhaṅkārātmānasammanastatvaṃ yī(?)kriyairbuddhīndriyaiḥ saha jātaṃ rājasa(sā)haṅkārāt 
karmākṣāṇi karmendriyāṇi jātāni (l5)
 bhūtādyahaṅkārāt tanmātrā jātāḥ| 

tathā mṛgendre - śrotratvakcakṣujihvānāsā ca manasā saha prakāśānvayataḥ satvāt taijasāśca sa sātvikāḥ vāk(l6)
pāṇi ca bhagaḥ pāyu pādau ceti rajodbhavāḥ mātrāḥ mātrāśabdena śabdādayo gṛhyante svaguṇagatabhidāḥ sveṣāṃ 
śabdādīnāṃ guṇā yathā| (l7)
 pṛthivyāṃ ghaṭapaṭādirūpaḥ śabdaḥ sparśaścāśitoṣṇarūpaḥ śuklaraktakṛṣṇādinā rekavidhaṃ rūpaṃ madhura-tiktadirūpeṇa 
raso'pi ṣaḍvidhaḥ gandhasu (l8)
 surabhyā surabhirūpeṇa dvividhaḥ etān svaguṇān gatāḥ pṛthivyādayasteṣāṃ bhindanti bhinnatvena vartanta iti 
svaguṇagatābhidāḥ halantāṭṭāpamicchanti kecidityā-kārāntaḥ (p32b, l1)
 śabdādīnāṃ sūkṣmarūpāstanmātrāḥ pṛthivyādisthitasvaguṇagaṇasamūhāt bhinnatvena vartata ityarthaḥ etāstanmātrāḥ 
tāmasāhaṅkārājjātāḥ|
 
tathā mṛgendre – śabdasparśaśca rūpaṃ ca raso (l2)
 gandhaśca pañcamaḥ guṇāviṣṭāstanmātrāḥ tanmātrāpadayojitāḥ prakāśakarmakṛdvargavailakṣaṇyāttamo bhavāḥ tābhyaḥ 
sūkṣmarūpābhyaḥ kāraṇarūpābhyaḥ śabdādimātrābhyaḥ (l3)
 yathākramaṃ pañcabhūtānyākāśavāyvagnijala-bhūmayosthūlarūpā jātāḥ||10||

jñānāt kevaliṣu sthiteṣu parato grandheśca kāṃścit patīn mantrāṇāmala(l4)
pākayogasadṛśānmantrācchivo'nyān gurūn| kṛtvā śuddhapathe śucau layakalān sūkṣmāṅgakānīśvaro rudrāṃścāpi 
vidhāya dehapurāgāñchiṣānvidhatte (l5)
 bha.....itthaṃ ṣaṭtraṃ(triṃ)śattatvotpattiṃ pradṛśya(pradarśya) tatra sthitānantādīn vaktumāha| jñānāditi śivaḥ 
śivaśabdenātra sṛṣṭyādipañca-kṛtyavyāpāravaśāt (l6)
 pañcabrahmakasaṃbandhīnibhiraṣṭatriṃśatkalābhirupetaḥ sadāśivatatve sthitaḥ sadāśivabhaṭṭāraka ucyate| uktaṃ ca tadvapuḥ 
pañcabhirmantraiḥ (l7)
 pañcakṛtyopayogibhiḥ īśatatpuruṣāghoravāmājjairmastakādikam| sadāśive pavitrāṅgaḥ sakalādiparicchadaḥ devaḥ 
sadāśiva iti| nanu sadāśivatatvoparisthita(l8)
śaktitatve śaktimatāṃ vara iti sūkṣmanādarūpe śivatatve dhvanipatiriti patibhinnatvaṃ śāstreṣu śrūyate| kathaṃ 
sadāśivatatvasthitasadāśivabhaṭṭāraka iti atrāyaṃ pari(p33a, l1)
 hāraḥ kṛtyabhedopacāreṇa śaktibhedavat patibhedaḥ paramārtha......bhedābhāvāt śivabhaṭṭāraka eva śakti-
śivatatvādhipatirnānyaḥ kaściditi uktaṃ ca kiṃ tu ..... (l2)
patibhedo'smin sa śāstre śaktibhedavat kṛtyabhedopacāreṇa tadbhedasthānabhedaja iti jñānāt| kevaliṣu vijñānāt 
prakṣīṇā karma māyīyabandhayuktānekavijñānakalātma(l3)
su grandheḥ māyātatvāt parata uparisthiteṣu vidyamāneṣu satsu māyā śuddhavidyā-ntarāle vijñānā kalānāṃ sthitiḥ 
māyordhvaṃ śuddhavidyādho malamātrāvṛto (l4)
 para ityuktatvāt parameśvaro vijñānakalāṃ madhye malaparipākatāratamyato'nantādyaṣṭavidyeśvarānīśvarānīśvara-
tatvanivāsinaśca karotītyāha| (l5)
 malapākayogasadṛśān malaparipākayogena malaparipakva-tāratamyasaṃbandhena sadṛśānātmanaḥ kiñcidyūnasamānarūpavān 
mantrāṇāṃ saptakoṭimahā (l6)
mantrāṇāṃ kāṃścit patīnananteśvarādīn vāmādiyuktān adhikāramalāṃśasahitān śuddhādhvabhūteśvaratatve prerakapreryabhāvena 
sthitān karoti (l7)
 taduktaṃ mṛgendre| tatrādau kevalāṇūnāṃ yogyānāṃ kuruteṣṭakaṃ vāmādiśaktibhiryuktaṃ saptakoṭiparicchadam| teṣāmanantaḥ 
sūkṣmaśca tathā (l8)
 caiva śivottamaḥ ekanetrakarudrau ca trimūrtiścāmitadyutiḥ śrīkaṇṭhaśca śikhaṇḍī ca rājarājeśvareśvarāḥ 
sarvajñatvādiyoge'pi niyojyatvaṃ malāṃśataḥ (p33b., l1)
 parasparaṃ viśiṣyante mantrāścaivamadhasthitāḥ iti tadvṛttau bhagavatā narāyaṇakaṇṭhena rājāno lokeśā indrādayasteṣāṃ 
rājānaḥ śatarudrāstadīśvarāṇāṃ maṇḍalī(l2)
prabhṛtīnāmapyete īśvarāḥ prabhavo viśyeśvarā iti vyākhyātam adhasthitānprati ūrdhvasthitānāṃ viśiṣṭamiti parasparaṃ 
viśiṣyanta ityuktam uktaṃ ca yo hi yasmāt (l3)
 guṇotkṛṣṭaḥ sa tasmādūrdhvamiṣyata iti mantrān saptakoṭi mahāmantrān parasparaṃ viśiṣṭān karoti uktaṃ ca| 
saptakoṭiprasaṅkhyākān mantrāṇāṃ sṛṣṭikāle mahā(l4)
māyāsaṃbandhinī śuddhādhvani sṛṣṭibhāvāt śuddhavidyābhuvanakṛtasthitīn kṛtvādhikārānte gurumūrtyadhiṣṭhānamanapekṣyaiva 
svayameva parameśvara(l5)
stānanugṛhṇāti uktaṃ ca tatvasaṅgrahe jagati kṛte tatrārthaṃ mantrāṇāṃ śivasamāhṛtān paṃsaḥ anugṛhṇāti vinā 
deśikamūrtiṃ prayukta(l6)
mīśamīśetianyat sārtha(rdha)trikoṭimakhile māyīye'dhvani adhikārasahitaṃ kṛtvā saṃhārakāle gurumūrtimadhiṣṭhāya 
parameśvaraḥ anugṛhṇāti uktaṃ ca (l7)
 prayoktṛdehasāpekṣatadardhamakhile'dhvani kṛtvādhikāraṃ sthityante śivaviṃśati seśvaramiti mantreśvaraiḥ śatarudrādibhiḥ 
sahitamityarthaḥ aśucaṃ na (l8)
 aśuddhādhvani īśvaraḥ ananteśvaraḥ aśuddhādhvani ananteśvarasya karta(rtṛ)tvāt layakalān pralayākalān 
sūkṣmāṅgakān kalādyārabdhasūkṣmaśarīrayuktān rudrānaṣṭādaśā(p34a, l1)
bhyadhikaśatarudrānvidhāya| sṛṣṭve-tyarthaḥ etān brahmāṇḍarakṣakatve mantreśvarāni karotītyarthaḥ uktaṃ ca 
tato'nantādyabhivyaktaḥ patīnāṃ granthitatvataḥ kalādyārabdhadehāna.....ro(l2)
nyaṣṭādaśaḥ śatamiti dehapuragān puryaṣṭakadehayuktān śiṣṭān pūrvoktātmaviśeṣitān brahmaviṣṇu-indrādīn bhave 
karmanibandhayuktasaṃsāre vidhatte bhuvanādhipati-tvenāṇḍa(l3)
madhye sṛjatītyarthaḥ| 

tathā mṛgendre - tānapyāviśya bhagavān sāñjanān bhuvanādhipān yebhyaḥ sarvamidaṃ eṣāṃ śaktiḥ karmanibandhaneti tadvṛttau 
nārāyaṇakaṇṭhe(l4)
......napi maṇḍalīprabhṛtinādhiṣṭhāya parameśvaro'nyān brahmaprabhṛtīniti vyākhyātaṃ taddīpikāyāmaghoraśivācāryeṇa 
brahmaprabhṛtīnityatra viṣṇuvāda(l5)
…..iti santi ślokoktam anye prādhānikā mantrā brahmaviṣṇvādayo parāḥ sāñjanāste'ṇḍamadhyastha iti||11||

sūkṣmasthūlaninādabindulipigaiḥ śaktyādi(l6)
kāryairjaganmāyī yannijanādagā ca kuṭilā yatte kalābhirjanān| kṣiptvā sā praticidvatāṃ prakaṭayatyekapradeśe malaṃ 
śambhoḥ sā tu niyogaśakti(l7)
klitā prāk kartṛśaktiṃ kalā| śivasamavetakriyāśaktirevopādānabhūtakuṇḍaliniśakti ktyotpannaṃ samastaṃ 
jagandharatītyāha| sūkṣmeti sūkṣmasthūlanināda (l8)
 ... ndulipigaiḥ ninādo dhvanirūpaḥ aṅguṣṭhāgrābhyāṃ svasya śrotre pidhāya ke kṛtvā yāṃ dhvaniṃ śruṇoti sa nādaḥ 
uktaṃ ca aṅguṣṭhābhyāṃ chādayitvā sādhakaśravaṇāvu...... (p34b, l1)
 tataḥ śruṇoti taṃ nādamambudapratimasvanamiti svaravarṇanivṛtyādikalānāmutpattilayasdhā(sthā)naṃ binduḥ| uktaṃ ca 
yasmāt pravartate sṛṣṭisvaravarṇakalātmikā yasmiṃśca lūyate bhūyaḥ tasmāt (l2)
 binduriti smṛta iti lipirakṣaraṃ sūkṣmasthūlarūpeṇa pratyekaṃ dviprakārā nādabindulipayastān gacchatīti tathoktaṃ 
nādabindulipisvarūpairityarthaḥ śaktyādi-kāryaiḥ (l3)
 śaktiśabdenātra mahāmāryākhyā kuṇḍalinīśaktirucyate| tasyāḥ ādikāryaiḥ prathamabhūtanādādikāryai(ryaiḥ) śakte 
dhvanirūpo nādaḥ prathamakṣobhaḥ nādādutpanno (l4)
 binduśca sañjalpātmako dvitīyaḥ kṣobhaḥ bindorutpannorvarṇātmakastṛtīyakṣobhaḥ uktaṃ ca śakternādo bhavet 
bindurakṣarama(raṃ) mātṛkā tata iti|
 
tathā (l5)
 ratnatraye - tacca śabdānuvedhena śabdarāśirabhradasau śabdarāśiśca bindośca bindornādādasāvapi| bindoḥ ranāhatādeṣaḥ 
kāraṇaṃ śuddhavatmanāmiti atra (l6)
 śuddhā-dhvani sūkṣmanādādikāryaṃ aśuddhādhvani sathūlanādādikaṃ kāryamiti vivekaḥ yathā 
kuṇḍalinīśaktirmāyākarmānusāriṇī nādarūpādikaṃ kāryaṃ (l7)
 tasyā ityuktvāt (ktatvāt) atra nādabinduvarṇākhyāni paribinduśakteḥ prathamakāryāṇi tatra kāryabindoḥ 
sthūlasūkṣme dve avasthe yathākramaṃ vyāpārabhedena madhya(l8)
mā vaikharīti sūkṣmā paśyantīti taduktaṃ sa binduḥ paranādākhyo nāda(p35a, l1)
bindvarṇakāraṇaṃ catasro vṛttayastasya vābhirvāptāsthithāṇavaḥ vaikharī madhyamābhikhyā paśyanti sūkṣmasaṃjñiteti tatra 
varṇasaṃjñā vaikharī tu aṣṭau sthānāni varṇānāmuraḥ (l2)
 kaṇṭhaśirastathā jahvāmūlaṃ ca dantāśca nāsikoṣṭau ca tālu ceti varṇotpattisthāneṣu vāyau niruddhe sati yā 
kṛtavarṇaparigrahā paścāditi śayena vāmayo-rūrdhvapra...... (l3)
ra eva bahiḥ prasaranibandhanāparaṃ pratyuccāryamāṇaghaṭādeḥ śrotragrāhyā vācikā vāk sā vaikharī tat pūrvāyā sā 
madhyamā yā atisthailyāvasthā (l4)
 sthitā uktaṃ ca ratnatraye tatra sā vaikharī śrotragrāhyā yārthasya vācikāsthāneṣu vidhṛte vāyau kṛtavarṇaparigrahāḥ 
prayoktṛṇāmiyaṃ prāyaḥ (l5)
 prāṇavṛttinibandhaneti kāryabindormadhyasthaulyāvasthā madhyamāyā budhyā kevalaṃ gamyamānā atontargatoccāraṇarūpā 
ata eva śrotrapathamanupayā(l6)
ntikramavarṇātmikā īṣadūrdhvaprāṇaprasaramiti kramyavartamānā sā vāṇi madhyamāhvayā paśyantyāḥ kāryam uktaṃ ca 
kevalaṃ budhyupādānakramavarṇānupā(l7)
tinī antaḥ sañjalparūpā tu na śrotramupasarpati| prāṇavṛttimatikramya vartate madhyamāhvayeti kāryabindoḥ 
prathamāvasthā paśyanti yā ca māyūrāṇḍarasagatapādapakṣmādi(p35b, l1)
vacchabdagatavarṇānāmavibhāgena sarvataḥ paurvāparyavidhurā ata eva sakalaviśeṣaṇaparisphuraṇābhāvādiniścāyamānā-
rthaviṣayā vāṇi svayaṃ prakāśate sā (l2)
 paśyanti sūkṣmāyā vikṛtiḥ taduktaṃ avibhāgena varṇānāṃ sarvataḥ saṃhṛtakramāḥ svayaṃ prā(pra)kāśā(śāḥ) paśyanti 
mayūrāṇḍarasopameti nādasaṃjñā sūkṣmā sakalaviśeṣaṇavidhura(l3)
lakṣaṇamarthasya svarūpamevāntaḥ prakāśikāyā vāṇī ca cirasthāyinī sā sūkṣmākhyā yaduktaṃ ca svarūpa-
jyotirevāntaḥ sūkṣmā vāgana-pāyinīti iyameva (l4)
 śuddhādhvavāsīnāṃ paramo bandhaḥ ya imāṃ vivekena pratyakṣīkurvanti te prayojyalakṣaṇaṃ sṛṣṭyādikartṛtvaṃ vihāya 
śuvatulyā bhavanti taduktaṃ (l5)
 ratnatri(tra)ye| yasyāṃ dṛṣṭasvarūpāyāmadhikāro nivartyata iti kiṃ ca saiva pralayākalānāmapi sūkṣmaśarīrā(l6)
tītāneva mokṣaparyantaṃ na muñcati yonibhyo yonyantaraṃ prāpayati ca ṣoḍaśakalāstu karmendriyapañcakaṃ jñānendriyapañcakaṃ 
mano'haṅkāra(l7)
buddhividyāścaturdaśa vākśaktibhyāṃ saha ṣośa(ḍaśa)kalāḥ puṃsāṃ karaṇādi| uktaṃ ca karaṇasvena bhedena svacityapi 
caturdaśa-vākśaktibhedādvau veti puṣkalā (l8)
 ṣoḍaśa smṛtā iti ata evāsukalāsu śaktyaparaparyāyā sūkṣmākhyā ṣoḍaśī kalā paramo bandhaḥ| (p36a, l1)
 taduktaṃ puruṣe ṣoḍaśakale tāmāhuramṛtā svayamiti evaṃ jagamātmakaṃ jagadviṣayaṃ nādādikāryaṃ pradarśitaṃ sthāvarātmakaṃ 
jagadviṣayaṃ nādādikāryaṃ prdarśyate nādenādhiṣṭhita.... (l2)
 jagatsthāvarajaṅgama-mityuktatvāt| atra mā(ma)hāmāyāśakternāda utpanna iti bhuvanātmakaḥ prathamaprasaraḥ ata eva 
nādakalābhuvanāni uddhikādīni śivatatvāntargatānyucyante atra (l3)
 nādakalādi indhikādyāḥ pañca indhikā dīpikā rocikā ūrdhvagā ceti binduśabdenātra bandujanitanivṛttipratiṣṭhā 
vidyāśāntiriti bindukalābhuvanacatuṣṭayamucyate (l4)
 etāsā..... ktitatve'ntarbhāvaḥ nādakalābhuvanānāmupādānabhūtaparabindoḥ śaktirūpeṇāvasthānātmakasadbhāvādeva 
sūkṣmatā bhuvanātmakavyatirūpeṇa sthūlatā (l5)
 śaktitatvāntarbhāvena sthitānāṃ nivṛtyādibhuvanānām sukṣmatā ātmanāṃ dehendriyaviṣayabhogasādhanatvāt 
pṛthivyādibhūtasaṃjñānāṃ nivṛtyādikalānāṃ sthūlatā evaṃ (l6)
 śaktyādikāryairmāyīyaṃ māyādi trividhaṃ māyā-svarūpaṃ jagatsthāvarajaṅgamātmakaṃ māyā| māyā ca māyā vai trividhā 
smṛtāḥ| ityuktatvāt athavā śaktyādikāryai(l7)
rityatra śaktiśabdena śaktitatvamucyate| ādiśabdena sadā-śivāditatvamucyate| sūkṣmasthūlaninādabindulipigaiḥ 
yathākramaṃ śaktitatve sūkṣmanādabindusadāśivatatve sthūlanādabindū īśvaratatve sūkṣmalipisaṃyuktā ananteśvarādayaḥ 
vidyātatve sthūlalipisaṃyuktāḥ saptakoṭimahāmantrāḥ vaikarīrūpāḥ (p36b, l1)
 kāmikādyaṣṭāviṃśatitantrasamūhā vidyante uktaṃ ca nādo binduḥ sakalaḥ sadākhyaṃ tatvamāśṛtau kathitam| vidyeśāḥ 
punarīśe mantrā vidyāśca vidyākhya iti evaṃ māyīyaṃ samastajagatnidanā(l2)
dagāsvābhāvikanādasvarūpā kuṭilāḥ kandotthitāmaṅkurākāratvena sthitatvāt kuṭilā rūpā kuṇḍalinīśaktiḥ 
cakārāt śivasamavetakriyāśaktyadhiṣṭhitā ceti gamyante anyathā jaḍa(l3)
 rūpāyāḥ kuṇḍalinyādhāratvābhāvāt sā ca kuṇḍalini śaṃbhoḥ śaktiḥ śuddhā jaḍātmiketyuktatvāt| 

tathā kālottare - candrāgniravisaṃyuktā syādyā kuṇaḍalinī tu yā| (l4)
 hṛtpradeśe tu sā jñeyā aṅkurākāravat sthiteti asyāhyaśuddhānāṃ jagadaṅkurāṇāmādhāratayā śuddhānāṃ copādānatayā 
samastajagadadhiṣṭhānātta(l5)
dadhiṣṭhānāttadādhāratvopamānamupapannaṃ seyamādhāraśaktiḥ śivaśaktyadhiṣṭhitakuṭilai(le)ti vistāreṇāgameṣu pratipāditā 
tathā coktaṃ brahmāṇḍasya dale (l6)
 śaktirjagadādhārarūpiṇī śaivī sā ca kriyāśaktirviśvavikṣobhakāraṇī sā vijṛmbhati satveṣu sarvatatveṣu nityaśaḥ 
sarvabījeṣu sā śaktiraṅkura(l7)
prabhavā sthitvā sā tu brahmāṇḍamāvartya nirgatā kṣīrasāgarāt punaraṇḍaṃ samudbhidya ṣakandādaṅkuravasthitā 
aptatvamāditaḥ kṛtvā yāvadgarvā(rbhā)ntamudgatāḥ aptatva...sa(l8)
 cakaṃ jñeyamagnirucchranakāraṇaṃ samiraḥ prerakastasya vṛtyarthaṃ vyomakalpitaṃ tanmātrāṇyaṅkurābhāni suṣirāṇīndriyāṇi 
ca| (p37a, l1)
 ahaṅkṛt sarvamadhyasthamojaskaṃ vṛddhakāraṇam evaṃ saṃvardhate nityamaṅkuraṃ sarvakāraṇamiti| evambhūtā kuṇḍalinīśaktiḥ

 bhavati tathā janānanteśvarādīn dhatte teṣāṃ śarīrādīnā-mutpattihetubhūtatvāduktaṃ ca mantrayoniḥ parāyā tu nityā

 māyopari mahāmāyā sarvakāraṇakāraṇam| māyā māyī ca māyā ca māyā vai trividhā smṛtāḥ īśvaraścaiva sādākhyaḥ 
kuṭilā cādhvadhāriṇīti (l4)
 tasmānmāyodhvaśuddhādhvani śaktau bhogaḥ tasya bhoktāro vidyā vidyeśvarādibhuvanavartino mantramantre-śvarādayastat

 kalāḥ tataḥ kalādhva tataḥ tāsāmapyupādānaṃ kuṇḍalinīśaktiriti kathamabhimataphalamantareṇa kalotpattiriti cet

sampatyarthaḥ kalānāmutpattiḥ nocetteṣāmanugrahādiviṣayānadhikārādyathāmahadādyupādānaṃ pradhānaṃ rāgādikāraṇaṃ māyā tathā 
(l7)
 nivṛtyādikalā hetuḥ kuṇḍalinīti siddhā sahajamalatirastutajñānānāmanekabhavakarmā baliparikuṇṭhitānāṃ

nuvedanaṃ nāstītyāśaṅkyā(ya) malāvaraṇasya kiñcinnivartakena kenāpi bhavitavyamiti karmaphalasaṃbandhabhogya-

 śambhorananteśvarasyāśuddhādhvaviṣaye'nanteśvarasya kartṛtvāt śuddhādhvaviṣaye śambhoriti śivasya śuddhādhvani 
sūkṣmabhūtakalārāgādīnāṃ bindumādyamānatvāttatra (l3)
 kalāditatvānāṃ śivaḥ karmāśuddhe'dhvani śivaḥ kartā prokto'nanto site prabhuriti ityuktatvāt nanvacetanatvāt

jñānaṃ cātmagataṃ prakāśyamanujān prāk kartṛśaktyanvitā vidyā kṣeśa?gatākṣagocaradhiyaṃ saṃgrāhayantyā(l3)
ntarī bauddhādvaiṣadhakāt paraśca guṇato rāgaḥ pravṛtte nṛṇāṃ bhoge vyaktadṛśāṃ spṛhāṃ janayati jñātākhilārtheṣviha|

 viṣayajñānaprakāśane karaṇabhūtaṃ vidyātatvamastītyāha| jñānamiti nanu kalaivaikapradeśe malavidāraṇena jñānaprakāśike-

 kalayaiva jñānaprakāśike ...... bhūtaṃ tvena siddhe satyapi sādhakatamaṃ vinā viṣayajātaṃ prakaṭayituṃ na samṛ(ma)rtheti 
vidyāṅgīkaraṇīyetarthaḥ vidyate viṣaya(l6)
jātaṃ jñāyate anayeti vidyā prākkartaśaktyānvitān pūrvameva vidyamānakartṛśaktyā sahitān manujān bandhātmanaḥ 
pratyātmagataṃ malā-vacchannatvenātmanyantargataṃ (l7)
 vidyā(dya)mānamapyavidyamānavartiṣṭhamānamityarthaḥ jñānaṃ viṣayaparijñānasamarthanarūpaṃ prakāśya vyaktabhūtaṃ kṛtvā 
svakarmadvāreṇa jñānaśakte-rviṣayagrahaṇā(l8)
kṣamatālakṣaṇamalāvaraṇaṃ nivārayitvaviṣayajātaṃ prākaśatītyarthaḥ| 

tathā tatvaprakāśe - āvaraṇaṃ bhitvaiṣā jñānākhyāyā svakarmaṇā śakteḥ darśayati (p38b, l1)
 viṣayajātaṃ karaṇaṃ sohātmanaḥ paramamiti nanvātmano bodhavyaktyāśrayatvāt buddhibodha-saṃjñayocyate tataśca 
viṣayagrahaṇe budhyādinyeva karaṇāni santi (l2)
 kṛtastadvyatiriktavidyā sādhyata ityāśaṅkyāvidyātāvadātmano jñānaśaktivyañjakā manasā saṃyuktendriyāṇāṃ 
bāhyaviṣayān prakāśyamānā buddhiriti vidyābuddhyorbhinna(l3)
viṣayatvena buddherbhinnā vidyā siddhirastītyāha| akṣeśa iti tathoktaṃ buddhibodhanimittaṃ cedvidyā tadvyatirucyata iti 
akṣaśa gatākṣagocarayiyaṃ akṣā(l4)
ṇāmindriyāṇāmīśo manastadgatānāṃ manaḥ saṃbandhasahitānāṃ manasā sannikṛṣṭānāmityarthaḥ akṣāṇāṃ jñānendriyāṇāṃ 
karmendriyāṇāṃ gocarāṇi (l5)
 bāhya-viṣayavastūni teṣāṃ dhiyaṃ manaḥ saṃyogayuktendriyāṇāṃ viṣayagrāhikāṃ buddhiṃ saṃgrāhayati| grāhikā bhavatīti 
śeṣaḥ|

 vidyāvyaktāṇucicchaktinunnākṣeśākṣagocarān svīkṛtya puṃprayuktasya karaṇasyaiti karmatāṃ matiḥ tenetarā vidyeti

bhogyā sukhādirūpā tadā bhavet karaṇaṃ vidyeyaṃ karaṇaṃ syādviṣayagrahaṇe punarbuddhiriti vyākhyāne (l3)

 tataḥ tadgrahaṇe vidyākaraṇamiti vidyāsiddhiriti vyākhyātaṃ vidyāprakaṭīkṛtajñānaśaktyā dṛṣṭārthānāmapyātmanāṃ bhogyeṣu 
viṣayeṣu (l5)
 abhilāṣābhāvena pravṛttiṃ nāstīti tadabhilāṣa-janakaṃ rāgatatvam astītyāha| bauddhāditi bauddhāt guṇataḥ 
vaiṣayikāt guṇataśca parataḥ (l6)
 anyabhūtarāgaśca vairāgyāt buddhisaṃbandhaguṇājjāta iti keṣāmāśayaḥ atha 
sṛkcandanavanītādibrahmaviṣayasaṃbandhaguṇājjāta (l7)
 ityapareṣāmāśayaḥ tannirasārthaṃ bauddhādvaiṣayikācca guṇataḥ apara ityuktaṃ yadā vairāgyalakṣaṇabuddhirmātmako rāga 
ityaṅgīkriyamāṇaṃ cettarhi buddhi(l8)
rviṣayākārā sukhādirupāsamāsato bhogyetyuktatvāt viṣayākāratayā pariṇatayā buddherbhogyarūpatvāt 
bhogyābhilāṣajanana(p39b, l1)
sāmarthyaṃ nāstīti tasmādabhilāṣahetunā bhoktṛtvagatena bhoktṝṇāṃ bhojanakriyotpādakena rāgeṇānyena bhāvyamiti 
buddhiguṇādanyo rāgaḥ siddhaḥ yadi (l2)
 viṣayaguṇa eva rāgaścettannibāhyaviṣayasṛkcandanavanitādisannidhānamātrādeva sarveṣāṃ sārāgatā prāpnotīti na 
kaścidvītarāgo bhavediti vītarāgā(l3)
bhāvadoṣaḥ prasajyata iti bhoktṛgato rāgo'bhilāṣajanako viṣayaguṇādanya iti siddhaḥ bhogyaviśeṣarūpaḥ 
sṛgāditararāgaḥ nanu māyāyāṃ (l4)
 mohakasvarūpo rāgo'stu kimanyena rāgeṇātrocyate māyāyāḥ sākṣānna mohakatvaṃ kintu kāryadvāreṇetyadoṣa| (l5)
 ihāsmacchaivasiddhānte evaṃbhūto rāgaḥ viṣayeṣu satsūtpādanasamarthaḥ pravṛtte sukhahaṃ duḥkhahaṃ dukhyahamiti 
sukhaduḥkhādirūpeṇa pravṛttamāne (l6)
 bhoge bhogyānuraktavedanātmake taduktam| bhogasya vedanā puṃsaḥ sukhaduḥkhādilakṣaṇeti vyaktadṛśāṃ vidyayā 
rkataṭīkṛtajñānānnṛṇā (l7)
 jvala-saṃbandhātmanāṃ teṣāmeva hi rāgo rañjakaḥ na nirmalānāṃ vidyeśvarāṇāṃ jñātākhilārtheṣu 
dṛṣṭisamastasṛkcandanādibāhyavastuṣu spṛhā(l8)
micchāviśeṣajña(jñaṃ) nayati| utpādayatīti| rāga-tatvasya vyāpāraḥ| 

tathā śrīmanmṛgendre - tadabhivyaktadṛkchaktidṛṣṭātho vyāpivāsitaḥ naiti tajjana(p40a, l1)
rāgaṃ tasmādevāsṛjat prabhuḥ sa tena rañjito bhogyaṃ malīmasamapi spṛśan ādatte na bhuñjāno virāgamayi gacchati||
13||

kālo bhūtabhavadvibhāvi vikṛte (l2)
 jātaḥ purā treśituḥ śaktyākṣipracirādibodhakalanāt puṃsaḥ kṣipatyāditaḥ bhuktau svārjitakarmaṇāṃ niyatirapyājñe 
parājñāṃ nmṛ(nṝ)ṇāṃ kṛṣyādeśca niyāmakākhya(l3)
balataḥ śambhorniyantrī sthitā| ātmanāṃ kalalalavabudbudādibālyayauvanādikaṃ śarīrakaraṇaṃ kālavaśādeva jāyata 
iti kalāvidyārāgebhyaḥ (l4)
 pūrvameva vidyamānaḥ nanu kalasya kālādibhyaḥ pūrvatvaṃ cet tarhi māyānantaramevocyatāṃ kimarthaṃ rāgānantaram 
atrāyamarthaḥ ātmanāṃ hi kilānava(l5)
cchinnatvāt prāguktakalādikañcukatrayajanitasamaveda-nānāmeva kālena kalpyata iti jñāpanārthaṃ rāgānantaraṃ kālasya 
pāṭho yuktaḥ| (l6)
 

tathā tatvaprakāśe - nānāvidhaśaktimayī sā janayati kālatatvamevādāviti tadvyākhyāne bhagavatāghoraśivācāryeṇa 
nanu śrīmataṅgādau (l7)
 kalāvidyārāgebhyo'nantaramasya pāṭhaṃ śrūyate satyaṃ taduktamasyaivānena kalānāṃ tattat tatra pravṛttikramo darśitaḥ atra 
tu sṛṣṭikrama ityavirodha iti (l8)
 vyākhyātam| īśituḥ parameśvarasya śaktyādhiṣṭhitasya acetanasya kālasya cetanādhiṣṭhānaṃ vinā vyāpārābhāvāt 
kālo nitya iti keṣāñcinmataṃ tanmata(p40b, l1)
nirasārthamāha| bhūtabhavadvibhāvivikṛtaḥ bhūta iti gatakālaḥ bhavaditi vartamānakālaḥ vibhāviti bhaviṣyatkālaḥ evaṃ 
bhūtāditrividhārūpeṇa vikṛtaḥ pariṇāmīkṛtaḥ kālasya (l2)
 caitanyatve sati bhūtādirūpeṇa trividhatvādanekatve sati ghaṭādivadanityatvaṃ siddhamiti bhāvaḥ kālaḥ 
pūrvoktaprakāreṇa karta(rtṛ)bhūtaḥ kālasya kiṃ kāryamityāha| (l3)
 kṣipravirodibodhakalanāt kṣipraḥ śīghrakālaḥ muhūrtaṃ tiṣṭhati| praharan tiṣṭhatītyādi ciraṃ bahukālaḥ ekadinaṃ tiṣṭhati 
dvidinamāste ityādi ādiśabdena (l4)
 saṃvatsarādikālaḥ eteṣāṃ bodhasya jñānasya kalanāt saṃkhyānāt saṃkhyānānnāma truṭilavādikālena 
kalalavabudbudādi bālyayauvanādikaṃ tasmin (l5)
 kartavye tadanu saṅgati nātmana| dehasaṃbandho bhavati puṃsaḥ malabandhātmanaḥ kṣipati ākṣipati vaśīkarotītyarthaḥ| 
uktaṃ ca truṭyādipratyayasyārthaḥ (l6)
 kālo māyāsamudbhavaḥ ātmanāṃ pūrvārjitakarmaphalavaśāt yasmin vayasi yasmin kāle yasmindeśe yat 
sukhaduḥkhādikaṃ bhoktavyaṃ (l7)
 tatraiva na nyūnaṃ nādhikaṃ bhojayiṣyatīti niyatiriti tatvamastītyāha| bhuktāviti āditaḥ pūrvasmin kāle 
svārjitakarmaṇāṃ svaiḥ ākriyaiḥ ārjitakarmaṇāṃ kṛta(l8)
dharmādharmasvarūpāṇāṃ bhuktau sukhaduḥkhaphalabhojane niyatyabhāve anyopārjitānyapi karmāṇyanye bhuñjīranniti 
upamāpūrvakamāha (p41a, l1)
 jña...nṛpatīnāṃ ājñeva nṝṇāṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ kṛṣyādeḥ kṛṣyādikarmaṇotpannaphalasya niyāmikākhyabalataḥ 
niyamananāmadheyasāmarthyāt sarvajanavaśīkaraṇājñaiva niyāmi(l2)
kā rājanīyamabhāve kṛṣyādi-karmajanitaphalāni śatravopaharantīti bhāvaḥ yathā rājñāmājñā tattatkarmajanitaphalāni 
tattatkartṛsaṃbandhakāni kurvati tathā śambhorniyantritīyamana(l3)
rūpā niyatiśaktiḥ sthitā karmaṇo niyāmakatvaṃ nāstīti sūcanārthamapiśabdaḥ tasya phalajananamātreṇa 
caritārthatvāt nanu karmaṇaḥ phalajananatvaṃ niyāmakatva(l4)
mapyasta evaṃ tarhi phalajananārthaṃ yathā sva sva kāryeṣu pravartamānaiḥ dehendriyādibhiḥ saha karma puruṣārthasādhanakṣamaṃ 
bhavati tathā svakāryaniṣpādaka(l5)
niyatitatvasāpekṣaṃ karmaniyāmakaṃ bhavatviti na doṣaḥ anyathā sarvatatvānā-mabhāvaprasaṅgāt| (l6.)
 
taduktaṃ mṛgendre -  atha dehādisāpekṣaṃ tatpumarthaprasādhakaṃ tato niyatisāpekṣamastu karmaniyāmakamiti||14||

ātmā pañcabhirebhiratra kalito (l7)
 bhoktṛtvayukto yadā tatvānte puruṣatvameti gaṇanāt piṃstvādviśudhye malāt| bhoktṛtvaṃ prathamaṃ pravṛttiviṣayaṃ 
pūrvairgatasyātmano bhogārthaṃ (p41b., (side heading ātmā pañcabhiḥ) l1)
 prakṛtirguṇādivikṛtestatsādhanasyāśrayaḥ| ananta-nādha(tha)kṣobhitamāyotpannakalādipañcatatvopabraṃhitasyāṇoḥ 
puṃstvamalayogāt puruṣa-tatvamiti vyapadiśyata (l2)
 ityāha| ātmeti atra śaivasiddhānte ebhiḥ pañcabhiḥ pūrvoktakalāvidyārāgakālaniyatibhiḥ yadā yasminkāle 
tasminneva kāle kalitaḥ bandhitaḥ ananta-nātheneti śeṣaḥ ata eva (l3)
 bhoktṛtvayuktaḥ bhoktṛtvabhāvasahitaḥ ānmā puṃstvānmalāt puṃstvaṃ nāma kalādiniyatyantatatvapañcakena 
saṃyuktatvameva tasmānmalāt viśuddhyai dīkṣāyāṃ (l4)
 śodhanārthaṃ gaṇanāt idaṃ tatvānte tatvamadhye pañcaviṃśatisaṅkhyākam ekaṃ tatvamiti vyākhyākaraṇāt puruṣatvaṃ 
tatpuruṣatatvākhyameti prāpnoti (l5)
 ātmanaḥ paramārthato tatvābhāvajñāpanārthaṃ niṣedhavācino itiśabdaḥ kṛtaḥ ata eva puruṣatatvasya bhuvanādhāratvaṃ 
nopapadyata iti puruṣatatva(l6)
pāṭhitānāṃ bhuvanānāṃ rāgatatve sthitiḥ atra śloke yadā śabdavihitasāmarthyāt yadā kalādinā bandhitaḥ 
tadaivātmanaḥ puṃstvamiti nirdeśaḥ bandhanakālāt (l7)
 pūrvakāle pu(puṃ)stvaṃ nāstīti jñāpitam ata eva sakalasyaiva puṃstvaṃ na vijñānakalapralayākalayoriti jñāpitaṃ 
taduktaṃ puṃstvaṃ tata evābhūt (l8)
 puṃpratyayanibandhanamityetat ślokavyākhyānāvasare bhagavatā nārāyaṇakaṇṭhena puṃpratyayanibandhanamiti puṃpratītihetuḥ 
anena māyāgarbhasthaibhyodhikāri(l9)
rudrāṇustat bhuvananivāsibhyaśca vailakṣiṇyamuktaṃ teṣāṃ puṃpratyayāyogāditi vyākhyātaṃ pūrvoktaprakāreṇa kalādinā 
(p42a, l1)
 bandhitatvāt bhoktṛtvaṃ prāptyātmanaḥ bhogārthaṃ prakṛtyāditatvamastītyāha| bhoktṛtvamiti pūrvaiḥ kalādipañcatatvaiḥ 
gatasyātmanaḥ prathamaṃ pūrvaṃ pravṛttivaṣayam| (l2)
 yatnaviṣayaṃ bhoktṛtvaṃ bhavatīti śeṣaḥ kalādibandhanādevātmanaḥ prathamaṃ bhoktṛtve pravṛttirjāyata ityarthaḥ| bhoktṛtve 
pravṛttasyātmanaḥ bhogārthaṃ sukhādi-bhoganimittaṃ prakṛtiḥ (l3)
 tatvādhanasya bhogasādhanasya guṇādivikṛteḥ guṇatatvādikāryasyāśrayakāraṇabhūtā prakṛtiḥ 
prakṛtijanyaguṇabuddhyāditatvasamūhāt sukhādikaṃ jānatītyarthaḥ| (l4.)

tathā kiraṇe – tasyāśuddhasya saṃbandhaṃ samayāditi śivāt kalā| tayodbalitasāmarthyo vidyākhyāpitagocaraḥ 
rāgo'pi rañjīkatvena (l5)
 pradhānaṃ ca guṇātmanā buddhyādikaraṇānika-saṃbandhāt buddhyate paśu| tato niyatisaṃśleṣāt svārjite'pi niyamyate| 
kālo'pi kāla sa....... (l6)
 bhavettadanusaṅgata iti||15||

satvāderlaghutāprakāśanamathopaṣṭambhavyāvṛtirgauravyā niyamaśca vṛttaya idaṃ gauṇaṃ vimiśraṃ paśau| (l7)
 vṛddhiḥ sā puruṣasya khātikaraṇeḥ saṃgrāhikā bhogyakā bhāsvat bhā(bā)dhyavasāyikāśca viṣaye sapratyayā bhāvagā| 
prakṛtitatvasya kāryamāha| satvāderiti (l8)
 satvarajastamoguṇānāṃ laghutāprakāśanaṃ laghutayā sūkṣmākāratayā prakāśanaṃ vyatikaraṇaṃ yathā kāñcane 
kaṭakamakuṭādyābharaṇātmikā (p42b, l1)
 kriyāśaktirūpatayā sthitā tathā śaktyātmanā sthitaṃ prakṛtitatvaṃ svakāryasya guṇasya janakaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ nanu 
satvādiguṇā eva prakṛtiriti yeṣāṃ mataṃ tanmatanirāsārthaṃ śaṅkāvāci (l2)
 athaśabdaḥ kṛta ityavagantavyaḥ tathā hi guṇā nāmā caitanye satyanekatvāt ghaṭādīnāmiva 
vibhāgāvasthātmanākāraṇena  bhavitavyamiti satvādiguṇāt bhinnā prakṛtiriti (l3)
 siddhā yathā pṛthivībhāgānāṃ ghaṭādikāryāṇāṃ pṛthivyātmakādhāre avaśyaṃ vibhāgo bhavatīti tadvat satvādīnāmapi 
guṇabhāgānāṃ kasmiṃścidādhāre (l4)
 avaśyaṃ bhāgo bhavatīti prakṛtitatvamevādharatvena sthitamityāha upaṣṭambhakaṃ satvādeḥ| sāpyādha(dhā)ratayā sthitā 
guṇatatvakāryamāha vyāvṛttiriti (l5)
 vrariti rāvaraṇaṃ vigatamāvaraṇaṃ vyāvṛttiḥ prakāśaḥ satvasya vṛttiḥ gauravyā gurutā vyāpāraḥ rajaso vṛttiḥ 
vyāpārasya gurutvamiti gauṇa(l6)
prayogeṇa vyāpārārthavācini gauravyā ityuktārmavyāpāro mahābhārarūpeṇa vartata iti lokavākyayā prasiddhaḥ niyamaḥ 
niyamyate (l7)
 uparamyata iti niyamaḥ saṅkocaḥ tamaso vṛttiḥ cakāro'nuktasthairyādi-samuccayārthaḥ ata eva prakāśa-
vyāpāraniyamātmakāstisro vṛttayaḥ (l8)
 satvādīnāṃ yathākramaprāyānyenoktāḥ anyā api vṛttayaḥ santi taduktaṃ śrīmanmataṅge sthairyaṃ dhairyaṃ dhṛtirdākṣyamārjavaṃ 
laghutā tathā| (p43a, l1)
 santoṣo mārjavaṃ śaucaṃ vyavasāyastathā smṛtiḥ| sauhityaparatā kṣāntirdayā dānaṃ ghṛṇā sadā| autsukyaṃ ceti 
satvasya vṛttayaḥ samudāhṛtāḥ krauryaṃ madotsahaḥ sābhimānaḥ (l2)
 saṅkalpitāpi ca| dārḍhyaṃ ca nirdayatvaṃ ca bhedo dambhaśca vṛttayaḥ rājasā pratipattavyāstantre'smin pārameśvare| 
aratirmandatā dainyaṃ paiśūnyaṃ gurudā sadā| nidrā(l3)
lasyaṃ madālasyaṃ nirodho mugdhatā ca yā tamaso vṛttayaḥ khyātāḥ siddhāḥ sarveṣu janmasvīti idaṃ gauṇaṃ guṇatatvaṃ 
paśau bandhātmani vimiśraṃ sahitaṃ (l4)
 satvādiguṇatatvaṃ sūkṣmadehadvāreṇātmani vyāpakatvena sthitam anukta-samuccayārthacakārādidaṃ gauṇaṃ 
sukhaduḥkhamohakatvena yukta(l5)
mityavagantavyam| 

tathā tatvaprakāśe - avyaktāt guṇatatvaṃ prakhyāvyāpāraniyamarūpamiha satvaṃ rajastamo'pi sukhaduḥkhamohamityapi 
(l6)
 ceti buddhitatvakāryamāha| buddhiriti tacchabdaprayogo yacchabda-prayogo'tra draṣṭavyaḥ yā buddhiḥ puruṣasya kalādinā 
bandhātmanaḥ khādikaraṇaiḥ (l7)
 khādayaḥ ākāśādayaḥ karaṇāni jñānendriyāṇi etaiḥ sādhanaiḥ sahitā buddhiḥ saṃgrāhikā samyak 
viṣayajñānasaṃpādanasamarthā ayamabhiprāyaḥ| (p43b, l1)
 ātmā buddhyā saṃyujyate buddhirindriyaiḥ indriyāṇyarthaiḥ saha jñānasyotpattirevamātmā buddhyādisahitaḥ viṣayajñānaṃ 
gṛhṇātītyarthaḥ| ata evātmano buddhiviṣaya(l2)
jñānasaṃgrāhike-tyucyate| bhogyagānanuviṣayāṇāmeva bhogyatve kathamātmanaḥ bhogetyucyate viṣayeṣu 
pratibimbitākāratayā buddherbhogyatvamiti (l3)
 bhogyaketyuktā viṣaye dravye bhāsvat bhādhyavasāyikā bhāsvanti nirdoṣatvāt prakāśamānā nibhāni 
cakṣurādīndriyāṇi ..... (l4)
 cakṣurādisādhanairadhyavasāyikā idaṃ rajatameva na śuktiriti niścayātmikādhyavasāyena buddhiḥ siddhā 
cakāro'nuktasatvādiguṇasamu(l5)
ccayārthaḥ etena satvarajastamorūpā taduktaṃ tatvaprakāśe bhavati guṇatrayatodhīrviṣayādhyavasāya-rūpiṇī ceha sāpi 
trividhā guṇataḥ (l6)
 prāktanakarmānusāreṇeti bhāvagā aṣṭavidhabhāvasvarūpā dharmajñānavairāgyaiśvaryākhyā buddhiguṇā sātvīkasya bhāvā ucyate 
adharmājñānā (l7)
 avairāgyānaiśvaryāstāmasābhāvāḥ avairāgyo rājasabhāvaḥ evamaṣṭavidhaṃ buddhau vāsanātmikayā sthitā dharmādayo'ṣṭau 
bhāvāḥ (l8)
 ātmano liṅgaṃ bhāvayanti prakāśayantīti bhāvāḥ| bhāvayanti yato liṅgaṃ tena bhāvā iti smṛtāḥ ityuktatvāt 
sa pratyādharmā(p44a, l1)
divya utpannasidhyādipañcāśatpratyayaiḥ sahitā dharmādaya eva prakarṣāvasthā prāptāḥ sthūlena rūpeṇa bhogyadaśāṃ 
pratipannāḥ pratyayā ityarthaḥ (l2)
 taduktaṃ pratyāyayanti kṣetrajñaṃ pratyayāstena te smṛtāḥ iti siddhayo'ṣṭau tuṣṭayo navā(va) śaktayo'ṣṭāviṃśatiḥ 
buddhiviparyayabhedāḥ pañca evaṃ (l3)
 pañcāśatpratyayāḥ eteṣāṃ nāmadheyāni lakṣaṇāni ca mataṅgapārameśvarādāvevāvaseyāni itthaṃ bhūtā sā buddhiḥ| 

tathā mṛgendre - (l4)
 buddhitatvaṃ tato nānābhāvapratyayalakṣaṇaṃ paraṃ tadātmano bhogyaṃ vakṣyamāṇārthasaṃskṛtamiti||16|| (l5.)

ceṣṭante marutaḥ śarīranilayāḥ puṃsāṃ yato grāhake saṃrambhādvyavasāyameva kurute satvādigāhaṃ kṛtiḥ| akṣāṇāṃ 
viṣayapravṛtti(l6)
karaṇaṃ sañcāri śīghraṃ sadā saṅkalpaṃ kurute'vadhānajanakaṃ tvicchāsvarūpaṃ manaḥ| ahaṅkāratatvasya 
saṃrambhagarvajīvanākhyatrividhakāryamāha| (l7)
 ceṣṭanta iti grāhake ahaṃ grāhaka iti pratītyadhyavasāyātmake anena garvo jñāpitaḥ grāhyaparāmarśātmāno 
buddhikāryadvilakṣaṇasyāhamiti| (p44b, l1)
 grāhakaparāmarśasya yatkāraṇaṃ so'haṅkāro garva ityarthaḥ paṭamahaṃ jānāmītyādiḥ itthaṃrūpagrāhake vidyamānasamrambhāt 
samrambho nāma āhaṅkārikaḥ (l2)
 prayatnātmakaḥ yaduktaṃ samrambho'haṅkṛtervṛttiriti sa ca samrambhaḥ ghaṭādikānaśaktyāśrayabhūtayā ghaṭādiviṣaya-
kriyāśaktyotpanno bhavati ata eva (l3)
 samrambhā-dvyavasāyaṃ vyāpārameva kurutetyuktaṃ vyāpārasya prayatnapūrvakatvāditi bhāvaḥ ayameva samrambhaḥ 
prāṇādirūpasyā(l4)
ntarasya vāyoḥ pravṛttinimittatvāt jīvanarūpamityāha yata ityādinā preraṇākarṣaṇe vāyoḥ prayatnena vinā kuruta 
(l5)
 ityuktatvāt yataḥ prayatnātmakasamrambhāt puṃsāmātmanāṃ śarīra-nilayāḥ dehāntarvidyamānāḥ marū(ru)taḥ 
prāṇāpānodānavyānasamānākhyāḥ (l6)
 pañcavāyavaḥ ceṣṭante svaṃ svaṃ vyāpāraṃ vidadhati prāṇādipañcavāyūnāṃ prāṇādirūpeṇa pṛthak nāmāni bhinnavyāpāra(l7)
vaśādevoktāni na ca pāramārthikāni vāyurūpakatvenā-viśeṣāt| 

tathā mṛgendre - prāṇāpānādayaste tu bhinnā vṛtternava(l8)
stuta iti ke prāṇādivyāpārā ucyante| tatra prāṇasya prādhānyena caturvidhavyāpārā ucyante| (p45a, l1)
 dehasthasya vāyoḥ dehādbāhye dvādaśāntaṃ yāvat ūrdhvāpāyanameko vyāpāraḥ jīvanamekaṃ loke prāṇe vidyamāne sati 
śarīro (l2)
 jīvatītyucyate vimarśātmakatvaṃ vicāryamāṇāyāḥ buddheḥ preraṇakaraṇamekaṃ balakaraṇamekamiti caturvidham| 

tathā mṛgendre - vṛttipraṇayanaṃ nāma yattajjīvana(l3)
mucyate| yattadūhaṃ matiḥ pu(puṃ)sāṃ bhrametyartheva mārgati tat kurvannucyate prāṇaḥ prāṇo vā prāṇayogata iti 
apānavyāpārastu (l4)
 bhuktapītānnajalasya jāṭharāgninā kriyamāṇavipākāvasthānantarajātaviṇmūtraretasāṃ adhaḥ prāpaṇakaraṇaṃ taduktaṃ 
tathā(l5)
panayanaṃ bhuktapītaviṇmūtraretasāṃ kurvanna pānaśabdena hīyate tatvadarśibhiriti samānavyāpārastu bhuktapītānnapānarasasya 
(l6)
 śarīre sarvatra sāmyena samarpaṇaṃ taduktaṃ samantato'nnapānasya samatvena samarpaṇaṃ kurvan samāna ityuktā iti 
vyānavyāpārastu (l7)
 annapānādivaiṣamyadvāreṇa vyādhiprakopanakaraṇena dehasya kāśyākaraṇam| 

tathā śrīkālottare - vyāno'pi nimayatyaṅgaṃ vyāno (l8)
 vyādhiprakopanaṃ prītervināśakaraṇo vyāpanāt vyāna ucyata iti udānavyāpārastu vaktuḥ (p45b, l1)
 puruṣasya vivakṣāprayatnapūrvakeṇa dehāntarākāśasya guṇarūpadhvaniṃ vaktrādisthānāntareṣu vibhajya vāgindriyeṇa saha 
varṇatāpādanakaraṇaṃ uktaṃ ca (l2)
 vivakṣāyatnapūrveṇa koṣṭhavyomaguṇadhvaneḥ vāgindriyasahāyena kriyate yena varṇatā sa udānaḥ śarīre'sminniti 
tathānyatrāpi ākāśavāyuprabhavaḥ śarīrāt (l3)
 samuccaran vaktramaiti nādaḥ sthānāntareṣu pravibhajyamāno varṇatvamācchati yaḥ sa śabda iti| 

tathā kālottare - spandayatyadharaṃ vaktranetragātra(l4)
prakopanaḥ udvejayati marmāṇi udāno nāma māruta iti satvādigā satvarajastamoguṇarūpī ādiśabdena 
taijasavaikṛtabhautādiriti (l5)
 tra(tri)vīdharūpā gṛhyate ahaṅkṛtiḥ pūrvoktagarvāditrividhakāryeṇa ahaṅkārasya siddhiḥ manasaḥ kāryamāha| 
akṣāṇāmiti akṣāṇāṃ (l6)
 bhokturātmano bhogasādhanānāṃ bāhyendriyadaśakānāṃ viṣayeṣu śabdādiṣu pravṛtteḥ pravṛttanarūpavyāpārasya karaṇaṃ 
sādhakatamam (l7)
 ātmendriyārthasannikarṣe satyapi ātmano mano'dhiṣṭhānaṃ vinā bāhyendriyāṇiviṣayeṣu na pravṛtta ityarthaḥ yatra vastuni 
svādu surabhisvarūpamṛdutvaṃ (l8)
 mṛduśabdavati āsvādyamāne sati tatra vastuni manaḥ tattadviṣayagrāhakendriyaiḥ saha śīghracāritrādeva 
kamalapatraśatabhedavat alakṣya (p46a, l1)
 sūkṣmakarmabhiḥ yugapat pañcajñānotpādanatvamastītyāha| śīghramāśusañcāriviṣayeṣu sañcaraṇaśīlam|
