tathā mṛgendre - devapravartakaṃ śīghracāri saṅkalpadharmi ceti yadā (l2)
 manasastattadindriyeṣu samakālameva śīghreṇa sañcāritatvamasti tadā tattadindriyaviṣayajñānamasti yadā śīghreṇa 
sañcāritatvaṃ nāsti tadaikendriyajñānameva nānyat yathā rūpānubhāva(l3)
kāle sparśānubhāvo na bhavati rasānubhāvo vā jñānaṃ tadakṣayogāt tatkramayogitayā kramāt tathāpyābhāti yugapat 
nāśusañcaraṇādata (l4)
 ityuktatvāt paraṃ jānāmītyuktatvāt| evamicchāsvarūpā manaḥ sadā sārvakālam avadhānajanakam 
avadhānamekāgratā tatja(jja)nakamutpādakaṃ saṅkalpaṃ (l5)
 mānasaṃ karma kurute utpa(pā)dayati kiṃ manaso'vadhānajanakam| saṅkalpavyāpārakatvāt etat manobuddhyahaṅkārābhyāṃ 
bhinnaṃ taṃ (l6)
 buddhyahaṅkārau kriyātmakarūpakatvenāpratyayarūpatvāt gṛhyagrāhakapravṛtyayayo hetustanmana iti manasaḥ siddhiḥ tacca 
manaḥ āntarātmakamānasakarmaṇā (l7)
 bāhyendriyādhiṣṭhānavyāpāreṇa ca dvidhādhikārītyuktaṃ śrīmanmataṅge| dvidhādhikārī taccittaṃ bhoktṛbhogopapādakam| bahiḥ 
(l8)
 karaṇabhāvena svocitena yataḥ sadā| indriyāṇāṃ tu sāmarthyaṃ saṅkalpenātmavartinā| karotyantasthitaṃ bhūyaḥ 
tenāntaḥkaraṇaṃ mana iti (p46b, l1)
 buddhyahaṅkārāvapyāntarīndriyau| 

tathā tatvasaṅagrahe - antaḥkaraṇaṃ trividhaṃ cittāhaṅkārabuddhinirvācyamiti||17||

śrotrādyaṃ vigrahāṅgaṃ svaviṣayaninadasparśarūpādi(l2)
bodhān cittarthaṃ kartṛbhāvānusaraṇabalataḥ pañcakaṃ naḥ karoti| vāgā cittadyaṃ dehasaṃsthaṃ svaviṣayavacanādānapūrvaṃ 
svakṛtyaṃ cittāptaṃ pūrvakarma vyatikaravaśataḥ (l3)
 pañcakaṃ nurvidhatte| atha bāhyendriyadaśakasya vyāpāraṃ vaktukāmaḥ pūrvāntāvat jñānendriyapañcakasya (l4)
 vyāpāramāha| śrotrādyamiti vigrahāṅgaṃ vigrahasya| pradhānaṃ śarīrasyāṅgam avayavabhūtaṃ cittadharmaṃ manasā sāmṛddhaṃ manaḥ 
saṃyuktamityarthaḥ pañcakaṃ pañca (l5)
 viṣayaṃ śrotrādyaṃ śrotratvakcakṣujihvānāsākhyaṃ na ca karṇakoṭarādeḥ śabdādigrahaṇatvamapi tu tatsthānasya 
kartṛbhūtendriyasya śaktirityāha| (l6)
 kartṛbhāvānusaraṇabalataḥ kartudhvanyādigrahaṇe kartṛbhūtasyendriyabhāvasya sanmātreṇa vidyamānasyānusaraṇe balataḥ 
anugamanasāmarthyāt (l7)
 indriyaśaktyabhāve dhvanyādigrahaṇābhāvo badhirādau dṛṣṭaḥ svaviṣayaninadasparśarūpādibodhān sveṣāṃ śrotrādīnāṃ 
viṣayāḥ yathākrameṇa grāhyabhūtāḥ ninado (l8)
 dhvaniḥ sparśitādi-rūpaḥ prakāśādiḥ ādiśabdena rasagandhau gṛhyante eteṣāṃ bodhān grahaṇajñānāni naḥ(nu) 
ātmanaḥ karoti viṣayajñānamutpādayati yathākrameṇa (p47a, l1)
 śrotrādīnāṃ śabdādi-grahaṇeṣu vyāpāritvāt tadanyarūpādigrahaṇābhāvāddoṣāntattadviṣayakavyāpāraḥ siddhaḥ| 

tathā tatvaprakāśe - śabdādīnāṃ grahaṇaṃ vyāpāraḥ (l2)
 kīrtitaḥ krameṇaiṣāmiti karmendriyavyāpāramāha vāgādyamiti dehasaṃsthaṃ śarīre vidyamānaṃ cittāptaṃ manasā saṃyuktaṃ 
pañcakaṃ pañcavidhaṃ vāgādyaṃ vākpāṇipāda(l3)
pāyūpasthākhyaṃ pūrvakarmavyatikaravaśataḥ pūrvajanmanyārjitapuṇyapāpavyatyāsavaśataḥ yathā mūkapaṃkvādau pāpakarmavaśāt 
vacanagamanābhāvo dṛṣṭaḥ (l4)
 svaviṣaya-vacanādānapūrvaṃ sveṣāṃ vāgādīnāṃ yathākrameṇa viṣayāḥ gṛhyabhūtāḥ vacanādānagamanamalavisargānandāḥ 
vacanamābhāṣaṇam ādānaḥ sīkara(l5)
ṇaṃ pūrvaśabdena gamanādiyuktaṃ svīkṛtya| sva sva viṣayavyāpāraṃ nanuḥ(nu) ātmanaḥ vidhatte saṃpādayati 
svakṛtyamityatra punarapi svaśabdakaraṇaṃ vāgvidināṃ (l6)
 karmendriyābhāvapakṣanirasanārthaṃ kṛtamityavagantavyam anyathā svaviṣaya-vacanādānetyatra svaśabdakaraṇāt punarapi 
svaśabdakaraṇamanarthakaṃ (l7)
 syāt sarvairvāgādinā karaṇena kriyamāṇakriyā dṛśyate yataḥ tataḥ karmendriyamiti siddham evaṃ cet bhrūlatā-
sphuraṇatvapādīnāṃ kriyatvāt karaṇāntarāṇi (l8)
 kalpanīyānītyanantakarmendriyaprasaṅgasaṃbhavati tanna karmendriyāṇāṃ viprakṛṣṭasannikṛṣṭavyāpāratvāt 
sarvaśarīravyāpakatvenābhyupagamāt (p47b, l1)
 .....dereva bhrūkāpādināṃ vyāpārastattadindriyaśaktīnāṃ na tatra sthāne viśeṣādhiṣṭhānādityarthaḥ vikṛṣṭaṃ viddhi devānāṃ 
sannikṛṣṭaṃ kriyātmanāmiti||18|| (l2.)

tanmātraṃ hvayaśiṣṭameva tu guṇaiḥ śabdādikaikādhikairyuktaṃ tadravapūrvanāmakathitaṃ bhūtāśrayaṃ pañcadhā| bhūtānyatra dharā tu 
pañcaguṇayuktatoyādi caikaikaśo (l3)
 hīnaṃ tairdharaṇagrahākhyapacanavyūhā-vakāśāḥ kriyāḥ| śabdasparśarūparasagandhānāmākāśādeḥ kāraṇabhūtānāṃ pratyekaṃ 
sūkṣmabhūtaśabdaika(l4)
guṇayuktaśabdi tanmātramiti sūkṣmaśabdasparśaguṇayuktasparśatanmātramiti| sūkṣma-
śabdasparśarūpaguṇayuktarūpatanmātramiti sūkṣmaśabdasparśarūpaparasaguṇa(l5)
yuktarasatanmātramiti sūkṣmaśabdasparśarūparasagandhaguṇayuktagandhatanmātramiti krameṇaikādyuttaraguṇayukta-sūkṣmarūpāṇi 
pañcatanmātrāṇi (l6)
 pañcabhūtakāraṇatayā siddhāni santītyāha| tanmātramiti śabdādikaikādhikaiḥ śabdādikaḥ śabdasparśarūparasagandhaḥ 
sa ekaivādhikaiḥ (l7)
 ekottaravṛddhyā yuktaiḥ ekadvitricatuḥ pañcakayukterityarthaḥ guṇaiḥ śabdasya yathā ghaṭaghaṭādiḥ sparśasya śītoṣṇādiḥ 
rūpasya śuklādiḥ rasasya madhurādiḥ (l8)
 ṣaḍvidhaḥ gandhasya surabhyasurabhiḥ evaṃvidhaguṇairyuktaṃ sahitaṃ hiśabdaḥ tathāpītyarthe vartate tathāpi 
sūkṣmabhūtaguṇairyuktamapi guṇaiḥ (p48a, l1)
 śabdādiguṇaiḥ avaśiṣṭameva śeṣitameva bījasthitavṛkṣarūpavat anabhivyaktaguṇayuktamityarthaḥ viśeṣārthadyotakena tu 
śabdena pūrvoktaśrotrādindriyāṇāṃ (l2)
 śabdādīni tanmātrāṇi sthūlasūkṣmarūpatvenādharatayā sthitānītyuktaṃ bhavati| 

tathā mataṅge - tanmātrāṇīha ghaṭavat mahābhūtānilepavaditi pañcadhā pañcaprakāratanmātraṃ śabdata(l3)
nmātramityādinā siddhamityarthaḥ| śabdāditanmātrāṇāṃ ravādināmāni santītyāha tacchabdāditanmātraṃ ravapūrva nāma 
kathitam| ravapūrvaṃ nāmadheyaiḥ proktaṃ (l4)
 yathā śabdasya ravadhvanininādanāmāni sparśasya śītoṣṇādirūpasya śuklakṛṣṇaraktādirasasya madhuratikta-
kaṣāyādigandhasya surabhyasurabhyādi (l5)
 etena sthūlarūpanāmadheyakaraṇena kiṃ jātamityāha| bhūtāśrayamākāśādibhūteṣu yathākramamadhiṣṭhānatayā sthitaṃ pañcadhā 
bhūtāni ākāśa(l6)
dhvanijalabhūmayaḥ? eteṣāṃ kati guṇāḥ ko vyāpāra ityāha| atrākāśādibhūtamadhye dharā bhūmistu pañcaguṇayuk 
gandharasarūpasparśaśabdā(l7)
khyaiḥ pañcaguṇaiḥ sahitā toyādi jalāgni-vāyvākāśaṃ ca ekaikaśaḥ yathākramam ekaikaguṇahīnaṃ virahitaṃ 
rasarūpasparśaśabdaguṇayuktaṃ jalam, (l8)
 rūpa-sparśa-śabdaguṇayukto vahniḥ, sparśaśabdaguṇayukto vāyuḥ, śabdaika-guṇayokto ākāśa| etenākāśasya 
(p48b, l1)
 śabda eka eva guṇam, vāyvādi....śabdaguṇo nāstīti pratyuktvāpalāpino nirastāḥ tathā hi ākāśe pratiśabdaṃ 
śrūyate śakaśakaśabdo vāyau kampanajñāpakaḥ (l2)
 dhamadhamaśabdo'gnau pa(pra)jvalanaṃ sūcayati phalaphalaśabdo'mbhasi pravāham, ghaṭaghaṭaśabdaḥ pṛthivyāṃ kaṭhinatvaṃ 
sūcayati vāyvādīnāṃ śabdāḥ parasparabhūtāḥ hativaśā(l3)
dutpannāḥ vyomni tattacchacabdasamānavarṇāḥ pratiśabdāḥ śrūyante| 

tathā mṛgendre - vyomaprabhañjanānyaṃbu bhūmayo bhūtapañcakaṃ śabdādyeko(l4)
ttaraguṇamavakāśādivṛttimat madhuna na jparana-plāva-kharatvā-vedino guṇāḥ śabdavāyvādiṣu vyomni savarṇa-
pratiśabdabhāgiti pṛthivyāṃ pañcaguṇā(ṇāḥ) (l5)
 yathā ghaṭaghaṭādirūpaḥ śabdasparśaścāśitānuṣṇaḥ śuklaraktakuṣṇādinānārūpaṃ kaṭvāmlalavaṇatiktamadhurakaṣāyo rasaḥ 
surabhya-surabhirūpeṇa (l6)
 gandhaḥ ambhasi caturguṇā(ṇāḥ) yathā chalachalādiśabdaḥ śītarūpasparśarūpaṃ śuklo rūpaṃ madhuro rasaḥ vahnau 
triguṇāḥ yathā dhama(l7)
dhamādiśabdaḥ uṣṇarūpasparśaḥ prakāśamānarūpam, vāyau dvau guṇau yathā aśītānuṣṇasparśaḥ, śakaśakādiśabdaḥ| 
ākāśe śabdaikaguṇo yathā śabdaḥ prati(l8)
dhvanireva| uktaṃ ca mṛgendre| i i iti pañcasu śabdo'yaṃ sparśo bhūtacatuṣṭaye| aśītoṣṇamahī vāyvoḥ, śītoṣṇau 
vāri tejasoḥ, bhāsvadagnau, jale śuklaṃ (p49a, l1)
 kṣitau śuklādyanekadhā| rūpaṃ triṣu, raso ambhaḥ sumadhuraṣaḍvidhaḥ kṣitau| gandhaḥ kṣitāvasurabhiḥ surabhiśca mato 
budhairiti dharaṇagrahākhyapacanavyūhāvakāśāḥ (l2)
 kriyāvyāpārāḥ taiḥ pṛthivyādibhiḥ yathākrameṇa saṃbhavantīti śeṣaḥ dharaṇaṃ kaṭhinatvāt dhāraṇaṃ bhūmervyāpāraḥ grahaḥ 
saṃgrahadravatvāt samūhīkaraṇaṃ piṇḍī(l3)
karaṇam| jalasya vyāpāraḥ pacanam uṣṇatvāt pākīkaraṇaṃ vahnervyāpāraḥ vyūhaḥ kampanāt avayavaghaṭanaṃ 
vāyorvyāpāraḥ avakāśaḥ (l4)
 niṣkalatvāt suṣirarūpatayā itarabhūtānām āspadasya dānam ākāśasya vyāpāraḥ| uktaṃ ca 
vyūmaho'vakāśadānaṃ ca paktisaṃgrahadhāraṇāḥ vāyu (l5)
 vyomahutāśāṃ budharaṇīnāṃ ca vṛttaya iti||19||

ūrdhvodhvāsakalopi bodhakatayā śuddhodharo grathito mohāḍyādaśucirviparyaya(l6)
hateḥ śuddho'pi puṃstvāt paraḥ tatvānyeva sahāyabhūtabhuvaneśānairyutānyātmanāṃ tiṣṭhantyāvilayādamūni ca 
bahirbhogapradāyinyalam| (l7)
 evaṃ śivādikṣityantatatvānyuktvā teṣāṃ śuddha iti śuddhāśuddha iti aśuddha iti trividhatvamastītyāha| ūrdhva iti 
ūrdhvo'dhvā ūrdhvatatvānyeva māyā-tatvāduparivartamānaḥ (49b, l1)
 sakalopi samasta evādhvā śuddhavidyādiśivāntaṃ pañcatatvaṃ bodhakatayā tatra sthitānātmanāṃ 
sarvajñatvasarvakartṛtvādijñānotpādakatayā śuddhaḥ śuddhatatvā-tmanāṃ bhoga(l2)
sādhanārthaṃ paramaśivena sākṣātkārakṣobhitasya bindorupādānatvāt ata eva vijñānakevalāḥ 
atikrāntakalādibandhāḥ pradhvaṃsīkṛtakarmapāśāḥ (l3)
 pakvamalānanteśā-dayaśca adhikāramalaśeṣataḥ aprāptiśivatejaskāḥ santo vidyante uktaṃ ca tenopodbalitāḥ sarve 
śuddhādhvabhuvaneśvarāḥ (l4)
 granthitaḥ māyātatvāt adharaḥ adhobhāge sthitaḥ kalādikṣityanta-tatvasvarūpo'dhvāmohāḍhyāt moho 
nāmānityāśuciduḥkhānātmasu nitya(l5)
śucisukhātmajñānapravṛtte hetuḥ tasya māyotpannajñānasyāḍhyādādhikyāt aśuciḥ aśuddhapuṃstvāt puruṣatatvāt paraḥ 
uparivartamā(l6)
naḥ rāgādikalāntatatvasvarūpo'dhvāviparyayahateḥ hānitaḥ kalo'bdalitasāmarthyādviparyayasya kiñciddhānitaḥ 
kiñci(l7)
jjñatvakiñcitkartṛtvāt śuddhaḥ api-śabdādaśuddhaḥ śuddhā ityarthaḥ| aśuddhatvaṃ tu sūkṣmadehadvāreṇa saddhātṛṇāmātmanāṃ 
sukhaduḥkhamohajanakatayā (p50a, l1)
 vijñeyaṃ prakṛtyādikṣityantānāṃ tu satvādiguṇena atyantaṃ sukhaduḥkhamohajanakatayā cāśuddhatatvameva evaṃ sṛṣṭāni 
tatvāni mahāsaṃhāraparyantaṃ tiṣṭhantītyāha| tatvānīti (l2)
 sahāyabhūtabhuvaneśānaiḥ sahāyabhūtāni bhogārthaṃ sādhanāni yāni bhuvanāni tattat tatva-sthitabhuvanāni īśānāḥ etat 
tadbhuvanādhipatayaḥ tairyutāni sahitāni (l3)
 ātmanāṃ vijñānākalādinā malamatyantaṃ bahirbhogapradāyinī bahiḥ śabdenābhyantarabhogā(gaḥ) bahirbhoga iti 
bhogasya dvaividhyaṃ sūcitaṃ sākṣācchivasya (l4)
 bhogapradāne kartṛtvācchuddhatatvodbhūtabhoga ābhyantarabhogaḥ anantādidvāreṇa kartṛtvāt aśuddhatatvodbhūtabhogā 
bahirbhogaḥ (l5)
 ābhyantara-bhogabhkatṛṇāṃ bhogāvasāne mokṣaḥ| uktaṃ ca adhikārapadārūḍho malasteṣāṃ virodhakaḥ tena 
śuddhādhvabhoga(gaḥ) syāt| (l6)
 pāke syāttamamalakṣayaḥ adhikāra-malakṣīṇe mosyādavyayaḥ para iti bahirbhogabhoktruṇām ābhyantarabhogānantaraṃ 
mokṣaḥ| (l7.)

tathā pañcāvaraṇastave - bhagavatāghoraśivācāryeṇa proktaṃ ye cātra śuddhabhuvanodbhavabhogakāmāḥ tvāmarcayanti 
vividhairyajanaprakāraiḥ tebhyaḥ (p50b, l1)
 pra.....parameśvarasādhakebhyo bhogān dadāsi tadanantarameva mokṣamiti amūni tatvāni kṣityādiśivāntāni 
āvilayāt mahāpralayaparyantaṃ tiṣṭhati vidyante (l2)
 ata eva vāstavyatvāt tatvānītyucyante| 

tathā tatvaprakāśe - āpralayaṃ yattiṣṭhati sarveṣāṃ bhogadāyibhūtānāṃ tattatvamiha proktaṃ na śarīraghaṭāditatvamiti||
20|| (l3.)

kṣmātatvaṃ śatakoṭiyojanamapaḥ prārabhya gauṇāntakaṃ paṭhantyā tadguṇanāt tathā prakṛtito māyāvasānaṃ śatāt 
sāhasrācca sadāśivāvadhi (l4)
 tataḥ śaktyantakaṃ lakṣataḥ syādūrdhvaṃ sa śivo-'pyameyavibhavo bindoradhiṣṭhāyakaḥ| pṛthivyāditatvānāṃ 
yojanapramāṇānyāha| kṣmātatva(l5)
miti pṛthvītatvaṃ śatakoṭiyojanamapaḥ aptatvāt prārabhya aptatvaṃ pūrvīkṛtetyarthaḥ gauṇāntakaṃ guṇatatvaparyantaṃ paṭhantyā 
daśa daśa saṃkhyā (l6)
 tadguṇanāt tasya pṛthivītatvapramāṇabhūtaśatakoṭi-yojanasya guṇanāt vardhanāt aptatvādiguṇatatvaparyantaṃ 
yojanapramāṇaṃ bhavati| (l7)
 tadyathā pṛthvītatvapramāṇabhūtaśatakoṭiyojanamekasthānaṃ kṛtvā ekasthānabhūtaśatakoṭiyojanaṃ daśasaṃkhyayā vṛddhīkṛtaṃ 
cet dvitīyasthānabhūtamaptatvapramāṇaṃ (l8)
 daśa bhavati evamevo-ttaratrāpi guṇīkṛtaṃ cet vahnyāditritīyādiśatādisthānaṃ bhavati tadyathā 
pṛthivītatvāddaśaguṇamaptatvaṃ taddaśasthānaṃ sahasrakoṭi(p51a, l1)
yojanapramāṇamityarthaḥ evamuttaratrāpi saṃkhyā kartavyā jalatatvādagnitatvaṃ daśaguṇaṃ tat śatasthānaṃ agnerdaśaguṇaṃ vāyuḥ 
tatsahasrasthānaṃ vāyordaśa(l2)
guṇam ākāśaḥ taddaśasahasrasthānaṃ vyomno daśaguṇo'haṅkāraḥ tallakṣasthānaṃ garvā-ddaśaguṇo buddhiḥ taddaśalakṣasthānaṃ 
buddherdaśaguṇo guṇatatvaṃ tatkoṭisthānam (l3)
 evaṃ śatakoṭirekādi koṭisthānasaṃkhyā jātā guṇatatvaparyantaṃ prakṛtyāditatvapramāṇamāha| tatheti yathā 
śatakoṭiyojanapramāṇabhūtapṛthivi(l4)
tatvamārabhya guṇatatvaparyantaṃ daśasaṃkhyayā vṛddhīkṛtaṃ tathā prakṛtitaḥ prakṛtitatvamārabhya māyāvasānaṃ māyātatvaparyantaṃ 
śatāt śatasaṃkhyayā vṛddhiḥ (l5)
 kāryā śatasaṃkhyayā vṛddhikaraṇāt jātasthānaṃ yathā guṇatatvāt śataguṇā prakṛtiḥ tatpadmasthānaṃ prakṛteḥ prakṛteḥ 
śataguṇo rāgaḥ tannikharvasthānaṃ (l6)
 rāgācchataguṇā niyatiḥ tanmahāsarojasthānaṃ niyateḥ śataguṇā vidyā tanmahāśaṅkhasthānaṃ vidyāyāḥ śataguṇā kalā 
tanmahāsamudrasthānaṃ kalāyāḥ (l7)
 śataguṇaḥ kālaḥ tattu parārthasthānamevamekādiparārtha-sthānaparyantaṃ gaṇanā kāryā sadāśivāvadhi māyātatvādūrdhvaṃ 
sadāśivatatvaparyantaṃ sahasrāt sahasra(l8)
.......māyātatvaṃ vṛddhikāryaṃ sahasrasaṃkhyayā vṛddhīkṛtamāyātatvapramāṇaṃ śuddhavidyā-tatvasya pramāṇamityarthaḥ 
śuddhavidyāpramāṇe sahasreṇa(p51b, l1)
.........īśvaratatvapramāṇaṃ tatpramāṇe sahasreṇa vṛddhikṛte sadāśivatatvapramāṇaṃ tataḥ sadāśivatatvādūrdhvaṃ śaktyantakaṃ 
śaktitatvaparyantaṃ lakṣataḥ (l2)
 lakṣapramāṇena sadāśivatatvapramāṇe vṛddhikṛte śaktitatvasya yojanapramāṇam ūrdhvaṃ śaktitatvādupari 
vidyamānordhvapradeśe vyāpyavyāpakatvena sthitvā (l3)
 ameyavibhavaḥ aparicchedyasāmarthya iyān saṃkhyāvāniti saṃkhyādibhiḥ pramāṇīkartumaśakya ityarthaḥ sadāśivaḥ 
bindorapi adhiṣṭhāyakaḥ (l4)
 adhiṣṭhānasthānabhūtaḥ syāt| uktaṃ ca binduḥ śaktyāḥ śivenaiṣā nānyena vyāpyate śiva iti| 

tathā mataṅge - yojanapramāṇaṃ bhūmestasmāddaśaguṇaṃ (l5)
 toyaṃ toyādagnisto'nilaḥ anilāccāyamākāśaḥ śāstre'smin pārameśvare| ākāśāccāpyahaṅkārastasmācca parato 
(l6)
 mahān mahataḥ parato jñeyaṃ guṇānāṃ tritayaṃ mune| guṇatrayā pradhānākhyaṃ tatvaṃ śataguṇottaram| śatottaraguṇāni syuḥ 
śeṣā(l7)
hyuktāni śāsane| māyāntāni tu tatvāni ste(te)bhyastūrdhvaṃ sahasrayā| yāvat sadāśivaṃ tatvaṃ tatparaṃ lakṣayā 
sthitam| catuṣkaṃ śaktiparyantaṃ tato meyaḥ paraḥ śiva iti||21|| (p52a, l1.)

srotāṃsi pratipuṃsi sūkṣmatanugaistatvairyadānekathā(dhā) māyājānyakhilātmabhogyabhuvanādyādhāratatvaistdā| sthityai tāṃśca 
narān niyojayati tairno lakṣito(l2)
'dhītai svārthāptyai tanudevabhāva-bhuvanairbhoge śivaḥ sādhanaiḥ| pratipuruṣaṃ sūkṣmadehātmanāṃ bhuvanātmanāṃ ca 
māyākāryāṇyanekāni santītyāha| srotasāṃ(l3)
sīti pratipuṃsaḥ puṃsa iti jātyekavacanāt pratyājanāṃ sūkṣmatanugaiḥ sūkṣmadehasvarūpaiḥ tatvaiḥ kalādikṣityantaiḥ 
māyāni māyājātāni (l4)
 srotāṃsi sthānāni anekadhānekaprakārāṇi ātmanāṃ nivāsasthānāni sūkṣmaśarīrāṇi sthūlaśarīrāṇītyarthaḥ 
sakalātmanāṃ sūkṣmasthūladeha(l5)
tvena dvīrupakvāt nikhilātmabhogyabhuvanādhāratatvaiḥ nikhilānāṃ samastānāṃ bhogyāni tattadbhuvanapatitvena 
tattadbhuvananivāsi(l6)
tvena ca bhoktuṃ yogyāni bhuvanapatitvaṃ kālāgnirudrādināmiti jñeyaṃ tannivāsakatvaṃ tatparivārāṇāṃ bhuvanādyānāṃ 
kālāgni(l7)
bhuvanānīnāmādhāratatvaiḥ tattadbhuvanādhiṣṭhānabhūtakalādikṣityantyaiḥ māyājātāni srotāsyanekaprakārāṇi 
sakalātmaviṣayāṇi sakalānāṃ (p52b, l1)
 pralayākalātmanām avyaktaśarīrāṇi avyaktabhuvanāni māyāgarbhāṇi anekāni santi sakalānāṃ vijñānakalānāṃ 
mahāmāyotpannāni śarīrāṇi bhuvanāni ca santi (l2)
 yadā yasminkāle svena sṛṣṭāni tātvikabhuvanāni śarīrakāraṇabhūtakalādikṣityantatatvāni vidyante tadā tasmi-
nkāle śivāhyātmanāṃ dehādisaṃbandhaṃ (l3)
 niyojya sṛṣṭyādipañcakṛtyaṃ karotītyāha| sthityai iti adhiṣṭhitaiḥ śivenādhiṣṭhitaiḥ tatpreryairityarthaḥ tairātmabhiḥ no 
lakṣitaḥ (l4)
 apratyakṣabhūnaḥ śivaḥ ātmani prevakaśive vidyamāne'pi malāvacchinnatvāt adṛṣṭa ityarthaḥ svārthāptyai ātmanāṃ 
svakāryaprayojanaprāptyai bhoge (l5)
 sukhaduḥkhātmake sādhanaiḥ sādhakopāyabhūtaiḥ tanudevabhāva-bhuvanaiḥ tanavaḥ śarīrāṇi devāḥ devatādyotanāt 
bāhyārthaprakāśāt (l6)
 indriyāṇi bhāvāḥ dharmādayo'ṣṭau bhuvanāni kālāgnyādilokā etaiḥ sthityai sthitiṃ kartuṃ tāṃ narān malabaddhātmanaḥ 
niyojayati saṃbaddhayati (l7)
anena dehādikaraṇena śivasya sṛṣṭiruktā bhavati| bhuvanānāmutpādanam ātmanāṃ bhuvanasaṃbaddha(p53a, (side 
heading pāśānugrahaṇā) l1)
śarīrotpādanaṃ ca sṛṣṭiḥ sā sṛṣṭi dvividhā sākṣācchivenopādānabhūtabindunā ca bhuvanotpādanaṃ 
tadbhuvanavāsināmanantādināṃ baindavaśarīrakaraṇaṃ ca anandādināmā yotpannabhuvanakaraṇaṃ tannivāsi(sī)nāmātmanāṃ 
sūkṣmatātvikena bhauvanaśarīrakaraṇaṃ ceti||22|| (l3.)
 
pāśānugrahaṇādyathānuguṇato bhogyasya saṃbhojanaṃ puṃsāṃ tadbaladānato bhagavataḥ kṛtyaṃ ca saṃrakṣaṇaṃ līyante pariṇāmatastu 
(l4)
 samaye tatvāni vailomyato māyāyāṃ parato'pi tāni ca mahā-māyāniśājñayā| tirobhāvādikṛtyamāha| pāśeti 
pāśānugrahaṇāt (l5)
 pāśānāṃ malādināmanugrahaṇāt asyāyaṃ pāśaḥ asyāyaṃ pāśa iti saṃbandhīkaraṇāt yathā vijñānā kalānāṃ 
malamātraṃ (l6)
 pralayākalānāṃ malakarmaṇi sakalānāṃ malamāyākarmaṇi yathānuguṇataḥ tattat pāśānuguṇāna(nu)kulaḥ bhogyasya 
bhoktuṃ (l7)
 yogyasya vastunaḥ saṃbhojanam ātmanāṃ bhogadānaṃ tirobhāvānugrahakṛtyaṃ (p53b, l1)
 pāśānugrahaṇatāratamyādātmanāṃ svarūpajñānaṃ tāratamyādācchādayitvā tadanuguṇabhogabhojanadānaṃ tirobhāva ityarthaḥ 
śivo brahmādideheṣu (l2)
 kiñcijjñastāratamyata ityuktatvāt| puṃsāmātmanāṃ tadbaladānataḥ tasmin bhogyavastuviṣayabhogasaṃbhojane baladānataḥ 
yathā bhogyavastusaṃpādane (l3)
 tathā sthirīkaraṇasāmarthyaḥ pradā(dhā)nataḥ bhagavataḥ śivasya yat kṛtyaṃ tat saṃrakṣaṇaṃ sthitikṛtyamīśvaraśaktyā 
nirudhyasya samastasya jagataḥ sva sva viṣayeti (l4)
 niyogasthāpanaṃ sthitirityarthaḥ saṃhārakṛtyamāha līyanta iti samaye mahāsaṃhāra iti avāntarasaṃhāra iti dvirūpe 
saṃhārakāle tatvā(l5)
misra(śra)ṣṭi(sṛṣṭi)krameṇotpannaśivāditatvāni vailomyataḥ pṛthivyāditatvakrameṇa pariṇāmataḥ prakṛteravasthāntaravat 
svarūpāt atra pṛthivyādi(l6)
pariṇāmabhautādikavaikārikataijasasaṃjñako'haṅkāraḥ yathākrameṇa gandhādipañcakasya prakṛtiḥ ahaṅkāraḥ pariṇāmataḥ 
(p54a, l1)
 prakṛtiḥ guṇapariṇāmaḥ tasyāpyuktaṃ prakṛtiḥ avyaktaḥ rāgaśca vidyā ca pariṇāmaḥ teṣāṃ kalātatvaṃ prakṛtiḥ 
puṃstvakālo niyatiḥ kalā ca pariṇāmaḥ teṣāṃ māyā (l2)
 prakṛtiḥ evaṃ prakṛti-vikṛtijñātavyo māyāyāṃ mayātatve līyante yasmādeva yasya jananaṃ tasyaiva tatrāntarbhāvena 
layaṃ gacchati parato'pi (l3)
 māyo'dhvantāni ca śuddhavidyāditatvāni śuddhavidyā yīśvaratatve īśvaraḥ sadāśive sadāśivaḥ śaktau śaktiḥ 
śāntikalāmastakasthaśāntyatītabhuva(l4)
nasvarūpe śivatatve śivaḥ mahāmāyātmani svopādānabhūtabindau īśājñayā śivājñayā yathākrameṇa līyante 
māyādhona(l5)
nteśvarājñayā līyante tathoktamaghoraśivācāryeṇa gandhe bhūḥ salilaṃ rase hutavaho rūpe marut sparśane śabde syāt 
(l6)
 khamahaṅkṛtau punarimāstanmātrikāstāmase karmākṣāṇi ca rājase sahamaro buddhīndriyaiḥ sātvike| buddhau tacca guṇeṣu 
(l7)
 sā guṇagaṇo vyakte layaṃ gacchati| tadrāgavidye ca kalāṃ prayānti puṃstvaṃ ca kālo niyatiḥ kalā ca māyāṃ ca 
vidyeśa....sau (p54b, l1)
 sa deśaṃ sa śaktimedhā śivameṣa bindumiti||23||

daiveṣvarkasahasravarṣagayugābdhīnāṃ sahasreṣvadho yāteṣveva layastrilokaviṣayastasyaiva (l2)
 sargeṣvapi prāpteṣvatra parārdhatāṃ ca vilayo vyaktāṃ tamasmāt punaḥ sargeṣvasya guṇādikasya ca tathā grandhau(thau) ca 
bindau layaḥ| prakṛtyavasānamavāntara(l3)
saṃhārajāla iti māyāvasānamāhā(ha) saṃhārakāla iti saṃhārakālasaṃkhyānāha| daiveṣviti devasaṃbandhasaṃvatsareṣu 
manuṣyāṇāṃ (l4)
 ṣaṣṭyadhikaśatatrayadinamānamekasaṃvatsaraḥ tādṛśasaṃvatsaramānaṃ devānāmekadinam evaṃ ṣaṣṭyadhikaśatatrayamāno 
devānāmeka(l5)
varṣam evaṃ saṃkhyā tadeva varṣamadhye arkasahasra-varṣagayugābdhīnām arkasahasravarṣagāṇāṃ dvādaśasahasravarṣapramāṇānāṃ (l6)
 yugābdhināṃ kṛtatretādvāparakaliyugacaturṇām atrāyamarthaḥ caturyugapramāṇaṃ devadvādaśasahasravarṣayuktaṃ tatra kṛtayugaṃ 
deva(l7)
catuḥ sahasravarṣaṃ pramāṇaṃ tasya sandhyākālaḥ śatāṣṭakavarṣapramāṇaṃ sandhyākālavarṣaprayuktakṛtayugapramāṇaṃ 
śatāṣṭādhikadevacatuḥ sahasravarṣaṃ tretā(l8)
devatrisahasravarṣaṃ tasyā sandhyāṃśaḥ ṣaṭśataṃ sandhyāṃśayuktatretā prāmāṇaṃ ṣaṭśatādhika-trisahasravarṣaṃ dvāparaḥ devadvisahasravarṣaṃ 
tasya sandhyāṃśastu (55a., l1)
 catuḥ śataṃ sandhyāṃśayuktadvāparapramāṇaṃ catuśśatādhikaṃ dvisahasra-varṣaṃ liḥ devasahasraikavarṣastasya sandhyāṃśastu dviśataṃ 
sandhyāṃśayuktakalipramāṇaṃ dviśatādhika(l2)
sahasravarṣaikavarṣamevaṃ sandhyāṃśayuktadevadvādaśasāhasravarṣapramāṇaṃ caturyugapramāṇamuktaṃ ca catvāryabdasahasrāṇi vai budhāni 
kṛtayugaṃ (l3)
 ekaikaśastato'nyāni tretādyāni yugāni tu sandhyā sandhyāṃśayuktānīti| 

tathā sūtasaṃhitāyām - divyai dvādaśasāhasrairvarṣaiḥ proktaṃ caturyugaṃ (l4)
 kṛtayugaṃ viprāḥ sahasrāṇāṃ catuṣṭayaṃ tasya sandhyā ca sandhyāṃśaḥ proktaścāṣṭaśatādhikaḥ tribhirvaleḥ sahasraistu tretā 
divyaiḥ prakīrtitāḥ kṛtā(l5)
rdhadvāparaḥ proktastadardhaṃ kalirucyate| kramāt sandhyā ca sandhyāṃśasturīyāṃśavivarjita iti kṛtayugasya śataṃ te ca 
dviśataṃ daśataṃ tat (l6)
 sandhyā tat saṃbandhi-sandhyāṃśastu dviśataṃ dviśataṃ bhavati evaṃ tretādīnāmapi yojyam evaṃvidhapramāṇayuktasya 
caturyugasya manuṣya(l7)
saṃvatsarasaṃkhyayā pramāṇaṃ pra.....dṛśyate divyaikasahasravarṣamānaṃ manuṣya-saṃkhyayā trilakṣaṃ ṣaṣṭisahasravarṣaṃ jātaṃ evaṃ 
manuṣyasaṅkhyā kṛtayugapramā(l8)
ṇaṃ saptadaśalakṣa-maṣṭāviṃśatisahasravarṣatretāyugapramāṇadvādaśalakṣaṃ ṣaṇṇavatisahasravarṣa(p55b, l1)
dvāparapramāṇam aṣṭalakṣaṃ catuṣṣaṣṭisahasravarṣa-pramāṇayugaṃ kaliyugapramāṇaṃ caturlakṣaṃ dvātraṃ(triṃ)śatsahasravarṣamevaṃ caturyugānāṃ 
pramāṇaṃ manuṣyasaṅkhyayā tricatvāriṃśa(l2)
llakṣaṇaṃ viṃśati-sahasravarṣaṃ jātamevaṃ saṃkhyātaṃ caturyugānāṃ sahasreṣu gateṣu satsūtrilokaviṣayaḥ 
trilokasthitajanaviṣayīlayaḥ saṃhāraḥ proktaḥ (l3)
 tasyaiva lokatrayeṣu sṛjyabhūtajanasya sargeṣvapi sṛṣṭiṣu caturyugānāṃ sahasreṣviti teṣu sṛṣṭikāla| atra 
kanakāṇḍamadhyasthitabrahmaṇaścatu(l4)
ryugasahasraparyantaṃ divākālastāvat kālaparyantaṃ rātriḥ tasya divākāle lokatrayajanasya sṛṣṭau satyāṃ 
divākālaparyantaṃ sthitikālaḥ (l5)
 brahmaṇo jāgradavasthākālatvāt tasya rātrau lokatrayajanasya saṃhārakālaḥ brahmaṇo nidrāvasthākālatvāt uktaṃ ca 
svavyāpāraniyuktā(l6)
nāmindrāṇāmāyurucyate| ekaikasya bhavedāyuryugānāmekasaptatiḥ taiścaturdaśabhiḥ śakrairdinaṃ kamalajanmanaḥ 
lokatrayasthitiriyaṃ (l7)
 samāsena tu tāviti| etāvatī rātriryat syāṃ lokatrayamahāmune| kṣayaṃ yāti tamaḥ prāpte tadavasthā maharmukhe 
brahmāpyutthāya śayanādā(l8)
vi(va)śyakaruṇātmanā| rudraśaktibalāt bhūyo nirma(rmā)ṇo jagat sadeti (56a, l1)
 avāntarakālasaṅkhyāṃ vaktuṃ brahmaṇo| dinamānamuktvā prakṛtitatvāvasānabhūtaṃ vyastajagatsaṃhārakālasaṅkhyāmāha atra 
brahma(l2)
saṃbandhīsaṃvatsareṣu brahmaṇo dinaṣaṣṭyadhikaśatatrayaṃ cet| brahmasaṃvatsaraḥ parārdhatāmuttaratra vakṣyamāṇaparārdhasaṃkhyāṃ prāpteṣu 
gateṣu satsu (l3)
 vyaktāntaṃ prakṛtitatvāvasānaṃ vilayaḥ saṃhāraḥ proktaḥ| 

tathā mṛgendre - caturyugasahasrāntamaharhe māṇḍajanmanaḥ niśātāvadyahorātra(l4)
manenāstu parārdhake| vilayo vyaktaparyantaṃ prakṛtyādīni vāryate| asmāt kāraṇabhūtaprakṛtitatvāt punaḥ asya 
guṇādikasya guṇādi(l5)
prathivyantasya sargeṣu sṛṣṭiṣu ca tathā parārdhakālasaṅkhyāparyantasaṃhārakālapramāṇaṃ tadanantaraṃ sṛṣṭikāla ityarthaḥ 
mahāsaṃhāra(l6)
kālasaṅkhyāmāha| granthai ca māyātatve bindau sarvopādanakāraṇabhūte layaḥ saṃhāraḥ prakṛtyādīnāṃ sthitiḥ 
parārthasaṃkhyā(l7)
saṃhārastu parārtha(rdha)saṅkhyeti dviparārtha(rdha)pramāṇaṃ sṛṣṭisaṃhārakālaḥ tāvati prakṛtyādi-sṛṣṭisaṃhāraparivartane parārtha(rdha) 
(l8)
saṃkhyāṃ gate prakṛtyādikrameṇa māyāyāṃ layaḥ śuddhavidyādikrameṇa bindau layaḥ taduktaṃ mṛgendre| evaṃ guṇādisargāṇāṃ 
parārthe(rdhe) (p56b, l1)
 guṇakāraṇaṃ kalālebhikalāmamāyā svādhikāraparāṅmukhī tannivṛttau nivṛttaṃ te devāstadadhikāriṇaḥ sargasthityādiko 
yasmādadhikāra(l2)
stadāśrayaḥ evaṃ matre(ntre)śamukhyeṣu viśatsvabhi-mataṃ padaṃ vidyāmatti sadā tatvaṃ tadbindubaindavaṃ dhvaniḥ 
nāmamattiparāśaktiḥ śaktimiṣṭe (l3)
 svayaṃ hara iti||24||

ekaṃ paṅktiśataṃ sahasramayutaṃ lakṣaṃ prapūrvāyutaṃ koṭiścābaśadakaṃ ca padakamadho (l4)
 kharvaṃ nikharvaṃ tathā| bṛndaṃ caiva mahāsarojamaparaḥ śaṅkhaḥ samudro'ntimān madhyaṃ caiva parārdhasaṃjñikamimāṃ (l5)
 saṅkhyāṃ viduḥ paṇḍitāḥ| ekādiparārdhāntakaṃ viṃśatisaṃkhyāsthā| namastīsyāha| ekamiti|| (l6)
 ekamekasthānaṃ paṅktiḥ daśasaṅkhyā vā cidaśottaraguṇanena sarvaṃ guṇa-nīyamiti dvitīyasthānaṃ paṃtī(paṅktī)śabde(l7)
noktaṃ śatadvitīyamānandaśaguṇaṃ kṛtvā śatasaṅkhyākaṃ tritīyasthānaṃ taddaśaguṇaṃ cet sahasraṃ caturthasthānam ayutaṃ 
daśasahasra(p57a, l1)
saṅkhyākaṃ pañcamasthānaṃ taddaśaguṇaṃ cellakṣaṃ śatasahasrasaṅkhyākaṃ ṣaṣṭhasthānaṃ prapūrvāyutaṃ praśabdapūrvāyutaṃ prayutaṃ daśalakṣaṃ 
sapta... (l2)
sthānaṃ taddaśaguṇaṃ cet koṭiḥ śatalakṣa-saṅkhyākam aṣṭamasthānaṃ taddaśaguṇamarbudaṃ navamasthānaṃ taddaśaguṇakaṃ padmakaṃ 
daśamasthānaṃ taddaśaguṇaṃ (l3)
 kharvam ekādaśasthānaṃ taddaśaguṇaṃ nikharvaṃ dvādaśasthānaṃ taddaśaguṇaṃ bundaṃ trayo-daśasthānaṃ taddaśaguṇaṃ mahāsarojaṃ (l4)
 caturdaśasthānam aparaḥ śākha iti śaṅkho mahāśaṅkha ityarthaḥ mahāsarojaṃ daśaguṇaṃ cecchaṅkhaṃ (l5)
 pañcadaśasthānaṃ taddaśaguṇaṃ mahāśaṅkhaḥ ṣoḍaśasthānaṃ taddaśaguṇaḥ samudraḥ saptadaśasthānaṃ taddaśaguṇam antimān 
mahā(l6)
samudraḥ aṣṭādaśasthānaṃ taddaśaguṇaṃ madhyam ekonaviṃśatisthānaṃ taddaśaguṇaṃ parārdhasaṃjñakaṃ viṃśatisthānaṃ paṇḍitā 
śivāgamaśāstrajñāḥ evaṃ mīmāṃsasaṃkhyāṃ viduḥ vadanti||25|| (p57b, l1.)

saṃhāre bhuvaneśvarā hariradho brahmā ca tatprāpiṇo vijñānā kalatāṃ prayānti purataḥ kāle tadātve'pi ye| ṛkpāke 
svapade-cchayā virahitā śarva(l2)
sya te'nugrahānmuktāsyu(syuḥ) svapadaṃ dadhatyapi pare tattat paraṃ te pare| kālāgniprabhṛti śatarudrādayo bhuvaneśvarāḥ 
pralayakāle (l3)
 triḥ rūpamāśra(śri)tya tiṣṭhantītyāha| saṃhāra iti saṃhāre madhyapralaye mahāpralaye ca bhuvaneśvarāḥ śatarudrādayaḥ 
harirviṣṇuḥ brahmā (l4)
 caturmukhaḥ tatprāpiṇaḥ saṃhāre gacchantaḥ vijñānākalatāṃ vijñānākalātmaprāptitāṃ prayānti gacchanti etena 
mahāsaṃhārakālaḥ sūcitaḥ (l5)
 vijñānā kalā prāptermāyāṃ tatvāt māyāparyantaṃ saṃhāro mahāsaṃhāra ityuktatvācca| prakṛtiparyantaṃ madhyapralaye tu 
bhuvane(l6)
śvarāḥ śaktirūpatayā prakṛtisthakṣityādikalāntatatvārabdhasūkṣmadehāḥ santaḥ svaśaktyā bhuvanasthitāṇūn tatrādhiṣṭhāya 
tiṣṭhanti| (l7.)

tathā mṛgendre - tadā rudraśataṃ vīraśrīkaṇṭhau dvau pradhānapāḥ śaktyākramya jagatsūkṣmaṃ sūkṣmadehāśca cidvataḥ 
prakṛtisthā(l8)
śayāt kālaṃ yāvat svāpāntamāsa iti punaravāntarasargā prārambhā kāle'nantādirudrapreritāḥ santaḥ tattat sva 
bhuvanādhikāraṃ prakurvantī (p58a, l1)
 uktaṃ ca śive'ṣṭamantraprabhṛti maṇḍalādhipatīritāḥ kāle sagatsamutpādya svādhikāraṃ prakurvata iti mahāsaṃhāre tu 
śaktirūpatayā māyāyāṃ vidyamānajaga....(t)(l2)
su atisūkṣmaśarīratayā saṃyuktāḥ tadadhikāriṇaḥ prakṛtyādibhuvanasthitabhuvaneśvarāśca viratavyāpāraḥ santaḥ vijñānā 
kalātmatāṃ prāpnuvanti teṣāṃ madhye (l3)
 yeṣāṃ mahāsaṃhārakāle malaparipākāste muktā bhavantītyāha| tadātvaikāle mahāsaṃhārasvarūpakāle purataḥ 
saṃhārakālāt (l4)
 pūrvameva ye bhuvaneśvarāḥ ṛrutpāke malaparipāke sati svapadecchayā virahitāḥ santaḥ śarvasya parameśvarasyānugrahāt 
(l5)
 paramokṣapradānāt muktāsyurmuktā bhavanti pare'pi malaparipākābhāvāt| svapadecchayā yuktā anyeva svapadaṃ māyāyāṃ 
śakti(l6)
rūpatayāvasthitaṃ svasthānaṃ dayati dhārayanti para te śuddhādhvanyadhikṛtā anantādayaḥ tattatparaṃparakaivalyabhūtaṃ (l7)
 śivasvarūpaṃ dadhati anenānugrahakṛtyaṃ śivasya sūcitaṃ bhavati||26||

viśrāntyai bhavināṃ ca khinnamanasāṃ pākāya (l8)
 karmāvalermāyāvāgbalasaṃbhavāya kurute śambhurmahāsaṃhṛtiṃ śiṣyante paśavaḥ sa śaktiramalaḥ (p58b., l1)
 kurute bhogāya puṃsāṃ punaḥ| ātmanāṃ prakṛtiprayojanārthameva mahāsaṃhāraṃ śivaḥ karotītyāha| viśrāntyā iti 
bhavināṃ saṃsāriṇāṃ (l2)
 bhavādhvabhramaṇatvāntānām ata eva khinnamanasāṃ duḥkhitamanasāmātmānaṃ viśrāntyai saṃhāre maraṇajananābhāvāt sukhena 
sthīyamānatvena viśramaṇārthaṃ (l3)
 saṃsārajāta...ni parihārthamityarthaḥ kiṃ ca karmāvaleḥ anekajanmārjitakarmasamūhasya pākāya pacanārthaṃ saṃhāre 
bhogābhāvāt karmajanitaphalapari(l4)
ṇāmāyetyarthaḥ kiṃ ca māyāvāgbalasaṃbhavāya māyāvācoḥ anavarataṃ 
tanukaraṇabhuvanādyanekakāryajananasantatyāpacitaśaktyoḥ (l5)
 aśuddha-śuddhādhvopādānabhūtayormāyābindoḥ balasaṃbhavāya punaḥ sargakaraṇasāmarthyotpādanāya śambhuḥ sarvajanahitarataḥ 
parameśvaro (l6)
 mahāsaṃhṛtiṃ mahāsaṃsāraṃ kurute karoti avāntarasaṃhārastu anantādinetyarthaḥ| 

tathā tatvaprakāśe - saṃhāre khinnānāṃ nikhila.... (l7)
 prāṇīnāṃ prabhuḥ kṛpayā kurute mahārthasaṃhṛtimeteṣāmeva viśrāntyai iti mahāsaṃhārakāle sarveṣāmabhāvāt nityatvena 
(l8)
 śeṣitāraḥ ka ityāha| paśavaḥ malasaṃbandhāt sarve buddhātmanaḥ saśaktiḥ samavetajñānakriyāśaktiḥ 
anādimalarahitaḥ śambhuḥ (p59a, l1)
 śaṃ sukhaṃ preryatārūpaṃ bhāvayatīti śambhuḥ parameśvaraḥ cakāreṇātra śivāḥ muktātmano nādyāvārakatvena 
nityatvavyāpitvayukto mala śvagṛhyante| māyādvayaṃ mā.... (l2)
 binduriti aśuddhaśuddhajagadupādānadvayaṃ śiṣyante śeṣitā bhavanti nityatvena vidyanta itirthaḥ| vāsanātmakatvena 
sthitakarmaphalabhogābhāve hyātmanāṃ karma .... (l3)
 bhāvena muktirna saṃghaṭata iti kṛtvā karmaphalabhogārthaṃ punaḥ sṛṣṭyādikṛtyaṃ karotītyāha| tataśca saṃhārānantaraṃ 
dayayā kṛpayā puṃsātmanāṃ (l4)
 bhogāya prāgārjitakarmaphalabhogāya punaḥ paścāt sṛṣṭyādyaṃ sṛṣṭyādipaṃ ca kṛtyaṃ kurute bhogena karmanāśaṃ vidhāya 
paśūn (l5)
 mocayituṃ punarapi sṛṣṭyādikṛtyaṃ karotītyarthaḥ taduktaṃ kiraṇe| aneka-bhavikaṃ karmadagdhaṃ bījamivāṇubhiḥ 
bhaviṣyadapi saṃruddhaṃ (l6)
 yenedaṃ taddhi bhogata iti saṃhāre śambhvādīnāṃ nityatvena tatvatrayavacanaṃ śambhuḥ puruṣo māyā nityaṃ vibhuḥ 
kartṛśaktiyuktaśca supte'pi (l7)
 vikṛtijāte tri(tṛ)yaṃ jāgarti tatvānāmiti||27|| 
jñānapādaṃ samāptam|| 

kriyāpādamucyate -
śrījñānāmbāsameta śrīkālahastīśvarakri(kṛ)pākaṭākṣasiddhirastu oṃ śubhamastu| (p59b, l1)
 mūle kālena paktve patati naravare śaktirādyā bhave'smiddoṣo niśreyasecchāgu āvarabhajanaṃ tadgete śo'sya hautryā 
(l2)
cośān sañchintya śaktyā nayati śivapadaṃ vyañjayantacchivatvaṃ dīkṣāpradatoṅgapāśakṣayakarasamayācārameṣo'nutiṣṭet|| 
(l3)
 itthaṃ jñānapādaviśiṣṭaṃ ṣaṭtriṃśa-tatvarūpaṃ nirūpyedāniṃ kriyāpādamārambhamāṇastatrāpi dīkṣāsvarūpa(l4)
 vaktukāma dīkṣāyāśca malaparipākatvena śaktipātapūrvakatvāt prathamaṃ tāvacchaktipācavicāramāha| mūla iti mū(le) 
(l5)
 anādimale kālena kenāpi viśiṣṭakālena pakve paripāki(kya)vasthāṃ gate sati yathā cakṣuḥ paṭalādeḥ ....lena 
(l6)
 pākaḥ sañjāyate tathā malasya dravyatvāt kālāntareṇa pākaḥ saṃbhavatīti kālena pakva ityuktaṃ viśiṣṭakālastu 
(l7)
 paripākasya dharmādharmasvarūpasya karmaṇaḥ samavibhāgarūpaḥ atrāyamarthaḥ yasmin kāle karmaṇaḥ samatā (p60a, 
l1)
 bhavati sa kāla eva malaparipākakāraṇamityucyate karmasamatvābhāvena kaśchinnnūnaḥ kaścidadhiko bhavatīti 
vaktavyaḥ nyūnādhikatve sati ....(l2)
rmaṇordharmā-dharmātmakayormadhye य़ḥkaściddharmātmako vādharmātmako vādhikyāt balavattaraḥ sa pūrvaṃ bhogado bhavati tasmin 
karmaṇi (l3)
 bhogaṃ datvā nivṛtte sati paścāditaro bhogada syāṃ na tulyaḥ tasmāt samatve sati yo bhogaḥ saḥ tasyātmanaḥ 
kathaṃ bhavati| uktaṃ ca (l4)
 karmāṃśo yo'dhikaḥ pūrvaḥ bhogadastvitaraḥ punaḥ samatve sati yo bhogaḥ kathaṃ tasya prajāyata iti| yadi samatve 
satyapi .... (l5)
 karmaṇi bhogade bhavataḥ| tarhi tadā yugapadevātmā svarganarakaphalamanubhavet| tathā na tasmādādhikyena bhogamiti 
siddhaṃ malaparipākādādyā (l6)
 śaktiḥ tirodhānaśaktiḥ narapare puruṣaśreṣṭhe patati patitā bhavati nanu śaktiḥ patatītyuktaṃ sarvatra vyāpakatvāt 
vyāpakasya vastutaḥ kasmāt (p60b, l1)
 chaktāt sthānādapatanaṃ saṃbhavatīti śaktipātasyāsaṃbhavaḥ prāptaḥ atrāyaṃ parihāraḥ śabdānāmarthapravṛttirmukhyā gauṇī 
cet .....pra.... (l2)
 tasmācchaktiḥ patatyatra gauṇiśabdapravṛttirna mukhyā| yathā loke asya puruṣasya dṛṣṭirasyāṃ nāminyāṃ patitetyukte 
kiṃ (l3)
 tatpuruṣādvimucya dṛṣṭiḥ kāminyāṃ patitā dṛśyate atra tu dṛśyadarśakasaṃbaddhamātreṇa saṃbandhā dṛṣṭiḥ patitetyupacāreṇa 
śabdānāṃ (l4)
 pravṛttiṇocyate tadvadihāpyasmin puṃsyanugrāhyānugrāhakalakṣaṇa-saṃbandhena saṃbandhāśaktiḥ patitetyupacāreṇoktānu.... 
(l5)
 tu mukhyata ityadoṣaḥ|

tathā kiraṇe - upacāreṇa śabdānāṃ pravṛttiriha dṛśyate yathā pumān| vibhurnityo vinaṣṭaḥ śivatāṃ gata iti| (l6)
 asyāpyayamarthaḥ| yathā pumānityo'pi dehapātādupacāreṇa vināśaṃ hata ityucyate| yathā vibhurvyāpako'pi 
vyaktyaśiva....... (p61a, l1)
pacāreṇa vināśaṃ gata ityucyate evaṃ śaktipāto'pyupacārataḥ prokta iti mantavyaḥ| atha bhavatu tāva-
daupacāriko'yaṃ śaktipa.... (l2)
pyamūrto'yamātmanaḥ kiṃ kartuṃ śaknoti tatpātānantaramātmā kiṃ karoti yathā loke kasyacidvastuno kvacit| 
pātobhayapado bhavati .......... (l3)
merājñopadravo bhaviṣyatyayaṃ śabdanipātaḥ amūrto'pi tadgrāmavāsināṃ ja....nāṃ bhaya.... (l4)
do dṛṣṭaḥ| yataḥ śaktipātāt pūrvaṃ saṃsārabhoga eva paramaṃ sukhaṃ tataḥ śaktipātādi-ṣadu(ḍu)nmīlijñānacakṣuḥ 
saṃsārasyāsā(l5)
ratāmālocya svasthānadācāryaṃ bhavabhayaharaṇadakṣaṃ prāpnoti uktaṃ ca| nipātobhayadoyadvandvastu naḥ sahasā bhavettasmā.... 
(l6)
...tayātyeva tathātmadeśikaṃ pratīti imamevārthaṃ manasi nidhāyāsya śaktipātaḥ sañjāta iti kathaṃ gamyata iti 
śaktipātacihnamāha atrāya....śakti.... (l7)
...vijñānakalapralayākalayo-raśarīriṇoḥ śaktipādasya durvijñeyatvāt sa śarīrasya sakalātmano viṣayaḥ 
adhvamevārtha....... (p61b, l1)
 ityuktvā jñāpitaḥ| sakalasya sādhāraṇatvādvijñānakalapralayākalayo-rnirādhāratvāditi bhāvaḥ asmin bhave 
vartamā......... (l2)
tītyāgecchāni śreyasecchāmokṣacchā guruvarabhajanaṃ guruvarāṇāṃ guruśreṣṭhānāṃ parameśvarādavicchinnasaṃpradāyajñānavatā.... 
varyā .... (l3)
ṇāmityarthaḥ| bhajanaṃ śiśrūṣeṇādinā sevanam ayamabhiprāyaḥ| asmin kāle ko'haṃ kimu duḥkhāddu(dduḥ)khaṃ paramiti 
saṃcitya (l4)
mumukṣoḥ sakalasya puruṣasya yathāvasthāt saṃsārikajñānādityutkaṇṭhādiliṅgaṃ yat svarūpavijñānaṃ bhavati tat 
svarūpa(l5)
jñānārthaṃ yadā gurubhajanaṃ ca jāyate tadā sā śaktiḥ patitetyucyate| uktaṃ ca| yeṣāṃ śarīriṇāṃ śaktiḥ patatyapi na 
vṛttaye (l6)
 teṣāṃ talliṅgamautsukyamuktau dveṣo bhava sthitam| bhaktiṃ ca śivabhakteṣu śraddhā tacchāsane vidhau| anena sthiramiti| 
tadocyataḥ śiṣye (l7)
 heto sthūladhiyāmapi| īṣadarthanivṛtte tu rodhakatve tamaḥ pateḥ bhavantyetāni liṅgāni kiñcicchiṣṭe ca dehināṃ yadā 
svarūpavijñānaṃ ...... (p62a, l1)
teti tadyocyata iti guruvaralakṣaṇaṃ cāgameṣūktam| vidyāpādārthakuśalaḥ kriyāpādagatakramaḥ 
yogapādakṛtābhyāsaścaryāpādānuvartakaḥ| śivaśāstra(l2)
paro nityaṃ pūjāgnitatpara iti| tadgateśaḥ tasmin guru arogataḥ adhiṣṭhānarūpatvena sti(sthi)taḥ īśaḥ parameśvaraḥ 
śivādanyasya pāśaviśeṣaṇa(l3)
sāmarthyābhāvāt ācāryamātramadhikaraṇamadhiṣṭhāya śiva eva pāśavimocaka ityarthaḥ uktaṃ ca ācārya-mūrtimāsthāya 
caturthaśaktipātitaḥ (l4)
 bhagavānanugṛhṇāti sa eva sakalānapi ata eva guruvaraḥ śivasvarūpa iti siddhaḥ uktaṃ ca| yo guruḥ sa śivaḥ 
proktaḥ yaḥ śivaḥ sa guru (l5)
 smṛtaḥ gururvā śiva evātha vidyākāreṇa saṃsthitaḥ yathā śivastathā vidyā tathā guruḥ śivavidyāgurūṇāṃ ca pūjayā 
sadṛśaṃ phalaṃ sarvadevātmakaścā (l6)
 saut sarvamantramayo guruḥ tasmāt sarvaprakāreṇa tasyājñāṃ śirasā vahet iti asya svarūpajñānārthaṃ gurusamīpaṃ 
gatasya śiṣyasya (l7)
 pāśāt malamāyākarmaṇi kṣautryākaṇḍamaṇḍalapūrvakayā māntrīkayā dīkṣāyā śaktyā pāśasūtragrandhicchedayatā kartari 
svarūpayā jñānaśaktisañchi(p62b, l1)
.....cadanakṛtvā uktaṃ ca kartari jñānaśakti(ktiḥ) syādyathā pāśā.....natyasau sakalā paramā sūkṣmā mantrāṇāṃ 
bodhani parā| kartari ...... (l2)
 deśikottameriti| tacchivatvaṃ śivasvarūpaṃ vyañjayan samyak pradarśayan śivapadaṃ sāmānyavācinā śivaśabdenātra 
mandādiśakti-pātānusāreṇa (l3)
 rudrāṇāṃ mantreśānāṃ vidyeśānāṃ śivasya vā padaṃ sthānaṃ nayati prāpayati| uktaṃ ca|| rudramantrapatīśānapadabhājo 
(l4)
 bhavanti te| sthitau yānanugṛhṇāti gurumāsthāya cidvata iti dhīkṣāpradetaḥ dīkṣayā pavitrīkṛtaḥ eṣaḥ samayi 
putrakasādhakanāmnoktaḥ (l5)
 śiṣyaḥ aṅgapāśakṣayakarasamayācāraṃ aṅgaṃ nityanaimittikāṅgabhūtaṃ pāśakṣayakaraṃ malādipāśā-nāśakaraṃ samayācāraṃ 
(l6)
 svasiddhāntavihitācāraṃ anutiṣṭhet anuṣṭhīyamānatvena pālayet sabījadīkṣitānāṃ tu samayācāralopane 
anṛtaprāyaścittānāṃ (p63a, l1)
 piśācapada-prāptitvāt uktaṃ ca| samayācāralopena yānti śaivapiśācatāmiti samayācārastvāgameṣūkto yathā 
sama........... (l2)
 sādhakasya viśeṣataḥ nityaṃ krameṇa kathitaṃ yāvajjīvaṃ śivājñayā| snānaṃ pūjā japo dhyānaṃ homaṃ caiva tu pañcamaḥ 
iti rītyāṃ sada...... (l3)
...nāṅgaṃ procyate punaḥ aṣṭamyāṃ ca caturdaśyāṃ pakṣayorubhayorapi pañcadaśyāṃ dinadhvaṃse saṃkrāntau dakṣiṇāyane māsa(l4)
...viddhau dinādhikye ṣaḍaśīti mukheṣvapi ravi(vī)ndugrahaṇe caiva dviguṇārcā japādikaṃ cāturmāsye'pi tadvat syādvrataṃ 
mattaṃ(taṃ) (l5)
 haviṣyabhuk aṣṭamyamā-caturdaśyāṃ na bhoktavyaṃ kadācana| iti nityāṅgamevoktaṃ naimittikamathocyate| dīkṣā caiva 
pratiṣṭhā ca (l6)
 jñeyaṃ naimittikaṃ ...dhā nityaṃ naimittikaṃ kāmyamācāryasyoditaṃ trayaṃ guruṇāpi trayaṃ kāryaṃ vyākhyānaṃ deśikena tu 
nityaṃ ..... sā....saśāstroktaṣaḍānanā(na) samayiputrakābhyāṃ tu nityamevaṃ prakīrtitaṃ svaśāstroktaṃ prakartavyaṃ paraśāstraṃ 
na kārayet na nindet kāraṇa..... (p63b, l1)
 .... na sāṣṭitaṃ na guruṃ sādhakaṃ caiva liṅgacchāyā na laṅghayet ........ nirmālya(yaṃ) na dadyācchivadīkṣitaḥ 
śiva....gurupūjā ca (l2)
 kartavyā jīvitaḥ ....śūnyamastakaṃ liṅgaṃ dṛṣṭvā yā(pā)yāt kadācana ityādisamayācārāḥ 
pālani....śivārthibhiriti|| ka ||1||

svāmnāyajānusiddhā ca ra....... (l3)
 kalaṃ cācaranvīplutau taṃ niṣkṛtyādhyomṛto'nte śivanihitamanāstvayāgācchivatvaṃ labdhvā vyāpyārptaviśvaḥ paraḥ 
śivasamo neśitastena ..... (l4)
 naijāhlādaikapūrṇā nikhilaparivṛḍhaḥ śaṅkaraprītirāste|| evaṃ śivadīkṣābhiṣiktaḥ samayācārapālako dehānte śivapadaṃ 
(l5)
 prāpnotītyāha| svāmnāyeti svāmnāyajñānasiddhā-caraṇam| svāmnāyaḥ svasiddhāntakāmikāditantraṃ tasya jñānena 
dharmopadeśakatvādayaṃ dharma iti (l6)
 siddhāntavihitavijñānena siddhaṃ idamiti niścitam ācaraṇamanuṣṭhānanitya-naimittikādikam avikalaṃ 
sakāmākāmabhedena sañjātaloparahitaṃ (l7)
 yathā bhavati tathā caret anuṣṭīyamānatayā kurvan cakāro vedādivihitānuṣṭhānasūcanārthaḥ tadanuṣṭhānaṃ ca 
āgameṣu(ṣū)ktatvāt| 

tathā kā......(raṇe) (l8)
śrutismṛtyādisaṃsiddhamanuṣṭhānāṃ tu yadbhavet āvaśyake vā śauce vā snāne vācamane'pi vā sandhyāyā vandane vāpi 
tarpaṇe ..... (ho)(p64a, l1)
makarmaṇi vedādhyāyanasaṃskāre somayāgādike'pi vā garbhādhānādike vāpi dahane śrāddhakarmaṇi| aṣṭakākaraṇe vāpi 
nityanaimittike'pi vā (l2)
 vedākyayajanādau vā dvijānāṃ vihitaṃ tu yat tatsarvaṃ caiva kartavyaṃ na vāvaśyaṃ tu śaivakaṃ kartavyaṃ cedanuṣṭheyaṃ 
śaivakarmāvirodhi...... (l3)
 virudhyaṃ(ddhaṃ) cet parityajyaṃ na tu śaivaṃ parityajet saṃgrāhya vaidikaṃ sarvaṃ śaivavākyā-rodhata iti| 

tathā mohaśūrottare – vedasmṛtipurā(l4)
ṇādyā āgamādharmadeśikāḥ| sāmānyaṃ ca viśeṣaṃ ca śaivaṃ vaiśeṣikaṃ vaca iti idaṃ śrātānuṣṭhānaṃ śaivānuṣṭhānaṃ ca 
lokadharmiṇyā (l5)
 dīkṣitānāmeva proktam| na tu śivadharmiṇyā di(dī)kṣitānāṃ vaidikānāṃ teṣāṃ śaivānuṣṭhānamevāvaśyakaṃ 
tyaktalaukikā(l6)
cāraśivadharmiṇītyuktatvāt śivadharmiṇo mūlaṃ śivadharmaphalāśraya iti vacanācca vaidikaṃ ca purā kṛtvā tataḥ śaivaṃ 
(l7)
 samācarediti lokadharmiṇiviṣayavacanam| itarastu virāgātmā laukikārthaparāṅmukhaḥ kuryāt brāhmiṃ na vā kuryāt 
śaivameva niyogata iti (l8)
 ......dharmiviṣayavacanaṃ śrautasandhyānuṣṭhāne tu kāmacārato vaikalpikavacanaḥ| (itarastu virāgātmā laukikārthaparā) 
makhyataḥ bhau...... (p64b, l1)
 naiṣṭhikastadvat brāhmi ka(ku)ryāt purā na vā śaivi tu niyamenaiva sevyāni yatamānasairityuktatvāt viplutau 
sakāmākāmabhedena samayācāralope sati atra (l2)
 sakāmaḥ svecchālopaḥ devādinā paravaśatvādakāmaḥ uktaṃ ca| devādrogāttathā mohādrājacorabhayādibhiḥ ebhiḥ 
doṣairakāmitvaṃ (l3)
 śeṣaijñe(rjñe)yā tu kāmitā idaṃ karmaivamanuṣṭheyamānamiti śāstrādijñānarāhityaṃ mohaśabdārthaḥ tanniṣkṛtyā 
tasyācāralopasya niṣkṛtyā (l4)
 pūraṇātmakavidhānamasya lopasyedaṃ prāyaścittamiti śāstroktānuṣṭhānetyarthaḥ āḍhyaḥ samṛddhaḥ prāyaścittasaṃyukta 
ityarthaḥ| tathoktā prāyaścittaṃ (l5)
 prakartavyaṃ śāstroktaṃ gurubhāṣitam anyathā niṣpa(pha)lā dīkṣā karmakāṇḍaṃ ca tasya gatiḥ ante prāṇāvasānakāle 
śivanihitamanāḥ prathamaślokoktavat hṛdayādi-sthānato (l6)
 brahmādikāraṇeśvaratyāgakrameṇa mūlādhārānvitanādasvarūpaṃ prāsādamantroccāreṇātmānaṃ yogasāmarthyaṃ kalpayet| 
brahmaradhra(rudra)bhedena ..... (l7)
ścat brahmarandhra-bhedabhāvanayā dvādaśāntasthaparaśivena sahaitya(kya)bhāvanayā ca śivaniścalatvena sthitamanāḥ mṛto 
gataprāṇaḥ| 

tathā triśatikālottare - (p65a, l1)
 āsanaṃ padmakaṃ badhvā yathāsukhamathāpi ca mūrdhni cittaṃ samādhāya manasoccārayeddharaṃ śanaiḥ  śanaiḥ samākṛṣya 
bāhyadāḍhyāṃ vyavasthitaḥ rudra ........... (l2)
...yet kuṇḍalyagre ru(ṛ)ṇaprabhaṃ dhāvantaṃ ghaṇṭikāmārgaṃ tato hitkāṃ ca kārayet saṃpūjya prakṣipe(pet) skandavāyuṃ 
śrotre vyavasthitaṃ niṣpīḍyordhva...... (l3)
tayā vattat svastikaṃ gataṃ sadāśivoṃ tu tasyordhvaṃ vibhunādaṃ tatopari bindunādaṃ parityajya tataḥ śaktimanuvrajet 
śaktiṃ tyaktvā vrajedante śivaṃ parama(l4)
kāraṇaṃ yatra gatyā na jāyeta pratijñānanuputraka iti saṃkṣepataḥ proktā utkrāntistava suvrateti evamaśaktasya 
prāṇāvasāni kāladhyānavyāpāraḥ|| (l5)
 aśaktasyaiva tatsarvaṃ tamuddiśya deśikādinā kāryam| 

tathā kāmike - paṭhedutkrāntisamaye karṇopāntena susaṃhitāṃ viśeṣācchivamantraṃ ca tathā pañcākṣara(l6)
...vā| etat sarvaṃ vidheyaṃ syāddeśikenetareṇa veti| antyayāgāt gatāsordehasya vidhīyamānasaṃskāro'ntyayāgaḥ 
(p65b, l1)
 antyeṣṭhiḥ tasmādantyā yāgyakaśivatvaṃ labdhvā prāptaḥ nanu antyayāgācchivatvamiti nopapannaṃ pūrvameva dīkṣākāle 
pāśacchedena śivatve prāpte (l2)
 vidyamānatvāt atrāyaṃ parihāraḥ dīkṣānantarakālamajñānotpannasamayācāralopayuktasyājñānādevākṛtaprāyaścittasya 
dīkṣitasya (l3)
 śudhyarthamantyaiṣṭhaḥ kāryeti manasi nidhāyoktam ata evāgameṣu nityādi samyaganuṣṭhātri(tṛ)ṇāntasya 
śuddhisahitāmantyeṣṭhiṃ vihāya kevalaśivāgnināntyeṣṭhiḥ kāryā (l4)
 samācāralopayuktānāṃ tu tatvaṃ na śuddhiyutāntyeṣṭiḥ kāryetyuktā| 

tathā kāmike - ācāryāḥ sādhakā vā syuḥ putrakāḥ samayasti(sthi)tāḥ gadā sa...... (l5)
 yadā teṣāṃ kāryāntyeṣṭīstu dīkṣitaiḥ| ācāryādyāstrayaścaite yadyācāravivarjitāḥ prāya-ścittamakurvāṇā 
mṛtāścāntyeṣṭikarmaṇāṃ ta...... (l6)
ddhiṃ ca nityādikarma samyaganuṣṭhitaṃ parihṛtya niṣiddhaṃ ca yaisteṣāmapramādīnāṃ tatvaśuddhiṃ vihāyaiva kevalāntyeṣṭirucyata 
iti śivaprāptyananta.......ktātmane .... (l7)
kṣaṇamāha| vyāptyā vyāpakatvena āptaviśvakiñcijñātvādirāhityena sarvajñatvādiprāptyā prāptaviśvaviṣaṭhikṛjñānaḥ| 
anena sarvajñakādayo guṇa.... (p66a, l1)
vanti sarvajñatā tṛptiranāditvo go svatantratā nityamaluptaśaktiḥ| anantaśaktiriti| ata eva paramaśivasamaḥ 
paramaśivasamānarūpaḥ eteti| ....... (l2)
najñānaṃ nityanirmalamiti dyotitaṃ bhavati paśudṛgbhirudīritatvāt pāśasaṃbandhena teṣāṃ paramaśivasamānatvābhāvāt uktaṃ 
ca| anyatantreṣu ........ (l3)
 paśavo matā iti sarvajñaparamaśiva-praṇītāptavākyāgamajñānameva niśreyasahetuḥ uktaṃ ca| raurave 
paśūnābhuktimuktyarthamāptaḥ paśu..... (l4)
 saḥ yadāptavākyama-karodāptavākyaṃ tadāgama iti| 

tathā ca vāyupurāṇe - upamanyukṛṣṇasaṃvāde śivāgamoktaṃ yat jñānaṃ tadeva jñānamuttamam| (l5)
 śrīkaṇṭhena śivenoktaṃ śivāyaiṣa śivāgamaḥ| śivāśritānāṃ kāruṇyāt śreyasāmeṣa sādhana-miti evaṃ muktānāṃ 
śivasya ca samānatve sati śivasya (l6)
 ko viśeṣa ityāha tena śivasamānatvena śaktaḥ pañcakṛtyakaraṇe samartho'pi neśitā 
sṛṣṭisthitisaṃhāratirobhāvānugraheṣu iṣṭeśāsviti .... (l7)
 īśitāsana bhavati tatra narīśitā| jagatsṛṣṭisthitidhvaṃsatirobhāvavimuktayaḥ kṛtyaṃsakārakaphalaṃ jñeyasyaitadeva 
hitivacanāt ajñānamapi tatprasādata eva svarūpalā..... (p66b, l1)
 .........śivasyānāditvamuktatvādityabhiprāyaḥ naijāhlādaikapūrṇaḥ svābhāvikānandamukhyaparipūrṇanijaparivṛḍaḥ 
samastamuktāmabhiḥ parivṛtaḥ taiḥ saha iti yāvat (l2)
 anena paraśivasamānatayā muktātmāno bahavo vidyanta ityarthaḥ śaṅkaraprītiḥ śivasamānasantoṣaḥ āste tiṣṭhati 
anena punarāvartanādikaṃ nāstīti dyoditavarayā (l3)
 mṛgendre śaive siddho mūrdhni bhātītareṣāṃ muktasṛṣṭau puṃvarobhyo'ti nāthaḥ viśvānarthān svena viṣṭabhya dhāmnā sarveśānāṃ 
miśitaḥ sarvadā tu iti||2||

atyartha(l4)
tīvrapāke ruji ca bhavamahākleśadairghyārditaḥ sa na śambhormuktiḥ kadeti tvetanuviduraṇe nispṛho me sicinvaka māṃ 
koṣṭhādatra śaktiḥ śivapadamatulaṃ yoktamityā(l5)
ri(ni)layya(malasya) prāpyainaṃ chinnapāśaḥ svatanuvirahito yāti sadyaḥ śivatvam|| pūrvaṃ 
tāvattīvramalapākanivṛ(vṛtti)śaktipātenā(na) sadyo nirvāṇadīkṣāṃ sakalaviṣaya... (l6)
 muktve-dānīṃ vijñānakalapralayākalasakalānāṃ tīvratare malapāke tīvrataraśaktipātena sadyaḥ śivatvaṃ sadyo 
nirvāṇadīkṣayāha| atyarthamiti| ....madhye atyarthamatyantaṃ tīvrapāke tīvratare pāke sati 
cakāro'tivrataraśaktipātanārthaḥ nanu satvaprala? (p67a, l1)
 ...nilayo karmasamatve sati śaktipātaḥ saṃbhavati vijñānā kalasya karmāntā vāk sa śa....... (l2)
 kathaṃ bhavati yaduktaṃ vijñānā kalasya karma nāstīti tanmāyīyakaraṇameva karma tasya nāstītyuktaṃ nanu sarvadhā 
dharmakṣayaḥ tasmāt kasyacit karmaṇaḥ phalameva (l3)
 tasya vijñānā kalāvasthā kāraṇaṃ tasyāpi pradarvadevasya kilāvasthāyāmūrjitamiti sūkṣmaśarīritvāddurlakṣyaṃ karma 
samānatvaṃ śaktipātakāra(l4)
ṇasti(sthi)tyavagantavyam| anyathā vijñānā kalasya śakti-pātābhāvāt dīkṣābhāvaḥ dīkṣābhāvānmokṣābhāvaḥ 
dīkṣaiva mocayatyūrdhvaṃ śaivaṃ (l5)
 dhāmanayatyapītyuktatvāt śaktipātāt bhaveddīkṣetyuktvācca bhavamāhā keśadairghyārditaḥ bhavaḥ saṃsāraḥ tasya mahākleśo 
mahā(l6)
roga tasya dairghyeṇārditaḥ duḥkhitaḥ ata eva śambhormaheśvarāt kadā kasminkāle mama muktirbhaviṣyatīti vicinvan 
vicārayan ata eva svatanu(l7)
......ṇe svadehadhāraṇe nispṛhaḥ icchārahitaḥ atra trividhātmamadhye yaḥ kañcanātra bhuvaneṣu asmat kāle saṃsārāt 
kaḥ puruṣaḥ kā śaktiḥ māmevaṃ saṃsā(p67b, l1)
...........laṃ sāmyarahitaṃ śivapadaṃ śivastā(sthā)naṃ yoktuṃ yojituṃ samartha ityākalayya(ya) vicārya enaṃ 
saṃsāroddharaṇakṣamaṃ śambhuṃ prāpya .......... (l2)
 ........titasamastapāśaḥ san sva tanu virahitaḥ pāśacchedānantarameva sva dehatyāgataḥ tatkṣaṇameva yāti gacchati 
atra tīvrataraśaktipātanotpannaḥ sadyaḥ (l3)
 śivatvavijñānakalapralayākalānāṃ mukhyatayoktamityavagantavyaṃ sakalānāṃ tu sāmānyataḥ vijñānakalapralayākalānāṃ 
(l4)
 śarīraprārabdhakarmabhogyatvāt karmasāmyakālatāratamyavivakṣānusāreṇa keṣāṃcinmandāmandatarā vā śaktiḥ patatiḥ 
keṣāṃciti tīvrātivratarāvāśaktiḥ patati (l5)
 itthaṃ śaktipātasya nānāprakāratvā(va)sakalānāṃ sadyaḥ śivatvaṃ sāmānyameva| 

tathā śrīmatā varuṇaśivena - ācāryamūrtimāstāya caturthā śaktipātataḥ| (l6)
 bhagavānanugṛhṇāti sa eva sakalānapītyuktatvāt||3||

mandādyaiḥ ke'pi pakve tamasti ca samayāṃsaṃ smṛtiṃ tavaśiṣṭāṃ labdhā liṅgārcanādyai....patita..... (l7)
raudramaiśaṃ padaṃ ca laukikyā yojitā ye patisu yajanataḥ pretyabhyusatvaiva loke retā........ (p68a, l1)
ste bhuvanajagurubhiryānti sākṣācchivatvam| idānīṃ sakalānāmeva śaktipādabhedena (l2)
 samayādidīkṣāvidhānādrudrādipadāni bhavantītyāha|| mandādyairiti| tamasi male mandādyaiḥ mandamandataratīvrataraiḥ 
pakve pāke sati mandādiśaktipāto bhavati mandādiśaktipātānāṃ (l3)
 cihnānyatha tīvrataraśaktipātasya dīkṣānantarameva dehapātacihnaṃ tīvraśaktipātasya dīkṣānantaraṃ mūrchā 
mandaśaktipātasya dīkṣānantaraṃ śarīrakampanaṃ śarīramandaśaktipātasya dīkṣānantaraṃ (l4)
 romāñca uktaṃ ca| caturthaśaktipātena tulyadaivaphalaṃprati tīvratā sta(ta)thā tīvro mando mandataro'pi ca sadyo 
mūrchadhā(tā) nā kampādromāñcatastheti (l5)
 evaṃ mandādiśaktipātaṃ jñātvā dīkṣābhedaḥ kāryaḥ mandatarasya samayadīkṣā kartavya(vyaṃ), mandasya viśiṣṭadīkṣā,  
tīvrasyā(ya) sadyo nirvāṇā, tīvratarasya sadyo nirvāṇā ceti| manda(l6)
mandatarāddeśāt kartavyā guruṇā bahiḥ śakti-pātānusāreṇa śiṣyo'nugrahamarhatīti vrātī vratarāceti pāśo'pi 
kṣayabhedataḥ yadā tu nivṛttiḥ sadyaḥ (l7)
 saiva tīvratarā matā tīvratāmajīvato'nyeva puṃsaḥ pāśaviśodhiketyuktatvāt nanu dīkṣānantaraṃ dehapātādi cihnāni 
jñeyāni dīkṣāpūrvaṃ kathaṃ jñeyānītyukte guruṇā (l8)
 sva sāmarthyajñānena śiṣya bāhyakra(kri)yayā ca jñānena jñātavyāni anyathā gurordoṣotpattiḥ 
śiṣyasiddhāderhānirbhavati| uktaṃ ca|| yatra śaktirnipatitā tatra siddhirjāyate|| (p68b, l1)
 na vidyā na śivācāro na mukti......siddhayaḥ tasmāt liṅgāni saṃvīkṣya śaktipāta sa?| bhūyaśaḥ jñānena 
kra(kri)yayā vātha guruḥ śiṣyā viśodhayet yo'nyatra kurute .... (l2)
 .... naśyati durmatiḥ| tasmāt sarvaprakāreṇa guruśiṣyān parīkṣayet uttamāṃ cādhame kṛtvā nīcānuttamakarmasu 
ākṛṣṭāstāḍitā vāpi ye viṣādana yāntyapi te yogyastha...yatā..(l3)
śūdrāḥ śivasamskārakarmaṇi.... ke'pi sakalamadhye mandatarāḥ mandāśca yathākramaṃ guroḥ sakāśāt samayāṃ 
samayākhyāṃ saṃskṛtīṃ dīkṣāṃ tadviśiṣṭāḥ samayasaṃskāre kañcidviśeṣa(l4)
yuktāṃ dīkṣāṃ labdhvā prāptaye mandadarāḥ teṣāṃ samayamātrameva ye mandāsteṣāṃ samaya-samskārapūrvaṃ kṛtvā viśeṣasaṃskāraṃ 
kāryamiti sūcanārthaṃ ..... (l5)
 śiṣṭāmityatra tacchabdaḥ ataḥ liṅgārcanādyaiḥ nityamātrādhikāritvāt nityakarmāṇi vihitaśivapūjā 
snānasandhyāvandanajapanityāgnikāryādibhiḥ niyati tata? navaḥ yāvajjīva(vaṃ) (l6)
 śivārcanādikaṃ kṛtvā tyaktaśarīravantaḥ samayino viśiṣṭasamayinaśca yathākramaṃ raudraṃ rudrasaṃbandham aiśaṃ īśvarasaṃbandhaṃ 
padaṃ ca sthānaṃ prāpnuvantīti śeṣaḥ (l7)
 tathoktaṃ varuṇaśivena yatra rudrapade yogo yogo yatreśvare pade śikhācchedo na yatrāsti dīkṣā sā samayī 
dīdhe(kṣe)ti| kāmike| śikhācchedavihīnasya nitya(l8)
karmaṇi yogyateti ye tīvraśaktipātayaktāḥ sakalāḥ pūrvaṃ samayaviśeṣasaṃskārābhyāṃ saṃskṛtāḥ paścāllaukikyā 
loka.....ṇyā nirvāṇadīkṣāyā yojitāḥ prā.... (p69a, l1)
tāḥ santaḥ pati suyajanataḥ pateḥ parameśvarasya su yajanataḥ samyak pūjādikaraṇataḥ| pretyaprāṇāntya kṛtvā loke 
śuddhabhuvane bhogān bhūtānte śuddhi bhuvanasthitaṃ bhuvanajaṃ marubhiḥ ........ (l2)
dhipairvairāgyāt bhogecchārāhityāt dīkṣitāḥ santaḥ sākṣāt pratyakṣaṃ śivatvaṃ yānti gacchanti uktaṃ ca|| 
dīkṣāpūtā gaṇapatigurormaṇḍale janmuvantiḥ? siddhānmasmai ruṇadinakramamaṇḍalo.... (l3)
si dehāḥ| bhuktvā bhogān suciramarastrinikāyairupetāḥ srastotkaṇṭhāḥ paraśivapadaiśvaryabhājo bhavatīti||4||

nānāvaikalyahīnāṃ śivapadaviṣaya..... (l4)
pyadīkṣāṃ tadūrdhvaṃ caryāmiśasya bhaktyā bhuvi carati ca yo yāti tadviplutī-ścet ante kravyādavāptyā| 
śatavitatasamo bhuñjīto'nte'pi vakva?... (l5)
kṣā vaikalyayuktāḥ samayanipatitāḥ sandi-hānā nayanti| samyak dīkṣāyukto nirvighnena samayādyanuṣṭhānavān 
śivapādaṃ yānti akāmenānuṣṭhā.... (l6)
lāpavān kṛtaprāyaścittaḥ piśācatvaprāptyanantaraṃ śivapadaṃ yāti dīkṣā vaikalyayuktānāṃ śivapadaṃ kadācinna 
saṃbhavatītyāha| nānā.... (l7)
...yaḥ puruṣaḥ ......myāṃ nānā vaikalyahīnāṃ nānā vaikalyaśabdena dīkṣāṅgānāṃ nyūnatvamucyate tacca 
mandataraśaktipātasya puruṣasya nirvāṇa.... (l8)
dha....mantrakriyākalādilopaśca evaṃ nānāvidhavaikalyarahitām ata evaṃ śivapāda-viṣayāṃ śivapādaprāpikāṃ dīkṣāṃ 
prāpya gatvā? ...(p69b. l1)
di.....rakālam īśa......ma(parame)śvarasya bhaktyā pūjākaraṇāstītyabudhyā caryāṃ samayavihitanityanaimittikā-
dyanuṣṭā(ṣṭhā)nācaraṇaṃ carati karoti ....... (l2)
 yāti gacchati śivatvaviśeṣaḥ tadviplutiḥ samayādyanuṣṭhānatyāgaḥ buddhipūrvakṛtasya samayācāratyāgasyā(ya) 
kṛtaprāyaścittas-yāntyeṣṭikarmaṇo ..... (l3)
rmāstijñāpanārthaṃ ceti tiśabdaḥ kṛta ityavagantavyaḥ buddhyā kṛtasya samayādityāgastyā 
kṛtaprāyaśctittasyāntyeṣṭyā śa(śu)ddhi cet prāyaścitta(l4)
śāstrasyānarthatya(kya)prasaṅgāt samayalaṅghanena piśācakatva miti vihitavākyasya virodhācca ata eva samayalaṅghanena 
piśācatva(l5)
prāprirastītyāha| ante prāṇatyāgakāle kravyādavyāptyā śatavitatasamaḥ śatasaṅkhyayā vismṛtasaṃvatsaraḥ san 
śatasaṃvatsaraparyantaṃ pi..... (l6)
 ca padaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ uktaṃ ca| samayollaṅghanāt proktaṃ kravyādatvaṃ śataṃ samā iti bhuktitaḥ saṃbhojanāt ante 
avasāne śivatvaṃ yāti| di(dī)kṣa... (l7)
 vaikalyayuktāḥ dīkṣāyāḥ vaikalyamaṅgapratyaṅgalopaḥ tena sahitāḥ na ca sarvadhā dīkṣā hīnā ityarthaḥ vaikalyamātra-
yuktatvāt ...eva (p70a, l1)
 kaivalyadīkṣāyuktānāmapi samayācārānuṣṭhānena śivapadamastītyavagantavyaṃ samayānuṣṭhānena vaikalyaparihārāt| uktaṃ 
ca| dīkṣājñānavihitā ye (l2)
 liṅgārādhanatatparāḥ te prayānti harasthānaṃ sarvaiśvaryasukhāvahaṃ nātho yānti pinardevi saṃsāre duḥkhasāgare śivaṃ yānti 
tataścordhvaṃ śrīka(l3)
ṇṭhena nirīkṣitā iti samayanipatitāḥ samācārabhraṣṭāḥ sandihānāḥ satkarmānuṣṭhāne kriyamāṇe sati 
anuṣṭhīyamānakarmasaṃskārajātapha...(la)(l4)
mastināsti ceti kena dṛṣṭamiti yena sa dehaṃ kurvanti te sandihānāḥ karmaphalasandehenānuṣṭhānakaraṇe 
niścāyakabuddhirahitā ityarthaḥ ye dīkṣā(l5)
vaikalyasamayācārasandehairyuktāḥ te śivatatvaṃ yānti na gacchanti te saṃsāreṣveva vartanta ityarthaḥ||5||

mantraṃ ṣaṭkalamāsya randhra(l6)
mukulādyādhāramaiśaṃ kramāt turyaṃ dhāma viśantamena samakṛdya(tya)sya prasādādhvidhā siktāsau dharasaiḥ 
prayāntyabhimatāntyante yadi (l7)
 yamāha śambhostasya namaḥ śivāya sumanurjapyaṃ satāraṃ satām| pūrvoktavaddīkṣitānāṃ 
niṣkalaprāsādānuṣṭhātṛṇāmācāryāṇāṃ śambhu ca (l8)
....ntaraṃ paraśivatejorūpatvamastītyāha| mantramiti| mantraśabdenātra śivasiddhānteṣu mukhyatvāt prāsādamantro 
vivakṣitaḥ prāsādasya nādamava......... (p70b, l1)
 mantro ....yaḥ ...paraśivājjātaḥ nādāt binduḥ bindorādyasvaraḥ tasmāt sarve varṇā jātā ityanukramaḥ| 

tathā kāmike - nādo mandra(ntra) iti proktaḥ sa (l2)
 nādo jāyate parāt nādāt binduḥ samutpannaḥ bindorādyasvaro bhavet tasmāt svaradvayau dvau dvau teṣāṃ bhedāt 
caturdaśasvarāḥ (l3)
 ṣoḍaśa jīvākhyā kādayo dehavatmaḥ saṃkhyāyā te catustriṃśat ta eva vyañjanāni tu taireva racitā śāstraṃ 
parāvaribheditam| nava(l4)
khaṇḍaṃ tu nādākhyaṃ pañcāśadbījagarbhitamiti mantraśabdo manutve bodhane'tra rakṣaṇe iti dvidhātujaḥ| mananaṃ 
paraśivadhyanaṃ sa śivaḥ svādhyā(l5)
nayuktānāṃ sarvaprāṇināṃ saṃsārasāgarāt trāṇaṃ karotīti mantraśabdārthaḥ| ata eva prāsādamantro dhyātavyaḥ uktaṃ ca| 
mananaṃ sarvadevā(l6)
nāṃ trāṇaṃ saṃsryanugrahāt| mananatrāṇadharmitvān mantra ityabhidhīyata iti ṣaṭkalaṃ hṛdayādyaṅgaṃ dīrghasvarairyuktam anena 
niṣkala-prāsādaḥ sūcito (l7)
 bhavati| īśānādipañcabrahmākṣaraiḥ parameśvarasya śarīratvāt tadvācakasya prāsādasyāpi sakalatvaṃ tasmāt 
sakalaprāsāde brahmāṅgākṣaraiḥ (p71a. l1)
 miśrīkṛtyo'dhyāyaḥ niṣkala-prāsādo'ggākṣarairevo'dhyāyaḥ| 

tathā śrī kālottare – niṣkalaṃ paramaṃ mantraṃ śivaṃ sākṣāt sadāśivaṃ bhuktimuktipradaṃ (l2)
 puṃsāṃ pañcāṅgasahitaṃ vibhumiti netramantrarāhityādeva pañcāṅgatvaṃ yuktaṃ pañcāṅgasahitatvameva prāsādasyātra kathaṃ 
ṣaṭkalatvamiti cedasya pari(l3)
hāraḥ netraprāsādayorhakāroparisthitau kāraṇe samānatvāṃ netraṃ mūlaṃ mantre'ntarbhāvaṃ vidhāyu(tṛ) 
pañcāṅgasahitamityuktatvāt (l4)
 prāsādasya parihāraḥ ṣaṭkalatvaṃ yuktameva ata eva mokṣakaṅkṣiṇāṃ niṣkalapra(prā)sāda eva dhyātavyaḥ hrasvo dahati 
pāpāni dīrgho mokṣa(l5)
prado bhavedityuktatvāt| āsyarandhra-mukulādyādhāram| adrasyaśabdena lalāṭamadhyasthitabindusthānamucyate| randhraṃ 
brahmarandhraṃ mukulaśabdena hṛdayā(l6)
ditattatsthānasthitakamalamukulamucyate| adadiśabadena brahmarandhropari sthitaśaktyādisthānamuktaṃ bhavati eteṣu 
sthāneṣvādhāram adhiṣṭhānasthānabhū(l7)
....................neṣu svasāmarthyānuṇena cintanīyamityarthaḥ aiśaṃ paraśivasaṃbandhi turyaṃ dvādaśāntasthānagataṃ dhāma 
paraśiva.........(p71b, l1)
...dikāraṇatyāgakrameṇa hṛdayādisthānatyāgataḥ yasya paraśivasya prasādāddīrghā bhāvanāmikayā buddhyā sakṛt bahuvaraṃ 
viśantaṃ paraśivatejasā sahaitya(l2)
.........pūrvoktamantraṃ bhāvayediti śeṣaḥ anena bhūtaśuddhiruktā bhavati evaṃ pratidinaṃ saudharasaiḥ 
brahmarandhroparisthitaniṣkalaśakti .......... (l3)
 prasṛtāmṛtarasaiḥ siktāḥ secitāḥ antarbahirabhiṣiktā ityarthaḥ| abhimatāni vātaśuddhabhuvanasthānāni prayānti 
gacchanti ante bhogāvasāne (l4)
 yadīyaṃ paraśivasaṃbandhimahaḥ tejaḥ prāpnuvantīti śeṣaḥ tasya śambhornamaśśivāyeti sumanuḥ śobhanamantraḥ satāṃ 
dīkṣitānāṃ mahātmanāṃ (l5)
 satāramoṅkārasahitaṃ japyaṃ japtuṃ yogyaṃ paramokṣapradānatvādatra śivamantraye vadikṣite-rjaptuṃ yogya ityarthaḥ prāsādena tu 
(l6)
 japtena sarve saptānusaṃśaya ityāgamāt||6||

mūlāṇuṃ layanāntamuccarati yaḥ śaktiṃ jagatkāraṇaṃ kṣubdhvāṃ tat prasṛtāmṛtaughasaraṇaiḥ prāṇānta......sitaḥ|| (l7)
 madhye saṃplavanāt svadehamacirādāgatya nāḍyā bahiḥ sontasthān plavatīha mānasamidaṃ snānaṃ tadāpyāyakam| 
dīkṣitānāṃ mānasaṃ snānavidhimāha|| (p72a, l1)
 mūlāṇumiti| mūlamūlādhārastatrāṇumantraḥ prasādaṃ layanāntaṃ dvādaśasthānaṃ layanāntamucyate ata eva prāsādasya 
mūlādhāramārabhyoccārakrameṇa dvādaśānta(l2)
paryantamuccārarūpatvaṃ mūlāṇurityādināmāgameṣu prasiddhaṃ hṛtkaṇṭhatālubhrūmadhyabrahmarandhrāntaṃ brahmācyutarudreśvarasadāśiva-
sthānam akārādimātṛkā(l3)
kramatyāgena tyājyabhūtaṃ brahmarandhropari śaktyādikalācatuṣṭayasthānamanāhataśivādhiṣṭhitaṃ 
tadantonmanyantasaptamasthānabhūtadvādaśāntasthānaṃ (l4)
 para-śivādhiṣṭhitam evaṃ layanāntaṃ mūlāṇuṃ yo dīkṣitaḥ uccarati mūlamantroccārakrameṇātmānaṃ paraśivenaikyabhūtaṃ 
bhāvayatītyarthaḥ| (l5)
 janāt kāraṇaṃ jagadupādānakāraṇabhūtāṃ śaktiṃ kuṭilāśaktiṃ kṣubdhvāṃ kṣobhitāṃ kṛtvā saṃkṣobhaṇasāmarthyamāha| 
prāṇān niyamya sthitaḥ (l6)
 prāṇāyāmaṃ kṛtvā sthita ityarthaḥ tat prasṛtāmṛtaughasaraṇaiḥ kṣubdhaśaktiparisṛtāmṛtasamūha-pravāhairmadhye 
bahulāmṛtasalilamadhye (l7)
 svadehamacirāt āghreṇa saṃplavanāt snānāt ata eva saṃplavanāt pūrvaṃ yogāgninā dehadāhaḥ sūcito bhavati 
dahedyogāgninā deha... (l8)
 pādatalamastakamiti vacanāt nāḍyā brahmanāḍyā bahiḥ āgatya sṛṣṭkrameṇa saṃplāvitadehahṛdayādisthāneṣvātmānaṃ 
saṃsthā(p72b, l1)
pyetyarthaḥ sa evaṃ pratidinaṃ bhāvanā.....māyukta iha sthūladehe anasthin plavati saṃplavati saṃplavayati āpyāyakaṃ 
dehasyāplāvanabhūta idaṃ mānasaṃ (l2)
 snānamiti prasiddham| 

tathā kra(kri)yākrame - mūlamantraṃ nādāntamuccaran kṣubdhaśaktiparisṛtabahalāmṛtadhārābhi(bhiḥ) svāntanumartaḥ 
bahirabhiṣiktāṃ bhāvaye kramāt (l3)
 sa snānaṃ sarvatra vidheyamiti||7||

svadehaṃ bhūtabījaṃ harividhiracitaṃ svāṅkuraṃ karmamūlaṃ bhavyaṃ kālādisāraṃ viṣayadalagataṃ khādiśākhopaśākhaṃ (l4)
 bhāvāśeṣaprasūnaṃ vyavahitasuphalaṃ puṃvihaṅgopabhogyaṃ nyagrodhaṃ cordhvamūlaṃ viṣayarasamavākśākhinaṃ bhāvayecca| (l5)
 atha svadehe vaṭatarurūpabhāvanayā bhūtaśuddhimāha svadehamiti| yathā loke kṣetranikṣiptasya bījasyāṅkurādyutpatti(l6)
krameṇa sthūlākāraṃ vṛkṣarūpaṃ dṛśyate tadvaddehasyāpi bījādisvarūpaṃ darśayati bhūtabījaṃ sthūlaśarīrāpekṣayā cedamuktam| 

tathā siddhāntadīpikāyām - jātikulādyabhimānāspadamidaṃ sthūladehaṃ pṛthivyaptejo vāyvākāśādīnāṃ samavāya iti 
sūkṣmabhūtāṃśaiḥ saṃyuktamindriyabījaṃ yoni(p73a, l1)
kṣetre nikṣiptaṃ śarīrākāreṇa jāyata iti prasiddham| sūkṣmasthūlaśarīrābhyām āṇavamalaṃ bījamāyākṣetraṃ 
malamāyākalārūpaṃ .... yeddehapādapāmapi tyakta(l2)
tvāt harividhiracitaṃ viṣṇubrahmābhyāṃ brahmaṇaḥ sṛṣṭikartṛtvāt kṣetraṃ sṛṣṭaṃ viṣṇoḥ pālanakartṛtvādrakṣitamityarthaḥ 
sṛṣṭirakṣaṇābhyāṃ bījamevāṅkurākāreṇo(l3)
tpadyamānaṃ karmamūlaṃ yathā mūle bhūmyāṃ tataḥ samyak praviśyamāno vṛkṣaḥ sukramārākārau jāyate tadvat 
pūrvajanmārjitapuṇyapāpānukūlena (l4)
 brāhmaṇādideham| indriyapāṭavaṃ niyatāyuṣaṃ nīyata bhogandharatīti karmaṇo mūlatvaṃ pratipāditaṃ bhavyam uttarakāle 
bhaviṣyatkālāditatva(l5)
sāraṃ sthitāṃśādivṛkṣasyeti śeṣaḥ| ādiśabdena rāgaṃ niryūṣaṃ puruṣajīvaṃ prakṛtyādimahākāyaṃ gṛhyate viṣayadalagataṃ 
śabdādiviṣayaṃ (l6)
 ...lagataṃ śabdādiviṣayapātragataṃ khādiśākhopaśākhaṃ karmendriya jñānendriyameva yathākrameṇa śākhopaśākhaṃ 
mahāśākhālpaśākhā(l7)
bhuyuktaṃ bhāvopaśeṣaprasūnaṃ pūrvoktapañcāśat bhāva eva samastapuṣpaṃ vyavahitasuphalaṃ budhyadhyavasāyasaṅkalpaphalaṃ 
puṃvihaṅgo....bhogyaṃ (p73b, l1)
 ...pakṣiṇo bhoktuṃ yogyaṃ viṣayasaṃbhogyavasturasam ūrdhvamūlamavākśākhināmadho gataśākhāyuktam evaṃ bhūtaṃ svadehaṃ 
nyagre(gro)..... (l2)
yat| vaṭavṛkṣākāreṇa dhyāyet||8||

kumbhāpūrakarecakārdhayugalaiḥ sāmādimantrān paṭhan bhūrādisthaguṇasthitikramayutaṃ patrādiyogānvitāt| (l3)
 koṣardadāha tu bhasma tadvyapanayācchuddho na bhogābhāvanācchaktyāplāvita-mantravigrahayutaḥ pūjāṃ vidadhyāddhṛdi| 
śarīradāhāplāvanādinā (l4)
 yadetuhaṃ vidhāya mānasapūjāṃ kuryādityāha kumbheti| kumbhā pūrakarecakārdhayugalaiḥ atra ca(cha)ndobhaṅganirodhena 
kumbhakaśabdaḥ (l5)
 pūrvamuddiṣṭaḥ pūrakābhāvena kumbhakavidhānāsamarthyāditi bhāvaḥ| vidhānakrameṇa pūrakakumbhakarecakānām| ardhasya 
yugalaiḥ (l6)
 yugmaiḥ pūrakādīnāṃ pūrvārdhenā-parordhenetyarthaḥ pūrvaparārdhaṃ tu kālavaśāt jñeyaṃ pūrakādīnāṃ kālastāvattrividhaḥ 
uttamamadhyamādhama(l7)
bhedena| hastena jānuma...lamātramāvṛtya choṭikātrayeṇa yuktā ekamātrājānu-maṇḍalamāvṛtya mātrā 
syācchoṭikātrayādi tatsuktatvāt tāvatibhirdvādaśabhirma(p74a, l1)
...trābhistālākhyāḥ kālaḥ tālo dvādaśabhirjānupariṇāhaparibhramairityuktatvāt tālai dvādaśabhiḥ kaniṣṭhaprāṇāyāmaḥ 
caturviṃśatyā madhyamaḥ aṣṭacatvāriṃśa(l2)
cchottamaḥ| 

tathā sarvajñānottare - tāladvādaśakaṃ jñeyaṃ prāṇāyāmastu kanyasaḥ madhyamarcacaturvi(rviṃ)śat śreṣṭhastadviguṇo bhavediti| 
pūrakakumbhakarecakasamudāyarūpaḥ (l3)
 prāṇāyāmaḥ tābhirdvādaśa-mātrābhiḥ pūrakaḥ kumbhakasthadhāre ca kaścaivamākhyātaḥ prāṇāyāmaḥ kanīyasa ityuktatvāt 
dvādaśamātrā kāla eva (l4)
 pratyekaṃ recakapūrakakumbhakānāṃ kāla iti ekapakṣaḥ|

tathā kāmike - mātrādvādaśabhi hīnadviguṇairmadhyamo mataḥ triguṇairuttamaḥ kālaḥ pratyekaṃ (l5)
 racakādiṣviti ata eva pūrvoktatāladvādaśapramāṇakaniṣṭhaprāṇāyāmasya pūrakakumbhaka-recakānāṃ pratyekaṃ 
dālacatuṣṭayapramāṇaṃ jātam| itthaṃ kāla(l6)
krameṇa kaniṣṭhapramāṇā yāmasya pūrakapūrvārdhapramāṇaṃ dvitālaṃ jñātaṃ tāvadevāparārdhapramāṇaṃ kumbhakarecakayoḥ pūrvāparārdhayoḥ 
(l7)
 evamevaṃ pramāṇaṃ yojyaṃ madhyamaśreṣṭhayostāvat kālapramāṇena pūrakakumbhake recakānāṃ pūrvāparārdhapramāṇaṃ yathākrameṇa 
jñātavyam itthaṃ (p74b, l1)
 pūrvoktapūrakādīnāṃ pūrvāparārdhapramāṇena hlādimantrān paṭhannatra lavarayahakārāḥ yathākrameṇa pṛthivyādīnāṃ bījāni| 

tathā kāraṇe - pṛthivyādīni bījā.......... (l2)
 ....kārārdhapramāṇena hlāmādi| iti hṛdbījayuktalakāraḥ sabinduko nivṛttikalābījaṃ śiraḥ śikhā 
kavacabījairyathākrameṇa yuktavarayahakārā pratiṣṭhā......śānti(l3)
śāntyātītakalāsijāni nivṛttiḥ prathivijñeyā pratiṣṭhā āpa ucyate| vidyāṃ tejobījāni yadvāyuḥ śāntiḥ 
prakīrtitāḥ śāntyatītā bhavedvyometyuktatvāt (l4)
 bhu(bhū)rādisthaguṇasthitikramayutaṃ bhu(bhū)rādiṣu pṛthivyaptejo vāyvākāśeṣu sthā vidyamānāḥ guṇāḥ 
gandharasarūpasparśaśabdāḥ (l5)
 teṣā(ṣāṃ) stithitīḥ? pañcacatustridvyekasaṃkhyā pṛthivyādīnāṃ sthitiḥ tasyāḥ kramayutaṃ yathāsaṅkhyāsahitaṃ yathā 
bhavanti tathā paṭhan pṛthivīṃ pañcaguṇa(l6)
vināśāya pañcavāraṃ me(ma)nasā samuccaran ityarthaḥ tattadguṇasaṅkhyayā jalādīnāṃ mantroccāraṇaṃ kāryaṃ patrādyayogānvitāt 
patrādīnāṃ (l7)
 sthūlaparṇapuṣpaphalānā(nāṃ) sūkṣmavarṇādīnāṃ cāyogaḥ saṃbandhābhāvaḥ| tanānvitādyuktāt atrāyamabhiprāyaḥ pūraka-
pūrvārdhena bhūmeḥ pañcaguṇā nāśāya (l8)
 nivṛttikalābījaṃ pañcavāraṃ samuccaran| stū(sthū)lapatrapuṣpaphalādirahitaṃ taddehaḥ pādapaṃ vicintayet pūrakā parārdhena 
jalasya caturguṇavināśāya pratijñākalābījaṃ (p75a, l1)
 caturvāramuccaran sūkṣmapatrapuṣpaphalopetaṃ vicintayet śeṣācchuṣkasvarūpadhyānāt dāhakabhasmatadvyapanayāt 
...hakandadhvaṃ bhasma-bhasmibhūtaṃ tadvyapanayāt (l2)
 bhasmāṃsyapanayān daśadikṣu prakṣepāt vidyākalābījaṃ trivāramuccaran vane hastiguṇanāśāya 
dakṣiṇapādāṅguṣṭhotthitakālāgninā kumbhakapūrvārdhena śuṣka-maparārdhena (l3)
 dagdhaṃ vicintayet recakapūrvārdhena vāyo dviguṇanāśāya śāntikalābījaṃ dvivāramuccaran bhasmibhūtaṃ daśadikṣu pralīnaṃ 
kuryāt śa(śu)ddho na bhogā(l4)
vanāt śuddho nabha iti chāndasaprayogaḥ nirmalākāśadhyānāt recakaparārdhenākāśasaikaguṇanāśāya 
śāntyatītakalābījamekavāra(l5)
muccaran| śuddhasphaṭikavarṇirmalākāśaṃ dhyāyet| nanvākāśasyāmūrtatvena dhyeye'kārāsambhavāt kathaṃ śuddho'nubho 
bhāvanādītyuktam (l6)
 atocyate dyāvāpṛthivyorantarālavartinakṣatrādyākāreṇa mūrtatvādaupādhikākāreṇa gaganasya 
dhyeyasvarūpatvāttannakṣatrā(l7)
dīśā hītyetannirmalākāratvādākāśasya dhyeyaḥ svarūpatvamastīti śuddho nabho bhāvanādityuktam| 

tathā niśvāsakārikāyām - nirguṇaṃ sāñjanātītaṃ (l8)
 grahanakṣatravarjitaṃ śuddhasphaṭikasaṃkāśaṃ paraṃ vyomavicintayediti| śaktyā parāśaktyā parisṛtabahalāmṛtadhārayā 
dvādaśānte śaktimaṇḍalaṃ pari.... (p75b, l1)
..saduśaṃ bahalāmṛtamayaṃ vicintya prāsādapūrvakaṃ vauṣaḍantaśaktyā recakeṇa sṛṣṭimārgeṇa parisṛtabahalāmṛtadhārābhiḥ 
sakalanāḍīmukhaṃ praviṣṭābhiḥ (l2)
 sa bāhyābhyantaraṃ sarvaśarīramāplāvitaṃ mantravigrahayutaḥ mantrairmūlabrahmāṅgai(l3)
raṣṭatriṃśatkalābhiśca yathā sthitiṃ nyastaśarīraḥ pūjāṃ śivapūjāṃ hṛdi hṛdayakamale vidadhayāt kuryāt||9|| (l4.)

īśenāṣvehmo'stu vo mahe hindrayānnarebhyo dvirebhyaḥ śarvebhyo rudrebhyo ghoravedamaulau malamalamalanā vāmadevena 
mocamimo ve (l5)
 triṇītmāṃ vadvitayamavikalaṃ sadyamantre kalānāṃ pañcābdhiṃ cāṣṭaviśvopyahiriti ca yathāsaṃkhyamaṣṭādhitriṃśat|| 
aṣṭatriṃśatkalānyāsamantra(l6)
kramamāha| īśa iti| aṣṭatriṃśatkalānyāso dvividhaḥ saśaktīkaviśaktīkabhedākam īśānaḥ sarvavidyānāṃ śaśinyai 
namaḥ| iti kramaṃ nyāso saśaktiḥ| (l7)
 śaśanīcordhvamūrdhā syāt pūrvamūrdhāṅgadā(tā) bhavediti kra(kri)yākramo(ma)dyotikāyāmuktakrameṇa vihitaṃ nyāso 
viśaktiḥ īśe īśānamantre (l8)
 nandvenāmiti ṣaṣṭhībahuvacanaṃ tasya dve dvirūpenā(ṇā)ntaparyantaṃ pūrvamuccārya paścāt śaśinyādiśaktimuccāraṇaṃ prokyam 
ayamabhiprāyaḥ īśānasa....lāda-(p76a, l1)
śaśinyādayaḥ pañca tāsāṃ pūrvam īśānaḥ sarvavidyānām īśvaraḥ sarvabhūtānāmiti dvirāṃ tarpayantamuccāraṇaṃ kṛtvā 
tadanantaraṃ śaśibhyādikalā yojyā iti (l2)
 bhuvamevottaratrāpi draṣṭavyaṃ heti brahmādhipati brahmaṇo'dhipati brahmaparyantamuccāraparyantaṃ proktam| tadanantaram iṣṭāyai 
namaḥ iti yojyam astu śivo me (l3)
 astu paryantam| omiti sadāśivomityantam eva-mīśānakalāpañcakasya tattadakṣaraparyantoccāraṇaṃ jñeyam| he 
ityekākṣareṇa (l4)
 tatpuruṣāya vidmahe ityantaṃ hi iti mahādevāya dhīmahi paryantam dra iti tanno rudrāntaṃ yāmiti pracodayāntaṃ nare 
iti puruṣa iti tatpuruṣakalācatuṣṭayasya (l5)
 proktaṃ bhyodviriti aghorebhya ityantam atha ghorebhya ityantaṃ rutighorāntaṃ bhya iti ghoratarebhya ityantaṃ śarva iti 
sarva ityantaṃ (l6)
 bhyo ityantaṃ rudra iti namaste rudra ityantaṃ bhyo iti rūpebhyo ityantaṃ śivebhya ityantaṃ ca .......te ghore 
tvaghoramantreṇa (l7)
 evamaghorakalāṣṭakaṃ vedaścaturmakāraḥ vāmadevāya namaḥ iti jyeṣṭhāya namaḥ iti rudrāya namaḥ iti kālāya namaḥ 
iti caturmakārāntaṃ gṛhyate| la iti (p76b, l1)
 kalaparyantaṃ ma iti vikaraṇāya namaḥ iti .... la yiti va la paryantaṃ ma iti vikaraṇāya namaḥ ityantaṃ la 
iti balaparyantaṃ ma iti pramathanāya namaḥ ityantaṃ punarapi ma iti sarvabhūtadamanāya namaḥ ityantaṃ (l2)
 vāmadevamantrena(ṇa) ma iti manaparyantaṃ ma iti unmanāya namaḥ ityantam iti vāmadevakalāstrayodaśa ma iti 
sadyojātaṃ prapadyāmītyantaṃ ma iti sadyojātāya vai nama ityantaṃ ve (l3)
 iti trīṇi bhave ityantaṃ punarapi bhave ityantam| anādibhave ityantaṃ ca gṛhyate māmiti bhajasvamāmityantaṃ iti 
dvitīyaṃ bhava iti tatrāpyekaṃ śaśinyai namaḥ iti vidhīyamāno (l4)
 nyāsaḥ saśaktīka ityarthaḥ kevalaśaśinyai namaḥ ityādiko nyāso viśaktikaḥ kalānāṃ yadā(thā)saṃkhyā 
kramamāha| pañcābdhiḥ īśānasya kalā (l5)
 pañca abdhiḥ tatpuruṣasya catuṣkalāḥ aṣṭa gho(agho)rakalāḥ viśvo trayodaśa vāmakalāḥ ahiḥ aṣṭa 
sadyojātakalā aṣṭa iti yathāsaṅkhyam aṣṭādhitriṃśat (l6)
 uktaṃ ca| īśānasya kalāḥ īśānasya kalāḥ (rep)
 pañca – pūṣasya catuṣkalāḥ – aghorasya kalā cāṣṭau – vāmadeva-strayodaśa| sadyojātakalāścāṣṭau (l7)
 aṣṭatriṃśatkalā smṛtā iti||10||

mūrdhānaḥ pañcavaktrāṇyatha hṛdayagale dvyaṃ sanābhiśca kukṣiḥ pṛṣṭhoro guhyaśiśnorukayugamaparaṃ jānuyugma (l8)
 sarvavidyānāṃ śaśinyai namaḥ| (in the left margin mava...(ṣi)kaṃ māntaṃ udbhavāvāya namaḥ itya....sadyamantre 
iti sadyojātakalāṣṭakaṃ ala........evaṃ ...)
 (p77a, l1)
....spikakaṭyāṃ ca pārśvau caraṇakarayugaṃ ghrāṇamaulī ca bāhusthānāni syuḥ kalānām| śivatanukaraṇe triṃśatāṃ 
sāṣṭakānām|| aṣṭatriṃ(triṃśat)kala(lā)nāṃ sthānānyāha| mūrdhāna(l2)
....mūrdhānaḥ ūrdhvapūrvadakṣiṇo-ttarapaścimamastakādīni pañca|  īśānakalā-pañcakānāṃ sthānāni vaktrāṇi mukhāni pūrva-
dakṣiṇa-uttara-paścimāni tatpuruṣakalācatuṣṭaya......ni| (l3)
 atheti praśne puruṣasya catuṣkaletyuktatvādūrdhvavaktrābhāva iti ata yeva aghora-śivācāryeṇa avyaktakalāyai nama 
iti ūrdhvavaktra ityuktam| (l4)
 hṛdayaṃ kaṇṭhādhaḥ pradeśaḥ galaḥ| kaṇṭhaḥ dvau aṃsau| dakṣavāmabāhū śirasi nāsaḥ jaṭharasya varti nimnasthānaṃ kukṣiḥ 
nābhyadho vartijaṭharaskhānaṃ pṛṣṭhaṃ – paścimapṛṣṭhabhāgam uraḥ - (l5)
 hṛdayādhaḥ pradeśaḥ enānyevāghorakalāṣṭakasthānāni| guhyaṃ gudaṃ śiśnaṃ liṅgam ūrukayugaṃ – dakṣavāmoruyugmam – 
aparaṃ jānuyugmaṃ - jaṅghordhva(l6)
madhyasthitasandhisthānam| dakṣavāmajaṅghe jānvadhaḥ pradeśau dakṣavāmajau dvispṛk pṛṣṭa(pṛṣṭha)pārśvapradeśo dakṣavāmajaḥ 
kaṭyāṃ sūtrabandhanasthāne pārśvau dakṣavāmajakakṣādhaḥ (l7)
 kaṭiparyantapradeśau vāmadevasya stri(tra)yodaśakalānāṃ sthānāni caraṇau dakṣavāmapādau karayugmaṃ dakṣavāmapāṇiyugmaṃ 
ghrāṇaṃ nāsā mauli śiraḥ bāhu (p77b, l1)
 dakṣavāma [garaja?]daṇḍau śivatanukaraṇe śivasamānaśarīrotpādane sāṣṭhakānāṃ triṃśatāṃ kalānāṃ sthānāni suḥ(syuḥ) 
bhaveyuḥ uktā ityarthaḥ uktaṃ ca īśānaḥ pañcadhā mūrdhni vaktre (l2)
 vaktracatuṣṭaye pañcamaṃ praṇavai(ve)naiva tato gho(agho)rakalāṃ nyaset hṛdi kaṇṭhe sayo? nābhau pukṣau pṛṣṭhe ca vakṣasi 
guhye liṅge tathorvośca jānunorjaṅghayoḥ (l3)
 sphicoḥ kaṭyāṃ ca pārśvayoścātha vāmadeva-kalāṃ nyaset pādayoḥ hastayorghrāṇe mastake bhujadaṇḍayoḥ 
sadyojātakalā yojyā iti||11|| (l4.)

hṛtpate manasā vibhāvitamahāmāyoparisthāsane dhyeyo dīpaśikhākṛtīḥ sukusumaṃ bhāvaḥ kṣamārghyaṃ jalaṃ 
lepodhīravasāyini paraśivaḥ (l5)
 prāṇe .....lo mānase pātre'haṅkṛtīreṣa eva paramo dhūpo yimānātmakaḥ| dīpaḥ sadviṣayāḥ sutṛptīkaraṇāḥ gandhādayaḥ 
kṣmādikāḥ| (l6)
 pīnīyayādika-darpaṇa-vyajanaka-stotrādikā vedanaṃ naivedyaṃ svayamudyatā samanayā sūkṣmāṅga-hīno yadā yukta syāt 
svayameva nādamanunā jāpaḥ (l7)
 sanābhyarthataḥ| antaryajanaprakāraṃ yugmenāha| hṛtpadma iti śivapūjā dviprakārā bāṅyābhyantarabhedena tatra 
tāvadādhyātmajñāninām antaryāgasteṣāṃ bāhyapūjā (l8)
 ..........ṣpādyāharaṇe doṣaprasaṅgāt kriyāvatāṃ bāhyapūjā vihitā uktaṃ ca| yajanaṃ dvividhaṃ proktaṃ bāhyābhyantara 
bhedataḥ| ahiṃsādibhi-rabhyarce .... (p78a, l1)
...yāgībhirbahirbhavet dravyahiṃsā na kartavyā tatra tatra yataḥ śivaḥ kriyāvadbhiḥ sadābhyarcyo'ṅge sthaḥ 
kusumādibhiriti bāhyapūjāvatāṃ puṣpādyāharaṇe doṣaprasaṅgābhāvaḥ (l2)
 uktaṃ ca| yathā rājāṅgabhogārthamāhartuḥ kusumādikam udyānaṃ ca tataḥ tasya pratyavāyo na vidyate tadvadīśvarapūjārthaṃ 
puṣpahantā na doṣabhāk| bāhyapūjā(l3)
... ratairapi mānasabhāvenāntaryajanaṃ kartavyam antaryāgaṃ vinā bāhyapūjā na sidhyatīti taduktam antaryāgaṃ purā 
kṛtvā bāhyayāgamathācaret jyotirliṅgaṃ hṛdisthaṃ yat (l4)
 tadevābhyarcyate bahiḥ yataḥ tasmāt purā kāryam antaryāgamihocyate| akṛtvā mānasaṃ yāgaṃ yo 
bahiryāgamācaret| vṛthā pariśramastasya śāstrasyeti vini(l5)
ścaya iti hṛtpadme hṛdayakamale svābhāvika iti śeṣaḥ loke tāvat kandādutthitaṃ kamalaṃ dṛśyate| tadvadatrāpi 
kandādudgatamiti jñeyam| tathā coktam| kandasthānaṃ (l6)
 manuṣyāṇāṃ dehamadhyaṃ navāṅgulam| caturaṅgulamutsedham āyāmaṃ ca tathāvidham| aṇḍākṛtīvākāraṃ bhuṣṭikaṃ 
tatvagādibhiḥ tatkandādutthitaṃ jñeyaṃ (l7)
 hṛtsarojaṃ sakarṇikamiti taddhṛdayakamalakarṇikāyāṃ sūryasomānalaiḥ saṃyukta(ktā)yām anante sadāśivaparaśivā 
svabhāvatastiṣṭhanti| 

tathā niśvāsakārikāyām - kadalīpuṣpa(p78b, l1)
saṃkāśaṃ hṛdayaṃ sarvadehinām| tasya madhye sthitaṃ padmamaṣṭapatraṃ sakarṇikaṃ vitastimātraṃ hṛdayaṃ kamalaṃ caturaṅgulam 
aṣṭāṅgula-pramāṇāntaṃ nābherūrdhvaṃ vyavasthitaṃ padmanālaṃ navadvāraṃ (l2)
 samantāt kaṣṭakānvitam aṅguṣṭhaparvatulyāgrakarṇikābhyaḥ sakesarākarṇikāyāṃ bhavet pūrvastasya madhye tu candramaḥ 
candramadhye bhavedvahnistasya madhye maheśvaraḥ| sadāśive (l3)
 maheśaśca tasyāpi parataḥ śiva iti evaṃ svabhāvikasiddhaṃ hṛdayakamalaśivārcanaṃ sarveṣāmuttamottamaṃ manasā 
mānasajñānena bhāvitamahāmāyo-paristhāne (l4)
 bhāsitaṃ bhāvanāyā kṛtaṃ mahāmāyāyāḥ svābhāvikena vidyamānakuṇḍaliniśakteruparisthāsanamūrdhne vidyamānasasihmāsanaṃ 
yasya tasmin hṛtpadme vakṣyamāṇa(l5)
vadā-dhāraśaktyādiśaktiparyantāsanadhyānasahita ityarthaḥ hṛdayakamale svābhāvikena sthitā kuṇḍalīti 
sādhyatriśaktyām| candrāgniravisaṃyuktāpyādyā kuṇḍalini (l6)
 tu yā hṛtpradeśe tu sā jñeyāpyaṅkurākāravat sthiteti tatrāsane| dīpaśikhākṛtiḥ dīpajvālāsamānaḥ etena 
nādasvarūpaṃ dyotitam| nādo'ntaṃ saḥ (l7)
 sakalaprāṇināṃ dehamadhye sthito haṃsaḥ sadāśivaḥ haṃsahaṃseti yo brūyāti sā nāma sadāśiva iti vacanāt 
di(dī)paśikhākāre niśvāsavacanaṃ śravaṇāṅguṣṭha(l8)
saṃyogādyaḥ śabdaḥ saṃpravartate| dīpavahniśikhātulyo so'pi tannādasaṃjñakam| paśoḥ pāśāpanodāya 
prakāśīkṛtamadbhutamiti evaṃ paraśivo dhyeyaḥ|| (p79a, l1)
 āvāhanādimudrādibhirāvāhanīyaḥ bhāvo mānasaśuddhiḥ| sukusumavasupuṣpaṃ bhāvaśabdenā... hiṃsā-dyaṣṭapuṣpaṃ sūcitam| 
ahiṃsā pṛthakpuṣpam indri.... tu nigrahaṃ (l2)
 kṣāntipuṣpaṃ tu dayāpuṣpaṃ jñānapuṣpaṃ paraṃ mitam| tapaḥ puṣpaṃ satyapuṣpaṃ bhāvapuṣpamathāṣṭamamityaktatvāt kṣamāpuṣpā-
ṣṭamadhyoditakṣāntirevārghyaṃ pādyācamanayoḥ (l3)
 kṣamaiveti śeṣaḥ| jalaṃ snānīyodakam amṛtasvarūpaṃ kṣubdhaśaktiparisṛtabahalāmṛtasvarūpeṇa payasābhiṣekaṃ 
kuryādityarthaḥ|| (l4.)

avasāyinī śudhyavatsvāya svarūpā niścayātmaketyarthaḥ dhīḥ buddhiḥ lepaḥ vilepanaṃ kuṅkumakarpūracandanādi mānase 
pātre manomayā(l5)
ṅkārapātre prāṇavāyuranalaḥ agniḥ ahaṅkṛtiṃ garvaḥ eṣa eva paramo dhūpaḥ śreṣṭhadhūpavidhānaṃ taduktam agniḥ prāṇaṃ(ṇaḥ), 
manaḥ pātraṃ, garvaṃ dhūpaśikhākala iti dhīmānātmajaḥ, cit buddhiḥ, svarūpaḥ sarvārdha(rtha)prakāśakatvāditi padavīmiti 
dīpaḥ, sadviṣayāḥ uktabhogyavastusvarūpāḥ, su āstikaraṇāḥ atyantasaṃpūrte sādhakatamāḥ, gandhādayaḥ 
gandharasarūpasparśaśabdāḥ gandhaśabdenātyantaparimalayuktadravyaṃ sūcitam| rasena svādubhūtaṃ, rūpeṇa (p79b, l1)
 manoharabhūtaṃ, sparśena sūkṣmaślakṣṇabhūtaṃ, śabdeno.......nibhūtadravyaṃ sūcitam| kṣmādikāḥ pṛthviyaptojo vāyvākāśāni 
etena pañcopacārāḥ sūkṣma............ (l2)
yādīni darpaṇavyajanakastotrādikāvedanaṃ, pātuṃ yogyaṃ pānīyam| pānīyādikamapi saṃbandhopacārāḥ, 
darpaṇādikamagnisaṃbandhopacāraḥ| vyajanakaṃ vāyu(l3)
calanārthaṃ kṛtaṃ tālavṛntādivyajanādikaṃ vāyusaṃbandhopacāraḥ stotrādikamākāśasambandhopacāraḥ eteṣāmā(l4)
vedanaṃ samarpaṇaṃ naivedyaṃ pṛthivīsaṃbandhopacāraḥ pūrvoktametatsarvaṃ yogasāmarthena sūkṣmaśarīrayuktasya pratyakṣeṇa (l5)
 dṛśyate yogasāmarthyarahitasya sthūlaśarīrayuktasya sarvaṃ mānasabhavena kāryamityāha yadā sūkṣmāṅgahūnaḥ 
sūkṣmaśarīrarahitaḥ (l6)
 tadā samana.....manobhāvanayā yukta(ktaḥ) syāt sūkṣmaśarīrasahitasya svayamudyatā svayamevotpannena nādamanunā 
nādātmakamantreṇa (l7)
 svayameva nābhyutthitaḥ mūlādhāra(rā)dutthitaḥ sa jāpaḥ mantra-jalpanaṃ bhavati| sthūlaśarīrasahitasya manobhāvanayā 
nābhyutthito (l8)
 jāpaḥ kāryaḥ||12||

vahnāvevamapi prapūjya juhuyāt pūrvoktayogena tatsthānasthāmiḍayā sruveṇa ca sudhā(l9)
mante suṣumnā srucā pūrṇāntaṃ havanaṃ samāpya kṛtavānīśaṃ kṣamāpyādarādante bhāvavivarjite parapade līnaḥ samādhiṃ 
vrajet|| mānasaśivāgnikāryaṃ samādhiṃ (p80a, l1)
 cāha| vahnāviti pūrvoktayogena pūrvoktasaṃbandhena svatassiddhanābhikuṇḍe homasthānaṃ bhāvayitvā 
tatsaṃbandhakrameṇetyarthaḥ vahnau nābhikuṇḍe vidhānokta(l2)
vadāvāhitasiddhaśivāgnau evamapi prapūjyaḥ hṛdayasthāne yathā pūjitaḥ tadvat śivapūjanaṃ kṛtvetyarthaḥ juhuyāt homaṃ 
kuryāt| tatsthānasthāṃ bindusthāne (l3)
 vidyamānā sudhāmṛtijyayā sruveṇa iḍānāḍīṃ sruvākāreṇa bhāvayitvetyarthaḥ aṣṭottaraśatādisaṃkhyayā 
pūrakākṛṣṭabinduprasṛtāmṛtājyena homaṃ (l4)
 vidhāyānte saṃkhyāhomānte suṣumnā srucā suṣumnānāḍiṃ srugāgāreṇa bhāvayitvā pūrṇāntaṃ havanaṃ pūrṇāhutiparyantaṃ 
homavidhānaṃ (l5)
 samarpyā(rpya) kṛtavān ........bahumānāt īśaṃ kṣamāpyāntaḥpūjitadevāya sarvaṃ samāpyānte samarpaṇāvasāne 
bhāvavivarjite sadāśivākārādi(l6)
svarūpabhāvakārarahite niska(ṣka)lasvarūpa ityarthaḥ parapade dvādaśānte līnaḥ tatraivāntarbhūtaḥ samādhiḥ 
mālādhārādidvādaśānta-sthāneṣu ekākāreṇa (l7)
 śivadhyānaṃ samādhiḥ tādrūpasamādhiṃ vrajet gacchet uktaṃ ca atha madhyandīnaṃ yāvat samādhistho bhavedyathā 
jñānajyotirmayaṃ liṅgaṃ śaraccandravadujvalaṃ bhrūmadhye (l8)
 saṃsthitaṃ .....manaḥ prāṇaṃ ca yojayet tallīnastanmayodayogī cittaṃ śūnyaṃ nayecchanaiḥ naṣṭe citte cidānandaṃ 
svayamudyetate paraṃ samādhiḥ kathito .........maṇi(p80b, l1)
..... guṇā..........nāha tadhāritopanirrādyāgradhyadhaḥ paṅkajaṃ vidyā cordhvaśivāntapamakhacitaṃ pi(pī)ṭhaṃ śivasyāsanaṃ 
sarvādhvādhikabhāga(l2)
saṃyutaśivajñānākhya........ sarvādhvā-dhikabhāganālasahitaṃ pīṭhaṃ kriyāśaktitvam| 
itthamantaryajanādiśamādhidhyānāntamuktvedānīṃ bāhyapūjārthaṃ liṅgameva śreyaskaramiti (l3)
 liṅgādhāratayā bāhyaśivapūjāṃ vakti| seśā-nāhateti svamūrtau guruḥ mūrtau vā vidyāpīṭhenanale jale sthaṇḍile 
sthiraliṅge vā cale vā kṛtasannidhau yajanaṃ (l4)
 śreṣṭa(ṣṭha)metebhyo liṅga evoktalakṣaṇe tasmānintyaprayatnena liṅgā-rādhanamācarediti vacanāt| seśānāha 
tathārikopariseśā īśvarasahitā (l5)
 śivādhiṣṭhitetyartha......nābhatāvyāpakarūpadhārikā ādhāraśaktiḥ śivasamavetakriyāśaktirevā-dhārarūpetyarthaḥ pūrvaṃ 
liṅgapīṭhamadhye adho(l6)
bhāge kūrmaśilāyāṃ pūjanīyā tathoktam|

adha(dhaḥ)kūrmaśilāsīnāṃ kṣīrodasitavigrahām|
maulau bījāṅkurākārāṃ varadāmabhayapradām||
pāśāṅkuśa*dharāṃ śaktiṃ kriyāmādhārarūpiṇīm| iti *(l7.)

athavā seśānā īśvarasahitā āhatā kandādaṅkuravadutthitā punaraṇḍaṃ samudbhidya kandādaṅkuravat sthite (l8)
 ....ttatvāt| tasyāḥ dhārikāśakterupari ūrdhve dharādyā bhūtatvādi-kalātatvāntāḥ granthayaḥ grantheḥ māyātatvādhaḥ 
pradeśaḥ atra kramaḥ pṛthvītatvaṃ kandarūpaṃ aptatvaṃ (p81a, l1)
 secakam ucchāvasthā kāraṇaṃ vāyuḥ prerakarūpaḥ ākāśaṃ vṛddhyarthaṃ gandhādi-pañcatanmātrāṇi aṅkurākārāṇi 
daśendriyāṇi chidrarūpāṇi ahaṅkāratatvaṃ buddhikāraṇaṃ bu?ddhi(l2)
guṇaprakṛtipuṣā(puruṣā)ntacatustatvāni dharmajñānavairāgyaiśvaryarūpāṇi siṃhākārāṇi pādarūpāṇi rāga-niyati-vidyā-
kalāntāni catvāri tatvāni adharmājñānāvairāgyā(l3)
naiśvaryāṇi pādaurdhvagatāni gātrarūpāṇi kālatatvaṃ nālarūpaṃ nāladalayoḥ saṃyogasthānaṃ māyā-tatvamantrāntare 
māyātatvamadhacchadanam| śuddhavidyā(l4)
tatvamūrdhvacchadanam| śuddhavidyā ūrdhvapaṅkajaṃ māyātatvopari vikasitarūpakamalaṃ śivāntapadmakhacitaṃ 
śivatatvāvasānaka-malavyāptam evaṃ vidhaṃ (l5)
 pīṭhaṃ śivasyāsanam| atrāyaṃ kramaḥ vidyeśvarāṣṭakadalayuktaṃ pañcāśadbījagarbhitaṃ catuṣṣaṣṭikesarayuktapītakarṇikāyuktam 
atra pañcā(l6)
śadbījamiti akārādikṣakārāntapañcāśat varṇa evoktaṃ catuṣṣaṣṭikesarai(raiḥ) yuktamityatra 
kakārādimakārāntapañcapañcavargāḥ yaralavaśaṣa(l7)
sahāśca pratyekaṃ makārabindubhaayāṃ sahitābhiruccāritā eva kesarāṇāṃ catuṣṣaṣṭirityuktā pūrvādikesarāgreṣu madhye ca 
navaśaktibhiryuktaṃ patrāgra(l8)
.......karṇikāgra.....ṣu sūryasomāgnimaṇḍalani tadadhipatayaḥ brahmaviṣṇurudrāḥ karṇikā-madhye śaktimaṇḍalaṃ 
tadadhipatirīśvaraḥ tadupari kṣityādimaṇḍalāntavyāptikaśiva(p81b, l1)
......sañcintayet iti .........śaktyādiśaktiparyantamāsanaṃ parameṣṭhina ityuktatvāt 
sarvādhvādhikabhāgasaṃyutaśivajñānākhyaliṅgātmakaṃ sarvādhva.... (l2)
ṣaḍadhvānam adhikaśreṣṭhabhāgaḥ rudraviṣṇubrahmābhidhānabhāgaḥ tena saṃyuktaṃ sammitaṃ śivajñānākhya-liṅgātmakaṃ 
jñānaśaktimayaśivaliṅgaṃ śivasya jñānaśaktidehatvamadhiṣṭhāna(l3)
miti prasiddhaṃ śivasamavāyinī jñānātmakāśaktireva mūrtyupacāreṇa upacaryata iti mataṅgaparameśvaretyuktam atha 
patyuradhiṣṭhānaṃ svaśaktikiraṇātmakamiti| (l4)
 

tathā varuṇapaddhatyām - jñānaśaktyātmake liṅge kriyāśakteḥ samarpaṇaṃ pīṭhasya vidhinā yogaṃ pratiṣṭhā sā ca pañcadheti 
sarvādhvādhikabhāganāla(l5)
 sahitaṃ ṣaḍadhvanāmaśreṣṭa(ṣṭha)rudraviṣṇu-brahmabhāgayuktagomukhasahitaṃ pīṭhaṃ liṅgādhārabhūtaṃ kra(kri)yāśaktikaṃ 
kriyāśaktisvarūpaṃ pīṭhe kalpyam atra rudraviṣṇu(l6)
brahmabhāgānāṃ ṣaḍadhvayojanākramaḥ pradarśyate| liṅgaṃ trikhaṇḍaṃ parikalpya ūrdhve rudrabhāge śāntyatītakalā, viṣṇubhāge 
śāntikalā, brahmabhāge (l7)
 vidyā-māyā-nivṛttikalā iti pañcakalānyāsaḥ| śāntyatītāyā śivaśaktitatve, dve śānti-kalāyāṃ 
sadāśiveśvaraśuddhavidyā iti tritatvāni virudyāyāṃ – māyā-kalā-(l8)
kāla-vidyā-niyati-rāga-puruṣākhyaṃ saptatatvaṃ pratiṣṭhāyāṃ prakṛti-buddhi-ahaṅkāra-manas-śrotra-tvak-cakṣu-jihvā-nāsā-
vāk-pāṇi-pāda-pāyu-udara-upastha-śabda-sparśa-(p82a, l1)
rūpa-rasa-gandha-................tejo jalaṃ ceti| tri(tra)yoviṃśati tatvanivṛttau pārthivatatvamekam| iti tatvanyāsaḥ 
śāntyatītāyām anāśratādinivṛtyantaṃ pañcadaśabhuvanāni| śāntikilāyāṃ sadāśivādi(l2)
vāmāntāṣṭādaśa bhuvanāni| vidyāyām aṅguṣṭhāmātrādi vāmāntaḥ saptaviṃśati bhuvanāni| pratiṣṭhāyāṃ 
śrīkaṇṭhādyamareśāntāni ṣaṭpañcāśat bhuvanāni nivṛttau bhadrakālyādaka...... (l3)
 aṣṭottaraśatabhuvanāni evaṃ pañcakalānāṃ ca caturviṃśo-ttaraśatadvayabhuvanaṃ tayā proktamadhvani māse purāṇi 
śāntyatītāyāṃ bhavanti daśapañca ca anāśritā tathā (l4)
 nāthā anantā vyomarūpiṇi vyāpini cordhvā caiva mlocikā rocikā tathā dīpikā indhikā caiva śāntyatītā 
ca śāntikā vidyā caiva pratiṣṭhā ca nivṛttiśca (l5)
 tathā smṛtā| kalāyāṃ śāntikākhyāyāmaṣṭādaśa purāṇi tu sadāśivaḥ śikhaṇḍi ca śrīkaṇṭhaśca trimūrtikaḥ 
ekarudrastathā caiva ekanetrastathā smṛtāḥ (l6)
 śivottamastathā caiva sūkṣmaścānantasaṃjñakaḥ manonmani tathā jñeyā sarvabhūti damanyapi balapramathini caiva tathā 
vikariṇi bhavet kalavikaraṇi caiva (l7)
 kālī caiva tathā parā raudraśreṣṭhā ca vāmā ca vidyāyāṃ vakṣyate kramāt| tasyāttu bhuvanānāṃ ca saptaviṃśatikaṃ matam 
aṅguṣṭhamātram īśānau ete (l8)
 .......kapiṅgalau udbhavaśca bhavaścaiva vāmadevo mahādyutiṃ śikhaidaśca kaliraśca pañcāntakaścaiva śūrakaḥ piṅgo jyotiśca 
saṃvarto (l9)
 krodha......kaśivau tathā anantājñaṃ tathā caiva vāmadevo mahādyutiḥ śikhedaścaiva umāpatī pracaṇḍakau
