ekavīrastathaiśākṣe bhaveśograuśca bhīmakaḥ vāma... (p82b, l1)
........... pratiṣṭhāyāṃ tu vakṣyā....... śrīkaṇṭhādi......treva ṣaṭpañcāśat purāṇi tu śrīkaṇṭhau dyau tathā jñeyau kaumāraṃ 
vaiṣṇavaṃ tathā brāhmaśca bhairavau taṃ caiva tathā kṛtaṃ brāhmyaṃ praje..... (l2)
.........caiva tathaindrakaṃ gandharvaṃ ca tathā jñeyaṃ yakṣarākṣasasaṃjñakaṃ paiśācaṃ tathā jñeyaṃ sta(stha)leśvara iti smṛtaḥ 
stū(sthū)leśvaraṃ śaṅkhaṃ karṇo kālāñjana iti smṛtaḥ maṇḍapaśca ramākoṭau daśaṇḍaṃ ca gala..... (l3)
 .... vasthā....rasaṃjñayitaproktasvarṇākṣaṃ tathā paraḥ bhadrakaṇṭhaśca gokarṇo mahābala iti smṛtaḥ avimukte 
rudrakoṭirvastrapādakasaṃjñakaḥ bhīmeśvaro mahendraśca aṭṭaśca bhāsastataḥ paraḥ vimaleśastathā prokto nakha........ (l4)
 nākhalastathā kurukṣetraṃ gayā caiva tathā bhairavasaṃjñakaḥ kedāraśca mahākālo madhyameśastatho bhavet āmrātakaśca 
jalpeśo śrīśailaśca tathā paraḥ hariśca rudrastathā prokto lākūlīśastathā smṛtaḥ bhā...... (l5)
rabhūtistathā caiva ḍiṇḍimuṇḍistathaiva cā(ca) oṣadhiḥ puṣkaraścātha naimiśaṃ ca tathā bhavet prabhāvasajñakaścādhā 
amareśa iti smṛtaḥ nivṛttau tu kalādhīnaṃ tu puramaṣṭottaraṃ śataṃ bhadrakālī tathā proktā (l6)
 vīrabhadrastathaiva ca trilocanaṃ ca ......nabhaścaiva tathā paraḥ vibhāvaśca tathā proktaḥ saṃvāhiśca tathaiva ca 
tridaśeśastathā proktāḥ tryakṣaścaiva tathā smṛtaḥ gaṇādhyakṣo .......... (l7)
 ścaiva śambhurdaṣṭri ca vartikaḥ phaṇīndrodumbariśca proktāgrasano mārutāśanaḥ krodhanānantasaṃjñau ca vṛṣasārastathā 
smṛtaḥ vṛṣo baliḥ priyaḥ proktā bhūtapālastathā (l8)
 bhavet jyeṣṭhaśarvau tathā proktau sureśau vaidapāragaḥ jñānabhuk ca tathā proktaḥ sarvajña tathā paraḥ īśavidyādhipau jñeyau 
prakāmaprasādakau śrithak caiva tathā prokto ratna.... (l9)
.....thā paraḥ lakṣmidhṛk ca tathā proktā jaṭādhara iti smṛtaḥ saumyadahaśca dhānyaśca rūpāvāṃśca tathā bhavet 
nidhīśaśca tathā prokto meghavāhani ityapi kapardi ca tathā proktastathā pa....śi(p83a, l1)
kho bhavet pañcāntakastathā proktaḥ kṣayāntaka iti smṛtaḥ| tīkṣṇasūkṣmo vāyuvego laghu śīghrau prakīrtitau sunādo 
meghanādaśca jalāntaka iti smṛtaḥ dīrghabāhuriti proktaḥ (l2)
 ......tathā paraḥ śvetaśvetadhā prokto mahābala iti smṛtaḥ pāśahasta iti khyātastathā cātibalo bhavet| balo daṃṣṭra 
ca vijñeyo tathā lohitadhūmakau virūpākṣo ..... (l3)
 phaśca bhayānaka iti smṛtaḥ| krūradṛṣṭistathā hantā māraṇe  niṛtistathā dharma iti prokto dharmaścāhi tathā paraḥ 
liyoktā ca tathā proktaḥ saṃyoktāśca tathā paraḥ kartusaṃjño vidhā.... (l4)
tā ca tathā ca haramṛttikau yāmyakṣayāntakaścaiva bhasmāntaka iti smṛtaḥ babhruścaiva tathā proktārdahanajvalakau tathā 
haraśca ghātakaścaiva piṅgalaśca hutāśanaḥ agni(l5)
rudrastathaiva ca tridaśādhipasaṃjñakaḥ pinākī caiva śāstā ca avyayaśca vibhūtikaḥ pramardana iti khyāte vajradehastathā 
paraḥ buddho hyajaṃ kavālīśo rudraṃ (l6)
 vaiṣṇavamityapi brahmā ca hāṭakaścaiva kūśmāṇḍaṃ ca tathā bhavet kalāgninā saha proktaṃ puramaṣṭottaraṃ śatam evaṃ 
pañcakalānāṃ tu bhuvanānāṃ śatadvayaṃ caturviṃśo(l7)
ttaraṃ caiva sphuṭaṃ proktaṃ mayā kramān anāśritādi-bhuvanaṃ śivatatve daśa sthitaṃ bhuvanaṃ śāntyatītaṃ dīpaṃ ca proktaṃ ca 
śaktike| ekaṃ sadāśivaṃ proktaṃ bhuvanaṃ ca sadāśive (l8)
 śikhaṇḍādyaṣṭabhuvanaṃ tatve īśvarasaṃjñake manonmanyādibhuvanaṃ śuddhavidyākhyake navaḥ aṣṭāvaṅguṣṭa-mātrādi puraṃ māyākhyake 
smṛtaṃ śikhedaścaikavīraśca tatve kālākhyake bhavet pañcāntaka(l9)
......śca kalāyāṃ bhavati sphuṭaṃ piṅgaśca jyotī(p83b, l1)
rite kva tatve vidyākhyake navaḥ| aṣṭāvaṅguli..........paraṃ māyākhyake smṛtaṃ śikhedaścaikavīraṃ ca tatve kālākhyake 
bhavet pañcāntakaśca śiraśca kalāyāṃ phavati sphuṭaṃ ......... (l2)
jyātiryevaṃ tatve vidyākhyake mataṃ saṃvartaścaiva krodheśastatve niyatike bhavet ekaṃ śivādibhuvanaṃ rāge pañca 
vyavasthitam ekavīra......bhuvanaṃ ṣaṭ tatve (l3)
 puruṣe bhavet| śrīkaṇṭhādyaṣṭabhuvanaṃ prakṛtau ca vyavasthitam| brāhmyādibhuvanaṃ caiva buddhāvaṣṭau bhavet sphuṭam 
ahaṅkārasya tatve ca sthaleśvara iti smṛtaḥ anādighrāṇaparyanta(l4)
ṣaṭke sthūleśvaro bhavet| pañcake śaṃkukarṇastu tatve karmendriyasya ke kālāñjanādibhuvanaṃ chagalaṇḍāntameva ca| 
śabdādigandhaparyante pañcakeśa(l5)
 bhavet sphuṭaṃ sthāṇumārabhya bhūvanamaṣṭakam, vyomni saṃsthitaṃ bhi(bhī)meśvarādibhuvanamaṣṭakam, maruti sthitaṃ 
bhairavādyaṣṭabhuvanam,  tejastatve vyavasthitaṃ lakulīśādi(l6)
bhuvanamaṣṭakam, jale sthitaṃ bhadrakālyādibhuvanamaṣṭottaraśataṃ bhavet, pṛthvītatve tathā caiva punaranyāḥ prakīrtitāḥ, 
śāntyatītāyā makārādivisargāntāḥ (l7)
 ṣoḍaśasvarāḥ śāntau kakhagāḥ vidyāyāṃ ghakārādi-jñakārāntasaptamātṛkāṃ pratiṣṭhāyāṃ tu 
ṭakārādihakārāntatrayoviṃśativarṇaḥ kṣamityekamakṣaraṃ (l8)
 nivṛttikalāyāmiti varṇanyāsaḥ padanyāsastu dvividhaḥ ekāśītipado'dhvā caturthavatī padoddhārabhedena tathādhvanyāse 
proktaṃ kalāyāṃ śāntyatīyāṃ (tītāyāṃ) otītye.... (p84a, l1)
 tanmataṃ vyomavyāpimārabhya padānyekādaśa sphuṭaṃ nityaṃ yogīnapayantaṃ śāntikāyāṃ bhavanti hi dhyānāhārāyamārabhya 
vyāpin vyāpin padāntkam| vidyākhyāyāṃ kālāyā..... (l2)
padānāṃ viṃśatirbhavet pratiṣṭhāyāmarūpyādipadānāmekaviśatiḥ maheśvarapadāntaṃ ca sphuṭaṃ proktaṃ yathākramāt mahādeva-
padādīnāmaṣṭāviṃśati saṃkhyayā nivṛtta... (l3)
kalāyāṃ ca padānāṃ vitatirbhavet itthaṃ nyāsakramaṃ proktamekāśītipadasya ca athavānyaprakāreṇa padanyāso 
vidhīyate| caturnavatisaṃkhyānāṃ padā(da)nyā(l4)
 ca yathākramam| atrāpi śāntyatītāyāṃ ekaviṃśati saṃkhyāni vidyāyāṃ ca padāni tu vyāpin vyāpin samārabhya 
śarva śarvapadāvadhi| pratiṣṭhāyāṃ tu dvātriṃśat (l5)
 padānāṃ vitatībhāvena bhava bhaveti samārabhya oṅkārapadakāvadhi aṣṭāviṃśati saṅkhyāni nivṛttau tu padāni ca 
ekāśītipadānāṃ ca uddhāraḥ pūrvamucyate (l6)
 omityekākṣareṇaiva prathamaṃ padakā smṛtaṃ vyomavyāpina[mi]tyeva vyomarūpāya caiva hi sarvavyāpina ityevaṃ pañcārṇaṃ 
tripadaṃ mataṃ trakṣaraṃ ca śivāyeti anantā(l7)
....padaṃ tathā anāthāya tathā proktaṃ dvipadaṃ caturakṣaram anāśritāya pañcārṇaṃ dhruvāya tryakṣaraṃ matam| śāśvatāya padaṃ 
cāpi caturakṣarameva hi aṣṭākṣaraṃ yoga(l8)
[pī]ṭhasaṃśritāya padaṃ tathā nityaṃ yogī.....tyeva ....nā hāraya caiva hi| ........ padau ca ti(tri)dhā proktau? 
pañcākṣarasamanvitau? tadhau(thau) namaśśivāyeti ṣaḍakṣaramiti ............. (p84b, l1)
rvaprabhave pañcā.... śiva ..... tryakṣaram īśānamūrdhāya padaṃ ṣaḍakṣaramiti smṛtaṃ tatpuruṣavaktrāyeti padaṃ saptākṣaraṃ bhavet 
aghorahṛdayāyeti .... (l2)
 saptākṣaraṃ bhavet vāmadevaguhyāyeti padaṃ saptākṣaraṃ matam| sadyo-jātamūrtaye ca padaṃ saptākṣaraṃ bhavet oṃ namo .... 
(l3)
vaṃ padaṃ pañcākṣaraṃ bhavet guhyātiguhyāya padaṃ ṣaḍakṣaramiti smṛtam| goptre ceti padaṃ caiva dvitīyaṃ parikīrtitam 
anidhanāyeti padaṃ pañcā-kṣaramiti (l4)
 smṛtaṃ nidhanāyeti caturarṇa(rvarṇa)samanvitaṃ sarvayogādhikṛtāya padamaṣṭākṣaraṃ bhavet jyotirūpāyeti padaṃ pañcākṣaramiti 
smṛtaṃ parameśvaraparāya (l5)
 padamaṣṭākṣaraṃ bhavet acetana acetane ceti padamaṣṭākṣaraṃ bhavet vyomin vyomin padaṃ paścāt vyāpin vyāpin padaṃ 
tathā dvau padau ca pari(l6)
mleyau caturarṇa(rvarṇa)samanvitau| arūpin arūpin prathamaprathameti dvipadau ca parijñeyau ṣaḍakṣarasamanvitau| 
tejastejaḥ padaṃ paścāt jyotirjyotiḥ padaṃ tathā (l7)
 ubhau padau parijñeyau caturarṇa(rvarṇa)samanvitau| arūpeti padaṃ paścādanagniśca tataḥ param adhūmeti padaṃ paścāt 
abhasmeti tataḥ param| anāde ca tathā proktaṃ tryakṣaraṃ (l8)
 pañcakaṃ padaṃ nānā nānā padaṃ paścāt dhūdhū dhūdhū tathaiva ca| dvipadaṃ ca parijñeyaṃ caturarṇa (rvarṇa)samanvitam oṃ bhūśceti 
padaṃ proktaṃ dvyakṣareṇa samanvitaṃ bhuvanaśca padaṃ (p85a, l1)
 paścāt oṃ suvaśca tataḥ paraṃ dvipadaṃ ca parijñeyaṃ tryakṣareṇa samanvitam anidhaneti padaṃ proktaṃ caturakṣarasamanvitam| 
nidhaneti padaṃ proktaṃ tryakṣareṇa samanvitam| nidha...... (l2)
 bhaveti padaṃ paścāt pañcākṣarasamanvitam| śiveti padakaṃ paścāt śarveti padakaṃ tathā dvipadaṃ ca parijñeyā dvyakṣareṇa 
samanvitam| paramātmapadaṃ paścāt ma....... (l3)
śvarapadaṃ tathā mahādevapadaṃ proktaṃ tripadaṃ ca caturṇakam(caturvarṇakam) sadbhāveśvara iterapadaṃ pañcākṣaraṃ bhavet| 
mahātejapadaṃ proktaṃ caturarṇa(rvarṇa)samanvitam| (l4)
 yogādhipade ca padaṃ pañcākṣarasamanvitam| muñca muñca padaṃ paścāt caturarṇasamanvitam| prathama prathama padaṃ 
ṣaḍakṣaramiti smṛtam| śarva śarva padaṃ .... (l5)
 proktaṃ caturarṇasamanvitam| bhavatbhavasa padaṃ paścāt bhavodbhavapadaṃ tathā| dvipadaṃ ca parijñeya(yaṃ) caturarṇasamanvitam| 
sarvabhūtasukhapradaṃ pada(l6)
maṣṭākṣaraṃ bhavet| sarvasāṃnidhyakareti padaṃ saptākṣaraṃ matam| brahmaviṣṇurudraparapadamaṣṭākṣaraṃ bhavet| anu(na)cci(rci)tā 
anarcitetyasaṃskṛtasaṃsnateti ca| pūrva.... (l7)
pūrvastita smṛtamaṣṭākṣarakatrayam| sākṣi sākṣi padapaścāt turu turu padaṃ tathā dvipadaṃ ca parijñeyaṃ 
caturarṇasamanvitam| pataṅga pataṅga padaṃ ṣaḍakṣara[sa](l8)
manvitam| piṅga piṅga pakṣaṃ pakṣaṃ paścāt jñāna jñāna padaṃ tathā śabda śabda padaṃ paścāt| sūkṣma sūkṣma padaṃ tathā 
catuṣpadaṃ ca vijñeyaṃ caturarṇasamanvitam| ...... (l9)
vetipada......paścāt sarveti padakaṃ tathā dvipadaṃ ca parijñeyaṃ dva(dvya)kṣareṇa samanvitam| sarvadeti padaṃ paścāt tryakṣaraṃ 
parikīrtitam| oṃ namo ..... (p85b, l1)
 padaṃ pañcākṣara........mā namaśśivāyeti padamaṣṭākṣaraṃ matam| omityekākṣaraṃ caiva padamanye prakīrtitam| ekāśīti-
padasyaivamuddhāraḥ prokta..... (l2)
bahiśca..........muddhāraḥ kathyate'dhunā ekāśītipadoddhāre caturṇavatikaṃ bhavet oṃkārapadamārabhya dhyānāhārapadāvadhi 
trayodaśapadāni sure.... (l3)
ti pade yathā dhyānāhārapadādūrdhvamodityekapadaṃ ma...dvyakṣareṇa namaśceti tasmādūrdhvaṃ prakalpayet| tryakṣareṇa śivāyeti 
tasmādūrdhvaṃ prakalpayet| tasmādūrdhvaṃ (l4)
 prakartavyaṃ namaśceti padaṃ tathā guhyādiguhyamārabhya dhūrdhveti padakāvidhipadanāṃ viṃśatī proktamekāśītipade yathā 
dhūrdhvatipakādūrdhvamomityekaṃ (l5)
 bhṛ ityekākṣareṇaiva tasmādūrdhvaṃ padaṃ na bhavet omityekaṃ padaṃ proktaṃ bhuvaśceti tataḥ padaṃ omityekaṃ padaṃ proktaṃ 
suvaccheti tataḥ pada...(l6)
 anidhanapadāṃ ceva sarvadāṃ padakāvadhi dvātriṃśatpadakaṃ proktamekaviṃśatipade yathā sarvadācca padādūrdhvamomityekapadaṃ 
smṛtaṃ dvyakṣareṇa (l7)
 na meyeti śivāya tryakṣaraṃ ca tataḥ omityekapadaṃ proktaṃ namaśceti padaṃ tataḥ namaśceti padaṃ proktaṃ śivāyeti padaṃ 
tathā omityekākṣareṇaiva (l8)
 manye ca padakaṃ taṃ smṛtaṃ caturnavatisaṃkhyasya uddhāraḥ prokta eva ca| āgameṣu(ṣū)bhayaṃ proktaṃ tasyācca dvipadaṃ kṛtaṃ 
dvī saṃkhyānāṃ padānāṃ ca nyāsaḥ ..... (p86a, l1)
kto yathākramaṃ dvisaṅkhyapadamadhye ca nyāsamekena kārayet| śāntyātītāyāḥ śiveśānāstramantrāḥ| śāntikalāyāṃ 
tatpuruṣakavacau mantrau vidyāyāṃ śighāghorau pratiṣṭhā(l2)
tuṃ śiro vāmau nivṛttikalāyāṃ sadyojātahṛdayamantrau iti ṣaḍadhvanyāsakramaḥ||14|| 

svādhāre gatamānasaḥ śivamanoḥ śaktyātmako dānato mātrā dīptikaleśvarakramagate (l3)
 mantraṃ samuccārayan nītvā kāntakamañjaliṃ paraśivaṃ prāptaikabhāvaṃ tato ghaṇṭānādalalāṭagaṃ kusumagaṃ lakṣye mahonta 
nyaset| svādhāra iti svādhāre mūlādhāre gatamānasaḥ (l4)
 prāptamanāḥ prathamaṃ mūlādhāragatabhāvanāyuktamanaska ityarthaḥ śivamanoḥ śaktyātmakaḥ śivamanormūlamantrasya 
śaktyātmakaḥ sāmartha(rthya)yuktasvarūpaḥ prāsādamantrajapā(l5)
nuṣṭhānenaivamāha nadī karmakaraṇe dakṣa ityarthaḥ mātrā dīptikaleśvarakragateḥ mātrāḥ akāra-ukāra-makāra-bindu-nādāḥ 
eteṣāṃ sthānāni yathākramaṃ hṛtkaṇṭhatālu(l6)
bhrūmadhye brahmarandhrāṇi ābhyaḥ mātrābhyaḥ dīptayaḥ prakāśamānāḥ tajjātā ityarthaḥ kalāḥ vijayādayaḥ vijayā – 
saumyā aṅgusajā dhṛtīrmardanā bandhini mocani māyā mohīnī (l7)
 bodhinī| iti hṛtsthā akārajāḥ daśakalāḥ gulmā manoramā dīptā sudīptā homakārikā sādhūmā piṅgalā 
pi(pī)tā vāruṇi śīghragā calā śaśāṅkā suprabhā ke ca (l8)
 jñānā mokṣadā pārā etāṣṣoḍaśakalāḥ kaṇṭhasthāśca ukārajāḥ śravaṇi śrāvaṇi jalpakarī ca ghrāṇavartinī suśabdā 
vāhinī cordhvamanani pārvati tathā ..... (p86b, l1)
dinu prabhāvā ca niyokṣati ratopavāhinī pitṛmārgānugādeva mārgā gāmargadarśinī| āpūryā(rya) prāṇabadhnā ca 
vittasthā suviddhā dhūmrā dhūpā iti catirvi.... (l2)
 tālusthā makārajāḥ nivṛtti(ttiḥ) pratiṣṭhā vidyā śāntiretā lalāṭasthā bindusaṃbhavāḥ catasraḥ kalāḥ| indhikā 
dīpikā rocikā mocikā ūrdhvagā vyāpinī vyoma...... (l3)
 anantā nāthā anāśritā iti daśa etā nādajāḥ brahmarandhrasthāḥ etāsāṃ kalānāmīśvarāḥ yathākrameṇa 
brahmaviṣṇurudreśvarasadāśivāḥ eteṣāmīśvarāṇa.......... (l4)
teḥ kramatyāga-mārgasya dānataḥ svīkārā mantraṃ samuccārayan ācārya iti śeṣaḥ| añjaliṃ puṣpāñjaliṃ, kāntakaṃ 
kasya, śirasyaḥ śirasaḥ, brahmarandhrasthānasyetyarthaḥ tadanta...... (l5)
kamuṃ manyanta saptamasthānarūpadvādaśāntaṃ nītvā atrāvāhanakramasampradāyaḥ sūcitaḥ prathamapuṣpāñjaliṃ mūlādhāre dhṛtvā 
krameṇordhvamānayan (l6)
 hṛtpradeśe kiñcitkālaṃ sthitvā mūlamantrasya akāratyāgena brahmaṇastyāgaṃ kṛtvā kaṇṭhasthāne sthitvā 
ukāratyāgena viṣṇostyāgaṃ kṛtvā tadūrdhvaṃ tālusthāne sthitvā (l7)
 makāratyāgena rudratyāgaṃ kṛtvā tadūrdhvaṃ bhrūmadhye sthitvā bindutyāgeneśvaratyāgam| tadūrdhvaṃ brahmarandhre sthitvā 
nādatyāgena sadāśivatyāgaṃ kṛtvā, tadūrdhvaṃ dvādaśānte (l8)
 sthitvā paramaśivamummantyacintasthaparaśivaṃ so'haṃ bhāvanayā prāptyaikabhādhaṃ dvitīyasvarūpaṃ sañcintya 
tataścintānantaraṃ puṣpāñjalimadhogamayanabhrūmadhye kiñcitkālaṃ (p87a, l1)
 sthitvā gha(ga)ṇārādalalāṭakaṃ ghaṇṭāravat svāyamānaṃ bhrūmadhye samāniyānena nāda(l2)
svarūpeṇa kiñcit sakalatvaṃ coktamityānusandheyaṃ tataḥ kusumagaṃ hṛtpradeśasthapuṣpāñjaliga.....mahaḥ itthaṃ rūpaṃ tejaḥ 
lakṣyaliṅgapratimādau antaḥ liṅgādi hṛdayakamalamadhye nyaset (l3)
 āvāhayet| 

tathā śrī parākhye – evaṃ śāktaṃ vapuḥ kalpya tasmin paramakāraṇaṃ paramaṃ śāśvataṃ nityaṃ jñānānandasukhātmakaṃ 
vyāpakaṃ sarvatatvānāmapameyamanaupamaṃ vācya(l4)
vācakarāhityaṃ vāṅmanātītagocaraṃ vyaktiṃ kuryādviśeṣeṇa niṣkalaṃ sakalātmani| parāvāhanametaddhi 
śivamantraniyantraṇāditi uktaṃ ca bindunādasamāyuktaṃ sānta(l5)
mantrakriyānvitaṃ niṣkalaṃ paramaṃ bījaṃ taduccāryo-nmanāntakaṃ mantramūrtiṃ śivaṃ sṛṣṭyā bhrūmadhye manasā smaret| 
candrabimbasamaprakhyā smṛtvā puñpāñjalau (l6)
 sthitam āvāhinyā punarnītvā sthāpinyā sthāpayeddhṛdīti||15||

āvāhana-sthāpana-sannidhāna-nirodhana-avakuṇḍanasauthamudrāḥ| pādyaṃ tathā cācama(l7)
nārghyapuṣpadainairmatā saṃsmṛtayo daśaiva| āvāhanādidaśasaṃskārānāha| āvāhaneti| atrāvāhana-miti| āvāhyate 
anena śivamantreṇāhūyata ... (l8)
 ityāvāhanam| nanu anena śivasya vyāpakābhāvaḥ sarvatra sūcitaḥ vyāpakasya vastunaḥ kathamāvāhanaṃ pūrvameva 
sarvatra sti(sthi)tatvāditi bhāvaḥ loke dāvadavidyamānasyā.... (p87b, l1)
te tadvadatrāpi avidyamānasya śivasyāhvānaṃ kriyata iti śivasya vyāpakābhāvaḥ prāptaḥ śivo vyāpakaḥ ata 
evāhvāna-menva(?)nasaṃghaṭata iti cet sarvāgameṣvāhvānakarmaṇo vidyamānatvāt .... (l2)
 ata evāhvānakarmaṇā vyāpakābhāva eva prāpta iti pūrvapakṣaḥ ayamevābhiprāyaḥ yathā kāṣṭhe sarvatra sthitasyāgnerekatra 
mathanādi prakāśaḥ kriyate tadvadatrāpi sarvatra sti(sthi)tasya vastunā .... (l3)
kāśa eva kriyata iti vyāpakatvabādhā nirāsaḥ| uktaṃ ca| āmūlāgrasthitasyāgne-rabhivyaktiryathā tarau tadvadekatra 
cidvyaktivyāpakasya na sādhyata iti| atrāvāhanamiti mano (l4)
 vyāpāraḥ sa ca niratiśayaśraddhāviśeṣaḥ tena saha bhi(abhi)dhānaṃ mūlamantravādena 
saṃbodhanamabhimukhīkaraṇamāvāhanam| uktaṃ ca sarvatra vyāpinaṃ śāntaṃ devadevaṃ (l5)
 sadāśivam| abhimukhī kurute yacca tadā vāhanamucyata iti taccāvāhanaṃ dvividhaṃ pratiṣṭhākāle liṅgādau 
jīvanyāsapūrveṇa citprakāśarūpamāvāhanamityekaṃ (l6)
 pratyahaṃ pūjākāle abhimukhīkaraṇarūpaṃ yadāvāhanaṃ dvitīyaṃ tadabhimukhīkaraṇaṃ pañcavaktramadhye 
ūrdhvasti(sthi)tasyaiśānavaktrasyaiva kriyate uktaṃ ca yasyāṃ (l7)
 diśi bhavedvāraṃ tāṃ prācīṃ parikalpayet| tatra tatpuruṣaṃ vaktraṃ cordhvaṃ cābhimukhaṃ bhavet| ūrdhvavaktre tu dātavyam| 
sammukhaṃ ca parāṃ ca mukhamiti atra sthāpyata iti sthāpanaṃ (l8)
 madanugrahārthaṃ bhagavān kṛpānidhiḥ liṅgādāvāsta iti granthayā manobhāvasthāpanam| bhaktyā niveśanaṃ tatra sthāpanaṃ 
kathitaṃ budhairityuktatvāt sannidhīṃ yata iti sannidhānaṃ (p88a, l1)
 pūjyapūjakayoranugrāhyānugrāhakalakṣaṇasaṃbaddhaḥ prasādaparaparābhimukhibhāva iti yāvat nirudhyata iti nirodhanaṃ 
bhagavatparaśiva(l2)
tanmayi anugrahaparo bhaveti prārthanā-viśeṣo nirodhanamityarthaḥ satāṃ guṇo'yaṃ prārthanarūpavidhāna iti pakvamalānāmapi 
satvavyaktinirodhanamavakuṇṭhanaṃ śiva (l3)
 śivasya bhakteṣu pratyakṣeṇa prakāśo'vakuṇṭhanaṃ teṣāṃ nirodhanakaraṇatvāt śivasya hṛdayādisthāneṣu hṛdayādimantrāṇāṃ 
vinyāsaḥ| kavacaveṣṭanamityeke saudhamudrā dhenu(l4)
mudrā anenāmṛtīkaraṇaṃ dyotitaṃ bhavati uktaṃ ca| hṛdayaṃ hṛdaye śubhraṃ pītaṃ śirasi tacchiraḥ śikhāyāṃ tu śikhāṃ 
raktāṃ kri(kṛ)ṣṇaṃ dehe tacucchadam| astraṃ vahnisamaprakhyamastrasaṅkhyeṣu (l5)
 vinyaset| mūlenāropya devasya puṣpaṃ śirasi mantritaṃ śivaikavaktratā dhyāyedaṅgānāmaṅginā saha amṛtīkaraṇaṃ 
kuryānmantrāṇāṃ dhenumudrayeti mudreti| āvāhanādimudrāḥ sūcitāḥ mudrā(l6)
śabde dvidhātujaḥ mṛduhṛrṣe drave gītāyāṃ gatau devānāṃ modanā surāṇāṃ dravāṇāṃ mundretyarthaḥ uktaṃ ca mudaṃ karoti 
devānāṃ drāvayatyasurāṃstathā modanāndrivaṇāccaiva (l7)
 ...dreti parikīrtitā hastāvañjaliṃ kṛtvānāmikā mūlaparvaṇyaṅguṣṭhasaṃyojanena vāhini iyamevādhomukhisthāpāni 
saṃlagnamutthitamaṅguṣṭhau karau sannidhānitāveva garbhāṅguṣṭājiḥ (p88b, l1)
 ubhau muṣṭiṃ badhvā tarjanīṃ prasārya maṇḍalākāreṇa calanāt avakuṇṭhanamudrā anyonyagrathitāṅguliṣu 
kaniṣṭhānāmikayormadhyamā tarjanyā ..... gāstanākārā saudhamudrāyā .... (l2)
 pādaśaucārthaṃ jalaṃ sahṛdā namontamūlena pādayordeyam ācamanaṃ svadhāntena pañcamukheṣu deyam arghyaṃ svāhāntena 
pañcamūrdhāsu puṣpādānaṃ dūrvā puṣpākṣatādi(l3)
dānaṃ vauṣaḍantena dadyāt| uktaṃ ca| padbhyāṃ nivedayet pādyaṃ dakṣahastena sammukhaṃ mukheṣvācamanaṃ tadvadarghyaṃ pañcasu 
samūrdhasu dūrvāpuṣpākṣatādikam| paścāllokopacā(l4)
rataḥ namaskāra svadhā svāhā vauṣaṭ jātiyutā hṛdā catuṣṭayamidaṃ tatra paripādya nivedayediti evaṃ saṃskṛtayaḥ 
saṃskārāṃ daśaiva daśasaṅkhyāvācinaḥ| uktaṃ ca| āvāhana (l5)
 sthāpanaṃ ca sāni(nni)dhyaṃ sannirodhanaṃ sakalīkaraṇai kṛtva(tvā) avakuṇṭhanapūrvakaṃ pādyamācamanaṃ cārghyaṃ puṣpaiḥ 
saṃskṛtya śaṅkaramiti elākāreṇa pradhānabhūtāḥ ete daśa (l6)
saṃskārā-stadaṅgabhūtāḥ anyā iti jñāpanārthaṃ kṛtamityavagantavyam||16||

gandhaṃ mūlaphalādikandakusumādyannādikaṃ pārthivaṃ cāpyaṃ vāri payaḥ paṭādi dadhikaṃ cāgneyamiṣṭaṃ ma... (l7)
di pādyābharaṇādimārutamatho dhūpaṃ tathā cāmaraṃ ghaṇṭāgītaravādinā samami pañcopacārāḥ śive|| śivasya 
kriyamāṇāḥ sarvopacārāḥ pañcopacārasamudāyena (l8)
 ....hābhūtasaṃjñā saṃsiddhina ityāha| gandhamiti| gandhaṃ śrīcandanaṃ mūlaṃ hrīberādikaṃ phala cūtaphalādikaṃ kabhaṃ 
sūraṇādi musumādi puṣpādi patrādi annādikaṃ caivetyādi sūpāpūpādikaṃ ... (p89a, l1)
 bhiḥ kriyamāṇamupacārā pārthivaṃ pṛthivīsaṃbandhaṃ vāri udakaṃ payaḥ kṣīraṃ paṭādivastra-kauśeyādidadhikam āpyaṃ vāri 
saṃbandhamiṣṭaṃ mahat prabhāyuktamityarthaḥ maṇiḥ navavidharatnaṃ dīpāditailaṃ .... (l2)
 dīpārādikaṃ dīpamābharaṇādi svarṇarajatādinirmitam āgneyam agnisaṃbaddhaṃ dhūpaṃ sajarasādi cāmaraṃ 
cāmaravyajanādimārutaṃ vāyusaṃbandhaṃ ghaṇṭāgītaravākī ghaṇṭādhvani gānamurajāḥ so.... (l3)
dīkaṃ rābhasam ākāśasaṃbandham anena prakāreṇa śive śivapūjāviṣaye pañcopacāraḥ tathā coktam| pārthivaṃ 
gandhapuṣpādyaṃ kandamūlaphalādikam| arghaṃ vāripayostraṃ dadhi...ga.... (l4)
trakādikam| āgneyaṃ hemaratnādidīpamābharaṇādikaṃ vāyavyaṃ cāmaradhṛpaṃ vyajanakandamūlaphalādikaṃ arghaṃ vāripayovastraṃ 
dadhi gomūtrakādikaṃ (l5)
 āgneyaṃ hemaratnādidīpamābharaṇādikaṃ vāyavyaṃ cāmaradhūpaṃ vyajanaṃ vātakarakaṇaṃ nābhasaṃgeya-vādyādyaṃ 
ghaṇṭāvedasvanādikaṃ ete pa....... [pañcopa](l6)
cārāstu kathitā śāstravedibhiriti||17||

svādhāroditanādataḥ śivapathe medhādikārkāḥ kalāmātrastatpadasaṃsthitān tadadhipān matvā layaṃ tatparam| 
karṣannaṅguli .... (l7)
kṣa nikaraṃ caikaikaśo mānasājjaptvopāṃśa-samarpayecchivamanuṃ deve japaṃ svākṛtim| atha japadhimāha svādhāreti 
svādhāroditanādataḥ mūlādhāro..... (l8)
ddhitanādāt tadvācakamahakā(ṅkā)rabhūtādityarthaḥ śivapathe niṣkalaśivaṃ prati-gamanamārge medhādikārkāḥ medhā oṃkāraḥ 
tadādikāḥ tatprathamāḥ arkāḥ dvādaśasaṅkhyāḥ kalā iti ....... (p89b, [in the folio number 31a] l1)
 oṃkāra-ekāra-ukāra-ikāra-akāra-ūkāra-aikārāḥ aukāra-visargāntākārāḥ tā eva mantrā ityucyate 
..........dasaṃsthitān au e ...... (l2)
deṣu saṃsthitān tadadhapān aukārādipañca varṇānāmadhipān| athavā hṛtkaṇṭhatālubhrūmadhyabrahmarandhrāntasthāneṣu 
yathākrameṇa sthitaḥ akāra-ukāra..... [bi](l3)
ndunādapadeṣu sthitāt eteṣāmadhipān brahmaviṣṇu-rudrasadāśivān layaṃ matvā layaṃ gatāniti vicintya tatparaṃ 
tasmāt brahmarandhrasthānāt (l4)
 pa....rdhvavidyamānadvādaśāntasthaparaśivaṃ matvā tadgatamanobhūtatvetarthaḥ akṣanikaraṃ japamālāyāṃ

tadagre puṣpamāropya śaṅkha-sannibhamudrā.... (l7)

dīkṣā bhautikanaiṣṭhikīyamabhidhātvādyādvidhā laukikī dehodhvaṣṭaguṇarthideṣṭabhavane sat bhogadāsāyake anyā 
sā(syā)cchivadharmiṇi yamathā (l3)
 cādyā sabījāhvayā śaktā nāma parātvaśaktaviṣaye nirbījakākhyā tathā| pāke tīvratare rujaḥ sati nare sadyastu 
nirvāṇadā nirbījā pariśodhita-svasamayācāreśa(l4)
bhakte sthitā vijñānapralayākalāmasunirādhārā śivenaiva sā sādhārā sakalātmanā na ca tanorjāteśca śaivātmanaḥ|| 
dīkṣābhedamayugmenāha| dīkṣeti dīkṣā(l5)

anyatrāpi dīyate jñānasadbhāva.... (l8)

 smṛtiniṣṭhā kaivalyaṃ tat saṃpadā pitri naiṣṭhikīdīkṣā niravadhikatapasvino naiṣṭhikāsteṣāṃ saṃbandhini 
niṣṭhīgṛhapadavarjitā katipayadinavratapālanāntara ... (l4)

nirapekṣā (l6)
 uktaṃ ca śārvaṃ nayati yathāmasamayādyanuvartakā viśiṣṭātmaguṇaprāyaḥ sāpekṣā cocyate budhaiḥ samayādyakṣamaṃ

tathā siddhāntaśekhare – bhautikī naiṣṭhikī ceti dvidhā syācchivadharmaṇi tatrādyā laukikīti nāmadheyā lokadharmo 
vaidikādiḥ tatsahitā lokadharmiṇi lokadharmaṇyaḥ phalamāha ....... (p96a, l1)
 saguṇardhitā dehordhve śarīrapātānantaram aṣṭaguṇardhidā aṇimādyaṣṭaguṇasamṛddhikarā| aṇimāmahimā caiva gi(ga)rimā 
laghimā tathā| īśitvaṃ ca vaśitvaṃ ca prāptiḥ prākāmyamiti (l2)
 guṇāṣṭakam| iṣṭabhuvane svecchitabhuvane sadbhogadā samyagbhogakarā| sādhake sādhakaviṣaye lokadharmiṇyādikṣitaḥ 
sādhaka eveti sādhakalakṣaṇamapyuktaṃ bhavati uktaṃ ca (l3)
 bhogabhūmiṣu sarvāsu duṣkṛtāṃśeha te sati dehokṣarāṇimādyarthaṃ śiṣṭeṣṭā loka-dharmiṇīti| anyā śivadharmiṇīti 
abhidhā nāmadheyuktā yasyāṃ śivadharmiṇyāṃ kṛtāyāṃ (l4)
 tyaktalaukikavaidikācāraḥ śivabhuk mānuṣā na tat para evātmā bhavati| sā dīkṣā śivadharmiṇītyucyate|

virbhāvo bhavatītyarthaḥ śivadharmiṇo mūlaṃ śivadharmaphalāśriya ityuktatvāt tatratāvat śivadharmiṇi liṅgināṃ 
śikhācchedasamāyuktā lokadharmiṇi gṛhasthānāṃ (l6)
 śikhācchaidarantatā uktaṃ ca dvedhā nirvāṇadīkṣā ca laukikī śivadharmiṇi grahiṇā laukikī jñeyā liṅgina 
śivadharmiṇi śikhācchedo na yatrāsti dīkṣā sā lokadharmiṇi (l7)
 śikhācchedasamāyuktā dīkṣā sā śivadharmiṇīti tathānyatrāpi dīkṣāyā lokadharmiṇyā gṛhasthā na dīkṣayet guruḥ 
tathā vai śivadharmiṇyā dīkṣayecca tapādhanā(na) śikhācchedasamāyuktā dīkṣā.... (l8)

kṣetre rudrapadi kṛte grahapadairmadhye sitaṃ paṅkajaṃ kuryāt koṇacatuṣṭayeṣṭiṃ padakaiḥ (l8)
 svarṇaprabhān svastikān dīkṣādityapadeṣvadholayalayai liṅgāni pīṭhāni taccheṣaṃ bhinnarajo vilopyamatulaṃ bhadraṃ supūrvaṃ 
param| subhadrākhyamaṇḍalalakṣaṇamāha| (p101a, l1)

 iti nāmadheyaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ||54||

syāt svastikaṃ ceṣṭitapadeṣu caiśāt koṇāt (l4)
 samārabhya padatrayeṣu madhyasthakoṣṭhānyacatuṣpadena koṣṭhānviśālo'pyānyapadāni caivam| 
svastikamaṇḍalalakṣaṇamāha| syāditi| iṣṭipadeṣu ṣoḍaśapadeṣu (l5)
 iṣṭiriti śrutivācakaḥ tathā śrutau smṛtivacanam| ṣoḍaśehanyabhiṣṭeṣṭimadhyā pañcadaśe'hani caturdaśe jayanyeṣṭiḥ pañca 
sapta bhavediti| pūrvapakṣe kṛte yaṣṭimārabhya (l6)
 uttarapakṣe ṣoḍaśe'hani kṛte'-ṣṭermukhyatvāt| ṣoḍaśasaṅkhyāvācakaḥ prāgudakpañcasūtraiḥ kṛte ṣoḍaśapade svastikaṃ 
kārayedityapi aiśāt (l7)
 koṇāt samārabhya padatrayeṣu pūrvamadhyasthitacatuṣpadaparyantam agnikoṇamukhapadatrayeṣu madhyastha-koṣṭhāntacatuṣpadena 
pūrvoktatripadasamīpe niṛtivāyutatpadasyānapadena (l8)
 sahetyarthaḥ vedakoṣṭhān catuṣpadān vilopya mārjayitvā anyapadādi anyādi uktavat kṛtvā svastikaṃ bhavet| 
agnikoṇādārabhya dakṣiṇadiśi niṛtimukhapadaka(p102a, l1)
...ya tatsamānamadhyapadena saha catuṣpadaṃ niṛtikoṇādārabhya vāyumukhapadatraye tatpadasamānasthamadhyapadena saha 
catuṣpadānvilopayet| vāyumukhakoṇādārabhya pūrvapada(l2)
trayatatsamānamadhyapadena saha catuṣpadānvilopayet| evaṃ kṛte sati svastikaṃ syāt||56||

kṣetre vidavibhājite'dhikamitaḥ prācīndubhāgā(gān) nyaset madhye bhāgamadhītyajenna(l3)
dhipadairardhendumantarmukhaṃ kṛtvordhve dvipadaṃ galaṃ tu paritaḥ paṅaktistu saumye padaṃ vṛttaṃ trīṇi yame nyasecca kamalaṃ 
caṇḍasya ṭaṅkaṃ matam| caṇḍeśvarapūjārtha....ndumaṇḍala(l4)
lakṣaṇamāha| kṣetreti kṣetre grahītakṣetre vedavibhājite prāgudaggatapañcasūtreṇa catuṣpadīkṛte idaṃ caturthā(rdhā) vibhājitāt 
kṣetrādadhikamādhikyena prāgindu(l5)
bhāgaṃ dvipadapramāṇasthe diśyekabhāgaṃ nyaset kārayet caturthā(rdhā) vibhājitakṣetraṃ punarapi pūrvadiśi 
ekapadapramāṇapaṅktyādhikyena yuktaṃ kārayedityarthaḥ|| (l6)
 adhā(thā)dhikyapadakaraṇānantaraṃ madhye bhāgaṃ dvipadapramāṇaṃ tyajan adhipadaiḥ adhikapadaiḥ saha 
antarmukhamardhendumardhacandrākṛtimaṇḍalaṃ kuryāt| ūrdhve (l7)
 ardhacandrādūrdhve dvipadaṃ galaṃ kaṇṭhaṃ bhavati paritaḥ bāhyesthitapadāt| paṅktiḥ kāryā saumye uttarapadaṃ madhyasthaikapadaṃ 
vṛttaṃ vṛttākāraṃ kārayet| trīṇi trīṇi...jāni saumyapade (l8)
 kārayedityarthaḥ yame dakṣiṇapade kamalaṃ nyaset kārayet| caṇḍasya caṇḍeśvarasya ṭaṅkaṃ ṭaṅkākhyamaṇḍalaṃ mataṃ 
caṇḍapūjāyāṃ sammatam||57|| (p102b, l1.)

pratyak dvārakamaṇḍale kuśayute saṃsthāpya śiṣyaṃ padādūrdhvaṃ prokṣya sutāḍya vīkṣya bhasitaiḥ kebhyukṣya nābheradhaḥ 
ullikhyāstrakuśaiḥ paraṃ ca hṛdaye saṃspṛśya saṃcintyataḥ mantranyāsa...... (l2)
bandhanamadho grāhya ktapuṣpau karau| itthaṃ maṇḍalabhede prakāramuktvā idānīṃ samayaviśeṣadīkṣāvidhānaṃ pratyak 
dvāraketyādipañcaślokenāha| pratyak dvārakamaṇḍale tūktatvāt prathamaṃ (l3)
 maṇṭapapūjākaraṇaṃ sūcitaṃ bhavati tadyathā maṇṭapaṃ salakṣaṇaṃ vidhāya kṛtamityāhnikadvayaḥ sāyāhne sāyantanīṃ sandhyāṃ 
viśeṣasandhyāṃ ca samāpya viśeṣasandhyāvidhānaṃ tu mūlamantrādīnāṃ (l4)
 vidyeśvarāṇāṃ tatvādhipānāṃ ca tarpaṇaṃ kṛtvā| 

tathā mṛgendre - tataśca punarācamya pāṇyagreṇa gurūttamaḥ| brahmādipañcakaṃ sarvān vidyā vidyeśvarānapi tatvānyadhipatīn 
(l5)
 sarvān dhyāyan kālāntamaṇḍale| caturthyantādhi(bhi)dhānena svāhāntena tu tarpayet| tato yajñopavītena munīn 
kaṇṭhāvalambinā sanakādīn sabhṛgvādīn sagotra(l6)
pravarādhipān| kaniṣṭhā mūlamārgeṇa bhūtādīn parvasandhibhiḥ tathāpasavyena pitrūn sapatnikān pitāmahān prapūrvāṃśca 
gurūneva codakān bodhakān parān|| (l7)
 bhrātri(tṝ)n sakhīnupādhyāyān sa mātāmahamātulān svadhāntenābhidhānena namontena tu tarpayediti| 
śivasūryābhyarcanaṃ dvārāṇāṃ dvārapālānāṃ ca pūja........... (l8)
 vighno-dvāsanānantaramātmaśuddhikaraṇamaṣṭatriṃśatkalānyāsapūrvakamātmani śivāvāhanaṃ maṇḍalādhipatiṃ cādhiṣṭhānatvena 
śivaitya(kya)karaṇaṃ pañcagavyasādhanaṃ tajjalena (p103a, l1)
 yāgamaṇḍapaprokṣaṇaṃ vikirakṣepaṇaṃ yāgeśvarārādhanaṃ vardhanyāṃ pāśupatāstrārcanaṃ lokapālānāṃ śivājñakaraṇaṃ vardhanī 
śivakumbhabhramaṇaṃ jñānakhaḍgasamarpaṇaṃ (l2)
 vighneśagurupūjākaraṇaṃ maṇḍale sarvādhvavyāpinaṃ śivasyāvāhanaṃ pañcāvaraṇapūjākaraṇa-magnikāryakaraṇaṃ 
śivavaktrāgnivaktrayoḥ sandhānaṃ mūlena śivatarpaṇaṃ brahmāṅganāṃ daśāhutikaraṇam|| (l3)
 deśāpātana-kālāpātana-durnimitta-sunimitta-śāntikaraṇaṃ śiṣyapraveśāhutikaraṇaṃ sthālīpākavidhānaṃ bhāgatrayakaraṇaṃ 
śivaprārthanānugrahakaraṇaṃ evaṃ samastakaraṇānantaraṃ guruḥ (l4)
 maṇḍapādbahirnisrityodagāsyaḥ śiṣyānāhūya pratyagdvāramaṇḍale maṇṭapasya paścimadvāraṃ pratyagdvāraṃ tasmāt bahiḥ 
sammukhaṃ gomayenā-stramantreṇa caturaśramaṇḍale kuśayute (l5)
 śiṣyamukhānukūlena uttarāgra-pūrvāgradarbha-puṣpākṣatādipraṇavāsanayute śiṣyaṃ saṃsthāpya śuklavastrādinā yaktamiti śeṣaḥ| 
uktaṃ ca praṇavāsanage tatra śiṣyaṃ snātamupoṣitaṃ (l6)
 sitavastradvayopetaṃ śivabhaktaṃ parīkṣitaṃ bubhukṣaṃ prāṅmukhaṃ sthāpya mumukṣuṃ cottarānanam ūrdhvakāyaṃ svadakṣasthaṃ svayaṃ 
pūrvānanasthita iti| udaṅmukho vā śiṣyaṃ (l7)
 ....... sthāpya sthitvā svaya-mudaṅmukha iti padāt pādādārabhya ūrdhvāyāḥ huṃphaḍantaṃ pokṣya tasya snānārtham 
astreṇa prokṣaṇaṃ kuryāt| atra pādādārabhya (l8)
 prokṣa.......ma| mūrdhādārabhya pādaparyantaṃ prokṣaṇaṃ kāryam evamavalokanā-dvāvapi mantavyam| 

tathā siddhāntaśekhare - mastakāt pādaparyantaṃ ..... (p103b, l1)
 ...streṇa huṃphaḍantena prokṣiṇyārghyodakena ca saṃprokṣya snānaśuddhyarthaṃ patākāmudrayā suchivrati| sutāḍya bhasmanāstreṇa 
hṛdi tāḍayitvā vīkṣya divyacakṣuṣā prāsādenāvalokya .....taiḥ (l2)
 ...[bha]smabhiḥ ke śirasi bhasmasnānārthamastreṇābhyukṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā nirīkṣaṇa-prokṣaṇa-tāḍanā-bhyukṣaṇākhyacatuḥ saṃskāraḥ 
yathākrameṇa vihitaḥ bhavati atra tu viparyeṇa vihitatvānmumukṣuviṣayamiti mantavyam| (l3)
 

tathā mṛgendre - mumukṣuviṣayavacanaṃ bhāge maṇḍalakaṃ kṛtvā nyasya praṇavamāsanaṃ prokṣya tāḍyāmbhasollikhya bahiṣeti 
athavā gurusaṃprāyakramāgata iti jñātavyaḥ kriyākrame (l4)
 tu avalokanakrameṇoktatvāt jñānaratnāvalyāṃ prokṣaṇapūrvakena vihitatvāt nābheradhaḥ nābhimārabhya 
pādaparyantamityarthaḥ astrakuśairastramastreṇa japtvā darbhamūlai(l5)
rullikhyāghamarṣaṇāya trivāramullekhanaṃ kuryāt| paraṃ ca nābherārabhya mastakānta-pradeśamastramantreṇa kuśāgrairmārjanāya tridhā 
samullikhet itthaṃ mumukṣuviṣayaṃ (l6)
 bubhukṣostanādverūrdhvaṃ paścānnābhyadhastādullekhanaṃ kāryam| 

tathā siddhāntaśekhare - kuśāgrairastramantreṇa mārjanāya labhacca tān| ūrdhvaṃ nābhe sthidhā(tā) mantrī kuśa(l7)
mulesca(śca) nābhyadhaḥ pāpāpanuttaye paścāt pāpaviśleṣaṇāya iti astreṇa darbhāgreṇa nābherūrdhvaṃ mārjanāya darbhamūlena 
nābhyadhastāghamarṣaṇāya tridhā tridhā samullikhet (l8)
 bhautikasya naiṣṭhikasya pūrvaṃ nābhyadhastāt paścānnābherūrdhvaṃ samullikhyeti kriyākramavacanam| hṛdaye 
saṃspṛśyāstramantreṇa hṛtpradeśe śiṣyasya sarvaguṇotpādanāya saṃsparśanaṃ kṛtvā (p104a, l1)
 saṃcintya tatraiva mūrtimantreṇātmānaṃ dhyātvā taṃ śiṣyaṃ mantranyāsapaṭākṣibandhanaṃ mantranyāsaḥ śiṣyadehe 
mastakādārabhya pādādvā saṃhitāmantranyāsaḥ sakalīkaraṇamiti yāvat paṭākṣibandha(l2)
paṭenāstraprokṣitakavacāya kuṇṭhitamūlamantrajaptakārpāsādisitavastradvayenākṣibandhanaṃ netramantreṇa dvinetrabandhayutaṃ 
kāryamiti śeṣaḥ| 

tathā kāmike - sakalīkṛtya taṃ śiṣyaṃ netrabandhaṃ vidhāya ca| astreṇa (l3)
 prokṣitenaiva kuṇṭhitena ca varmaṇā saṃskṛtenaiva mūlena sitavastreṇa deśika iti ayaṃ netrabandhastu 
pāpināmanavabodhakāya kartavyaṃ netramantreṇa badhniyādanyāvalokāya pāpināmityuktatvāt| ārtapuṣpau (l4)
 añjalinā svīkṛtapuṣpau karau śiṣyahastau grāhya guruḥ sva kareṇa gṛhītvā maṇṭapaṃ praveśayet||58||

gatvā dakṣiṇato'grasaṃsthitiradhodṛgbandhahāniṃ śive puṣpakṣepamathāṇu...ma ca (l5)
 paraḥ saṃveśanaṃ taddhṛti| ātmasthāpanamantrabhūtaviṣayā śuddhiḥ manūnāṃ sthitistat ketvīśakarasthitiḥ śivaghaṭā 
varcāpayoktaṃ punaḥ| dakṣiṇataḥ maṇṭapaṃ pradakṣiṇamārgeṇa gatvā praviśyāgra(l6)
saṃsthitiḥ devasyāgre devadakṣiṇe svātmano dakṣiṇe ca śiṣyasya sthitiḥ kāryā atra brāhmaṇādivarṇakrameṇa 
svadakṣiṇavāmabhāge saṃsthitiḥ kartavyā|

brahmaśambhupaddhatyām – tasya dakṣiṇataḥ tiṣṭhet (l7)
 brāhmaṇakṣatriyo'pi ca vāmabhāge tu vaiśyastu śūdraścaiva yathā guroriti atho devāgre sthityanantaraṃ 
dṛgbandhahāninetrapaṭasya vimocanaṃ kuryāt| śivena puṣpakṣepaṃ kusumāvakaraṇaṃ (l8)
 śiṣyena(ṇa) kārayitvā puṣpakṣepānantaraṃ netrapaṭavimocanaṃ kuryādityekapakṣaḥ praveśya puṣpakṣepaṃ tu kārayitvā 
darśayediti kāmikepyuktatvāt utratrāpi ānīya dṛgāvaloktuṃ (p104b, l1)
 ...kaṃ vasana...mādityuktatvāt| kriyākrame tu netrapaṭamapanīya tasme puṣpaṃ kṣepayitvā ityuktatvāt pūrvaṃ 
netrapaṭavimocanaṃ kṛtvā tadanantaraṃ puṣpakṣepaṃ kāryamityaparapakṣa....... (l2)
ṇubhiḥ īśānādimantraiḥ nāmadheyaṃ dvijādivarṇānukrameṇa śivadevagaṇāntakaṃ kartavyaṃ puṣpe maṇḍalasthaśivasya 
īśānavaktre patite sati brāhmaṇasya īśānaśivaḥ iti nāma kartavyaṃ kṣatriyasya īśade..... (l3)
 iti vaiśyasya īśagaṇa iti śūdrasya īśagaṇa iti ca tatpuruṣe puṣpaṃ patite sati viprādivarṇakrameṇa 
tatpuruṣaśivaḥ, tatpuruṣadevaḥ, tatpuruṣagaṇa iti nāma kartavyam aghorā(l4)
diṣvevaṃ nāma kartavyaṃ hṛdayādiṣu patite sati hṛdayaśivaḥ, hṛdayadevaḥ, hṛdayagaṇa iti iti nāma kartavyaṃ strīṇāṃ 
śaktyantanāmadheyaṃ kāryamiti īśaśivaśaktiḥ īśadevaśaktiḥ īśagaṇa(l5)
śaktiḥ iti athavā īśa, śivīśa, devīśa, gaṇīśa iti ca| 

tathā kāmike - tatpātasūcitasthānapūrvaṃ śivapādottaraṃ nāmāvadhāryam| viprasya śivadevagaṇāntakaṃ kṣatriyaviṭśūdrādīnāṃ 
strīṇāṃ (l6)
 śaktyantasañjūkamiti| 

tathā siddhāntaśekhare – puṣpapātavaśātteṣāṃ viprādīnāmanukramāt| ācāryo nāma kurvīta yathāvadadhunocyate| mastake 
patite puṣpe (l7)
 viprasī śaśive.....vet| īśadevo nṛpaḥ prokto vaiśyaḥ śūdragaṇo bhavet| ete tatpuruṣāghorā 
vāmā(ma)jāsyeṣvanukramāt sthāneṣu ca hṛdādīnāṃ puṣpe nipatite satī| viprarājanya(l8)
vaiśyānāṃ śivadevagaṇāntakam ācāryo nāma kurvīta śiṣyāṇāṃ svecchayāthaveti viprakṣatriyayoḥ śiva-devagaṇāntakaṃ 
vaiṣyaśūdrayoḥ gaṇāntaṃ nāma sāmānyenoktaḥ tathā (l9)
 mūle........vāntakaṃ dvijendrāṇām itareṣāṃ gaṇāntakamiti| puraḥ saṃveśanaṃ śivātmanorabhimukhaṃ devasya dakṣiṇe 
praṇavāsanayuktamaṇḍale śiṣyaniveśanaṃ kāryaṃ (p105a, l1)
 ta....di śiṣyasya hṛdaye'streṇa puṣpeṇa santāḍya iti śeṣaḥ ātmasthāpanamantraṃ bhūtaviṣayā śudadhiḥ 
ātmasthāpanasaṃhāramudrayā caitanyamātmamantreṇa tasyai hṛdayakamala-karṇikāyāṃ tila(l2)
dhā(dā)nasaṃrakṣaṇamantraviṣayā śuddhiṃ nivṛtyādimantraiḥ bhūtaśodhanamāplāvanaṃ manūnāṃ mantrāṇāṃ sthitiḥ| sakalīkaraṇaṃ 
tat ke śiṣyaśirasi īśakarasthitiḥ śivahastasanniveśanaṃ guruḥ sva (l3)
 dakṣiṇahastaṃ tejorūpaṃ dhyātvā śivahastaṃ vidhāya mūlamantraṃ layāntamuccaran pāśasamūhanivāraṇaṃ dhyātvā 
śiṣyaśirasi niveśya śivamantramuccaran sarvāṅgasparśanaṃ kuryāt taṃ śiṣyaṃ (l4)
 punaḥ paścādapi śivaghaṭāvanalabdhakumbhasthaśivāvarcāpayet| śiṣyadakṣiṇakarṇe mantramuccaran guruḥ śiṣyeṇa sragādinā 
devārcanaṃ praṇāmaṃ kārayedityarthaḥ| mūlamantraṃ (l5)
 grahan karṇe puṣpaṃ deve pradāpayedityuktatvāt||59||

kuṇḍāgneyakamaṇḍale sthitiradhodarbheṇa nāḍisthitirhomo murdhnaṇubhiḥ saṃpātahavanaṃ mūlāhutīnāṃ (l6)
 śatam uktiḥ śaiva-manośca niṣkṛtiradho vāgīśapūjāgniko śiṣyaprokṣaṇatāḍanāntaragatiścidbhedagordhvā hṛdi| 
kuṇḍāgneyakamaṇḍale kuṇḍasyā-gnidigbandhapraṇa(l7)
vāsanayuktamaṇḍale sthitiḥ śiṣyaniveśanam anena guroḥ pūrvābhimukhatvaṃ śiṣyasya uttarābhimukhatvaṃ dyotitam atho 
niveśanānantaraṃ nāḍisthitiḥ mūlamantra-mantritaṃ darbhamūlaṃ śiṣyakarata... (l8)
 matvā darbhāgraṃ svajaṅghāsandhau sanniveśya yadvā darbhāgraṃ śiṣyakaratale tanmūlaṃ svajaṅghāyāṃ niyojayet| uktaṃ ca 
kuśāgramātmasaṅghāyāṃ tanmūlaṃ tatkare hṛda.... drāvipa ........ (p105b, l1)
.........rūpāṃ kuśavyājena bisatannunibhāṃ śiṣyanāḍīṃ piṅgalāyakṛṣya svanāḍyāmiḍayā praviśyaikabhāvaṃ matvā 
śivamantreṇa recakena vinisṛtya śiṣyasya pūrakavāyunā praviśya (l2)
 recakeṇa vinisṛtya svahṛdayaṃ pūrakeṇa praviśya evaṃ kumbhamamḍalavahnisthaśivaiḥ saha nāḍīsandhānanāḍīsthitiḥ 
homanāḍīsandhānārthaṃ mūlenāhutitrayadānaṃ mūrdhni śiṣyamastake aṇubhiḥ sahitā (l3)
 mantraiḥ saṃpātahavanaṃ saṃpātahomaṃ kuryāt| mūlāhutīnāṃ śataṃ śivahastasthirārthaṃ mūlamantreṇa vahitāhutīnāṃ 
śatasaṅkhyākāṃ kāryam| brahmāṅgānāṃ daśāṃśato homaṃ ca kartavyaṃ (l4)
 pūrṇāhutiśca vidheyaḥ uktaṃ ca sthirārthaṃ śivahastasya mūlenāṣṭottaraṃ śataṃ brahmāṅgānāṃ daśāṃśena ghṛtena saha pūrṇatā 
iti śaivamanoḥ śivādibrahmapañcakaṣaḍa....sya (l5)
 uktiḥ uccāraṇamupadeśa iti yāvat niṣkṛtiḥ mūlenāṣṭottaraśatena prāyaścittāhutiḥ kartavyaṃ śivārcanaṃ kārayitvā 
anuṣṭheyāṃśca brūyāditi samayadīkṣāvidhāna..... (l6)
viśeṣadīkṣā prārambhārthaṃ samayoktavadyāgādyaṃ śiṣyapraveśaṃ śivahastapradānaṃ ca vidheyam agnike kuṇḍāgnau ādhāraśaktiṃ 
vinyasya vāgīśvarāsanaṃ ca vidhāya tatrāsane vāgīśvarī (l7)
 vāgīśvaraṃ cāva(vā)hayet iti| agnagetyuktaṃ vāgīśīpūjā vāgīśvarī vāgīśvarapūjayoḥ abhyarcanaṃ santarpaṇaṃ kuryāt| 
śiṣyaprokṣaṇatāḍanāntaragati| śiṣyasya prokṣaṇamastramantreṇa (l8)
 tatraiva....kṣasahuṃphaḍantāstreṇa puṣpeṇa tāḍanamantaragatiḥ tāḍanānantaraṃ kriyamāṇakarmamārgāt bheda(l9)
kordhvacaitanyabhedena vidhīyamānamārgaḥ anenāyamarthaḥ saṃpannaḥ huṃphaḍantāstrātma-mantreṇa bhedanaṃ jyeṣṭhayā viyojanaṃ 
punarātmamantreṇāṅkuśamudrayotakarṣaṇaṃ (p106a, l1)
 śiṣyasya dvādaśāntamānayanaṃ tatra triṇāgrandhiṃ durūpaṃ jīvabhāvanamātmamantreṇa saṃhāramudrayā saṃgraṇaṃ ca hṛdi sva 
hṛdaye||60||

svātmasthāpanarecanāya hira.......... (l2)
........svātmakapāpavīkṣaṇahutaṃ bījāśayā hārataḥ| yonestadbhavadehato'pi havanaṃ rudrāṃśahomastato 
jātīśāṇugayajñasūtradharaṇaṃ mūlāhutīnāṃ śatam| svātma-sthāpanarecana..... (l3)
 svātmanī(ni) pūrakeṇa śiṣyacaitanyapraveśanaṃ kumbhakena svātmanā saha samarasīkaraṇam ātmasthāpanaṃ recanā 
mūlamantraṃ layāntamuccarannā-tmamantreṇodbhavamudrayā brahmādikāraṇe-śvara..... (l4)
meṇa dvādaśāntasaṃsthānānantaraṃ sṛṣṭyā bhrūmadhyamānīya dakṣiṇanāḍyā nissaraṇaṃ recanā itaḥ bahiḥ recanānantaraṃ bāhye 
vāgīśī sthiti| tayoḥ saṃyogaṃ vibhāvyātmamantreṇa vāgīśva..... (l5)
nau vāgīśvareṇa kṣipyamāṇaṃ vibhāvanayā garbhadhānādisaṃskāratarpaṇaṃ ca garbhasthātmakapāpavīkṣaṇahutaṃ vāgīśvarīgarbhe 
vidyamānasyātmanaṃ pāpavīkṣaṇena garbhā........ (l6)
disaṃskārārthaṃ homa kriyamāṇe sati tadā vahnau vidyamāniṣṭhāgandhādinā brahmahatyādinā kṛta prāktanapāpadarśanena hutaṃ 
homaṃ kāryam uktaṃ ca garbhādhānādisaṃpattau śa... (l7)
tapañcaśivāṇunā home tadagnijai cihnairūhayecchiṣyakalmaṣam| viṣṭhāgandhe sa bhū hartā brahmahā gurutalpagaḥ surāpo 
guruhantā ca goghnaśca kṛtanāśanaḥ kṛṣṇe (l8)
 magnoru ca gandhe ca garbhabhartavināśanaḥ bhramati strīvadhe vahniḥ kampate hemahartari| vaye sphuṭati bālasya nistejo 
garbhavātakaḥ| evamādiṣu cihneṣu sahasraṃ bahurūpiṇā| pāpa....ṇa.... (l9)
 .... maye dvi navātmanā| atra khaṇḍarūpī aghoramantraḥ navātmanā navākṣarayuktaśivamantreṇa pāpabhakṣaṇamantro yathā 
śikhāṇḍārdhendu...raṃ ca viśvarūpaṃ śaśi..... (p106b, l1)
 ......vāyuḥ ṣaṣṭhasvarānvitam| indubinduśikhopetaṃ phaṭ phaṭ dvayavibhūṣitam| praṇavādinamontaṃ ca 
pāpabhakṣaṇasaṃyutam|| iti ......yāṅgārataḥ pūjavṛtpannadoṣaśā..... (l2)
 ......dehadoṣaśāntyarthamāharataḥ āśā(cāra)doṣanivāraṇārthaṃ yoneḥ upasthadoṣanivāraṇārthaṃ tat bhavadehataḥ 
pūrvoktabījādibhyaḥ utpannadehadoṣaśāntyarthaṃ ........ (l3)
kāryaṃ tilena trivāraṃ rudrāṃśo homabhoga ātman rudrāṃśo bhavatviti mūlenāhutitrayaṃ kāryam| 

tathoktaṃ kāmike - āhārabhāvadoṣābhyāṃ yonibījaśarīrataḥ| śuddho dvijo bhava.......tmā(l4)
 bhagavan parameśvara| rudrāṃśo pātahomamāhutitrayamācaret| ayaṃ rudro bhavatvātmā bagavan tvatprasādataḥ| iti 
rudrāṃśāpātanamiti śaktigarbhe śiṣyātmano (l5)
 yoja....nakāryārthaṃ yogyatvāpātanaṃ tataḥ rudrāṃśāpātanānantaraṃ jātiśāṇugayajñasūtradharaṇaṃ jātīśāḥ 
dvijātijādipradhānāḥ aṇavaḥ tatpuruṣādimantrāḥ tān .......... (l6)
....jātiśāṇugānāṃ yajñasūtrāṇāṃ yajñopavītānāṃ svajātivahitasūtrasaṃkhyāyā ca dhāraṇaṃ kāryam| 

tathā kāmike - tatpuruṣāghoravāmājānmantrānviprādi ..... (l7)
 sva sva jātyuktamantreṇa dhāryaṃ tadupavītakaṃ kārpāsanirmitaṃ sūtraṃ triguṇaṃ triguṇīkṛtam| sūtraṃ tadekaṃ śūdrāṇāṃ 
vaiśyānāṃ dvisaraṃ bhavet| trisaraṃ kṣatriyāṇāṃ tu ......... (l8)
...ptasaṅkhyakaṃ ....dvā saptapañcasaraṃ teṣāṃ nava sūtramathāpi veti| mūlāhutīnāṃ śataṃ yajñopavītadhāraṇaṃ dṛḍhīkaraṇārthaṃ 
mūlādimantrāṇāṃ śatena homaṃ kāryam| mūlamantra(l9)
maṣṭottaraśatasaṅkhyayā taddaśāṃśato brahmāṅgānāṃ ca tarpaṇaṃ kartavyam||61||

brahmāṅgā iti pañcasaṃskṛti hute pūrvātmayogaḥ śivo vāgīśyāṃ divi sṛṣṭipūrti......... (p107a, l1)
sya vikṣepaṇaṃ vahnyādau| samayā śrutiśca samayādyācāraśuddau kṛtaṃ bālādāvitaraḥ śivārcana-śivajñānādiyogyo 
bhavet| brahmāṅgāhutipañcasaṃskṛti hute vāgīśvarī ......... (l2)
...rbādhā na puṃsavanasīmantonnayanajātakarma nāmakarmaṇākhyapañcasaṃskārahutau brahmāṅgām iti pañcabrahmabhiḥ sadyojātādi-
mantrairgarbhādhānādīnāṃ yathākrameṇa sadyojātādiśivasmaraṇapūrvaka(l3)
mabhyarcanam aṅgāhuti pañcāṅgaiḥ| hṛdaya-śiras-śikhā-kavaca-astra-mantrairgarbhādhānādīnāṃ yathākrameṇa pratyekamāhutīnāṃ 
pañcakaṃ bhavati| sīmantonnayanakarmaṇi vaktrāṅgakalpanārthaṃ śikhāmantreṇa (l4)
 praṇavapañcabrahmanyāsapūrvamaṣṭatriṃśatkalānyāsaṃ ca kuryāt| viśeṣasamskārasiddhyarthaṃ pūrṇāṃ ca dadyāt| atra 
garbhasthātmani māyāgarbhāduddhtya śaktigarbhe śivabījatvāpātanena niyojanaṃ ga.... (l5)
dhānaṃ ātmano nirmalaguṇavṛttiḥ puṃsavanaṃ māyāgarbhasthabhuvanādūrdhvaṃ yadunnayanamadgamanaṃ tat sīmantonnayanaṃ śivasya putra 
traiva jananaṃ jātakarma ātmani śivatatvo-tpātane .....va (l6)
 evāyamiti karaṇaṃ nāmakaraṇam| uktaṃ ca māyāgarbhāt samuddhṛtya śaktigarbhaniyojanam ātmani śivabījatvaṃ 
garbhādhānamitīritaṃ puṃstvā(va)mātmaguṇā vyaktiḥ yadā vyaktistu nirmalam| māyā(l7)
sīmantamadhvānaṃ yat yat pratyonnayanaṃ hi tat sīmanto-nnayanaṃ jñeyaṃ janmarudrasya putratā| ata eva śivatve ca 
bodhanaṃ nāmakīrtanamiti| śiśśoḥ śiṣyasya pūrvātmayogaḥ pūrvavannirmalā-tma .......ca (l8)
 .... kartavyaḥ atra vāgīśvarī garbhasthātmanaṃ vahnijvālāgrasannibhaṃ saṃhāramudrayoddhṛtya pūrva-vahnijahṛdayakamale niveśya 
kumbhakena samarasibhāvaṃ kṛtvā mūlamantraṃ layāntamuccaran svātmaśivādviśle........ (l9)
hāramudrayā gṛhītvā brahmādikāraṇatyāgakrameṇa dvādaśāntaṃ nītvodbhavamudrayātmamantreṇa śiṣyabhogāya tanadehe recakayoge 
mana yojayediti pūrvātmayogaḥ vāgī........ (p107b, l1)
 sṛṣṭiḥ vāgīśvarīśvarayoḥ santarpaṇapūrvakaṃ tayorvisarjanaṃ kartavyaṃ pūrtihavanā prāyaścittārthaṃ mūlena sahasramardhaṃ śruvā hutvā 
pūrṇā homa kuryāt| vahnyādau vahnisthaśive taṇḍa(ṇḍu)lestha (l2)
 kuṇḍasthe ca śive puṣpasya vikṣepaṇaṃ kusumairabhyarcanaṃ śiṣyena kārayet| tadā gurupūjāmapi yathāśakti dravyādinā 
kṛtvā guruṃ praṇamet| samayaśrutisiddhāntavihitasamayācāro yathā (l3)
 ....na śivaṃ śivaśāstraṃ ca śivāgniṃ ca tathā guruṃ nindyānna laṅghayetteṣāṃ chāyāmapi kathañcana| nirmālyabhojanaṃ dānaṃ 
laṅghanaṃ ca visarjayet śivāgnigurupūjā ca kartavyā jīvitā vidhigdhā (l4)
 lādau samayānuṣṭhānakaraṇāsamarthabālavṛddha-vyādhisthādau samayādyācāraśuddhiviṣaye hutaṃ śatādisaṃkhyayā bhavanaṃ kāryaṃ 
tadyathā| aśaktānāmamūn sarvān pūrvoktasamā(ma)yānapi (l5)
 śodhayeddeśikaḥ śambhorājñāṃ labdhā tato yathā| maṇḍale kalaśe vahnau saṃpūjya parameśvaraṃ vidyeśvaraṃ gaṇeśānaṃ 
lokapānanale kramāt| saṃpūjya tarpayitvā du śodhayo (l6)
 tat śivāṇunā hutvā śatadvayaṃ pūrṇāṃ datvā vijñāpayecchivaṃ bhagavannakṣamaścāyaṃ samācārapālane| tasmādanena homena 
tatsarvaṃ tvaṃ kṣamāpayā(ya)| vidyeśvaragaṇeśā (l7)
 lokapālāśca sākṣiṇaḥ kṛtvā saṃyojayettasya samayān deśikottamairiri| guruḥ samayādyācārānuṣṭhānakaraṇe samarthaḥ 
śivārcanaśivajñānādi(l8)
yogyaḥ śiva-pūjāsiddhāntādhyayanamantraśravaṇādau yogyo bhavet| 

tathā kāmike - evaṃ samayasaṃskārayukto yaḥ śivapūjane homārcadhyāyane caiva mantrāṇāṃ śravaṇe'pi ... (l9)
 ca yogyasyādaiśvaraṃ raudraṃ padaṃ prāpnomīti mānavaḥ| tato nirvāṇadīkṣāyāmadhikārī bhavedvija iti||62||

kṣmātatvajaṃ hṛnmanuḥ praṇavataścāntya...... (p108a, l1)
 lokāt kālakṛśānutaḥ śatamalāvaṣṭau ca kāṇyantimāḥ gandhāt pañcaguṇāṃ ca gandhaviṣayaḥ kṣorṇo nivṛttau vidhiḥ 
prāṇāpānamiḍākhyapiṅgalayuge-pasthākhya nāsā caret| (l2)
 atha nirvāṇadīkṣāṃ vaktuṃ ṣaḍadhvāvāhanakramavidhānaṃ saṃhārakrameṇāha| kṣmeti| kṣmātatvaṃ nivṛttau nivṛtyākhyakalāyāṃ 
kṣmābhūtatvaṃ bhavati| smare dini vā saṃbandhaṃ kāryaḥ| aja..... (l3)
 sadyojātahṛdayamantrau antyāt praṇavataḥ ekāśītipadānāṃ madhye 
antyādvidyamānādoṅkārapadādārabhyābhavadvyantakamaṣṭāviṃśatipadaṃ bhavati| nanvatrāntyāt pādādārabhya mahādevapada.... 
(l4)
ntakamaṣṭāviṃśatipadaṃ bhavati| kathaṃ bhavediti padāntakamaṣṭāviṃśatiḥ| 

tathā varuṇapaddhatyām - antyāt padā mahādevapadāvadhilomataḥ| vyomavyāpipadānyaṣṭāviṃśatiḥ parisaṃ(l5)
khyeti ata evātra caturṇavatipadasaṃkhyāyā vyomavyāpipadādyānyāsakrame vihita iti mantavyaḥ varuṇapaddhatau 
ekāśītipadasaṅkhyayā vihitatvāt dviprakāro (l6)
 padanyāsa iti siddho bhavati| dviprakārapadādhvaprapañcastu pūrvameva sarvādhvāyikabhāgasaṃyuktetyādigrandhavyākhyāne 
vistāreṇa pradarśitaṃ tatraivāvadhāryam| (l7)
 kālakṛśānutaḥ kālāgnibhuvanādārabhya kālyantimāḥ bhadrakālyabhuvanaparyantāḥ lokāḥ bhuvanādhvani 
śatamaṣṭottaraśatam alau akāralakārau dve bīje (l8)
 gandhāt gandhaguṇādārabhya pañcaguṇāḥ gandharasarūpasparśaśabdākhyāḥ gandho viṣayaḥ kṣo kṣakāraḥ, varṇo varṇaḥ vidhiḥ 
brahmā kāraṇeśaḥ prāṇāpānau vāyu iḍākhya(p108b, l1)
piṅgale gudopasthākhya iḍākhya piṅgalayugaṃ iḍā piṅgalā nāḍīdvayamupasthākhyanāsaḥ| upasthā ghrāṇākhyendriye etāḥ 
smarennivṛttikalāyāmantarbhūtā iti cintyet| tathoktam| (l2)
 vṛttau pārthivaṃ tatra tvamekaṃ ca bhuvanāni ca| kālāgnibhuvanāntāni bhadrakālyaṃ tribhistathā| aṣṭottaraśatānyatra 
kṣakāro varṇa eva hi| padāni bhavamārabhya yāvadoṅkāramantikam| (l3)
 aṣṭāviṃśatisaṅkhyāni sadyo hṛdayaśambarau| bīje alau tathā nāḍī indhā paṅgalasaṃjñake| prāṇāpāne tathā 
ghrāṇopasthau vāyvindriye'pi ca| viṣayo gandha evaikaḥ (l4)
 śabdādiguṇapañcakam| maṇḍalaṃ pārthivaṃ gauraṃ vajrakaṃ kaṭhinaṃ gurum| vaidāśraṃ yojanānāṃ ca śatakoṭyucchrayaṃ dṛḍhaṃ 
jāgrādavasthāsaṃyuktaṃ kāraṇaṃ kamalāsanaḥ|| (l5)
 śuddhi kāñcanasaṃkāśaṃ sarvametadvibhāvayan| śiśorjānvośca pādāntaṃ guruḥ sūtre ca vinyaset||63||

tatvādhvā prākṛtāntaḥprabhṛti salilatoṭāntahādyā (l6)
 pratiṣṭhā śrīkaṇṭhāntāmareśādbhuvanasaraṇikā vāmadevaḥ śiro'ṇuḥ vyāpinyantāni śarvadvitayapadatastālupāyubhau dvau dvau 
gāndhārimadhyā nāḍīśrutiguṇarasako dānamānau (l7)
 hariśca| pratiṣṭhākalāyāmantarbhūtatatvādīnyāha| tatveti salilataḥ prabhṛti jalatatvādārabhya prākṛtāntaḥ 
prakṛtitatvaparyantaścaturviṃśatisaṅkhyākastatvādhvā| pratiṣṭhāyāṃ vidyata iti .......... (l8)
 pratiṣṭhākalā ṭāntahādyā ṭakāraparyantā hakārādyā trayoviṃśativarṇayuktā bhavati amareśā umareśā bhuvanādārabhya 
śrīkaṇṭhāntā śrīkaṇṭhabhuvana...........bhuvana... (p109a, l1)
ṇīka- bhuvanānāṃ ṣaṭpañcāśat saṅkhyakānāṃ paṅktiḥ| vāmadevaḥ śiroṇuḥ vāmadevamantro śiromantraḥ śarvadvitayapadataḥ 
śarvaśarveti padadvayādārabhya vyāpin ..........pinyā...... (l2)
 paryantāni dvātriṃśat saṅkhyakāni tālupāyū tāluśabdena jihvocyate| talorindriyatvābhāvāt jihvā pāyu āye? 
jihvā pāyu iti pāṭhaḥ kartavyaḥ ubhā ukāravakārau dvau bīje gāndhārī madhyanāḍī .... (l3)
... suṣumnā nāḍī ca śrutiguṇaḥ rasarūpasparśaśabdākhyaścaturguṇaḥ| rasakaḥ viṣayaḥ| dānamānau| udānasamānau| 
vāyu hariḥ| viṣṇuḥ kāraṇeśaḥ| uktaṃ ca tadatho vinyaset| pāśāṃ pratiṣṭhākalayānvitā| .... (l4)
ni prakṛtādīni tatvāni ca jalāvadhi| trayoviṃśati saṅkhyāni bhuvanāni ca saṅkhyayā| pañcāśadadhikānyatra 
ṣaḍbhiśrīkaṇṭhabhairavāt amareśāntakaṃ triṃśat padāni dviradhikāni ca| vyāpin vyāpin (l5)
 padāccharvaśarvāntāni hatāni caṭādihāntākṣarāṇīha proktāni ca samāsataḥ| trayoviṃśatisaṃkhyāni 
vāmadevaśirasraṇuḥ| bīje ubhau tathā nāḍyau gāndhāri ca sumnayā samānodāne (l6)
 vāyu ca jihvā pāyu tathendriye| rasaśca viṣayaḥ śabdasparśarūparasāni ca tatvāni vāruṇaṃ bījaṃ 
śvetamambhojalāñchitam ardhacandravadākāraṃ sthāvaraṃ śītalātmajam| ghaghārāvasamā(l7)
yuktaṃ kodasāhasrayojanaiḥ śataṃ ca svapnakāvasthaṃ kāraṇaṃ garuḍadhvajaṃ sarvametat sthitaṃ śiṣyanābherjānudvayānugaṃ bhāvayan 
vinyasettatra pūśasūtre ca sadgururiti||64|| (l8.)

māyātatva.........puruṣamathaśikhārūpiṇau jñādighāntaṃ vāmādyāṅguṣṭhamātra.........sarajagatasthārakāntaḥ padādhvā 
vyomānādīmarau dvau karivararasanā pūrvasajñātra nāga(p109b, l1)
 ...-nau .......... rūpāhvayaviṣayagatā rudravidyā guṇā strīn|| vidyākalā yāmalasūtatatvādīnyāha| māyeti| ādau 
prathame puruṣaḥ puruṣākhyatatvānārabhya māyātatvāntaṃ saptatatvam| śikhā(l2)
...piṇau śikhāghauramantrau ñakārādighakārāntaṃ saptavarṇaṃ sarajagataḥ sakāraḥ saptasaṃ.....repho dvisaṃyukta ityarthaḥ 
vāmādyaṅguṣṭhamātrāntima vāmākhyabhuvanamādiṃ kṛtvāṅguṣṭhamātraṃ bhuvanaparyanta(l3)
lokasamūhaḥ| vyominnādi vyomin vyomin padmādiṃ kṛtvā tārakāntaḥ aukāraparyantaḥ ekaviṃśatipadādhvā marau 
dvau makārarephau dvau bīje karighararasanāhastajihvā pūṣasaṃjñā pūṣākhyā dvidhā nāḍi (l4)
 atra vidyākalāyāṃ nagavyānau vāyupādākṣipādanayanendriye| rūpāhvayaviṣayagatā rūpanāmadheyā viṣayasahitā 
vidyākalā trīn guṇān gatā rudraḥ kāraṇeśaḥ uktaṃ ca| tadatho (l5)
 vinyase vidyākalāṃ pāśasya mantrataḥ māyādi-puruṣāntāni saptatatvāni vinyaset| 
aṅguṣṭhamātrabhuvanādyāvadyāmapurāntakaḥ vinyaset| bhuvanānyatra saptaviṃśati saṅkhyayā| padamoṃktaramā(l6)
...hya vyomin vyomin padāvidhi| ekaviṃśatisaṅkhyāni vidyāyāṃ ca padāni ca| khakārādi jñakārāntavarṇāsyuḥ 
saptasaṅkhyayā| mantrau ghoraśikhe proktā vidyāyāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ bījama(l7)
rau tathā nāsau pu(pū)ṣā ca karijihvakā| vyānanāgau tathā vāyu ca pādendriye-'pi ca| rūpaṃ ca viṣayaṃ 
śabdasparśarūpaguṇāni ca maṇḍalaṃ tejaḥ raktaṃ ca dāhākāraṃ trikoṇagaṃ ...... (l8)
 kā taducchrāyaṃ yojanāyutakoṭibhiḥ| kāraṇo rudradevaśca tathāvasthā suṣuptikā sarvametat nyasettatra bhāsvarā 
bhaṅguruttamaḥ hṛdayaṃ nābhiparyantaṃ śiṣyamehe ca ............. (p110a, l1)
 vidyādeśasadāśivau ca gakhakāḥ śāntiṃ nṛvarme nū vāmākhyādisadāśivāntimabhuvastīśodhipo binduyau| dhyānā-
hāramukhā yaśā ca gaganavyāptyāntimūlaṃ pu(pū)ṣā sparśārā........... (l2)
 tu karau sparśaśca pāṇitvacau śāntikalāyāmantarbhūtatatvādinyāha| vidyeti vidyā śuddhavidyātatvaṃ īśasadāśivau ca 
īśvara-sadāśivatatve evaṃ trīṇi tatvāni| gakhakāḥ varṇāḥ śāntikalā eva (l3)
 cintanīyeti śeṣaḥ| nuvame? mama nū tatpuruṣakavacamantre vāmākhyādisadāśivāntimabhuvanaḥ vāmākhyabhuvanamādiṃ 
kṛtvā sadāśivabhuvana-paryantāṣṭādaśabhuvanāni īśādhipaḥ parameśvaraḥ|| (l4)
 kāraṇeśvaraḥ binduyau binduyakārau bīje dhyānāhāramukhaḥ dhyānāhārapadādikaṃ gaganavyāptyantimaḥ vyomavyāpiparyantaṃ 
dvādaśapadam alaṃ pūṣā-yaśānāḍīdve (l5)
 sparśārāvaguṇau sparśaśabdau guṇau kūrmakṛkarau vāyuspaśo viṣayaḥ pāṇi tvacau pāṇi tvagindriye| uktaṃ ca 
sadāśiveśvarau| vidyāśāntau tatvatrayaṃ nyaset| sadāśivādivāmāntā(l6)
nyaṣṭādaśapurāṇi (ṇāni) tu| vyomavyāpinamārabhya dhyānahārapadāvadhi| padāni ravisaṅkhyāni varṇāśca kakhagāḥ 
smṛtāḥ bīje binduyakārākhye mantrau vaktratanucchadau| alaṃ (l7)
 pūṣāya śānāḍī vāyu kṛkarakūrmakau tvakkarā-vindriye proktau sparśo viṣaya eva hi| maṇḍalaṃ vāyavaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ cañcalaṃ 
ca ṣaḍaśrakam aṅkitaṃ bindubhiḥ ṣaḍbhiḥ dvau guṇau sparśa(l8)
śabdakau lakṣakoṭibhirucchrāyaṃ yojanānāṃ tu vāyavaṃ turyāvasthamanantaṃ ca kāraṇaṃ ceśvaraṃ nyaset| śyāmavarṇamidaṃ sarvaṃ 
phālāśca hṛdayāvadhi| dhyāyeddehe śiśostatra pāśasūtre ca (p110b, l1)
........rgatamabhekārāntimāśca svarāḥ īśānāstraśivau nivṛttibhuvanāccānāśritāntaḥpuraḥ ādyaṃ tārapadaṃ hadādayugalātītā 
sadeśaḥ kūhūśaṅkhīdevadhanañjayau ravaguṇa... (l2)
 .....vākiśrotrake| śāntyatītakalāyāmantabhūta(tata)tvādīnāyaha – tatva iti| śaktiśive ubhe tatve 
śaktitatvaśivatatvākhya-dvandvatatve visargataḥ akārasahitavisargādārabhya akārāntimāḥ hrasvākāraparyantā(tāḥ) 
(l3)
 svarāḥ ṣoḍaśa īśānādistrayo mantrāḥ nivṛtti bhuvanāni nivṛttyākhyābhuvanamārabhya anāśritāntākhyabhuvanaparyantāḥ 
puraḥ pañcadaśabhuvanāni ādyaṃ prathamabhūtatārakam oṅkāramekaṃ (l4)
 padaṃ sunālayugalā hakāranādākhyabījadvandvayuktā atītā śāntyātītetyarthaḥ| sa deśaḥ sadāśivaḥ kāraṇeśvaraḥ 
kuhuśaṅkhī kuhuśaṅkhīni nāḍyau devadattadhanañjayau (l5)
 vāyu ravaḥ śabdaguṇaḥ| śabdaḥ śabda eva viṣayaḥ| vākśrotrake vacanaśrotrendriye uktaṃ ca| dve tatve 
śāntyatītāyāṃ kramācchaktiśivātmake| tathā nāśritā śaktyādinivṛttiryāvada(l6)
ntikā tābhirādhiṣṭhitānyasyaṃ(syāṃ) bhuvanā nyūnaṣoḍaśaṃ nādajaṃ praṇavaṃ viddhi padaṃ sākṣāt śivātmakam 
akārādivisargāntā varṇāḥ ṣoḍaśa kīrtitāḥ īśānāstre śivā mantrāḥ (l7)
 bi(bī)je dve nādahau tathā nāḍyā kuhū ca śaṅkhinī ca devadattadhanañjayau taduktaṃ ca samākhyātaṃ kartā caiva 
sadāśivaḥ śikhāmārabhya phalāntaṃ dhyāyet sarvaṃ sitaṃ śiroriti||67|| (l8.)

vyāpyavyāpakabhāvanātṛnnipadairmantrāḥ pādānyakṣarairvarṇāste bhuvanaiśca tāni ca tathā vyāptāni tatvairime| vyāpyāḥ 
karmamalākhyamohabhavā ye vyāpakāste'pyama(l9)
vyāpakabhāvanā iha kalāstābhi śrī hitā ime| ṣaḍadhvanā vyāpyavyāpakabhāva(vaṃ) jñātvā dīkṣāṃ kartavyeti ke 
vyāpāḥ ke vyāpakāḥ iti vyāpyavyāpakavidhānamāha| (p111a, l1)
 yatra ṣaḍadhvani vyāpyavyāpakabhāvanaye padārthā svān padārthe vyāptuṃ yogyā bhavanti te vyāpyāḥ svān 
padārtheṣvantarbhūtā ityarthaḥ| (l2)
 vyāpyeti svān padārthān sveṣu garbhityāvatiṣṭhante| te vyāpakāḥ itthaṃ vyāpyānāṃ vyāpakānāṃ bhavanāvastutvena 
vidyamāna bhāvanā ucyanta iti śeṣaḥ padaiḥ vyomavyāpipadai(l3)
rmantrāda īśānādayaḥ vyāptāḥ mantrāḥ vyāpyaḥ padāni vyāpakānītyarthaḥ| padāni akṣaraiḥ varṇaiḥ vyāptāḥ padānāṃ 
vyāpakatvam akṣarāṇāṃ vyāpakatvamiti bhāvaḥ te varṇāḥ bhuvanaiḥ (l4)
 vyāptāḥ varṇānāṃ vyāpyatvaṃ bhuvanānāṃ vyāpakatvaṃ tāni bhuvanāni tathā tatvaiḥ ṣaṭtriṃśatatvaiḥ vyāptāni bhuvanānāṃ 
vyāpyatvaṃ tatvānāṃ vyāpakatvam ime tatvasamūhāḥ karmamālikhya (l5)
 mohakabhāvakarmapuṇyāpuṇyātmakaṃ malākhyamāṇavaṃ malaṃ, malaṃ mohakaṃ māyā vimohinī proktā viṣayā svādubhogata 
ityuktatvāt etebhyo bhavāḥ (l6)
 utpannāḥ pūrvoktatatvādayo vyāpyāḥ malakarmamādheyā vyāpakāḥ karmaṇā sukhaduḥkhakaraṇatva-tannimittaṃ keṣāṃcit 
padārthānāmāvirbhāvaḥ malasya bhoktṛkaraṇatvāt (l7)
 tannimittaṃ keṣāñcit padārthānāmāvirbhāvaḥ māyāḥ śarīrendriyaviṣayakaraṇatvāttannimittaṃ keṣāñcit padārthānāmāvirbhāvaḥ 
ye malādayaḥ tatvādīnāṃ vyāpakāḥ te malādayaḥ (l8)
 vyāpyā bhavatītyāha| iha ṣaḍadhvamadhye vyāpyavyāpakabhāvagāḥ vyāpyatvena vyāpakatvena vidyamānāḥ kalāḥ 
nivṛttyādayaḥ nivṛttivyāpyā pratiṣṭhā vyāpakā pratiṣṭhā, vyāpyā (l9)
 vidyāvyāpakā vidyāvyāpyā śāntivyāpakakā śāntivyāpyā śāntyatītā vyāpakāḥ viśvasya malādeśca 
vyāpyavyāpakatvāt kalā ityucyate| uktaṃ ca kalayanti yato viśvaṃ (l10)
 vyā......tāṃ kalāḥ śaktayastāḥ parāśakteḥ pañcabhedā vyavasthitāḥ yathādau pārthive tatve karmabhogo nivṛtyate| 
nivṛttiḥ sakalā jñeyā pratiṣṭhā kathyate'dhunā| anāditatva... (p111b, l1)
..........pānānukrameṇa tu| śivarāgānuraktātmā sthāpyate pauruṣe yathā| ..... pratiṣṭhākalā jñeyā tato 
vidyānigadyāni gadyate| māyā kāryā vivekena vetti (l2)
 vidyāpadā yathā| sa kalā paramā jñeyā vidyā jñānakalātmikā| malamāyā-dhikārādhā śāntiḥ puṃsaḥ punaryayā sā 
kalāśāntirityuktā svādhikārā svapadapradā śāntyatītā kalā taiśca (l3)
 nirvāṇānantabodhateti| tābhiḥ kalābhiḥ pūrvoktamalādayaḥ gri(gṛ)hi(hī)tāḥ vyāptā malādi (dī)nāṃ vyāpyatvaṃ 
kalānāṃ vyāpakatvamiti bhāvaḥ iti mekhalāsamūhāḥ bindunā vyāpteti śeṣaḥ .... (l4)
huśaktyā vyāptaḥ śaktiḥ śivena vyāptaḥ uktaṃ ca| vyāpako bhuvanādināmativyāptaḥ sabindunā| binduśaktyā 
śivenaiṣā nānyena vyāpyate śivaḥ etāḥ kalā sthāpitāścet (l5)
 sarve tatvādayaḥ sthāpikā bhavanti| śodhitāścet| śodhitā bhavanti ata eva kalā śodhanīyā iti prāptam||
68|| 

śiṣyasyokṣaṇa-tāḍane-astramanuto hṛt tatpraveśo (l6)
 hṛdā hyātmākṛṣṭiśikhāntasaṃgrahasahiḥ nissāraṇe svātmani| nāsā puragataḥ praviśya hṛdaye nyastvā tathotkravyaṃ 
saṃgṛhyātma tathaiva piṅgalagataniskārya śaktau nyaset|| (l7)
 pāśasūtre vidyamānaśivaśaktau śiṣyacautanyasya nyāsakramamāha| śiṣyeti śiṣyasya dṛgbhūtasyātrāpi 
pūrvoktavidyāgādicaruhavanaprāyaścittādikaṃ kṛtvā śiṣyasya maṇṭapapraveśādiviśeṣa- (l8)
samskārāntaṃ vidhāya nirvāṇadīkṣākaraṇārthaṃ mūlādimantrāṇāṃ tarpaṇadīpanāntaṃ karmanivṛtyā śivājñāprārthanānantaraṃ 
śivāgnerdakṣiṇamaṇḍale sthitasya triguṇena (l9)
 triguṇīkṛtena astra-kavaca-mūlena yathākramaṃ prokṣaṇamavakuṇḍa(ṭha)napūjitena suṣumnāmayapāśasūtreṇa śikhāmārabhya 
dakṣiṇapādāṅguṣṭhamavalambitasya deha(he)vidyamāne (p112a, l1)
 pāśānāṃ chedanaṃ bhedanādikarakaraṇānmaṃ sūtramayo dehaḥ kāryaḥ uktaṃ ca chedyā bhedyāśca te pāśāḥ sūtrasthānena 
pi(vi)grahe| tasmāt sūtramayo deho bhāvanīyastu deśikai..... (l2)
 astramanutaḥ astramatre(ntre)ṇa śiṣyasyokṣaṇāḍane kāryeti śeṣaḥ astreṇa hṛdaye suśāgreṇa prokṣaṇaṃ huṃpha[ḍa]ntāstreṇa 
tatraiva santāḍanaṃ kartavyaṃ huṅkārātmano recakeṇa..... (l3)
nigratya tatpraveśanaṃ tasya śiṣyasya hṛdayāpūrakeṇa śiṣyavāmanāḍyā praveśaḥ kartavyaḥ hṛdā 
hṛtsaṃpuṭitasadyojātāstrahuṃpha[ḍa]ntamantreṇa jyeṣṭhā śaktyā śiṣyahṛdayakamalaṃ kiñci..... (l4)
ccalitabhedanaṃ kāryātmatuṣṭiḥ hṛtsampuṭitasadyojātayuktātmantreṇāṅka(ṅku)śamudrayā karṣaṇaṃ kāryaṃ 
śikhāntasaṃgrahabahirnissāraṇe kṛtveti śeṣaḥ śikhāmānte...... (l5)
 dvādaśānte tadvādaśāntaparyantamātmānaṃ nītvetyarthaḥ saṅgrahaḥ ātmantreṇa dvādaśāntasthitātmanaḥ saṅgrahaṃ kāryaḥ 
bahirnissāraṇaṃ saṃgṛhītātmanaḥ śiṣyabhrūmadhyā....... (l6)
nantaraṃ bahirnirgamanaṃ kartavyaṃ svātmani nāsāpuragatavāmanāsāpu(pū)rakavāyunā praveśya - praveśanaṃ kṛtvā, hṛdaye 
nyastvā svahṛdaye kumbhakena svātmanā saha (l7)
 samarasibhāvena niveśanaṃ kṛtvā tamātmānaṃ tayotkṛṣya tathā brahmādikāraṇatyāgakrameṇa utkṛṣya dvādaśāntaparyantaṃ 
nītvā saṃgṛhyā(hya) tatra svātmānaṃ saṃgṛhyātmānaṃ (l8)
 dvādaśā........ bhrūmadhyamānīya piṅgalataiḥ svadakṣiṇanāḍinirgameṇa recakavāyornissārya bahirnirgamaṃ kṛtvā śaktau 
pūśasūtre vyāpakena sthite śivaśaktau (p112b, l1)
.......māntātmamantreṇa malādipāśamavakuṇṭhya tasyātmano nyāsaṃ kuryāt| nyāsānantaraṃ sannidhānāyāhutitrayaṃ mūlena 
deyaṃ bhoktṛtvakāraṇā...kāsamamalapāśa(l2)
.....du(duḥ)khakāraṇasitāsitarūpakarmapāśaśarīrendriyaviṣayakāraṇakarbūravarṇamāyāpāśānāṃ viṣṭhātryastamorūpāyāḥ 
śivaśakteśca nyāsaṃ sūtre sannidhānāyāhutīnāṃ (l3)
 ......... pṛthak pṛthak dadyāt| nanu śivaśaktiramalā sā kathaṃ pāśarūpā bhavitumarhati| satyaṃ kiṃ tu 
śodhyaśodhakatvenānuktā sadipāśūtyupacaryate iti| uktaṃ ca tāsā (l4)
 iheśvari śaktiḥ sarvānugrāhikā śivā| dharmānuvartanādeva pāśa ityuccaryate| iti pūrvoktavat tatvādigarbhitāṃ 
nivṛtyāditāṃ nivṛtyādikāṃ pāśasūtre saṃyojya sanni..... (l5)
nāyāhutitrayaṃ deyam||66?(69?)||

tasyā yoniṣu yojitasya yugapadgarbhāptaye janma te hotavyaṃ ca svakarmasañcayakṛte tadbhogabhuktau laye| māyāsrotasi 
(l6)
 niṣkṛtau ca havanaṃ tatve ca mūle nanu pāśānāṃ ca tirohitau vibhajjanacchede kalāhetinā| athātmano 
vahnivāgīśvaryāṃ garbhādhānādisaṃskārārthaṃ (l7)
 homavidhānamāha| tasyā iti prathamaṃ pūrvoktavat tatvādiyuktāṃ brahmādisthāvarāntacaturdaśabhūtasvargādiyuktāṃ 
nivṛttikalāṃ jānvadhaḥ sūtre hṛdayasampuṭita-caturthyantanivṛttikalā(l8)
...jenāvalokyāṅkuśamudrayā pūrakeṇākṛṣyā saṃhāramudrayā sumbhakena saṃgṛhya recakenodbhavamudrayā kuṇḍasthitādhāraśaktau

 nivṛttikalābījena sannidhānāhutitrayaṃ datvā tadadhipatiṃ brahmāṇamāvāhya prārthyā nivṛttikalāṃ vyāpakāṃ (p113a, 
l1)
 vāgīśvarīṃ vāgīśvaraṃ cāvāhya sannidhānāyāhutitrayaṃ dadyāt| evaṃ pratiṣṭhākalādiṣvapi gatvādisaṃyuktasyā-vāhanādikaṃ 
kuryāt| nivṛttikalāvyāpikāyāstasya vāgīśvaryā (l2)
 yonuṣu anekarūpayoniṣu nanu vāgīśvarī tāvadekayonisahitatasyānekayonitvaṃ kathaṃ satyam| nivṛttikalādiṣu

 kalāvyāpakatvenānekayonitvaṃ saṃbhavatyeva yojitasya pūrvoktavacchiṣyatāḍanādipūrvakamātmānaṃ saṃgṛhya svahṛdi 
niveśanānantaraṃ brahmādikāraṇatyāgakrameṇa bahirnissārya pitroḥ (l4)
 saṃyogaṃ vibhāvyātmamantreṇa yugapat sarvayoniṣvanekavyaktirūpeṇa saṃyogīkṛtasya nuḥ śiṣyātmanaḥ yugapadekavārameva 
sarvāsu yoniṣu karṇavaicitryāt (l5)
 strīpuṃnapuṃsakamiti mṛgapakṣyādikamiti| garbhāptaye garbhādhānaprāptyarthaṃ bho ātman garbhādhānayukto bhaveti mūnamantreṇa 
havanaṃ homaṃ kāryaṃ janmane sarvagarbhān (l6)
 ba...rjananārthaṃ mūlena homaṃ vidhānam| atra garbhādhānānantaraṃ janmanetyutvā(ktatvā)t puṃsavanasīmantonnayane kartavye 
iti jñāpite| puṃsavanaṃ strītvanapuṃsaka(l7)
pratiṣedhārtha........tasmādatra strīnapuṃsakajananasya vihitatvāt puṃsavanaṃ kartavyaṃ sīmantonnayanaṃ 
kāṇakubjakhañjāti(di)rūpapratiṣedhāya ca kṛtam| svakarmaṇā (l8)
 svena.......ṇā nādabāpikūpa-prapāti laukikānāṃ ca vaidika catvāriṃśatyakarmaṇāṃ ca sañcayaḥ samūhastasya kṛte 
ārjanakaraṇe mūlena hotavyaṃ tadbhogabhuktaṃ na tasya karmaṇaḥ (l9)
 ...........sukhaduḥkhavedanātmakabhogabhojale mūlenāhutiḥ kāryā bhogārthaśvettataḥ (p113b, l1)
 ....... chāyāṃ mahati pratibimbatā cidvittirātmano bhogakarmaṇāṃ kṣayakāraṇamiti layabhogādanantaraṃ bhogeṣu bhoktari 
ca bhedādhyavasāyarūpasaṃsmṛtau (l2)
 mūlenāhutiḥ kāryā uktaṃ ca| abhedāndadhyavasāyo yo bhogyabhoktoḥ parasparam| atyantamanurakto yaścidvṛtto 
yaścidvṛte mata iti| māyāśrotasi māyotpannabhuvanaśuddhau (l3)
 asya ............pabhuktabhuvaneṣu punarvyāptimābhūditi srotasāṃ śuddhiḥ tadardhaṃ mūlenāhutiḥ niṣkṛtau 
jātyāyurbhogasamskārādimūlena hotavyam| tatve ca vigatāmbuvyanavallāghavārthaṃ (l4)
 tattat kalāsthitaśuddhau mūlena havanaṃ kāryam| uktaṃ ca| upabhuktodakasyeva śaranmeghasya lāghavam| śuddhiratra 
vinirdiṣṭā tatvasya dakṣiṇakarmaṇa iti| pāśānāṃ malamāyā(l5)
karmaṇāṃ malamāyākarmayuktanivṛttikalādīnāṃ ca tirohitau malaśaktitirodhāne vibhajane malakarmamāyārūpapāśatrayeṇa 
malaśaktitirodhānapāśaviśleṣaṇastu (l6)
 śubhavati(titi)robhāvaḥ sahakārīvaśādiha bhogabījakalānāśe malavṛtterahetunaḥ bhoktṛtvaṃ prati pāśena tasmin 
vyāsaviśleṣaḥ ucyate| chede nivṛtyādikalā(l7)
pāśacchedene saptadhāstrajaptajñānaśaktyā kartarīti śeṣaḥ uktaṃ ca| kartari jñānaśaktistu yathā pāśācchinatya sau 
sakalā paramā sūkṣmāmantrāṇāṃ badhanīparā iti kalā(l8)
hetinā nivṛttyādimantrayuktahuṃphaḍantāstramantreṇa tattat pa...vadhiṃ chidyāt||70||

pāṇibhyāṃ ca vimardanaṃ hutamadho pāśāṅkurā bhāvataḥ prāyaścittavidhau cidaika(l9)
mānanaṃ sūkṣmāṅgaśuddhau hṛdāṃ śabdādyaṣṭaśukladānamadhipe viṣvānmoddhṛtiścārghyakam| vāgīśyeśahuto hṛdi hṛdaṇunā mūlena 
pūrṇā-hutiḥ| pāśacchedānantaraṃ mardādikṛtyamāha| (p114a, l1)
 pāṇibhyāmiti|| pāṇibhyāṃ hastābhyāṃ vimardanaṃ huṃphaḍantātreṇa vimardanānantaraṃ cheditapāśasūtrasya vartulīkaraṇaṃ 
ghṛtapūrṇasṛgagre nidhāya nivṛttikalāpāśaṃ juhomīti havanaṃ vidheyam adho (l2)
 kṛtānantaraṃ pāśāṅkurābhāvataḥ punaḥ pāśāṅkure nivṛttito huṃphantā(ḍantā)streṇa hutaṃ kāryaṃ prāyaścittavidhau 
nyūnātiriktakarmadoṣavināśavidhāne astreṇāṣṭāhutiḥ kāryā uktaṃ ca nyūnādhi(l3)
kā nyāyakarmaprasūtā śubhakarmaṇi| prāyaścittāhutīraṣṭau hutvā hānāya hetinā iti cidaikyamananaṃ samastaśarīravināśe 
caitanyasyeke saṃbhāvanaṃ kuryāt| tattatkarmajanitasamastadeheṣu (l4)
 bhinnatvena vidyamānātmanaṃ tattaddehavināśe caitanyasya ekatvaṃ saṃbhāvanamiti yāvat| sūkṣmāṅgaśuddhau 
sūkṣmaśarīraśuddhyarthaṃ sūkṣmaśarīraṃ tu puryaṣṭakavihitamuktaṃ ca| śabdasparśaśca rūpaśca (l5)
 raso gandhastu pañcamaḥ buddhirmanastvahaṅkāraḥ puryaṣṭakamidaṃ vapuriti| hṛdā hṛdayamantreṇādhipeṣu 
nivṛttyādikalākrameṇādhipeṣu brahmaviṣṇurudreśvarasadāśiveṣu (l6)
 śabdādyaṣṭakaśulkadānaṃ brahmādikrameṇa pūrvoktaśabdādyaṣṭakadānaṃ kartavyaṃ tadyathā hṛdayamantrema bho brahmin śabdasparśau 
śuklaṃ grahāṇeti brahmaṇi śuklaṃ samarpayet| evaṃ viṣṇau (l7)
 rasasamarpaṇam, rudre rūpasamarpaṇam, īśvare buddhyahaṅkārayoḥ, sadāśive manaḥ samarpaṇam| śabdādīnāṃ kāraṇabhūtāṃ 
baindhasaśaktiṃ sadāśive nyaset| uktaṃ ca śabdasparśau brahmaṇī (l8)
 ca śrīvatsāṅge rasaṃ nyaset| rūpagandhau tyajedrudre dhī garvāvi(vī)śvare tyajet| manaḥ sadāśive deve śabdādīnāṃ ca 
hetukīṃ śaktiṃ bindu....tatra tyajedityarthamanukramāditi| ātmoddhṛtiṃ (l9)
 nivṛttikalā pāśasamūhānmuktātmano dhāriṇaṃ kartavyam| uddharaṇāntaraṃ svahṛdi niveśya dvādaśāntaṃ nītvā 
śiṣyasūtradehe niyojanaṃ kāryam arghyakam āpyāyanāya vauṣaḍa(l10)
ntre(mantre)ṇa hṛdi dehaṃ rudre dhāraṇārthamātmanaṃ homaṃ kāryaḥ vauḍantamūlena pūrṇāhutiḥ samastakrayā parupūrṇā ..... 
kartavyam aṣṭādaśa| (p114b, l1)
 aṣṭādaśa-samskārāstatve gurūḥ paśoḥ malatrayanāśāya kurvīta śivatṛptaye ātmā ātmāstāḍanādane svakṛtiḥ 
tasya yojana.....no patyadhikāro bhogo layo'tha sretta(l2)
syāṃ śuddhiḥ tasya ca śuddhiśca paśutvatirohaṇaṃ pāśaviśleṣavicchedau taddāho vyutkramakriyākāraṇeṣṭiṃ paśuṃ 
dhārayeṣvevānyapi pūrṇayā samastasaṃskārā vijñeyā madhyavartinaḥ (l3)
 viśleṣātmatāḍalaṃ syāt| viśleṣaḥ karṣaṇāya ca karṣaṇagrahaṇāyaiva grahaṇaṃ yojagakāraṇaṃ garbhādhānāya yogyasya 
dānayānaṃ jananārthakaṃ jananaṃ cādhikārārthamadhikāro'pi (l4)
 vratayaiva bhūtaśca layahetu syāt layāntena ca niṣkṛtiḥ niṣkṛtiśca viyogārthaṃ viyogacchedakāraṇaṃ vikṣepau yaiva 
vicchedo'pi kṣepaḥ tasya kāraṇaṃ tataḥ tenaiva ca pūrṇā syāt| (l5.)

atha dhārādihetuka iti||71|| śrotrādyai(dyaiḥ) viplutau tatsthapatikasaṃhitaṃ kravyabhakṣyodbhavatvaṃ syādevaṃ tannivṛtyai 
tadadhipatiṣu ya sparśaśabdādiśuklaṃ śabdasparśādi(l6)
rū.....stanutaratanugāḥ śodhane rodhino vā brahmādye kāraṇe syācchivapadagamane deyametācca tri(tṛ)ptyai| 
brahmādipañcakāraṇadeveṣu śabdasparśādiśukla(l7)
samarpa....kāraṇamāha| śrotrādyairiti| śrotrādyaiḥ śrotrāccakṣurjihvāghrāṇendriyairviplutau tattadindriya-sambandhaviplutau satyāṃ 
śravaṇendriyatvagīndriyābhyāṃ viplutau vihitānācara.... (l8)
......yācaraṇarūpaśāstrā jñāpyutkrāntau brahmarākṣasādiyonipradā śrotratvagviplutiḥ, rasānāviplutiḥ| viṣṇuṃ 
śakravyādajaṃ madāghrāṇalocanajanitā viplutiḥ rudrakravyāda(l9)
.....prada...buddhyahaṅkārau plutau īśvarāṃśakravyadehapradā mano viplutiḥ sadāśivāṃśakravyādajanmapradeti tatsthapatikasahitaṃ 
kravyabhakṣyodbhavatvamityuktaṃ tat (p115a, l1)
 ......hyasitaṃ teṣāṃ śrotrendriyatvagindriyaviplutijanitaśabdasparśādinā| sthapatikasahitaṃ tadadhipatibrahmādisahitaṃ 
kravyabhakṣyodbhavatvaṃ kravyabhakṣyaṃ māṃsabhakṣikāḥ rākṣasādayaḥ (l2)
 teṣūdbha.......bhūyāt| śrotrādiviplutijanitaśabdādikāraṇeśvareṣu śabdasparśādiśulkam, ādiśabdena rasādiśulkam| 
deyaṃ dātuṃ yogyam| nanu pṛthivyāḥ ....... (l3)
....hmaṇi samarpaṇakriyāḥ yadvā nijaguṇagandha eka eva samarpaṇīyāḥ tat kathaṃ pārthivaśuddhatatvau śabdasparśau 
gaganapavanaguṇau brahmaṇī samapyete iti śaṅkānirākaraṇārtha.....| (l4)
....dirūpā iti śabdasparśādirūpāḥ tanutaratanugāḥ tanu-taratanūḥ atyantaṃ sūkṣmaśarīraṃ liṅgaśarīramityarthaḥ tāṃ 
gacchatīti tanutaratanugā bhavanti| uktaṃ ca| śabdasparśarūpaśca raso gandhaṃ ca pañcamaḥ (l5)
 buddhirmanastvahaṅkāraḥ puryaṣṭakamudāhṛdamiti| ata eva puryaṣṭaka-śodhanārthaṃ ete śabdādayo guṇāḥ puryaṣṭamātmikāḥ 
śrotrādindriyaviplutisaṃbhūtā evaṃ (l6)
 samarpyante śodhane pṛthivyāditatvaśuddhau nirodhino nirodhakā bhavanti| ata eva śivapadagamane śivapadaprāptinimittaṃ 
brahmādye kāraṇe brahmaviṣṇurudreśvarasadāśive tṛptyai (l7)
 brahmāditṛptyarthaṃ etacchabdasparśādiśuklaṃ deyaṃ syāt| atra tu śrotrendriyaviplutijanitaśabdaḥ sparśastu 
tvagindriyaviplutijanitaḥ pādapāṇipāyupasthaviplutiḥ sparśārthatvāddhetoḥ (l8)
 tvagindriyāntarbhūtā viplutiḥ| śabdārthatvāt śrutāvantarbhūtāvubhau brahmāṃśāviti brahmaṇi samāptyete tathā 
rasanāviplutijanito rasaḥ viṣṇau samapyete| tathā ca ghrāṇa (l9)
viśrutiḥ viṣaya-rūpagandhau rudrāya samapyete| evaṃ buddhyahaṅkāraviplutirīśvare tathā manoviplutiśca 
samastavikārikāraṇabinduśaktirapi sadāśive samarpyate| uktaṃ ca| śruti(p115b, l1)
..........hmī kravyādayonidā rasanā vaiṣṇavī raudrī ghrāṇalocanahetukim aiśvarī garvadhī mūlā manasi śivadevatā| 
sparśārthavākkama.........ścatvārastvārastviti saṃsthitā| śabdāḥ (l2)
 ....du tatra varṇavidhā patreti sūrayaḥ| bāhyārthā-stisṛbhiḥ sārdhamantaḥkaraṇavṛttibhiḥ pāśaviśleṣavat kṛtvā 
devatāstvadhirodhayediti|
