māyāntātmākhyatatve vikalavidhihṛtau bhāṣyakoccāratoṇoḥ sādeśākhyā(l4)
ntavidyātmani manuvikaloccāragopāṃśusaṃjñāt tatve śaktyantaśaive manasi kila gurorbhāvanā hānināśe 
syuccārānmānasācchiva-manuviṣayāṇyāhutīnāṃ śatāni|| (l5)
 ...(dī)kṣāvidyaśivākhyatatvaiḥ kriyāmantrādivaikalyaśuddhyarthaṃ mūlena homavidhānamāha| māyeti| māyāntātmākhya-
tatve pṛthvītatvamārabhya māyātatvāvasānātmanāmadheya-tatve (l6)
 māyātatvāntavyāpakātmatatva ityarthaḥ| vikalā vidhihutau kriyālopasaṃyuktaḥ kramaparihāre aṇoḥ mūlamantrasya 
bhāṣyakoccārāntaḥ svānyeva kiñcichśravaṇayuktoccā(l7)
raṇāt sādeśākhyānta-vidyātmani sadāśivanāmadheyatatvāvasānā vindvātmasvarūpatatve manuvikaloccāragopāṃśusaṃjñāt 
mantroccāravaikalyaśuddhyartha...... (l8)
prākṣusaṃjñāt svasyaiva kiñcichravaṇasaṃyuktoccāraṇaśaktyantaśaivatatve śaktitatvāvyāpakaśivatatve manasi mānase guroḥ 
śivasya bhāvanā hānināśe cintanātyāga...... (l9)
.......śivabhāvanāhānidoṣanivṛtyarthaḥ| mānasāduccarāt manobhāvanoccārāt| śivamanuviṣayāṇi śivamantraviṣayāṇi 
āhutīnāṃ śatāni| ātmatatvādiṣu pratyekaṃ (l10)
 śivamantreṇa śatasaṅkhyayā homāni kāryāṇi| 

tathā kāmike - (p116a, l1)
 śāntyatītakalāṃ śaktitatve līnaṃ vicintya ātmatatvaṃ ca māyāntamupasthāpya gurūttamaḥ| ātmatatvaṃ 
caturthyantamādau tatvasamanvitaṃ namaḥ praṇavasaṃyuktaṃ matvā saṃpūjya sannidhau| vidhivaikalyaśuddhyartha(l2)
svāhāntaṃ śivamantrataḥ saśabdena śataṃ hutvā vidyātatvamupāṃśuvat mantroccāraṇavaikalyāt śivamantreṇa śūddhyati| 
śivatatvamupasthāpya śaktyantaṃ manasā smaret| śatamaṣṭottaraṃ hṛtvā (l3)
 manovaikalyamātmanaḥ ucyate śivamantreṇa śikhācchedanamāharet||73||

śuddhādhvādimatatvayāgavasudhābindvākhyatatvāsano nāde dhyādikalāḍyadehakaraṇaḥ śarvaikadhīrdeśikaḥ (l4)
 śaktirdhīḥ samanāntarālyakaraṇaḥ śuddhādhvatatvonmanaḥ vidyordhvastaśivākhyatatvanicitaṃ śaivaṃ vibhāvya svayam| 
dīkṣānte pūrṇāhutividhānārthaṃ śivabhāvanāmāha|| (l5)
 śuddhādhveti| śuddhādhvādimatatvayāgavasudhā śuddhādhvādimatatvaṃ śuddhavidyātatvaṃ śuddhavidyātatvaṃ 
devayāgavasudhāyāgasāmamanobhāvanayā pṛthivyādi (l6)
tatvāni ........śuddhavidyātatvasvarūpaṃ yāgasthānaṃ cintayedityarthaḥ| bindvākhyatatvāsanaḥ tatra yāgavasudhāyāṃ 
bindunāmadheyatatvāsanāsīnaḥ nāde dhyādikalāḍya(l7)
 dehakara...... kalendhikādi-kalāsamṛddhaśarīraṃ bāhyakaraṇaḥ atrendhikādinādakalāśarīrayuktaḥ vyāpinyādyāśritāntaḥ 
kalā bahiḥ karaṇayuktaḥ śarvaikadhiḥ parameśvaradhyānai.. (l8)
........ācāryaḥ śaktireva dhiḥ buddhiḥ samanāntarā-lyakaraṇaḥ antarāle bhavamāntarālyamantaḥkaraṇaṃ 
samanāśaktyantaḥkaraṇa ityarthaḥ śuddhādhvā ta.......tmanāvidyordhastaśivā(l9)
.........vidyāsvarūpātmatatvonmanāsvarūpavidyātatvordhvastaśivanāmadheya-tatvatrayeṇa nicitaṃ vyāptaṃ śaivaṃ śivaṃ saṃbandhaṃ 
śivaṃ vibhāvya vicintya tathoktaṃ (p116b, l1)
 ...........karaṇaśuddhiśca kṛtvā saṃyojayecchive| vidyātatvapadācāryo bindukliptāsanasthitaḥ indhikā dīpikā caiva 
rocikā mocikā tathā| tathordhvagāminī caiva sūkṣmā(l2)
....kṣatratā smṛtāḥ bījaśaktiriti proktā nādaśakti kalādvijāḥ bahiḥ kalābhisaṃkṣiptadehayuk deśikottamaḥ 
vyāpinī vyomarūpā ca hyanantāhyaparā tathā| anāthāśritā vābhiḥ bahiḥ (l3)
 karaṇasaṃyutaḥ ātmatatvo manaścaiva samāpūritadehayugiti||74||

ājyāḍyāṃ nihitasravāṃ sṛcamathaḥ śaṅkhākhyayā mudrayā prorthāyodgatapūrvakāyasamapāt sṛṅmūlanābhi(bhiṃ) smaret 
pañcādhāra(l4)
samasthitādhvani dvayaḥ sṛkakumbhadaṇḍāgrakhagrīvāṃśeṣu ca śaktimājyasaraṇau vaktraḥ śivaṃ sṛṅmayam| 
śaktiśivāvāhanamāha| ājyāḍyāmiti ghṛtasamuddhām ājyasa(saṃ)pūritāmityarthaḥ| nirni(rnī/rṇī)ta(l5)
sravāṃ adhomukhatvena sṛgbilopari vinyastasṛvayuktāṃ sṛvaṃ śaṅkhākhyayā śaṅkhā-kāravat kṛtamudrayā ādāya gṛhītvā 
sadguruḥ prārdhāyāsanādutthāyodgatapūrva(l6)
 kāya unnatapūrvaśarīraḥ samapāt sadṛśasthitapādaḥ sṛṅmūlanābhiḥ nābhau dhṛtasṛṅmūlaḥ pañcādhārasamasthitādhvani ca yaṃ 
pañcādhāreṣu maṇḍalavahnikumbhaśiṣyasvātmā(l7)
khyapañcādhikaraṇeṣu samastā dhvani ca yaṃ samānākāreṇa pūrvaṃ vinyasta-ṣaḍadhvasamūhaṃ pañcasthāneṣu śivasya 
vidyamānatvāt pūrvaṃ ṣaḍadhvanyāsaḥ sarvatra kāryaḥ| idānīṃ sarvatrāva-(l8)
sthitā dhvanicayaṃ gṛhītvā sṛvyāvāhayet| sarveṣāṃ karmaṇāṃ sākṣimaṇḍale samavasthitaḥ yajñasya rakṣakatvena 
śivakumbhe vyāvasthitaḥ homādhikaraṇatvena pāvakasthasadāśivaḥ|| (l9)
 śikyadehe tu tacchiṣya-pāśamocanakārakaḥ anugrahakaro devo mama dehe vyavasthitaḥ pañcādhikaraṇo devaḥ so'hameva 
sadāśivaḥ ityuktatvāt| sṛkkumbha(p117a, l1)
.......grīvāṃśeṣu sṛkkumbhaḥ sṛṅmūlasthitakalaśādayaḥ daṇḍo vṛttākāradaṇḍaḥ agraṃ gaṇḍikaṃ khaśabdenākāśavācakatvāt 
bilamucyate bilayuktavedītyarthaḥ| gravakaṇṭhaḥ? ete (l2)
 .....śeṣu bhāgeṣu nivṛtyādikalākrameṇa sarvāni(ṇi) ca yaṃ smaret = cintayet| 

tathā mṛgendre - sṛci vādhvānamāropya kalaśaskandhagaṇḍikā| vedīgrīvamukhāṃśeṣu nivṛtyādivibhāgaśa iti sṛkmukhe 
(l3)
 śivāvāhanasya kṛtatvāt ṣaṭsthānaṃ vihitamanusandheyam ājyasaraṇau ghṛtanissaraṇa-paddhatau śaktiṃ smaret| vaktre 
sṛkmukhe sṛṅmayaṃ sṛksvarūpaṃ śivaṃ smaret| sarvādhvavyāpakaṃ devaṃ sṛgatyamanu(l4)
smaredityarthaḥ sṛksṛvapya(vya)padeśena śaktipiṇḍaḥ sadāśiva ityuktatvāt||75||

śiṣyātma(maḥ) svamanaḥ sya(sma)raṇa?samāyoge bhavetprāṇavaṃ nādātyaityavicintanāt manu viṣu kandānmanū(l5)
ccārataḥ grandhicchedata eva nāḍiviṣuvaṃ tanmadhyanādodgamāt ṣaṭ tyaktvādikalāpraśāntaviṣuvaṃ śaktau layaḥ saptame| 
śakteḥ sa dhvanirūrdhvameva virasettacchakti saṃjñākalāṃ saptendvaṣṭa(l6)
..........tṛ(tru)ṭibhiḥ śaktermanāntaṃ tridhā| tatprāṇepyaparaḥ parāpara-paraiḥ kālāhvayaṃ tatparaṃ tatvākhyaṃ viṣuvasravaṃ 
paraśiva-sthānaṃ tu cidvyaktikṛt| (l7)
 atha yugena ślokena pūrṇāhutisamaye prāṇamantranāḍipraśāntaśaktikālatatvasaṅkhyasaptaviṣuvabhede dhyānamāha| śiṣyeti| 
śiṣyātmasvamanaḥ smaraṇasāmayogaḥ śiṣyasyātmanaḥ (l8)
 ....manasaśca samīraṇena prāṇavāyunā samāyogaḥ anyonyādhikyasaṃbandhaḥ prā(pra)ṇavaṃ prāṇasambandhaviṣuvaṃ prathamaṃ 
bhavet| tathoktam ātmānaṃ ca manaḥ prāṇau saṃyojya viṣu.... (l9)
 ....... prā(pra)ṇavaṃ viṣuvaṃ khyātaṃ śiṣyasvamanasorapīti kandamūlādhāre vidyamānakamalaṃ dadyāt| tanūccārataḥ 
mantroccāraṇāt| nādākhyai vicintanāt| nāde dhvanimadhye (p117b, l1)
.........tmanaḥ aikyasvarūpadhyānāt| manuviṣuḥ mantraviṣuḥ dvitīyaḥ uktaṃ ca| ātmano nāda-madhye tu layaṃ 
sañcintya kandataḥ akārokāravarṇādisaṃyogenābhiyogataḥ ...(hṛ)dayādi ...(ba)lānte (l2)
 ....... ntra-sañjukamiti| grandhi-cchedatadmeḍrādidaśagrandhibhedanāttanmadhyanādodgamāt sarva sāṃsamadhye suṣumnāyāḥ 
pradhānatvāt suṣumnāmadhyagatanādasya prakāśamānānnāḍīviṣuvaṃ (l3)
 tri(tṛ)tīyam uktaṃ ca meḍrādhve kandanābhī ca hṛdayaṃ kaṇṭhamurpikāṃ ghaṇṭikāgraṃ bhruvormadhyaṃ lalāṭaṃ brahmaṇo bilam| 
śaktiṃ ca dvādaśāntaṃ ca daśagrandhimanukramāt| mūlamantratriśūlena hitvā nādaḥ (l4)
 pra..........nmādanāḍī samāyogāttannāḍī viṣuvaṃ matamiti| atrāpi sarvāsāmeva nāḍīnāṃ madhye yāṃsaṃ vyavasthitā| 
suṣumnā nāma sā jñeyā nābheḥ śakteḥ śivaṃ gatā| tatra (l5)
 pravāhayennā.......ḍī viṣuvamucyateti| ādikalāt akārokāramakārabindunādāntākhyaṣaṭkalāt ṣaṭ tyaktvā 
hṛtkaṇṭhatālubhrūmadhyabiladvādaśāntākhyaṣaṭsthāneṣu brahmaviṣṇurudre(l6)
śvarasadāśivaanāhatākhyaṣaṭkāraṇeśvareṇāṃ tyāgāt saptame śaktau śaktisthāne tvātmano layaḥ praśāntaviṣuṃ caturtham 
uktaṃ ca akārokāravarṇau ca makāro bindu(l7)
saṃjñitaḥ nādanādāntasaṃjñau ca tyaktvā brahmādiṣu kramāt saptame śaktimadhye tu śiṣyātmānaṃ layaṃ nayet| praśāntaṃ 
tatva(vaṃ) jānīyāt praśāntendriyagocaramiti| śaktau śaktimadhye sadhvani (l8)
 sanādaḥ ūrdhvameva visaret| yadā calettadā śaktisaṃjñukaṃ viṣupañcamam| uktaṃ ca| śaktimadhyagato nādaḥ samanāntaṃ 
prasarpati tacchaktividu(ṣu)vaṃ proktamiti triṭu(truṭi)bhiḥ bhrūkṣepa-mātrapramāṇakāla(l9)
bhrūkṣepamātro yaḥ kālaḥ sa triṭibhiḥ parikīrtita ityuktatvāt tābhi truṭibhiḥ kalāsaptedvaṣṭadaśaprakalpya 
kalāṣoḍaśasaptenduḥ saptadaśaaṣṭadaśasaṅkhyākaṃ kṛtvā| śaktyeḥ śa..... (l10)
mārabhya nādāntaṃ samanāśaktiparyantaṃ tridhā parikalpya tat prāṇe prāṇasyaikamantro (p118a, l1)
 ....ṇa tridhākaraṇā tridhā bhūtapramāṇe'pyaparaparāparaparaiḥ apara iti parāpara iti| para iti kālāhvayaṃ kālanāmadheyaṃ 
viṣuvaṃ viṣumantrāyamarthaḥ ṣoḍaśatruṭyā.... (l2)
.....prāṇoccāre aparakālaḥ saptadaśatruṭyātmako vyāpinyantaḥ prāṇa cāro parāparaḥ kālaḥ aṣṭādaśatruṭyātmakaḥ 
samanāntaḥ prāṇacāra parākhyā paraḥ kālaḥ evaṃ viṣu(l3)
 kalavyomnanāyāṃ kālavirahitāyāṃ sakalakalāvyāpikāyāṃ layaḥ etatkālaviṣuvam| uktaṃ ca| ādyaḥ 
ṣoḍaśikāyāṃ tu prāṇasyānte vyavasthitaḥ kālo bhrūkṣepamātrastu mantrānte kīrtito (l4)
 mayā| aparaḥ parāpareḥ punareva tridhā kuru| aparāḥ ṣoḍaśo yāvat saptā(pta)daśa parāparaḥ tasmāt parastu yaḥ 
kālāḥ sa prayeṣṭādaśa smṛtaḥ| prāṇamevaṃ (l5)
 tridhā kālaṃ jñātvā caivaṃ tyajet punaḥ apaḥ śaktimūrdhvāsto vyāpinyantaṃ dvitīyakam| tri(tṛ)tīyaṃ 
samanātantukālaṃ viṣuvamucyateti| tatparaṃ samanāśakteḥ param unmanyante (l6)
 vidyamānaṃ dhṛvaṃ vṛttavṛtaṃ paraśivasthānaṃ paraśivasya sathānabhūtaṃ cidvaktikṛt ātmavyaktikārakaṃ tatvākhyaṃ 
tatvanāmadheyaṃ viṣuvaṃ saptamaṃ bhavet| unmanyantataḥ proktaṃ (l7)
 tatvaṃ ....... sajñukamiti| uktatvāt unmanāyāḥ parasminnityaniṣkale paraśive śiṣyātmasaṃyojanaṃ 
tatvaviṣuvamityarthaḥ||77||

tasmin tat sudhayā (l8)
 dhya......kalaśā nāḍī sudaṇḍāṃ svahṛtpadmaśvabhragalordhvatālusuṣirā bindusṛvaksvāmṛtā| sṛgenāntaragāvagā eta 
ghṛtāpātena saṃyojayet| (l9)
 ...... ñcāvarapūrṇayā kṣaṇaka-lālakṣyaṃ tathopādayet| ātmasṛgbhāvanāpūrvaṃ tatvaviṣuve śiṣyātmayojanārthaṃ 
pūrṇāhutimāha| tasmin iti| nāḍīdaṇḍā (l10)
 madhyanāḍyuktaśobhanadaṇḍā sṛkda(gda)ṇḍa eva madhyanāḍī ityarthaḥ sṛk hṛtpadma-śvabhragalo (p118b, [number 
110 mentioned by pen in the middle of the folio.] l1)
 .........sucirākaṇṭhatālupathavyaktarandhrā bindusravat svāmṛtāt| bindusthānārtatarau svahṛtpadmaṃ svaṃ hṛdayakamalaṃ śvabhraṃ 
sṛkavedibilaṃ tena yuktavedabilameva yathā(l2)
kramaṃ hṛtkamalamityarthaḥ| galordhvatālusuṣirākaṇṭhatālupathavyaktarandhrā bindusṛvak svāmṛtāt bindusthānāt 
prakrāntaprasarat parasu-(śu)dhārayuktā evaṃ sṛgevāntabhāvagā (l3)
 antarabhāvanāṃ prāptā bhrūrūpatvenāntaraṃ bahiranukriyata iti bhāvaḥ evaṃ sṛgdhyānāttasmin cidvyaktikṛt paraśivasthāne 
punarāvṛttirahite dhṛve (l4)
 sat sudhayāḍya-kandakalaśāt śobhanāt amṛtasaṃpūrṇasṛgdaṇḍamūle vidyamānakalaśakandāt ghṛtapātena kandānnirgate 
varāmṛtabhāvanāyuktaghṛtadhāra(l5)
patena saṃyojayet| śiṣyātmānaṃ sandhayet uttakaṃ(uktaṃ) ca| pūrakaṃ kumbhakaṃ kṛtvā sarvadvārāṇi śodhayet| dvinādaḥ 
saṃbhāvayenmantraṃ prakriyāṃ mūla(l6)
māśritaḥ divyākaraṇamāsthāya parabhāvasamanvitaḥ śiṣyātmakumbhavahnibhyo maṇḍalādapi ṣaḍvidhā saṃgrāhyādhvānamāropya 
prāṇanāḍī tata(taḥ) sṛci| agre (l7)
 gurūkta....de mantramuccārayet kramāt| mūlamantratriśūlena bhitvā grandhīnanukramāt| prāsādādhvā śikhāntasthadvimānaṃ 
calayāvadhi| nītvā tyaktvā mānasaṃ vai vyāpa....... (l8)
 bodharūpayuk| śiṣye ṣaḍguṇasaṃpūrṇe cidānandasvare paraiḥ dhyāyanvitānamātmānaṃ śanaiḥ pūrṇāṃ nipātayan| tāvattiṣṭhet 
kumbhakena (p119a, l1)
 na yāvat sarpiṣasthitiḥ sthairyārthaṃ yojikāyāśca punaḥ pūrṇāhutiṃ kṣipet||78||

sṛṣṭhyāgnau ca niyojya tatra paradhā(yā) śiṣyasya cidvyaktikaṃ sarvajñatvasvatṛpya.... (l2)
miti svātantryasaṃjñāni ca| śaktipūrtimanīmanantaviṣayāṃ ṣaṭkaṃ guṇānānvitaścāpādyānalataḥ śivena sadṛśaṃ śiṣye 
tadā yojayet| śiṣyacaitanyasya sarvajñatvādiṣaḍguṇapada.... (l3)
ha| sṛṣṭyeti| śiṣyasya pūrvoktavat pūrṇāhutiparyantaṃ dīkṣitasya cidvyaktikaṃ dṛkkriyātmakacaitanyasya vyaktisvarūpaṃ 
parayā (l4)
 utkṛṣṭayā sṛṣṭāsṛṣṭimārgeṇa śiṣyacaitanyākarṣaṇenātmanaḥ mūlasthānādārabhya dvādaśāntaparyantaṃ nītvā punaḥ 
dvādaśāntāt ghaṇṭikāsthānamānīya (l5)
 nāsikādvāreṇa puṣpāñjaligataṃ dhyātvā tatrāgnau kuṇḍe vidyamānaśivāgnau niyojya saṃyojya(ja)naṃ kṛtvā sraveṇa 
tisṛbhistisṛbhirāhutibhiḥ sava(rva)jñatvasutṛptyānādikaṃ (l6)
 sarvajñatāparitṛptayuktā anādibodhatāsvātantryasaṃjñāni ca svatantraśaktyā pūrtimiti śaktimalupta-
śaktikāmanantaviṣayāmanantaśaktikāmiti evaṃ prakāreṇa (l7)
 karṣakamāpādya saṃpātayitvā naikasargakaguṇā nāmanādimalapāśakadatthitatvādutpatterjanayitvetyarthaḥ nanu iti 
padacchedanāt paścādagnau pūrṇā(l8)
hutyā ṣaḍguṇokte janānantaramitaḥ analataḥ| āvāhitaṣāḍguṇānvitāgnikāśādū(du)ddhṛtyeti śeṣaḥ tadatasmin kāle 
śivena svabhāvādanādipāśarahitaśivena sadṛśaṃ (l9)
 ....naḥ ṣaḍguṇaṃ śiṣye yojayet| saṃpātayet| 

tathā mṛgendre - dīkṣāpaṭalānte| guṇānāpādya tatraiva siddhānāhutibhistisṛbhiḥ svatantrādividherdāsyādicchindyaiva 
(p119b, l1)
 vipaścitimiti||79||

atha ācāryābhiṣekavidhiḥ| siktaḥ pāśavimocako guruvaro ratnauṣadhī bījakaiḥ pūrṇairbhūtaghaṭai(ṭaiḥ) 
sthirāsanakalāklipataiḥ sa pañcāvṛtaiḥ| (l2)
 sabrahmāṅgaśivārcanājjapayutairdīkṣāpratiṣṭhādike yogya(yaḥ) syāt guruṇā śivāgravihitaprāptājñayokta-vṛtaḥ| tripadārthaṃ 
catuṣpādabhuktimuktiphalapradaṃ saṃhitāṃ (l3)
 yo vijānādi(ti) śiṣyo'sau saikamarhatītyuktatvāt| evaṃ guṇaśīlasaṃpannasya śiṣyasyācāryābhiṣeka-vidhimāha| 
sikta iti| ratnauṣadhībījakaiḥ ratnādipañca(l4)
ratnāni tathā coktaṃ māṇikkaṃ caiva vaiḍūryaṃ muktāphalaṃ pravālakam indranīlaṃ ca ratnānāṃ pañcakaṃ parikīrtitam| 
ratnaśabdena svarṇādilohaharitālādi-(l5)
dhātaśca gṛhītāḥ oṣadhayaḥ saha devyādayaḥ| 

tathā mṛgendre - saha devī tathā brāhmī trāyantī lakṣmaṇāvacaḥ śaṅkhaṣvabalā bījā jyotiṣu(ṣū)tyaparājitā| 
viṣa........... (l6)
balā moṭā bilvadhātryabhayāmṛtā| śatāsvā śārvarī dūrvā kuśavyāghrī punarṇaveti| bījāni śālisiddhārthaṃ yavādini 
etaiḥ pūrṇaiḥ pratikalaśaṃ pūritaiḥ bhūtaghaṭaiḥ (l7)
 pañcakumbhaiḥ paścimottarayāmyapūrveśānādidikṣu saṃsthāpitaiḥ punaḥ kiṃbhūtai(taiḥ) sthirāsane ādhāraśaktyādisamagrakā-
sanena bāhyādidhānyasaptakāsanena ca (l8)
 kalābhiḥ yaṅā krameṇa parikalpyate sa pañcāvṛtaiḥ pratyekaṃ pañcāvaraṇasahitaiḥ sa brahmāṅgaśivārcanajapayutaiḥ brahmabhiḥ 
sadyādipañcabrahmabhiryathākrameṇābhimantraiḥ (p120a, l1)
 aṅgaśivārcanayā pratyekam aṅgasahitaśivapūjayā japena pratyekaṃ mūlamantrasāṣṭaśatajapena yutaiḥ kumbhaiḥ siktaḥ 
abhiṣiktaḥ tathā coktam| paścimaṃ sadyamantreṇa vāme .... (l2)
ttaratasthitaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ bahurūpeṇa nareṇa prāgvivasthitam| īśānenaiśamālabhya sarvān sāmbena śambhunā iti guṇā 
śivāśramavihitaprāptājñayā śivāgre stha....... (l3)
lamaṇḍalādisthitaparameśvarasannidhau vihitayā evamuttaratra kāryaṃ kurvatīkatayā| tathoktam| tvayādhikāraṇāvatsa 
gurupāraṃparāgatam| nityaṃ naimittikaṃ ka......(kartavyaṃ) (l4)
 sadasirdaśavāsinā| adyaprabhṛti kartavyaṃ sivicārya śivājñayā iti prāptājñayā śiṣyeṇa tatheti pratijñātagurvājñaya 
uktavrataḥ guruṇoktavratācāra(l5)
yuktaḥ| 

tathā coktaṃ svāyambhuve - athācāryavrataṃ śreṣṭhaṃ caredvācārya eva hi sadyādiśivaparyantamabhyaset| mantrasaṃhitāṃ 
pakṣatrayamadhaḥśāyi (l6)
 naktāśī carubhuk sadā maurī(nī) triṣavaṇastāyī dvandvānudvignasaṃyamī| śuklāmbaradharaḥ śāntaḥ 
śuklamālyānulepanaḥ| yogapaṭṭākṣasūtrī ca mauñjyā(l7)
jinapavitradhṛk| japaṃ ca pratyahaṃ kuryādanudvigna(ghna)sahasrakam| homaṃ pratidinaṃ kuryāt japānte śaktitaḥ kramāt 
ghṛtena vāghṛtāktairvā tilai(laiḥ) kṣīreṇa taṇḍulairiti| (l8)
 evamabhiṣiktaḥ śiṣyaḥ dīkṣā pratiṣṭhādike| dīkṣāyāṃ liṅgapratiṣṭhā pavitrādikaraṇe yogyaḥ syāt bhavet 
pāśavimocakaṃ (l9)
 malādipāśa...raṇadakṣa syāt| ata eva guruvaro bhavet guruvaraśabdena sarvavaśānāṃ dīkṣākaraṇena yogya..... 
(p120b, l1)
 siddhaṃ bhavet sthāpane prokṣaṇe caiva (l2)
 prāyaścittābhiṣecane| vyākhyānādau ca śāstre syāt svārthe vātha parārthake sarvadevārcane viprāstvādiśaivagurūttamaḥ 
viprādayostayorvarṇaḥ dīkṣāyā sthāpa(l3)
ne'pi ca| brāhmaṇakṣatriyādīnāṃ kṣatriyaśūdravaiśyayoḥ vaiśyaśūdrasya dīkṣāyāṃ sva sva jātāvapīṣyate svārthe'ṣṭau 
calaliṅgasya pratiṣṭhāyāṃ trayastvime| śūdro'pi śūdradīkṣāyāṃ (l4)
 svārthe tu calasaṃsthite bāṇaliṅgekṣaṇo vāpi sthāpayo yadi naiṣṭhikaḥ sanādākhilamantreṣu śivajñāne viśeṣataḥ 
śūdrasyāpi gṛhasthasya nityaiṣṭhāvaiva (l5)
 yogyatā tatrāpi nādarahitairmantraireva prakīrtitāḥ abhiṣeke tvayogyo'yaṃ kiṃ punaḥ sthāpanādiṣviti||80|| 

bindostasthamaheśvarāccha vakulaṃ gāyatri(l6)
śaktyaṅkitaṃ vidyeśādisuratramādudavahadadūrvāsaseha kṣitau| śiṣyādestatadanugrahodayajaṣo varṣe'bhavat bhārate 
śrīmanmukhyakadambabhūṣaṇayutaṃ (l7)
 cāmarmasajyāśramam| śivāgamavihitadīkṣāyāṃ viśeṣādhikāritvadyotanārthaṃ sarve pādāna?bindorārabhya 
śaivakulakramamāha| bindoriti śuddhādhvopādānatvaṃ (l8)
 na bindoḥ tasthamaheśvarāt sthāvarajaṅgamātmakajagatyutpattyarthaṃ bindukṣepāt sadāśivāt ata eva bindvādito 
niṣkala iti dyotitaḥ jagadutpatyarthaṃ vida....vindāle...ra eva bi.....dito niṣkala iti dyo....ditaḥ jaga.....tyata 
ciddoho? (l9)
 bhavatītyarthaḥ ciddehavatā sadāśivena bindukṣobhakāle gāyatryā śaktyā cinhi(hni)taṃ bindukṣobhaṃ kāle parā 
baindavaśarīriṇo vidyeśādutpanna(l10)
sadāśivastu niṣkalaśivājjātaḥ ādisurakramāt| vidyeśādananteśādikramataḥ ananteśena brahmāṇḍamadhye 
kailāsādhipatitvena śrīkaṇṭha......(ṭhādru) (p121a, l1)
dro jātaḥ śrīkaṇṭhādviṣṇuḥ, viṣṇoḥ brahmā, brahmā brahmaṇaḥ, indrādayaḥ sarvadevāḥ utpannāḥ punarapi śrīkaṇṭhāt| 
durvāsāḥ jātaḥ evaṃ krameṇa kṣitau bhūloke durvāsasadāśiva(l2)
kalamudbhavaṃ jātam| tathoktaṃ jñānaśivācāryeṇa agrāhi kila īśvaraḥ paraśivāttasmāt sadeśaḥ śivo nādātmā 
sakalākalaḥ prabhurato'nanteśabhaṭṭādikaḥ tasmādaṇḍaja(l3)
viṣṇuvāsavavidhiśrīkaṇṭharudraḥ tatastantrāṇāmavatārako munivaro durvāsanāmā gururiti| tatrāpi bhūloke bhāratavarṣe 
bharatakhaṇḍe tadanugrahodayayajuṣaḥ durvāsasā (l4)
 vihitadīkṣāyuktasya ṛṣyādeḥ kāśikaḥ kāśyapāgastyabhāradvājagautamādināmā śabdena sanatkumāra-sanaka-
sanātana-sanantādinā bhāratavarṣe bharatakhaṇḍe (l5)
 bhūloke jambudvīpe madhyasthitamaruparvatasya dakṣiṇapradeśe sthita-himagire dakṣiṇabhāgo bhāratavarṣa iti 
sahitamukhyakadambabhūṣaṇayutā kadambavṛkṣālaṅkārasahitam āmardakasaṃjñāśramam āmardaka.....miti| vihitanāmadheyena 
tapovanamabhavat| (l6)
 jātā cakāreṇānukta-maṭhāntaraṃ sūcitaṃ prathamamāmardakamaṭhasthānaṃ durvāsasā sthāpitaṃ sthānamāmardakaṃ nāma 
śrīdurvāsaniṣevita u(i)tyuktatvāt dūrvāsā(l7)
nāṃ grahapūrvakamāmardakamaṭhanivāsinaḥ kauśikakāśyapādayaḥ dakṣiṇāmūrti-rūpaśrīkaṇṭhamāgamajñānārthamupāsitavantaḥ 
śrīkaṇṭhādāgamajñānaṃ labdhvā mardakamaṭha(l8)
sthitavantaḥ eteṣāṃ śiṣyāḥ sanatkumārādayaścatvāro jātāḥ tasmādāmardakamaṭhāttrīṇi maṭhāni jātāni| raṇabhadraṃ 
golakī puṣpagirīti tasmādvinirgataṃ sthānaṃ (p121b, l1)
 raṇa..........la... tathā puṣpagiriśceti uktatvāt| āmardakādīnāṃ maṭhānāṃ dakṣiṇāmūrtirūpaṃ tu munayaḥ samupāsate 
agastya-kauśikaścaiva bhāradvājo'tha gautamaḥ| ....... (l2)
.....parameśānādāgamārthaṃ ca tatvataḥ jñātvā saṃprītamanasaḥ te sarve munayastathā teṣāṃ śiṣyāḥ sanantādyāḥ 
munayastānupāsire| te labdhāgamārthaṃ tu māṭhāpatyena (l3)
 .... sthitāḥ| āmardakamaṭhapatiḥ sanatkumārako muniḥ| kadambakavṛkṣaṃ syāt| tasya sāntānikāśca ye 
puṣpagirimaṭhapatiḥ sanako vṛkṣamiti arjunaṃ tasya sāntānikā kecit (l4)
 .... lakimaṭhanāyakaḥ sanātano ṛṣiḥ vṛkṣaḥ jambukastasya santatiḥ| raṇabhadrādhipaḥ proktaḥ sanantana iti 
smṛtaḥ nyagrodhavṛkṣa iti proktaḥ tasya sāntānikāśca ye godāvari naditīre (l5)
 śaivānāṃ vasatiḥ śubhaḥ puramandharakālindyāṃ tasyāṃ vasati śaṅkaraḥ utpannaḥ tasya śaivānāmācāryāṇāṃ viśeṣataḥ tatra 
mandharakālīśamabhitaścacurmaṭhān (l6)
 āmardakī puṣpagiri golaki karamabhadrakīti|| nanu kauśikaḥ kāśyapādinā kathaṃ śivasṛṣṭitvaṃ loke tāvat 
brahmasṛṣṭitvena prasiddhatvāt satyaṃ prathamaṃ śivasṛṣṭitvena utpannā kauśika (l7)
kāśyapādayo'nye brahmasṛṣṭitvenotpannā kāśikakāśyapādayaśtavapare tveke nāmadheyena samāna-kauśikakāśyapādayaḥ 
śivabrahmasṛṣṭichedena dvidhā bhavanti| atra śivasṛṣṭi(l8)
tvena kāśyapādayaḥ śaivasaṃbandhina iti na doṣaḥ| 

tathā kāmike – gotranirṇaye| kauśikaḥ kāśyapaścaiva bhāradvājo'tha gautamaḥ atravasiṣṭhaścāgastyo jamadagniśca 
(p122a, l1)
 bhārgavaḥ āṅgiraścāmanuścānyo dvividhāste prakīrtitāḥ śivasṛṣṭisamopetā munayasta-dvivarjitāḥ śivasṛṣṭyānvitā 
ete pañcavaktreṣu dīkṣitāḥ kauśikādyāśca munayaḥ .......sarvatra (l2)
 dīkṣitāḥ iti tathā tatraiva arcanāṅgapaṭale| śivasṛṣṭivinā ye tu jāyante brahmaṇo mukhāt te sāmānyā na teṣāṃ 
tu parārthe jyādhikāriti||81||

tatratyā munipaṅktikā ca (l3)
 .....japaitā mahākhye sthitā prājāpatyamukhāśca gocarayugodyuktā babhūvastataḥ ādyanteṣu ca śaucasaṃyamayatā 
sadgocarādudyayau| śrīmaddhyāpakaśambhupūrvaguru(l4)
yu... santānamevaṃ sthitam| ekavidhasyāmardakamaṭhasya cāturvidhyā kalājñānapūrvakaṃ svasantānakramamāha| tatratyeti| 
tatratyāmardakamaṭhasaṃpradāyotpannā munipaṅkti(l5)
........ (kā)śyapādiṛṣisamūha-paitāmahākhye svāyambhuvanāmadheyamanuḥ manuriti saṃjñayā jāto manujñāḥ manurityarthaḥ 
caturdaśamanu-prathamabhūtasvāyambhuvamanuḥ (l6)
.....ye sthitā ṛṣisaṃjñayā vidyamānā svāyambhuvamanukālaparyantamāmardamaṭha-ṛṣisaṃpradāyena sthityetyarthaḥ 
svāyambhuvarociṣyaktamastā samastāreva taścā...... (l7)
.....ścaiva vaisvata iti smṛtaḥ sūryadakṣabrahmadharmarudrasāvarṇayasta.........rocyā bhavya ime proktā manuvaśca caturdaśeti| 
tata(taḥ) svāyambhuvamanukālasamāptyanantaraṃ ...... (p122b, l1)
.....ṣamanukāle prājāpatyamukhāḥ prājāpatya iti nāmadheya..........kā gocarayugodyuktāḥ gocarāṇāṃ sva saṃpradāya-
bhra.......śānā ....yuge āmardakamaṭhādi... (l2)
...rvidhasaṅkhyāke ityuktasaṃpradāyāḥ prasiddhāḥ babhūvuḥ sañjātā..... rociṣamanukāle āmardakādicaturvidhasāṃpradāyabhūtāḥ 
babhūvurityarthaḥ catura-maṭheṣūt.. (l3)
..(pa)nnāḥ śaivabrāhmaṇāścaturvidhā bhavanti uktaṃ ca| sthānamātrardakaṃ nāma śrī dūrvāsaniṣevitam| tasmādvinirgataṃ 
sthānaṃ raṇarudraṃ ca golaki tathā puṣpagiri(l4)
śceti sthānamevaṃ caturvidham| etat sthānasamudbhūtāḥ śaivāste ca caturvidhāḥ brahmacāri gṛhastaśca vānaprastho 
yatistviti| svasantānakramamāha| ādityādityādinā (l5)
 .....ṣu caturvidhamaṭhasāṃpradāyeṣu śaucasaṃyamayutāt| brāhmabhūtamalasnānādikaṃ śaucasaṃyamaḥ indriyanigrahaḥ 
saṃ....maśabdoyaṃ niyamādīnāmupalakṣaṇaḥ| (l6)
 etaiḥ sahitān sadgocarāt kāśyapapāradvājādigotrakasahitādādyādāmardakamaṭhāt kaliyugaṃ prārambhakāle 
śrīmadhyāpakaśambhuriti nāmadheya(l7)
yuktaḥ bhuktimuktiprado mahāguruḥ udyayau jāto'bhūt| evaṃ pūrvaguruyuk pūrvakālo jātagurupāramparyasahitaṃ santānaṃ 
vaṃśakramaṃ sthitamanena prakāreṇa (p123a, l1)
 svajananakālaparyantajāta-miti| uktaṃ ca| atra pūrvakathāprasaṅgaḥ rājendracola ityākhyaḥ colabhūpo mahī vasan 
gaṅgāsnānārthamāgatya dṛṣṭvā śaivavarāṃ tadā| snātvā (l2)
 pratinivṛttaḥ san tān samādāya śaivakān svarājye sthāpayāmāsa śaivācāryavarān tataḥ| kāñcīmadhye colabhūmau 
sarvatraiva pravistarā iti||82||

||iti siddhāntasārāvalyāṃ kriyāpādaḥ samāptaḥ ||

śubhamastu| śrī jñānāmbāsameta śrī kālahastīśvarābhyāṃ namaḥ| vedādrināthāya namaḥ marundīśvarāya namaḥ| 
remaining portion blank. (p123b, l1)
 ......nābhyadha utthitastu nilayo nāmaṅkarāṇāṃ ca tanmadhye dhvādyasahasrasaparinadheścordhvaṃ śarīre sti(sthi)tāḥ| 
piṅgākalavinirgatā pratayati dvādakṣe ca vāme ...laṃ na.... (l2)
 sāraṃ dhṛtayugāśrite bahi(hī)radhaste dvādaśāntāt sthite| athedānīṃ yogapāda-vaktukāmastadarthaṃ 
suṣumnādisarvanāḍi(ḍī)nāmutpatti nāraṇabhūtatvāt .....ucyate| (l3)
 nābhyadhaḥ atra nābhyadhaḥ iti nābhisamīpasthādhaḥpradeśo na viva(va)kṣitaḥ kintu nābheddūrasthādhaḥpradeśo'pi 
vivakṣitaḥ nābhikamantu kandādātpannamiti jñāpanāt (l4)
 sakando nābhyadhaḥ utthita ityuktamanyathā kandasya meḍhrādhapradeśotpattatvāt nābhyadha iti vyarthaṃ syāt 
kandasthāna(naṃ) manuṣyāṇāṃ dehamadhyaṃ navāṅgulam (l5)
 ityuktatvāt| 

tathā coktaṃ siddhāntaśekhare - pāyośva(śca) dvyaṅgulādūrdhvaṃ liṅgācca dvyaṅgulādadhaḥ| madhyamekāṅgulaṃ tratradehamadhyaṃ 
pracakṣate| navāṅgulordhvatastasmāt (l6)
 astikandordhvasannibhaḥ caturaṅgulavistāra-mutsedhenābhitatsamaḥ tvaṅguṣṭahṛṣītaḥ kandastanmadhye nābhirucyate tasmāt 
tasmāt cakraṃ samubhū(dbhū)taṃ dvandvaśārayutaṃ samāñjīvobhra(l7)
tī cakre'smin prāṇārūḍhasvakarmaṇā tridvistaṃ tu maye cakre yathā bhṛtti lūtitācakreṇānena deho'yaṃ vṛddhitaḥ 
saṃpravartate kuṇḍaliniparitaḥ tasmāt kanda(l8)
kuṇḍalikṛtā aṣṭaprakṛtirūpā sā suptasarpasamākṛtiḥ| kandanābheradhorandhre nidhāya sva phaṇāṃ dṛḍhaṃ 
sanniruddhamarutmārgān pucche sumukhaṃ tadā| brahmara(p124a, l1)
ndhre saṃveṣṭyā pṛṣṭhasthā ca sahasthiteti| asmin grandhe(the) navāṅgulordhvatastasmādityatra tasmāddehamadhyādūrdhvata 
uparibhāge navāṅgulaḥ kandastiṣṭhatītyarthaḥ tanmadhye nābhi-rucyate ityatra (l2)
 kandamadhye chidrākāreṇa nābhistiṣṭhatītyarthaḥ nāḍyaṅkurāṇāṃ sarvanāḍyaṅkuropa(tpa)ttikānāṃ nilayaḥ| 
āvāsasthānabhūtaḥ tanmadhye kandasya madhye adhvamārgaḥ kuharamastītyarthaḥ (l3)
 tathā coktamūlālavālakuharāduditabhavāni nirbhidya ṣaṭ sarasijāni taṭillate veti dvi sahasrasaptataḥ nāḍi(ḍī)nāṃ 
dvisahasrasaṅkhyādhikasaptatisahasram adhaścordhva... (l4)
 dehamadhya(yā)dadhaḥpradeśamūrdhva-pradeśaṃ vyāpakaśarīre sarvaprāṇināṃ kandādvinirgatā nāḍyadhastāttiryagūrdhvataḥ| 
dvisaptatasahasrāṇi nāḍyā syuḥ sarvadehi(l5)
nāmiti| etāsā(sāṃ) nāḍīnāṃ madhye dvātriṃśannāḍyaḥ mukhyā syuḥ| tāsu ca suṣumnā piṅgalā iḍā ceti tisro 
nāḍyaḥ mukhyā syuḥ mukhyatvāt (l6)
 suṣumnādī nāḍīnāṃ vyāptimāha kandavinirgatā kandādutpannā piṅgalā piṅgalānāḍī pṛthak bhinnatvena kandotpannā 
iḍānāḍī dakṣe (l7)
 kandamadhyādvinirgatā suṣumnāyāḥ dakṣiṇabhāge piṅgalasthitā iḍā vāmabhāge bilāt brahmabilaparyantaṃ sthitā uktaṃ 
ca| nāḍiśāstreṣu māyā(l8)
.....vāme piṅgalā dakṣiṇe sthiteti| iḍāpiṅgale brahmarandhraparyantaṃ kathaṃ sthitetyāha| iḍāpiṅgale bahiḥ dehe 
bāhyapradeśe dṛśyamātre.... (p124b, l1)
 sā randhrayugāśrite nāsikāyārandhrayuge kuharayuge āśrite suṣumnānāḍigate piṅgale iḍā nāḍinā sā randhraparyantaṃ 
pṛthak............. (l2)
dhvasuṣumnānāḍimāśratetyarthaḥ dvādaśāntākaśikhāntāt adho bha(bhā)ge sthite dvādaśāntaṃ tyaktvā brahmarandhraparyantaṃ 
suṣumnayā sahasthitetyarthaḥ||118||

yidāṅguṣṭhayugārcavastikamalāt dvaidhaikadhā madhyataḥ paṅktyādhārakanāḍikoparigatā syādvādaśāntāvadhiḥ| 
pastirvedasirādalāmbujayutaṃ kandaṃ ca ṣaṭpatrago (l4)
 nābhiḥ paṅktidalāhṛdaṣṭadalayukkaṇṭhaḥ kalāpatragā| tālurviṃśativedapatrakamale bhūrvedapatrasvaro randhrābjaṃ kaparaṃ 
navacchadajaṃbordhve sahasra(l5)
cchadaṃ yuktaṃ śaktisukesaraiḥ samanayā satkarṇikāḍyāṃ raveḥ somāgnyo svakabimbakaṃ tadupari vyāpyunmanānte 
śivaḥ|| suṣumnāya vyāptipūrvakaṃ (l6)
 mūlādhārādārabhya dvādaśāntaparyantaṃ sthitakamaladalasaṅkhyākrameṇa paraśivāsanabhūtakamalasvarūpaṃ yagmenāha|| padāṅguṣṭhe 
tadvipaṭhādanā (l7)
 pādāṅguṣṭhayugāt dakṣiṇavāmapādāṅguṣṭhadvandvādārabhyavastikamavākkandasthā nādatha sthitasthānaṃ vastirityucyate tatra 
sthitakamalaparyantaṃ dvedhā dīprakāntā (l8)
 madhyataḥ| dehamadhyabhṛtakandasthānādārabhya ūrdhvaṃ brahmabilāntam ekadhā ekarūpā paṅktyādhārakanāḍikā paṅktyādhārataḥ 
pantyā vakṣyamāṇākāre ....diṣu (p125a, l1)
 sukhyāvāsthitādhārādidaśāntacakrāṇi ādhārasvādhiṣṭhānamaṇipūrakāhataviśuddhyā jñānākhyāni paryantāni tadūrdhvaṃ 
catvāri eva| daśa sadyo (l2)
 nigatati vyāpya sthitā nāḍikā suṣumnākhyā nāḍidvādaśāntāvadhi dvādaśāntaparyantamuparigatādūrdhvaṃ gatā 
brahmarandhrāntaṃ sakalarūpā| tadūrdhvaṃ dvādaśāntaparyantaṃ niṣkalarūpā (l3)
 sakalaśāstrajñairbrahmanāḍītyucyamānā suṣumnā dvādaśāntaṃ tiṣṭhatītyarthaḥ| vastiḥ kandādadhasthānamādhāramityarthaḥ| 
vedasirādalāmbujayutā vedasirayā (l4)
 caturnā sahitā vedālāmbujayutā catuṣpatrayuktakamalena sahitāvastiḥ kandaḥ ṣaṭhai patrayuktaḥ tasmādupari nābhiḥ 
patmanti dalā daśapatrasahitāgrat hṛdayamaṣṭadalayuk (l5)
 akṣapatrayuktaṃ kaṇṭhaḥ kalāpatrakaḥ ṣoḍaśaparṇasahitaḥ tāluḥ viṃśati vedapatrakamalaṃ viṃśativedāni 
viṃśatisaṅkhyāyuktacatuḥ saṅkhyākāni caturviṃśati (l6)
 saṅkhyānītyarthaḥ| etasaṅkhyākapatrairyuktakamalasahitaṃ tālusthānamucyate| bhṛṅaḥ bhrūśabdenātra bhrūmadhyamucyat| vedapatra-
catuṣparṇā sahitā (l7)
 randhrābjaṃ brahmarandhrasti(sthi)takamalaṃ svaraḥ ṣoḍaśapatrasahitaṃ ka paraṃ ka śiraḥ ...srākparaṃ ūrdhve vidyamānaṃ 
navacchadakajaṃ navasaṅkhyākaparṇayuktakamalam ūrdhve dvādaśānte (l8)
 saha....cchadasahasrasaṅkhyāyuktaputrasahitakamalaṃ tiṣṭhati| tatkamalasvarūpamāha| śaktisukesaraiḥ 
....mādinavaśaksvarūpakiñjalkaiḥ yuktaṃ sahitaṃ samanayā samanākhyā (p125b, l1)
 ....satkarṇa-kāḍyaṃ pañcāśadbījayuktakarṇikayā sahitaṃ rave sūryasya somaryo candravabhyoḥ svarasi bimbagaṃ 
svādhipasahitaṃ maṇḍalayuktaṃ tamupari maṇḍalatraya..... (l2)
 ......pari ūrdhve vyāpi sarvatra vyāpakaḥ| unmanānte unmanīśaktyante unmanye vātra śaktimaṇḍalaṃ bhavati śivaḥ 
paraśivastiṣṭhati||3||

kuṇḍaveṣṭitagehamadhyakalite paṭṭādikecchā(l3)
sane svasthaprāgrajupūrvakayagalakoru....dvijo recayet| śuddhau nāḍiṣu mandamandamudaraṃ saṃpūrayedvāmataḥ 
sendorbāhyasamīraṇena mihiraistālaḥ (l4)
 kramāt kumbhakam| kṛtvā śaktivaśādvirecayadidaṃ piṅgārkavāhāttathā nirgarbhaḥ satato japaṃ ca kurute khānāṃ sagarbha 
punaḥ| dhyānājjāpayutāt (l5)
 sa hanti sakalaṃ pāpaṃ niśodhyaṃ punaḥ prātaḥ sāyamahastha eṣa kurute tatvādi sākṣāt kṛtim|| 
yogābhyāsahetubhūtaṃ nāḍiśuddhiṃ ...gnenāha|| (l6)
 kuṇḍeti sandehayogyānusāraṃ vātādiśītanivāraṇārtham ekaprakāreṇa dvi tri prakāreṇa vā āveṣṭitagehamadhyakalite 
samakṣi dveṣṭitā madhya(l7)
pradeśe kṛte paṭṭādikena mukulacelājinādicchāditecchācasane svecchayā vihitapīṭhādau svasthaḥ sukhena sthitaḥ| 
uktaṃ ca| hitajīrṇāśan svastati krīṇḍadare? (l8)
 kṣite gṛhe bādhāśūnye vanādau vā svāsanastha udaṅmukha iti| paṭṭākṛtinā vartulakṛtinā vā vihitapīṭhādau sthitaḥ 
atrāsanaśabdena (p126a, l1)
 padmasvastikādini sābhyastā nigṛhītānī uktaṃ ca| svastiṃ gomukhaṃ padmaṃ bhadramāyūrakukkuṭe vi(vī)rasiṃhāsanaṃ 
kūrmamardhapi(pī)ṭhaṃ daśa kramāditi| siddhāntaśekhare| (l2)
 lakṣau tatraivoktamityanusandheyam| prāgajupūrvakāyagalakaḥ prātaḥkāle avakrākāreṇa sthitordhvaśarīrakaraṇasahitaḥ 
dvijaḥ dvija-śabdenātra dīkṣāyuktaḥ (l3)
 ālasyādirahita ucyate| uktaṃ ca| ālasyādivinirmukto gurudeveṣu bhaktimān| nispṛhaḥ sarvaviṣaye paraṃ 
vairāgyamāśritaḥ ityādiguṇasampanno (l4)
 nāḍīśuddhau praśasyata iti lagne sumuhūrte prathamāraṃbhaviṣayaḥ athavā lagnamamanana?saṃyamanayuktaḥ recayet recīkuryāt| 
uktaṃ ca| udayaḥ samīrasya (l5)
 recakāṃ devakā bahiḥ bāhyādāpūraṇādvāyoḥ pūrakapūraṇasaṃyamaḥ recayedudarāt samyak vāyu piṅgalayā śanaiḥ (l6)
 pūritasyodare vayo nirdhāraṇāt kumbhakasmṛtaḥ| iti nāḍīṣu nāḍinimittārthaṃ saṃśiddhau śodhanaṃ kartuṃ mandaṃ manda 
śanaiḥ śanaiḥ udaraṃ jaṭharaṃ vā mataṃ vāma.... (l7)
 sā pugena saṃpūrayet pūraṇaṃ kuryāt uktaṃ ca| iḍayā pūrayet samyak bāhyasthamanilaṃ tata iti 
sendorindusahitavāmabhāgasya bāhyasamīrami.... (p126b, l1)
pūritabāhyasthavāyunā mihiraiḥ dvāda...saṅkhyānaiḥ mihirāruṇapūṣaṇa iti sūryaparyāye uktatvāt tālaiḥ dvādaśamātrābhiḥ 
mātralakṣaṇaṃ dvividham| (l2)
 jānumaṇḍalasyaikatriparibhramaṇabhedena|

tathā coktaṃ siddhāntaśekhare - viṣṭayitvā tridhā jānumaṇḍalacchoṭikā sa kṛtya| kuryādveṣābhāvena mātrā kaniṣaṭhā 
kālamānataḥ āhūranyonyathā (l3)
 mātrā sakṛjjānupradakṣiṇaṃ choṭikāṃ ca sakṛtkuryādeṣā mātreti kāla iti atra tālairiti bahuvacanāt 
kaniṣṭā(ṣṭhā)dayo gṛhītāḥ dvādaśatālaiḥ (l4)
 kaniṣṭhāśca tu viṃśatyā madhyamaḥ aṣṭā catvāriṃśakyaḥ śreṣṭhaḥ| 

tathā coktaṃ sarvajñānottare - tālaṃ dvādaśakaṃ jñeyaṃ prāṇāyāmastu kanyasaḥ| madhyamaṃ ca caturviṃśa śreṣṭhasdha(sta)dviguṇo 
(l5)
 bhavediti kramāt kumbhakaṃ pūritavāyo pūraṇāt sarvajña tyāgasvarūpaṃ śaktivaśāt sāmarthyānusāreṇa 
kaniṣṭhādikālagrahaṇāt kṛtvāpigāt vāhāt (l6)
 piṅgalānāḍinārkamārgeṇa taṃ kumbhakaṃ virecayati [virecayati(rep.?)] virecanaṃ kurvati puruṣeṇa kumbhitaṃ recayedvāyuṃ 
śanaiḥ piṅgalayāṃ budha ityuktatvāni...rbhamantrasya (l7)
 dhyānam – nina(?)prāṇāyāmo nirgarbha ityucyate| 

tathā siddhāntaśekhare - agarbhaṃ ca sagarbhaṃ ca mantrahīno hyagarbhakaḥ sagarbho mantrasaṃyuktaḥ mantrāśca praṇavā...... 
(p127a, l1)
 prāsādaṃ japahaṃsāśca gāyatri vyāhṛti mukhā iti khānāmindiyāṇājñaya....rite uktaṃ ca| hantya garbho'pi 
deva(vā)nāṃ pa(?) balatvaṃ pradhāvatā?miti devānāmiti manaḥ sahita(l2)
yāṇāmityarthaḥ jāpayutāt mantrajāpasahitāt dhyānāt śivacintanāt prasiddhaḥ sagarbhaprāṇāyāmaḥ niśetthaṃ rātrikṛtaṃ 
sakalaṃ samastaṃ pāpaṃ hanti nāśayati (l3)
 pāratarniśākṛtaṃ pāpaṃ dinānte tu divākṛtamityuktatvāt punaḥ punaḥ divase yogasiddhipu(pa)ryantaṃ yogābhyāsaṃ 
kurvataḥ puruṣasya prātaḥ sāyamadhyamakastha prātaḥkālaḥ (l4)
 sāyaṃkālamadhyāhnakāleṣu kriyamāṇaḥ eṣa yogābhyāsaḥ tatvādi sākṣātkṛtitatvādīnāṃ pṛthivyādīnāṃ sākṣātkṛtiṃ 
pratyakṣeṇa tattatsvarūpatāṃ kurute (l5)
 yogābhyāsamācarataḥ puruṣasya dehe pṛthivyādisthitagandhakaṭhinyādiguṇān prakaṭīkarotītyarthaḥ pṛthvīsākṣātkāreṇa 
bāhyagandhe nispṛhaḥ sa (l6)
 bhavati agnisākṣātkāreṇā(ṇa) divyarūpaṃ jānāti vahnisantāpo nāsti vāyusākṣātkāreṇa dīpasparśa jānāti| 
aṇimādiguṇāṣṭakaṃ sidhyati (l7)
 gaganasākṣātkāreṇa divyajñānadivyomacāri ca bhavatītyādayo gunāḥ(ṇāḥ) yogābhyāsena bhavanti||5||

hṛdyomāmbukarṇikāt śaśabhṛt bimbāgniśaktyūrdhvakaṃ prāṇāyāmayuta.....ṇāla (p127b, l1)
......lasat sūtrapratikāśakaṃ jotista...tadgatama....so'pi parataḥ sva dvādaśāntasthitam| svaṃ cāmbhaḥ sadri(dṛ)śaprabhaṃ 
paraśivaṃ teyāni cidā paśyati| evaṃ pratidinaṃ yogābhyāsena saṃyuktaḥ ...... (l2)
 sva dvādaśāntasti(sthi)tā paraśivam| pratyakṣeṇa paśyatītyāha|| hṛdyometi yogī yogo nāma śivā tu 
noraikyasya prathamakālasaṃbaddhaḥ tādṛśayogayukto yogī prāṇātmayutiṃ (l3)
 pūrakakumbhakarecakasahitā yadhā(thā) bhavati tadhā(thā) yuktaḥ sa na hṛdvyāmāṃbujakarṇikārkaśaśabhṛt bimbāsti 
śaktūrdhvagaṃ hṛdvyomā-mbujasya hṛdayakuharakamalasya karṇikāyāṃ saṃsthitārkaḥ sūryaḥ (l4)
 śaśabhṛt candraḥ tayoḥ bimbayormaṇḍalayoruparividyamānaprakāśatvarūpajyotistejorūpaṃ hudayāmburuhasaṃsthaparaṃ 
jyotirūpaṃ parāpara-mityuktatvāt| tat (l5)
 gatamānasaḥ hṛdayakamalasthitajyotirūpaśive pratyakṣeṇa gatamānaḥ sa na parataḥ hṛdayakamalādūrdhvataḥ sva 
dvādaśāntasthitam ātmīyadvādaśānte vidyamānaṃ svacchābhaḥ sadṛśaprabhaṃ (l6)
 nirmalodakasamānakāntiṃ paraśivaṃ cidā jñānena paśyati pratyakṣeṇa dṛṣṭo bhavedityarthaḥ||6||

hṛtpadme prātararkodayanivahanibhaṃ madhyanāḍyāṃ (l7)
 vicintyaṃ madhyāhnebhrajasaroje paramaśivajaṃ cidrūpaminduprakāśam| sāyāhne hū(hyū)rdhvapadme hyamṛtajalanidhidyotanaṃ 
svotthanādaṃ rātrerardhe prameyaṃ tvavikalārthaṃ (l8)
 dvādaśānte mahat tat|| prātaḥkālādyupādhinā śivasmaraṇakramamāha| hṛdīti hṛtpadme hṛdayakamale prātaḥ prātaḥkāle 
sūryodayāt pūrvaṃ ..... (p128a, l1)
 ca nāḍikāparyantaṃ prātaḥkālaḥ arkodayanihanibhaṃ sūryodayasamūhasamaprabham anekāruṇavarṇamityarthaḥ madhyanāḍyāṃ 
suṣumnāyāṃ vicintya dhyātuṃ ...... (l2)
gyaṃ madhyāhne bhrūsaroje bhrūkamale paramaśivajaṃ paraśivasvarūpamanena viśvasya nimittakāraṇasvarūpamuktaṃ 
cidrūpajñānakriyāsvarūpa.........prakā...... ka(l3)
candravaddedīpyamānaṃ vicintya sāyāhne ūrdhve padme brahmarandhrasthitakamale amṛtajalanidhidyotanam amuta samudravat 
prakāśamānaṃ svotthanāda (l4)
 ātmīyamūlādhārotthitanādasvarūpam anenaukārasahitaṃ prāsādamantrasvarūpamuktaṃ bhavati| rātrerardhe ardharātre aprameyaṃ 
mano buddhyādibhijñātuṃ yāgyam (l5)
 avikalamatyantaṃ niṣkalam acalaṃ calanarahitam anena dṛkkriyāśakterudyogādhikāravyāpārarahitatvamuktaṃ sā hṛta 
sarvakāryatvāt dvādaśānte evaṃ vidhaṃ tat pra(prā)pti(l6)
siddhaṃ mahastejorūpaṃ vicintyam||7||

bāhyāntaḥkaraṇairaṇo sva viṣayajñānaṃ tu jāgraṃ ca tat svapno'ntaḥkaraṇasthavṛttiviṣayaṃ jñānaṃ suṣuptiḥ (l7)
 paraṃ dehe tvindriyavṛtti hīnamavaco grāhyā matīsturyagaṃ dehakṣobharatau sva cinmatīradhā(thā)tītaṃ śivatvodayaḥ| agre 
samalātmano jāgradādi(l8)
pañcāvasthā kramamāha| bāhyeti aṇoḥ samalātmanaḥ bāhyāntaḥkaraṇairatra bāhyakaraṇai cakṣuśrotrādibhiḥ 
antaḥkaraṇairmanobuddhyahaṅkāraiḥ atra prathama(l9)
mātmanāntaḥkaraṇaiḥ saha tadanantaraṃ bāhyakaraṇaiḥ saha sva viṣayajñānaṃ sveṣāṃ cakṣurādīnāṃ viṣayajñānaṃ 
grāhyabhūtarūpādijñānaṃ tatprasiddhaṃ jāgraṃ jāgradavasthānaḥ ......... (p128b, l1)
.....ṣayajñānamanobuddhirahaṅkāreṣu vidyamāna-pravṛttamānaviṣayajñānaṃ svapnaḥ svapnāvasthāt param antaḥkaraṇebhyo vyatiriktaḥ 
suṣuptiḥ nidrāvasthā dehe (l2)
 śarīrendriyavṛttihīnaṃ cakṣurādindriyapravartanavyatiriktam avacaḥ vacanabhāvaṃ grāhyā grāhituṃ yogyā matiḥ 
matiśabdenātra prāṇena vivakṣitaḥ prāṇayuktātmetyarthaḥ turyagaṃ (l3)
 turyāvasthā deho kṣobharatau dehe śarīre vidyamānendriyamānasādīnāṃ kṣobharatau calanādilayane svacinmatiḥ 
ātmajñānamātraṃ prāṇena yuktātmamatra mi(i)tyarthaḥ (l4)
 atītaṃ turyāvasthātītāvasthāyāḥ evaṃ samalātmanaḥ pañcāvasthā atha nirmalātmanaḥ śivatvodayaḥ 
śivasvarūpatvaprāptiḥ| 

tathā cintyāgame - malasya (l5)
 lakṣaṇamuktaṃ mukhe jāgramiti jñeyaṃ svapnaṃ tu hṛdayāntakaṃ hṛdayādisamārabhya nābhyatantu suṣi(ṣu)ptikam| tasya 
arthastu turyaṃ syātturyātītaṃ liṅgake| jāgrādi(l6)
sthānamevaṃ hi pravartakamihocyate| śrotratvakcakṣuṣā jihvā ghrāṇaṃ caiva tu pañcakam| śabdasparśaṃ ca rūpaṃ ca rasaṃ 
gandhośca pañcamaḥ| vākpāṇipāda(l7)
pāyū ca upasthaṃ caiva pañcamam| vacanadānagamanavisargānandapañcamaṃ prāṇastvapāno vyānaśca udānastu samānakaḥ| 
nāgaḥ kūrmaścakrakaro devadatte dhanañjayaḥ| (l8)
 mano buddhirahaṅkāraścittakṣetrajña eva ca| pañca ktra?paraśca vartante jāgrasthānaṃ vinirdiśet śabdādi vacanādīni 
prāṇādīni manādikam| atra prāṇādiśabdena nāgā...yaśca ..... (p129a, l1)
kṣedakakāryāṇāmudgarādīnāṃ darśanaṃ svapne vidyamānatvāt ete caranti vai svasnośeṣāṇyeva tu ...rjayet jāgre 
viṣayabhuktiśca parabha svasti eva ca.... prāṇayu... (l2)
manaṃ caiva kṣetrajñaśca suṣi(ṣu)ptike| ātmā prāṇasturyeṇa turyātīte visargaka iti| atra śivatvodayaśabdena 
nirmalātmanaḥ jāgrādipañcāvasthā uktā bhavati| 

tathā īśāna(l3)
devaguruṇāpyuktaṃ bījasyoccāraṇaṃ jāgratvāt svapno binduparigrahaḥ suṣuptirnādasaṃyogasturyaṃ śaktisamāgamaḥ 
ityā(ya)ti(tī)taṃ layaṃ śāntaṃ paṭhe mahasi (l4)
 paṭhyata iti||8|| iti siddhāntasārāvalyāṃ yogapādaḥ samāptaḥ||

ātmāno daśasṛṅ paścimagatān kṛtvā śivān kevalāṃ 
teṣām amantrapatipratyotha *parato jānuṃ tathā bhajya tat|* (l5.)
sākṣāddeva upādideśa sa śivaḥ śaivāntitaścāgamā 
rudrāṃścāṣṭadaśā vibodhya kathitāstairāgamāścāpare|| (l6.)

atha caryāpādaṃ vaktukāmastatrādau kāmikādyāgamānāṃ śivabheda-rudrabheda-saṃjñāvidhānamāha| ātma iti parameśvarajñānaṃ 
dviprakāram avabodharūpaṃ (l7)
 śabdarūpaṃ ceti tatra anādimalarahitam anādinidhanaṃ sarvārthaprakāśakaṃ sarvānugrahakaṃ śivaṃ śivajñānātmakam 
avabodharūpaṃ paramaśive vidyate śabdarūpaṃ tu (l8)
 śāstrākameva paramakāruṇikaḥ parameśvaraḥ sṛṣṭikāle svātmani vidyamānameva bodharūpameva śabdarūpaṃ kṛtvā 
sarvānujīghrakṣathā (l9)
 guru......parayā yogyeṣu pravartayati guruparāparākramaścātra dviprakāraḥ| mahaughakramalakṣaṇaḥ gurusaṃhitākramalakṣaṇaḥ 
śāstrāvatāraḥ (p129b, l1)
 atrāgamadhe pratisaha...guru ūrdhvasṛṣṭyā sṛṣṭāt ata eva kevalāṃchaśa(śu)ddhān abhivyaktasarvajñatvādi dharmayuktāni 
vṛttyarthaḥ| ata eva śivān śivasvarūpān sadāśivapadasthān  (l2)
 daśātmanaḥ daśasaṅkhyānām ajñān praṇavādīn sṛṣṭvā smaraṇamātreṇa kṛtvā eteṣā praṇavādināṃ 
sadāśivapadasthitiriti siddhaṃ tathoktaṃ śrīmat svatantra| (l3)
 sakalastu vrato devaḥ oṅkārādairanukramāditi oṅkārādyaiḥ praṇavādyairityarthaḥ teṣāṃ praṇavādidaśamātmajñānām 
amantrapatiprabodhaparataḥ (l4)
 mantrapatīnām anantādyaṣṭavidyeśvarāṇāṃ prabodho āgamajñānopadeśaḥ sa eva punaḥ utkṛṣṭaḥ paurvāka ityarthaḥ tasmāt 
parataḥ (l5)
 vidyeśvarāt prabodhanānantaraṃ praṇavādīnāmupadeśaḥ kṛta ityarthaḥ jñānānandarūpaśivajñānaṃ ca muktaṃ ca adṛṣṭavigrahāt 
chāntāt śivāt (l6)
 paramakāraṇāt nādarūpaṃ viniṣkāntaṃ śāstraṃ paramadurlabhamiti tathā daśaprakāreṇa vibhajya bhāgaṃ kṛtvā yaduktaṃ 
svāyu(ya)mbhuve| athotta malamāyākhyakarmabandha(l7)
vimu[kta]ye vyaktaye ca śivatvasya śivajñānaṃ pravartate| ekameva śivajñānaṃ bahudhā punaḥ daśāṣṭadaśadhā bhūyo 
daśāntaravisarjitamiti (l8)
 devaḥ sarvatra prakāśamānaḥ śivaḥ paramaśivaḥ sākṣāt pratyakṣeṇa upādideśa kāmikādidaśabhedenorpadeśaṃ kṛtavān 
tataḥ| vaktṛśrotrāṇāṃ (p130a, l1)
 śivarūpatvāt śaivāḥ śivasaṃjñayā vihitāḥ kāmikādayo daśāgamaḥ| 

tathā kiraṇe - sṛṣṭyanantarameva ......... smṛtvā daśātmakān jānameva ...... (l2)
 saṅkhyāyā vadat| kāmikam| praṇavākhyasya sudhavikhyasya ca yogajam| dīptākhyasya vicintyaṃ tu 
kāraṇākhyasya kāraṇam| ajitaṃ suśivākhyasamāraṃśākhyasyā..... (l3)
dīptakaṃ sūkṣmaṃ tu sakṣmasaṃjñasya kālasyāpi sahasrakaṃ sughrantaṃ yadgaṇeśasya aṃśusaṃjñasya cāṃśumān evaṃ prādhānikaḥ 
proktaḥ punaḥ (l4)
 nyojagu? kramaḥ  praṇavārtikālaḥ prāptaḥ kāmikaṃ trikālāddhara ityādinā draṣṭavyaḥ aṣṭadaśa 
aṣṭādhikadaśasaṅkhyakān rudrān anantādirudranāmadheyādīnya (l5)
 avabodhya jñānayuktān īśvaratatvasthitānevānugṛhya tat saṅkhyayā ekamevābodhajñānaṃ yathādhikāraṃ (l6)
 śabdarūpaṃ kṛtvā kriyādibhedena kathitāṃ śivenaivoktān taiḥ śivabhaṭṭārikeṇa rudrān śrotratatvena vidhāyakathanāt 
apare āgamāḥ (l7)
 rudrasaṃjñayā vihitāgamāḥ vijñādayo'ṣṭādaśā(śa) atrāparutyuktatvāt śivabhedarudrabhedayoryathākrameṇa parāparatva-
muktamiti dyotitaṃ bhavati| 

tathā kiraṇe - (l8)
 daśāṣṭasaṃṅkhyayāpyanyo rudrabhedo paro mataḥ daśāṣṭasaṅkhyayā rudrān pūrvamutpādya buddhimān  śivastat saṅkhyayaiveha 
punastān (p130b, l1)
 bodhayet khaga| rudrasyānādisaṃjñasya vijayaṃ tena bhāṣitaṃ parameśaṃ mahātantraṃ śrīrūpasya prabhāṣitaṃ niśvāsaṃ 
yaddaśārṇasya prodgītaṃ nāma śūlin ityādinā (l2)
 bodhavyaṃ śivabodhasya kāmikādeḥ pratyekaṃ trayaḥ śrotāraḥ rudrabhedasya vijayāde dvau śrotārau ubhayasammelanena 
ṣaḍadhikaṣaṣṭasaṅkhyākāḥ (l3)
 śrotāraḥ ṣaṭṣaṣṭibhiḥ krameṇaiva kathitaṃ tu śivājñayetyuktatvāt eteṣāṃ nāmadheyāni mūlāgameṣu draṣṭavyāni||1||

saṃbaddhāstu ..... (l4)
roṇunāthaśivayorānantasaumyo momāddaivagatāntarālavidito divyo muniṣvāgataḥ devā divyagato'pyadivyṛṣito(l5)
'pyukto munīndrānnare tasmācchiṣyagato'pyadivya iti ṣaṭchaivāgamānāṃ viduḥ|| śaivāgamānāṃ 
vaktṛśrotṛbhedenāvi....matāva(l6)
saktā ṣaṭ prakārāḥ santītyāha| saṃbandha iti aṇunāthaśivayoḥ aṇunāthaḥ saptakoṭisaṅkhyākā nāthaḥ 
ananteśvaraśivaḥ sadāśiva(l7)
bhaṭṭārakaḥ tayoḥ śrotṛvaktṛbhedenāgamānāṃ subandhaḥ parampara iti nāmadheyo noktaḥ saṃbandhaḥ prathamaḥ anantenātra īśvaraḥ 
somaśabdenātra śrīka...... (l8)

śrīmadraurave jagataḥ kāraṇaṃ devamana ...... (p131a, l1)
...raṃ gurūṃ tenoktaṃ ....rameśena śrīkaṇṭhāya mahātmana iti somāt śrīkaṇṭhāt devagataḥ indrādidevaṃ gataḥ antarālaḥ 
antarāla iti nāmadheyena viditaḥ uktaṃ ....... (l2)
 ........-āgataḥ indrādīn devebhyaḥ ṛṣiṣu uktaḥ divyaḥ divya iti nāmadheyena sahitaḥ caturthaḥ saṃbandhaḥ| 
devāddviyagataḥ vaktrabhūtadevāt śrotrabhūtamuniṣu prāptatvāt (l3)
 divya ityucyate| ṛṣitaḥ adivyaḥ vaktrabhū ṛṣisakāśāt śrotrabhūtanaraśreṣṭheṣu prāptatvāddivya ucyate| ata eva 
munīndrāt devaṣeḥ na .....manuṣyṛṣau (l4)
 uktaḥ saṃbandho iti pañcamaḥ proktaḥ tasmādṛṣeḥ śiṣyagataḥ svaśiṣyaṃ prāptasaṃbandhaḥ adivya iti ṣaṣṭhaḥ iti evaṃ 
prakāreṇa śaivāgamānā........ (l5)
kādinā stvandhadārṣaprakārā viduḥ| uktavantaḥ pūrvācāryaiḥ śāstrārthavicāreṇoktā iti śeṣārthaḥ| nanu 
kāmikāditantreṣu praṇavādaya(l6)
śrotāra iti śrūyante| tat kathaṃ śivānantayoḥ saṃbandha iti śaṅkāyāḥ parihāraḥ atra granthe tasyausagarbhigatvā ca 
āmalakṣaṇasaṃbandhaḥ proktaḥ (l7)
 evameva pratisaṃhitagurukramalakṣaṇasaṃbandhaḥ saṃyojanīyaḥ ananteśvaraprabodhānantaraṃ praṇavādīnāṃ pra........ (l8)
dhanatvāt ata eva saṃbandho dvividhaḥ ananteśvarādisaṃbandhaḥ praṇavādisaṃbandhaḥ iti śivapraṇavayoḥ paraḥ saṃbandhaḥ 
praṇavatrikālayormahān trikalaharayo(p131b, l1)
rantarālaṃ haradevayordivyaḥ devṛṣyordivyādivyaḥ ṛṣimanuṣyayoradivya iti ṣaḍvidhaḥ saṃbandhaḥ evaṃ 
śivabhedenāmūhanīyāḥ rudra(l2)
bhedena śreṣṭhadvayasaṃbandhaḥ pañcavidha eva śivānādirudrayoḥ paraḥ anādirudraparameśayormahān parameśadevayordivyaḥ deva 
ṛṣyor........ (l3)

siddham|

 praṇavameka.........kyaṃ tathyamīśvarabhāṣitamiti| nanu nityatvenāgamasyāpramāṇyaṃ vadanti kecit tadasat 
avabodharūpameva nityaṃ tadeva (l5)

kṛtakatvameva nāstīti keṣāñcinmatam| tadapyasatrūpamava(l6)

mithyājñānatvācca ata eva (l7)

śaivāḥ kāmikayogaje jītamatho cantyaṃ tu dīptāhvayaṃ sūkṣmaṃ kāraṇamāśumā....śca daśadhā sāhasrakaṃ suprabhuraudrāste 
vijayaṃ ca rauravamatho niśvāsamāgneyakaṃ santānaṃ kiraṇākhyasiddhavimalā śrī candrabhāsaṃ para.......... (p132a, l1)
...calitadvamākaṭamatho śrīnārasahmam| 

tathā śrīsvāyambhuvapārameśamukhayuk bimbāni bhadrāṇvayaṃ tenāṣā daśasakhya(ṅkhya)yordhvamukhataḥ sākṣāt sa deśe ....

 proktāḥ so hi parāpta eva nikhiloktirṇokhi  ..... jñānavān|| śivabhāvanānmadhye pūrve daśaśivabhedāḥ| pareṣā 
daśarudrabhedā iti aṣṭaviṃti tantrāṇāṃ na...... (l3)
yānyugmenāha| śaivā iti| kāmikayogaje'jitamatho cintyamiti granthakāreṇoktaṃ tathāpyatra 
granthe'nvayamukhenāgamānāṃ paurvāparyakramapāṭho jñātavyaḥ chando(l4)
bhaṅgānurodheno-ktatvāt kāmikayogaje kāmikamiti yogajamiti kāraṇamiti aja(ajita)miti dīptamiti 
sūkṣmamiti sāhasramiti| aṃśumāniti suprabhedamiti (l5)

analatantraṃ bhadrāhvayapadena vīratantramucyate| 

tathā (l6)
 mṛgendre - svāyambhuvaṃ tathāgneyavīrabhadraṃ sauravamiti mākaṭamiti| vimalamiti candrabhāsamiti candrajñānamucyate 
mukhayuk bimbamiti mukhabimbamucyate udgītamiti (l7)

tathā kāraṇe - kāmikaṃ yogajaṃ cantyaṃ kāraṇakaṃ tvajitaṃ tathā dīptaṃ sūkṣmaṃ ca sāhasram aṃśumān| suprabhedakaṃ 
vijayaṃ caiva niśvāsaṃ svāyambhuvamathānilaṃ vīraṃ ca (l3)
 rauravaṃ caiva makuṭaṃ vimalaṃ tathā| candrajñānaṃ ca bimbaṃ prodgītaṃ lalitaṃ tathā siddhaṃ santānaśarvoktaṃ pārameśvarameva ca 
kiraṇaṃ vātulaṃ tvetā aṣṭāviṃśati sahitāḥ teṣu (l4)
 pūrve daśa proktaṃ śivabhedeti saṃjñakaṃ daśāparāṣṭakaṃ tantraṃ rudrabhedamiti smṛtamiti tena siddhena sadeśena sadāśivena 
ūrdhvamukhataḥ īśānasya mukhāt sākṣāt (l5)
 pratyakṣaṃ te kāmikādayaḥ proktāḥ| nanu kāmikādyaṣṭāvi(viṃ)śatitantrāṇāṃ kathamīśānamukhāt utpannatvamūlāgameṣu 
pañcavaktreṣūpa(papa)nnamityuktatvāt|

 bhūtārtho'yamiti niścityādiri tatvāt anādimalabaddhāḥ sarve paśava ityucyante| aryaṃ tāvat

 dyotitaṃ bhavati imaṃ mo(me)vārthaṃ dṛḍha iti nikhiloktirṇaḥ nikhilebhyaḥ anādibhūtamalamāyādipāśebhyaḥ| uttīrṇaḥ

 ata eva kanyārāśau prāyaścittapūrvameva pavitraṃ karaṇīyaṃ tulāyāḥ kadācidapi na kuryāt|

 viṣṇusvapati karkaṭe| iti anyatrāpi caitrakṛṣṇadaśamyāṃ dineṣvarvāk caturdaśi ravau meṣagate darśadvayaṃ ca mithune

cet āgamāni saṃvatsare (l7)
 cobhayārthaṃ dvirāṣāḍhasaṃjñā vihiteti vaktavyā bhavati anyathottaratāsitaiḥ ṣaṭsu adhikayuktavartamānasaṃvatsare mithune 
svāpaḥ| karkaṭe ..... (p137a, l1)
 veti artho vaktavyaḥ vartamānavarṣe meṣāditriṣu adhimāsaṃ cet svabhāvāṣāḍhyakarkaṭakayukto bhavati .......vihitaḥ

 na yukto bhavati tathā mithune svāpasiddhaḥ evaṃ vartamānavihitasvāpasya sajati vacanaṃ ca pradarśanīyam|| bhavati 
........ vacanābhāvādeva mādhavādye ṣaṣṭhe svamā..... (l3)
 dvayaṃ yadā dvirāṣāḍhaḥ sa vijñeyaḥ śete ca śrāvaṇe'cyuta iti vacanaṃ saṃvatsarayuktakarkaṭake 
vihitasvāpakālajñāpakavacanaṃ bhavati uttaratra svāpaka(l4)
vacanaṃ meṣādimithunānteṣu yadā darśadvayaṃ bhavet abdāntare (ntare/nte) tadāvaśyakaṃ mithunārke hari svapet 
karkaṭāditrike vāpi yadā darśadvayaṃ bhavet (l5)
 abdāntare tadāvaśyaṃ karkaṭārke hari svapediti bhaviṣyakālasvāpaviṣayavacanaṃ yadyanena prakāreṇa svāpakālo na 
bhavet| tasyāṣāḍha(l6)

svāpakāle (l7)
 siddhe svāpakālasiddhyarthavihitāni vacanāni anekāni vyarthā bhavanti dvirāṣāḍhasaṃjñā ca vyarthaṃ bhavati| ata eva

tathā śivāgamavacanam - āṣāḍhe'pi prakartavyaṃ prasupto madhusūdanena ........ (p137b, l1)

 prasupte ca janārdane| prasupto'pi ca kartavyam aṣṭamiddhṛdayorapi| iti atra granthe| ekadāṣāḍhe| ....... svāpo

pade vā māsonasyādathā karkaṭakāṣāḍhe vā mithunāṣāḍhe vā ....... svāpayāvarta aṣṭama...........sya siddhikāt tatraiva 
pavitrārambhaṇe .... (l2)
dyogaḥ kartavyam aṣṭamigrahaṇasāmarthyāt ayaṃ granthaḥ prathamāṣāḍhaviṣayo na bhavati yadi prathamāṣāḍhaviṣayaḥ dvirāṣāḍhe 
aṣṭamyāṃ svāpatāvata| (l3)

svāpasya vihitatvāt| (l4)
 

tathā viṣṇurahasye - śrāvaṇasya site pakṣe karkaṭaste devākare dvādaśyāṃ vāsudevasya pavitrārohaṇaṃ smri(smṛ)tam|

tathā vaiṣṇave - vārṣamāsacatuṣkasya madhye kuryāścage dine| āṣāḍhapañcadaśyādiyāvātvekānakasya ca

ndrasaṃhyakam| ākarkaṭā tu tayoḥ| pavitrāropāvadhikālaṃ ca māsapakṣaṃ ca| sauraṃ madhyama-saṃjñakamiti tatha(thā)|

saurayāpi ca (l4)
 kārayet| jyeṣṭhāṣāḍhayo dvi karkaṭake candrā varagāḥ| tapadāślayuk kī(kā)rtikamārgaśīrṣayoḥ kārayediti

 dvike sahitaśrāvaṇyāṃ karkaṭake pavitrāropaṇaṃ kāryedityarthaḥ| śrāvaṇyā dvike sati śrāvaṇyāṃ sahakārayedityarthaḥ|

tathā kāmike - pūrvapakṣe catuśyā(rdaśyā)mevāṣāḍhe pavitrakam itareṣvanyamāseṣu pakṣayo śuklakṛṣṇayoḥ ...... 
(p139a, l1)
śyāmathāṣṭha(ṣṭa)myāṃ pavitraṃ vidhinā caret| śuklapakṣe .....stasya yaterubhayapakṣayoḥ| api māsadvayapavitrakaraṇe 
āṣāḍhaśuddhacaturdaśyārambhaḥ| (l2)
 bhādrapadaśuddhacaturdaśyāṃ parisamāptaḥ tatpaurṇamāsyāmavabhṛthasnānakaraṇam| ekamāsakaraṇe śrāvaṇaśuddhacaturdaśyāṃ 
parisamāptiḥ tatpaurṇamāsyā(l3)
mavabhṛthaṃ pavitre kārayet| gururi-tyuktatvāt evaṃ śuddhaṣaṭkapakṣaviṣaśe(ye) śrāvaṇaśuddhāṣṭamyāmārambhaḥ 
bhādrapadakṛṣṇāṣṭamyāṃ parisamāptiḥ (l4)
 śuklapratipadārabhya pañcadaśyantaṃ vā kāryaṃ māsadvayaṃ tu pakṣaṃ vā abhāvāddinasaptakam| ityuktatvāt 
āṣāḍhaśuddhacaturdaśi syācchubhadāṣṭamī ca śuklā tu (l5)
 kṛṣṇā tu vimuktiheturiti sarvamāseṣu tithinirṇayakāraṇe yathā aṣṭamyāṃ ca caturdaśyāṃ pratipat paurṇamāsyapi| 
pañcadaśyāntaraṃ kāryaṃ (l6)
 pūrvapakṣe prajāyate dvitīyā tī?(tu) caturthyādiṣaṣṭhyādīni viśeṣataḥ| aṣṭamyantaṃ prakartavyaṃ kṛṣṇapakṣe dvijottamāḥ 
sasaptāhaṃ caiva pañca.... (l7)
 tryahaṃ cāpi kārayet| ekāhatriṃca(śa)t prakurvīta aṣṭamyāṃ kṛṣṇapakṣake| caturdaśyāṃ tu śukle tu 
pavitrārohaṇakriyā| iti saptāhapakṣe (l8)
 ....(śrā)vaṇaśuddhāṣṭamīmārabhyataḥ śuddhacaturdaśyāṃ tu kāryam| evaṃ dinapavitrakaraṇe trayodaśyāmadhivāsaḥ caturdaśyāṃ 
pavitra(p139b, l1)
….(sa)marpaṇaṃ kāryaṃ tatra saṃpūrṇacaturdaśyā grāhyā uktaṃ ca yadekasmindine kāryaṃ sarvaṃ saṃpūrṇatithiyadhidi? tadātvekadine 
śambhoḥ pavitrā... (l2)
 karṣādikṛtanityastadā sandhyāṃ kṛtvā| naimittikī, sakṛt = samyak, gandhapavitraṃ ca datvopari pavitrakaṃ puṣpaṃ 
datvā tu caṇḍāya liṅgaṃ sva da........pūjayediti (l3)
 yadā caturadaśi vāradvayagatā bhavati tadāparadivāyuktacaturdaśyāṃ pavitrasamarpaṇaṃ kartavyam| 

tathā siddhāntaśekhare - pavitrakārye (l4)
 paragha(rpa)srayuktā caturdaśi syācchubhadāṣṭami ca iti tathāpyatra(trā)nyatra(trā)pyuktam| yadā khaṇḍatithistatra tathā 
tattithibhāgayoḥ gandhasūtraḥ (l5)
 pavitraṃ ca dadyādādyā caturdaśi paurṇamāsyardha-sammiśrā tadā kālāntare hitaḥ| yadvā tatraiva kartavyaṃ yadi vyagratayā 
tadā| godo........ (l6)
ritaṃ kri(kṛ)tvā sādhivāsanivartakam| caturdaśītidhi kuryātveṣa śeṣatithau carediti| asyārthaḥ yadā tasmin 
kāle khaṇḍatithiḥ divādvayagatatithirbhavati tadā tasmin (l7)
 ka(kā)le tattithibhāgayoḥ divādvayagatatithighaṭikasaṃbandhayuktabhāgayoryathākrameṇa 
gandhasūtramadhivāsadinasamarpaṇapavitrakaṃ punarapi prāta....... (p140a, l1)
samarpaṇapavitraṃ ca dadyāt parameśvarāya samarpayedityarthaḥ ayamabhiprāyaḥ rātriyuktacaturdaśyāṃ 
gandhapavitraduvīgataparadivasacaturdaśyāṃ (l2)
 pavitrasamarpaṇaṃ kartavyam| yadā paradivasagatacaturdaśi aparāhnayuktā bhavati tadā pūrvoktavat kartavyaṃ madhyāhnaparyantaṃ 
paradivasa-gatā cettadā kālā(l3)
ntare kartavyamityāha yadvā dvayasamānakāle sati pavitrasya paradivasa eva sa eva (l4)
 grāhya ityuktatvāt ubhayakālasamarpaṇasamānarūpacaturdaśi tyājyeti tadā kālāntare vihitamityuktaṃ yadi 
vyagratayā tatraiva śīghreṇa kartavya (l5)
miti vyagrasamānaḥ tayā yuktaṃ cet caturdaśi kṣaṇe godohāntaritagodohanasamānakālaparyantaṃ madhye vyavadhānaṃ kṛtvā 
sādhivāsa(l6)
pavitraṃ kuryāccaturdaśayuktapūrvarātreṇa adhivāsaṃ na kartavyaṃ pūrvadivasadivākāle pavitrasamarpaṇasamānarūpatvāt 
taduparyadhivāsa(p140b, l1)
nayogyamityabhiprāyaḥ śeṣaṃ grāmapradakṣiṇe(ṇo)tsavādikaṃ śeṣatithau paurṇamāsiyuktā parāhne kārayet tathoktaṃ saptamyāṃ 
vā trayodaśyāṃ kṛtvā (l2)
 gandhapavitrakam aparehni kartavyaṃ sa pavitramiti smṛtamiti etat khaṇḍatithiviṣayavacanaṃ kecidevamāhuḥ 
trayodaśiyuktarātrau adhivāsaṃ (l3)
 caturdaśiyuktā hyaparāhnakāle gandhapavitrasamarpaṇaṃ tadanantaraṃ caturdaśiyuktaḥ prātaḥkāle pavitrasamarpaṇaṃ (l4)
 yathā-krameṇaikadinaṃ kartavyamiti tathā tadvacanam athavā bhyāṃ? dinenaikaṃ kartavyaṃ vā pare jaguḥ| adhivāsaṃ 
tri(tra)yodaśyāmaparehni tithidvaya(l5)
miti ābhyā gandhasūtrapavitrābhyāmekaikadinaṃ prati kartavyamiti apare ācāryā jagaduḥ va(u)ktavantaḥ 
trayodaśyāmadhivāsaḥ parihi(ha)ni gandhasūtra(l6)
pavitrasamarpaṇaṃ kartavyaṃ sadyo'dhivāsapakṣe caturdaśi kṣi(kṣī)ṇo go dāhāntaritaṃ kṛtvā sādhivāsaṃ pavitrasamarpaṇaṃ 
kartavyaṃ caturdaśi alpaghaṭikā cettadā caturdaśi kṣa(kṣī)ṇe kuryāt sādhivāsaṃ pavitrakam| supavitraṃ 
paurṇamāsyāttithi viddhaistvayaṃ vidhiḥ| aṣṭamyāmapi boddhavyaḥ kramo'yaṃ sūtra(trā)rohaṇamiti 
guruśukramauḍhyasaṃyuktamāsaṃ tyaktvā śuddhamāse (p141a, l1)
 pavitrārohaṇaṃ kartavyaṃ yadā antamayaguruśukrābhyāṃ māsatrayo'pi udayo na syāttadā pavitrasya nityāṅgasūtratvāt 
kartavyameva| 

tathā siddhāntaśekhare - (l2)
 kavau śuddhau cāstamayaṃ prayāte māsatraye cāpyudayo na cāsti tathā tathāpi kurvīta pavitrakārya(ryaṃ) 
naimittikatvāduta naikyakatvāditi nanvā..... (l3)
dimāsatraye yadā guruśukrābhyāmudayo na syāttato-ditāśvayujakārtikama(mā)se kāryaṃ bhavati pavitrakaraṇe 
tanmāsasyāpi (l4)
 grahaṇāt| 

tathā kāmike - āṣāḍhāditrayo māsā gre(grī)ṣmā madhyamakanyasāḥ aśvani kārtikā ma(mā)sāvathavā saṃprakīrtitau 
iti evamukte'pi māsatraye kathaṃ (l5)
 pavitrakaraṇamityatra parihāraḥ māsatraye daivādinā apavitrakaraṇe satyetanmāsa-dvayasya grahaṇaṃ na mukhyata ityadoṣaḥ 
pavitrakaraṇe (l6)
 māsadvayasya mukhyatvāt atra śloke granthakāreṇaitat sarvaṃ manasi nidhāya tasmin darśayugamityādi uktaḥ tasmin 
karṭake(karkaṭake) darśayugarma(l7)
vāsyā vayaṃ yadiya(dya)-sminvatsare bhavettadā siṃhayuktordhvā śravaṇe pavitrodyamaḥ pavitrārohaṇakāryādyogaḥ kartavyaḥ 
āṣāḍhe svā(sva)bhāvāt (l8)
 prathamaṃ śravaṇe svāpe siddhe sati malamāsatvāt siṃha eva vapitrarohaṇaṃ siddham| 

tathā coktaṃ mohaśūrottare - (p141b, l1)
 dīkṣādi sthāpanādyaṃ ca pavitrādiśatakratoḥ| adhimāsi na kurvīta yadicchecchubhamātmana iti tathā ca smṛtau 
upākarmotsarjane ca pavitraṃ damanārpaṇam| (l2)
 avarohaśca hemantasamarpaṇaṃ baliriṣṭakā| īśānasya balirviṣṇoḥ śayanaṃ parivartanam| durgendrasthāpanoddhāne(re) 
dhvajotthānaṃ ca (l3)
 vaktriṇaḥ pūrvaṃ ca praṣi(si)ddhāni paraṃ cāntyaṃ ca daivikamiti atra viṣṇoḥ śayanamiti pūrvamāsaniṣedhavākyaṃ 
pūrvāṣāḍhaviṣayavacanam| (l4)
 pūrvāṣāḍhe viṣṇo(ṇoḥ) svāpābhāvāt pūrvaśrāvaṇe tu svāpasya siddhatvāt tadviṣayavacanaṃ na bhavatīti vaktavya(vyaṃ) 
viṣṇo svāpānantarakāle (l5)
 pavitrasya kriyama(mā)ṇatvāt mithunagataśuddhāṣṭamiṃ vinā taccaturdaśyāmārambhaḥ kartavyaḥ| uktaṃ ca śuddhāṣṭamīṃ vinā 
dvandvakuli(lī)re siṃhabhādrayoḥ (l6)
 sitāsitāsu kartavyaṃ caturdaśyāṣṭamī ceti| kārpāsotthanavātmasūtraśatadik klaptaṃ? śiromānakaṃ pūjāṃśāya caturthakaṃ 
trayamatho (l7)
 sūtrasya saṅkhyāvaśāt grandhiṃ binduśareṇa vā navakare  paṅktyāṣṭaviṃśaddhṛte sūtraṃ hastamite śruteḥ 
kilahṛte'vyaṣṭādaśagrandhayaḥ| bāṇādye.... (l8)
tamārciteṣu yadi vā śitaśca saikāṣṭayuk traṃ(triṃ)śadbinduśarākhyasaṅkhyamapi vātatve prakṛtyādikāḥ| (p142a, l1)
 gandhināmadhipā(pāḥ) śivāḥ śaśiyutā candrāṃśaṃ śaivākalāḥ sūtrāṇāṃ praṇavādayo'dhipatayo vyakteṣu jānvantakam|| 
atha pavitrārohaṇārtham (l2)
 ātma ...dyā śivagaṅgāvatārākhyapavitrāṇāṃ tu grandhyā(nthyā)dipramāṇamāha| kārpāseti kārpāsotthaṃ 
kārpāsādinotpannaṃ dvijakanyābhiḥ kartumityarthaḥ (l3)
 navātmasūtraṃ navasaṅkhyāsvarūpeṇa kṛtamekasūtramityucyate| tasya śatadikkliptamaṣṭottaraśatasaṅkhyayā kṛtam| 

tathā somaśambhapaddhatyām - (l4)
 sauvarṇaṃ rajataṃ tāmraṃ kṛtādi ca yathākramaṃ kalau kārpāsakaṃ vāpi yathāśakti pavitrakaṃ kartavyaṃ dvijakanyābhiḥ 
triguṇaṃ tri(tṛ)ṇīkṛtaṃ (l5)
 la.....taṃ śuklaśubhaṃ sūtramanyadapyapayujyate paṭṭa-valkalapadmotthaṃ kṣaumaṃ darbhaśaṇodbhavam| mu...yādisaṃbhavaṃ sūtraṃ 
pavitrāya (l6)
 pra....syata iti āndhe paṭṭakṣaumayoḥ sādhaka-viṣayatvena praśasyaṃ tathā darbhasya yativiṣayatvena praśasyaṃ (l7)
 mauñja....didaridraviṣayaḥ, dvijaśabdo = dvijasaṃskāraḥ, saṃskṛtaviṣayaḥ| tadabhāve varṇakaviṣayaḥ 
kanyāśabdavidhavānivṛttipara eva atra aṣṭāśata..... (sū)(l8)

tathā jñānaratnāvalyām - aṣṭādhikaśataṃ sūtraṃ rudrasaṅkhyā sadottamam| sūtrābhāve tadardhaṃ syāttasyārdha.... (p142b, 
l1)
 vā prakalpayediti aṣṭottaraśatādīnāṃ kriyamāṇapavitrakam| sthaṇḍilādipavitraviṣayam uktaṃ ca aṣṭottaraśataiḥ 
kāryaṃ (l2)
 sthaṇḍilādau pavitrakaṃ tasyārdhena tadardhena yathālābhena vā bharet sthaṇḍile calaliṅge ca bāṇaliṅge svayaṃ bhuvi| 
(l3)
 rohādau niyamo nāsti yathāpūrvamudāhṛtam aṣṭottaraśatādūrdhvaṃ na kartavyaṃ kadācana sarveṣāṃ niyamaḥ śakra 
ityājñāparameśvarīti (l4)
 śiromānakaṃ talliṅgaṃ śiraḥ pramāṇaṃ trayam ātmavidyāśivākhyapavitrayam| 

tathā ṣaṭsahasrakāyām - liṅgamūrdhni pariṇāhaṃ yathā (l5)
 caivātmikaṃ tathā vidyātmakaparīṇāhaṃ tato bhāgadvayaṃ vinā| tasmāt bhāgadvayaṃ navyaṃ? śivākhyaṃ yatpavitrakamiti 
atrāthośabdena (l6)
 tatvatrayāṇāṃ pratyekaṃ daśagrandhyādayaḥ kāryā iti arthānantaraṃ sūcitaṃ sthaṇḍile ca liṅgādau 
yathānuguṇamaṣṭāṅgulamanādinā tatvatrayapavitrakaṃ (p143a, l1)
 kāryam| 

tathā jñānaratnāvalyām – liṅgamastakaviṃśatyāmātmakaṃ ca pavitrakaṃ vidyāśivātmakaṃ tasmānyūnānyūnaṃ yathā śubham| 
acale tu cale (l2)
 kāryamaṣṭādyarkāṅgulāyataṃ kṛtvā kaṅkaṇavaktradaśagrandhisamantaramiti| anyatrāpi grandhibhiḥ| tadabhiryuktaṃ tāvadbhirvā 
samantarāmiti daśagrandhiyuktapavitra(l3)
trayaṃ kalpaśiromānayuktaliṅgādiviṣayaṃ bahuviśālaśiroyuktaliṅgādau sūtrasaṅkhyayā yathānuguṇena vā pavitra(l4)
.... kāryam| 

tathā mṛgendre – pūjāprarojanamūrdhnitrayaṃ tatvatrayātmakam| tadiṣṭagrandhisaṅkhyātaṃ śubhaṃ kāryaṃ vipaściteti pūjāṃśāya (l5)
 liṅgasya pūjābhāgāya caturthaḥ gaṅgāvatārākhyapavitrakaṃ pūrvoktavadaṣṭottaraśatādinottamamadhyamādhamena (l6)
 vidhātavyaṃ gaṅgāvatārasya aṣṭottaraśatena sūtreṇa kāryamiti kecit| 

tathā siddhāntaśekhare – sāṣṭaṃ śataṃ syāt pravaraṃ? (p143b, l1)
 pavitraṃ madhyaṃ tadardhaṃ ca tadardhamanyam| evaṃ pavitratṛtayaṃ vidadhyāt gaṅgāvatāraṃ ca sa śataṃ ca sāṣṭamiti sthiraliṅgena 
gaṅgāvatāronnatapramāṇaṃ (l2)
 liṅgamastakādārabhya pīṭhāntāvalambikāryamuktaṃ ca| gaṅgāvatārakaṃ nāma caturthaṃ śatasūtrakam| mastakāt 
piṇḍikāntatatsthiraliṅge (l3)
 ca nānyatheti sūtrasyāṣṭottaraśatādisūtrasya, saṅkhyāvaśāt = saṅkhyānusārāt, granthiṃ gaṅgāvatārasya kuryāt (l4)
 iti śeṣaḥ|| 

tathā mṛgendre – granthayastu saṅkhyātāsteṣvāvedipralambiṣviti āvedipralambiṣu pīṭhāvadhi pralambamāneṣu (l5)
 gaṅgāvatāreṣvityarthaḥ| uttamottamādikaniṣṭhāntanavaliṅgeṣvanyaprakārasūtrasaṅkhyānaṃ navaprakāragrandhisaṅkhyāṃ ca (l6)
 binduśareṇetyādināha| vāśabdena vaikalpikārtho dyotitaḥ pūrvoktavat aṣṭottaraśatena tadardhena navaliṅge pavitrakaṃ 
kāryaṃ (l7)
 navaprakāreṇa vā kāryamiti navaprakāre navahastādiekahastaparyantaliṅge paṅktyāṣṭottaraśatasūtrādārabhya daśa-saṅkhyayā

aṣṭottarapañcāśat (l2)
 sūtraṃ trikare aṣṭottaracatvāriṃśatsūtraṃ dvikare aṣṭottaratriṃśatsūtram ekakare aṣṭāviṃśatisūtramānena prakāreṇa na.... 
(l3)

(l4)
 mayā tava daśahrāsrāt samākhyātasūtrasaṅkhyā-purandareti kaṅgaṇatrayāṇāṃ sūtrasaṅkhyāyā pramāṇamanenaprakāreṇa (l5)
 kartavyam| granthisaṅkhyāpramāṇaṃ gaṅgāvatārasyoktagandhivadvādaśagranthayo vā kartavyāḥ navakare liṅge gaṅga(ṅgā)vatāre 
(l6)

 kuryāt śruteḥ apaharaṇabhūta(taṃ) ca saṅkhyāyāḥ navahastādekahastāntaṃ catuḥ saṅkhyāyā hrāsayedityarthaḥ navahastamite

tāre paścāt granthayaḥ kāryāḥ| aṣṭakare ṣaṭcatvāriṃśat, saptakare dvicatvāriṃśat, ṣaṭkare aṣṭāviṃśat. pañcakare 
catutriṃ(striṃ)śat, catuṣkare triṃśat, (l2)
 trikare ṣaḍviṃśat, dvikare dvāviṃśat, ekakare aṣṭādaśa granthayaḥ bindu-bāṇāṃsamārabhya yāvat syānnāgacandrakāḥ 
yugahrāsāt samākhyāta(l3)
granthayaḥ surasattama| jyeṣṭhādikanyakā hṛte pavitre sarvadevatā| iti navaprakāraliṅga evaṃ nidharmaḥ (l4)

 bāṇaliṅge svayambhivirohādau niyamo nāsti yathāpūrvaṃ udāhṛtamiti kila śabdenātra svāyambhuvādiliṅgeṣu 
pratimādiṣu gaṅgāvatāre (l6)

 dvyaṅgulāntaratastatva(vaṃ) kṛtvā granthicatuṣṭayam| itthaṃ pārśvadvaye kāryaṃ ṣauḍaśaivaṃ kalāmayaṃ śivaśaktimayaṃ prānte

 bāṇādiliṅgānāṃ pūrvoktāṣṭottaraśatādisūtrasaṅkhyayā pavitrārohaṇaṃ kartavyam ekaprakāreṇa mūtreṇetyāha| 
bāṇādyekatarārciteṣu bāṇa.... (l2)

saikāśītiḥ sūtrāṇāmiti śeṣaḥ (l3)

 prakṛtyādikā proktāḥ|

 kālottare – ajaṃ viṣṇuṃ ca rudraṃ ca īśvaraṃ ca sadāśivam| somasūryāgnidevāṃśca vinyase(set) navatantuṣviti|

nusāreṇa vṛtyā vinyastavyā iti vivekaḥ vyakteṣu pratimādiṣu jānvantaṃ jānupradeśaparyantaṃ gaṅgāvatārakaṃ kartavyamiti

krameṇa guhya-nābhi-hṛt pradeśaparyantaṃ kāryam|

 caturthaṃ parikalpayediti| śaivaṃ mantramathoccarantiṣu kalāyuk-cetanāsthānayuk-daṇḍāntānta-manāntakāvadhi 
śive'(l7)
 pyātmāditatvatrayaṃ sarvātmākhyacaturthakaṃ kramayutaṃ dadyāllayoccārataḥ sarvāntaṃ paramīkṛtau ca kusumaṃ pūrtyai viye 
vatsare| (l8)
 ātmā tatvatrayeṇa sārvā devatacaturthena ca śivasya kaṅkaṇatrayaṃ gaṅgāvatārāṇāṃ samarpaṇamāha| śaivamiti śiva-
sambandhimantramuccaran (p146b, l1)
 .....kālāyuk nivṛtyādipañcakalānāṃ śuddhiyuk anena bhūtaśuddhiruktā bhavati cetanāsthānayuk 
cetanāśabdenāntaryajanahavanadhyānā ucyate sthānaśabdena (l2)
 sthā dravya-liṅga-mantrāṇāṃ śuddhirucyate etaiḥ sahitaḥ ātmāditatvatraye caturthe ca śiva eva tiṣṭhatītyāha| 
daṇḍāntāntamanāntakāvadhi śive daṇḍo nādaḥ (l3)
 tasyānto nādāntaḥ tasyāntaḥ śaktiḥ etena ātmāditatvatrayāṇā(ṇāṃ) vyāptiruktā bhavati pṛthvītatvamārabhya 
prakṛtitatvāntavyāptike brahmakāraṇeśvareṇa (l4)
 yukte ātmatatve śiva evādhipatiriti tatvāntamuccāraḥ| tathā pṛthivītatvamārabhya śuddhavidyātatvāntavyāptike 
viṣṇukāraṇeśvareṇa saṃyukte (l5)
 vidyātatve śiva evādhipati īśvaratatvāntamuccārataḥ tathā pṛthvītatvamārabhya śaktitatvāntavyāptike rudreṇa 
kāraṇeśvareṇa saṃyukte śivatatve (l6)
 śivi evādhipatiriti śivāntamuccāracaḥ manāntakāvadhi atrāpi pṛthivītatatvā(va)mārabhya samanāśaktyantavyāptike 
paravācake śive paraśivā(l7)
dhipatiriti caturthapavitrasamarpaṇe unmanāntamuccāraḥ ātmāditatvatrayaṃ pūrvoktavat ātmatatva-vidyātatva-
śivatatvākhyatatvatrayaṃ liṅgamūrdhni (l8)
 dadyāt evaṃ dānavakramastu dudukṣu? viṣayaḥ babhukṣuviṣaye śivavidyātmākhyatatvatrayasamarpaṇam|

ninā mūlamāyāntamuccācarya pavitreṇārcayecchivam| (p147a, l1)

rūpaṃ pavitrakam| ātmavidyāśivaiḥ proktamumukṣūṇāṃ pavitrakaṃ vinirdiṣṭaṃ bubhukṣūṇāṃ śivavidyātmabhiḥ kramāditi| atra 
śivatatvādisamarpaṇe sarveṣāṃ pavitrāṇāmupari (l3)
 vidyamānaśivatatvasya adho(tho)pasthānatvamanucitamiti prathamasamarpitaśivatatvaṃ vidyātatvapavitrasamarpaṇānantaraṃ 
ucṛ(ddhṛ)tya vidyātatvopari sthāpya (l4)
 evaṃ vidyātatvamātmatatvasamarpaṇānantaramuddhṛtyātmatatvopari sthāpyamiti kecidvanti anye tu evaṃ 
samarpaṇavacanābhāvāt kaṅkaṇatraya(l5)
pramāṇaviśālena(nā)ntarbhūtaṃ bhavati upari uddhṛtyā(ya) samarpaṇaṃ na yuktamiti vadanti śivatatvādikaṃ kaṇatrayāṇāṃ 
pratyekaṃ krameṇa bhāgadvayamaunatvāt (l6)
 alpanyūnatvena kaṅkaṇatrayaṃ kāryamiti kecit| ātmāditatvatrayo nyūnādhiṣṭhatayā śivāya pavitrasamarpaṇamuktvādhunā 
sāmarthyena sarva tatve(l7)
ṣvadhiṣṭhātṛtayā aṣiṣṭhitāya śivāya pavitrasamarpaṇamāha| sarvātmākhyacaturthakaṃ sarvadevatāsvarūpaṃ gaṅgāvatāraṃ kramayutaṃ 
brahmādi(l8)
kā...........śvaratyāgakrameṇa sahitaṃ yathā bhavati tathā layoccārataḥ dvādaśāntaparyantasthito nādāntoccārā dadyāt 
(p147b, l1)
 ........ sarvakāraṇapālye .......tatveṣu suvrataḥ mūlālayāntam uccārya dadyāt gaṅgāvatārakam| brahmādināmadhiṣṭhātā

savottaḥ(rvoktaḥ) kāraṇaṃ yogapādake| eko vā nāmagai(kai)rbhedai(daiḥ) sthitaḥ sa parameśvaraḥ brahmatvādvyāpakatvācca 
brahma vā brahmayogataḥ viṣṇu (l3)
prapālaśivatvāt prabhavo jagataḥ prabhuḥ rujaṃdāvayate? yasmāt tena rudro rujakṣayāt| sadāśivaḥ sadā śāntaḥ 
śānto'sau saṃsthitaḥ śivaḥ (l4)
 saṃjñābhedai(daiḥ) sthitaṃ caivameka eva jagatprabhuḥ iti paramīkṛtau ātmāditatva-catuṣṭayasthitasya śivasyaikīkaraṇe 
sarvāntaṃ gaṅgāvatārasamarpaṇā(l5)
vasāne kriyamāṇaṃ kusumaṃ puṣpāñjalitrayaṃ dadyāt| namontamūlamuccārya datvā puṣpāñjalitrayamiti| 

jñānaratnāvalyām – (l6)
 gaṅgāvatārasamarpaṇānantaramuktatvāt vatsareṇa ekavarṣe vidheḥ kriyā(ya)māṇakarmaṇaḥ pūrtyai mantralopa-kriyālopa-
dravyalopa(l7)
yuktasya karmaṇaḥ samyak paripūraṇāya pavitrārohaṇaṃ kartavyamiti śeṣaḥ uktaṃ ca ....yaduktaṃ 
mantradravyakra(kri)yānvitaṃ pramādā daiva..... (l8)
nmohāt kāryavyagratayāpi vā| rājā corabhayālasyasvehasya sanakastitaḥ (p148a, l1)
 avaśyaṃ jāyate chidraṃ na chidraparipūrtaye| nṛṇāṃ hitāya nityāṅgaṃ pavitramadhunocyate iti nanvatra pavitrārohaṇaṃ 
nityaṅgamiti proktaṃ kathamasya nityāṅgatvaṃ bhavitumarhati pratya.... (l2)
yamānaṃ karma nityamityucyate| tannityameva saṅkrāntyādiviśeṣatithau dviguṇaṃ cennityāṅgamityucyate| uktaṃ ca 
kālottare – samayīputrakābhyāṃ tu sādhakasya (l3)
 viśeṣataḥ nityaṃ krameṇa kathitaṃ yāvajjīvaṃ śivājñayā| snānaṃ pūjā japaḥ sandhyāhomaṃ caiva tu pañcamaḥ iti nityaṃ 
sadā kuryānnityāṅgaṃ procyate'thavā (l4)
 aṣṭamyāṃ ca caturdaśyāṃ pakṣayorubhayorapi pañca-daśyāṃ dinadhvaṃse saṃkrānte dakṣiṇāyane māsavṛddhau janādhikye ṣaḍaśīti 
mukheṣvapi (l5)
 ravīndugrahaṇe caiva dviguṇārcā japādikam| cāturmāsye'pi tadvat syāt vrataṃ naktaṃ haviṣyabhuk| aṣṭamyāṃ ca 
caturdaśyāṃ na bhoktavyaṃ kadācana| (l6)
 iti nityāṅgamevoktamiti ata eva ca pavitrārohaṇaṃ tāvat kārpāsatantubhiḥ āṣāḍhādiniyatakāle karmaviśeṣatvena 
kriyate (l7)
 tat kathaṃ nityāṅgamityucyate| ata eva naimittikaṃ kāmyaṃ prāyaścittaṃ vā bhavatu viṣṇusvāpaniyatakālaṃ 
nimittīkṛtya kriyamāṇatve naimittika(l8)
....cyate| anena prakāreṇāpi naimittikatvaṃ nopapadyate| naimittikalakṣaṇasyāvyāptiriti vyāpte vidyamāne(na)tvāt 
tathā hi ānītakālasya (p148b, l1)
 dīkṣāde(deḥ) naimittika-śramaṇādavyāptiḥ tathāpyasahasrakāyām| dīkṣā caiva pratiṣṭhā ca naimittikamidaṃ 
dvividhetyuktatvāt tathā prātarādiniyatakālakartavyādīnāṃ sandhyāvandanādi(l2)
nāmapi naimittikatvāt prasaṅgādityativyāptiḥ| tathā coktaṃ pavitrāropaṇe kāryā yā sandhyā tannimittataḥ tena 
naimittikī proktā na naimittikarma(karma)ta (l3)
 iti| etasmādeva naimittikamiti na bhavati tathā kāmyamapi na bha(bhava)ti kāmyaṃ tāvat aṇimādikaṃ 
vaśyādikaṃ bhavati| aṇimādiprasiddhyarthaṃ kāmyaṃ tadapi vistaramityuktatvāt| atra (l4)
 kāmyasyāvihitatvādi tu kāmyamiti na siddham| sarvapūjāvidhicchidrapūraṇāya pavitramityuktatvāt saṃvatsare 
kriyamāṇe karmaṇaḥ mantradravyakriyālope (l5)
 sati tatpūraṇāya pavitrakarma kṛta iti prāyaścittaṃ bhavatīti vaktuṃ na śakyate tasmin kāle karmaṇaḥ lopo bhavati 
tasmin kāle prāyaścittaṃ śrūyate| (l6)
 pavitrakarma āṣāḍhādikāle naikatyāt prāyaścittaṃ na bhavati tathā hi loke tāvat vidhyuktānuṣṭhāṇe(ne) (l7)
 prāyaścittaṃ vidhāya prāyaścittaṃ kartavyamiti śrūyate| atra pavitrakarmaṇi tu lope sati prāyaścittaṃ vinā pavitrārohaṇaṃ 
kartavyamiti dṛṣṭatvāt| uktaṃ ca (p149a, l1)
 uktamāsatraye yastu na kuryāt sūtrarohaṇam| asau kanyāṃ gate bhānau japtvā dakṣāṇunā yutam| kuryāt pavitrakaṃ 
śambhostu lāyānna kathañcaneti anyatra ....... (l2)
...datra ye daivātha dvidhāya pavitraṃ lakṣayamaghorasya japtvā cāndrāyaṇaṃ carediti karmalopaparipūraṇārthaṃ datvā devāya 
yathākatha....... (l3)
 prāyaścittaṃ caitat tadā pavitrākareṇa pipatitvameva bhavitavyaṃ prāyaścittasya prāyaścittābhāvāt ata eva prāyaścittaṃ na 
bhavati tasmādidaṃ nitya.... (l4)
gamiti niścityoktam uktaṃ ca snānaṃ sandhyā tathā pūjā japo homaśca pañcamaḥ iti nityāṅgamuddiṣṭaṃ pavitrārohaṇaṃ 
tatheti atrāpi pratya..... (l5)
nuṣṭheyatvāt pratyabdamanuṣṭheyatvācca daivasikaṃ vārṣikaṃ ceti dvividhatvaṃ divase divase kriyamāṇaṃ daivasikaṃ varṣe varṣe 
kriyamāṇaṃ (l6)
 vārṣikayorekavāstu dvividhatvena kathaṃ prayojanamityuktatve pavitrakakarmaṇo bāhulyādeva pratyahaṃ 
vidhīyamānatvenāśaktatvāt| (l7)
 pratidinaṃ pavitraṃ puṣpakuśādibhiḥ sūtrapratinidhibhiḥ kriyamāṇamapyasaṃpūrṇameva jāyate tasmādanenaiva 
paripūrṇatvamāpādyata iti (149b, l1)
 daivasi ........ kṣayā vārṣikaṃ siddham| nityavratasandhyāvandanādilopavat nityabhūtapavitralope prāyaścittaśravaṇācca 
evaṃ cet daivasikakarmāstu vārṣika...vavidha.... (l2)
tāmityukte daivasikābhāve vārṣika(kaḥ) syāt pradhānatvāt nityotsave nayā pūjā tasyāṃ 
sāṃvatsarotsavamityuktatvāt ata eva daivasikāpekṣayā (l3)
 vārṣikā pavitraṃ nityāṅgamevetyuktam| 

tathā mohaśūrottare – patrikābhiḥ prasūnairvā kuśairvā parikalpayet| pavitraṃ pratyahaṃ (l4)
 śambhormahāpuṇyavikīrṣayā| iti nityakriyākhyāta śruṇu naimittikaṃ punaḥ āṣāḍhasya sitā grāhyā śrāvaṇasya 
sitāsitentina....... (l5)
vaṃ cet pavitrārohaṇaṃ naimittikamiti siddhaṃ kathaṃ nityāṅgamucyate| atra parihāraḥ nityabhūtadaivasikapavitre 
kriyamāṇanitya...... (l6)
di sarvakarmacchidraharatvapuranibandhaka-nibandhanapradhāna-jñāpapanārthaṃ vārṣakasya naimittikanāmoktiḥ na tu 
naimittikalakṣaṇayogāt| ata e.....(l7)
 naimittikaśabdaḥ svārthekapratyayaḥ daivasikanimittameva naimittikaṃ vārṣakasetyuktatvāt nityāṅgamiti siddham| 

tathā kālottare – dīkṣā.... (l8)
......saṃsmṛtaṃ nityaṃ taṃ kriyātmakaṃ dravyamantrakriyāpekṣā savikalaviphalaṃ strīṇām ato nityāṅgamevedaṃ pavitrārohaṇaṃ 
matam| uktavaikalpato ghora.... (p150a, l1)
panānīti pavitrakaṃ tasmāt kāle prakartavyaṃ prāguktaparameṣṭhinaḥ tena sāṃvatsarīpūjā saṃpūrṇā jāyate spuṭaṃ pūjāṃ 
gatvā(pūjāṅgatvā) prakartavyaṃ nityamevaṃ pavitrakaṃ pavitreṇa vinā (l2)
 jā tāmasī parikīrtite| ityuktatvāt| 

atra kecit mohaśūrottare – śivāgame naimittikā nāma kurvataḥ kāraṇāt niyatakālanimittatvāt naimittikamiti

 nityameveti vadanti nityāṅgameva nipuṇadarśāditi vidhīyadhe(te)ti kavau gurau cāstamayaṃ prayāte māsatraye 
cāpyudayo nā(na) cāstu tathāpi kurvīta pavitrakā (l4)
 .... naimittikatvāduta naimityakatvāditi siddhāntaśekhare||7||

śrutyordakṣiṇavāmayostu kha.....laṃ nyā(nya)set kalāḍhyaṃ tathā nāsāyāṃ (l5)
 sakalākalau kṣapaṇakaṃ cāntasthamakṣṇo nyaset| pūrvokteṣu viriñcakeśavahareśānāṃśca śaktiṃ śivaṃ dṛṣṭvānnasya ca darśanaṃ 
grahamathā śūnye (l6)
 na coṣṭhena ca||8||

khaṇḍādeḥ kṣapaṇāttu  saṃgrahamatho? bandhaṃ tatho? kṣepaṇaṃ grāsasyāsvānaṃ vimocanamadhaḥ sañcāriṇaṃ coṣṭhagam| kṛtvā 
yaḥ sakalāt kṛtāt khamalakāt śūnyāṃ tathā niṣkalāt dhyānācca kṣayatonnatoṣṭhyamanuto bhuñjīta lehyādikam||

(p150b, l1)
 brahmānnaṃ(ṇḍaṃ) tu rasoṇaristu bhagavān bhoktā maheśa svayaṃ dātāpyeṣa maheśvarastu yadi vā bhojyaṃ sva....tmakaṃ 
karta(rtā) tasya maheśvaraḥ kila paro bhoktā śivaḥ (l2)
 śaktimān prāṇāpānagamāgamiti matimān bhuñjīta niceṣvapi||10||

evaṃ bhāvayuteva deśikavaro gehe mahāpāpināṃ bhuñjāne tu do(śe)ṣabhāga(l3)
pi(mapi) tathā śrāddhādidurbhojane| bhukte'smin sakṛdeva vidaviduṣāṃ koṭirbhavet bhijatā| niśvāsoktamidaṃ 
trisaptakulajādātuṃ vrajeyuḥ śivam||11|| (l4.)

loke tāvat avicchinnasampradāyāśca bhavanti| vicchinnasampradāyānāṃ gṛheṣu vicchinnasampradāyo na bhavatītyajñānaṃ 
tat bhojanādi(l5)
karaṇena sampradāyayuktānāṃ doṣo bhavatīti manasi nidhāya tatparihārārtham aṣṭavidhaprāsādamantranyāsta(sa)pūrvakaṃ 
bhuñjāno (l6)

 khamalanāmadheyamantraṃ nyaset| dakṣiṇakarṇe nyāsaṃ kuryāt tathā kalāḍhyaṃ kalāḍhyākhyamantraṃ vāmakarṇe nyaset| 
nāsāyāṃ na.... (l8)
sikāyāṃ sakalākalau sakalaniṣkalākhyamantrau yathākrameṇa nāsikāyāṃ dakṣiṇavāma...........nyaset| kṣapaṇakaṃ

 antastham antasthākhya-mantram akṣiṇoḥ dakṣiṇavāmadṛṣṭyo nyaset| pūrvokteṣu khamalādimantreṣu 
viriñca.....hareśānāt brahmaviṣṇurudreśvarān (l2)
 nyaset| tathā śaktiṃ śivaṃ ca nyaset| adho eva nyāsānantaraṃ dṛṣṭyā nayanena śunyena śunyākhyamantreṇānnapradarśanaṃ 
kāṃsyādipātre sthitodanā(l3)
valokanaṃ kuryāt| oṣṭhyena karṇoṣṭhyākhyamantreṇa gṛhaṃ saṃgṛhaṃ samūhīkaraṇaṃ ca kuryāt aṣṭavidhaprāsādoddhāraṇastu 
(l4)
 śrīkālottarāgamādavadheyaḥ sakalaṃ niṣkalaṃ śūnyaṃ kalāḍhyaṃ khamalaṃ kṛtaṃ kṣapaṇaṃ kṣamayaṃ tasthaṃ kaṇṭhoṣṭhaṃ cāṣṭamaṃ 
smṛtamiti| khaṇḍādeḥ (l5)
 khaṇḍākāreṇa sthitaśākādeḥ saṃgrahaṃ samūhīkaraṇaṃ kṣapāt kṣapaṇamantrāt kāryam adho hi samūhīkaraṇānantaraṃ bandhaṃ 
sūpādinā (l6)
 ....sya sammelanam| tathā utkṣepaṇaṃ haste- śānasyordhvamuddhṛtya karaṇaṃ grāsasyāśanapiṇḍasya āsvādanaṃ bhakṣaṇaṃ 
vimocanaṃ (l7)
 oṣṭhagatahastagatamocanam oṣṭhagataḥ oṣṭhākhyasparśasya hastasya atha sañcāraṇaṃ yathākrameṇa sakalāt 
sakalamantrāt khamalāt khamala(l8)
kṛtamantrāt śūnyāt śūnyamantrāt niṣkalamantrāt kṣayataḥ dhyānāt kṣayamantasthasya mantrasya cintanāt kṛtvā evaṃ 
pañcamantreṇa bandhanādipañcakṛtyaṃ vidhāya (p151b, l1)
 yaḥ puruṣaḥ evaṃ nyāsaṃ dhyānādikaṃ karoti sa puruṣaḥ unnatoṣṭhamanutaḥ unnatā ityantakaṇṭhoṣṭhamantrataḥ 
lye(le)hyādikaṃ lehyapeyakhādyādikaṃ bhuñjīta bhojanaṃ (l2)
 kurvīta| brahmānnaṃ bhojanaprārambhakāle tadasvannaṃ brahmasvarūpamiti dhyāyet| raso hariḥ annādiṣu 
vidyamānamadhurādiṣaḍrasaḥ (l3)
 nārāyaṇasvarūpaḥ bhagavāniti ṣāḍguṇyasampannavān maheśaḥ maheśvaraḥ, bhoktā svayaṃ bhojanakartā eṣaḥ maheśvara evaṃ 
paraḥ annādi(l4)
dānā ata iti dhyānaṃ kuryāt| yadi vā bhojyaṃ bhoktuṃ yogyaṃ sarvapadārthaṃ sva māyātmakam atho 
māyo'dhvamāyāsvarūpamiti dhyāyet| (l5)
 tasya māyātmakasya kartā eṣaḥ maheśvara eva paraḥ sarvebhyotkṛṣṭaḥ| śaktimān samevataśaktisahitaḥ śivaḥ bhoktā 
bhojanaṃ kurvāmaḥ svasāmarthā(l6)
deva bhoktetyarthaḥ prāṇāpānagamāgamīti prāṇāpānavāyvoru(rū)rdhvādhorūpasañcaraṇajñānavān iti pūrvoktaprakāreṇa, 
matimān = (l7)
 buddhimān, maheśvarārpaṇabuddhiyukta ityarthaḥ nīceṣvapi vicchinnasampradāyeṣvapi bhuñjīta bhojanaṃ kurvīta| evaṃ 
pūrvoktaprakāreṇa (l8)
 bhāvanāyukta eva deśikavaraḥ ācāryaśreṣṭhaḥ mahāpāpīnāṃ gre(gṛ/ge)he bhuñjānaḥ bhaujanaṃ kurvāṇaḥ doṣabhāk bhāvayukto 
na bha[vet] (p152a, l1)
 tathā śrāddhādidurbhojane ........ yukto na bhavediti| tasmin pūrvoktavat dhyānayuktadeśikavare sakṛdeva 
ekavāra....yasya gṛhe bhukte bhojanaṃ kurvati sati tasya (l2)
 vedaviduṣāṃ vedaśāstrajñānayuktānāṃ, koṭi bhojitā bhavet = koṭi brāhmaṇabhojitaphalamasyāstīti bhāvaḥ, 
idamuktārthaṃ, niśvāsoktaṃ = niśvāsāgame proktaṃ, brāhmaṇyārthadyotanārthaṃ (l3)
 kṛtaṃ dātuḥ deśikavare bhojanādiyuktasya puruṣasya tri sapta kulajāḥ ekaviṃśasambhavāḥ pitṛjāḥ saptamātrajaḥ sapta 
ātmajāḥ sapta evamekaviṃśati(l4)
prakārāḥ athavā pūrve daśāpare daśa ātmanā sahaikaviṃśatiprakārāḥ śivaṃ śivapadaṃ vrajeyuḥ gaccheyuḥ||11|| (l5.)

pāpināṃ dravyaparigrahadoṣaparihārārthaṃ dviprakāreṇāṣṭāvidhaprasādanyāsaṃ ślokadvayenāha| hṛdyantabahireva mūrdhni nayane (l6)
 dakṣe ca savye tathā cūḍāyāṃ vadane tanau ca sakalādyo'ṣṭāntamantrā nyaset yadyoṣṭaṃ sakalāṅgakemṛtarucaṃ nyasyet

 śaṅkhābhā kamalaṃ kalāḍyamaruṇaṃ cāntasthamabjāmbujaṃ vaktre kundaśaśiprabhaṃ bahiratopyantastanau niṣkalaṃ

 sarvaśarīre sakalādyoṣṭhāntamantrān sakalaṃ niṣkalaṃ śūnyaṃ kalāḍhyaṃ khamalaṃ kṛtam| kṣapaṇaṃ kṣamayantasthaṃ kaṇṭhoṣṭhaṃ

kuṇḍamityādinā atra jalāśayasamīpe kuṇḍakaraṇāt pūrvaṃ nava hastaṃ saptahastāthavā maṇḍapaṃ kāryam uktaṃ ca

 vāyoḥ taṃ mṛdā sthaṇḍilaṃ karaṃ kuṇḍāgneyyāṃ citā khātaṃ caturhastamitāmayam| maṅgalāṅgulagambhīraṃ

 kṛtvā citā khātaṃ kvacinmatam| niṣpādyatritayaṃ caiva gomayenopalepayetidi| kuṇḍasya dakṣiṇa ityatra dakṣiṇasya

tathā kāmike - kūṭaṃ vāpi prapāṃ vāpi vidhāyaiva tadantare| ṣaḍaṅgulocchrayopetaṃ mekhalaikaṃ (l7)
 yugāśrakaṃ kṛtvā kuṇḍaṃ vidhānena hastakaṃ tammrādānile saṃkalpyaṃ sthaṇḍilaṃ tacca citā khātaṃ catuṣkaraṃ 
hastadvitayavistīrṇamaṣṭāṅgula-gambhīrakaḥ (l8)

haridracūrṇaiḥ ....tavarṇā (l2)

saha pūjyo (l5)

tathā jñānaratnāvalyām – kuṇḍe ca saṃskṛte tatra (p154b, l1)

iti saddīkṣācaraṇāñcitasya saddīkṣāyā samyak dīkṣāyā (l2)

tathā siddhāntaśekhare - (l6)
 āyāmairniścitaṃ liṅgaṃ tribhāgaṃ tat prakalpayet| mūle madhye tathordhve ca brahmaviṣṇuśivāṃśakā| brahmāṃśaṃ caturaśraṃ 
syādaṣṭāśraṃ viṣṇubhāgakaṃ kuryādvṛttaṃ .... (l7)
 rudrāṃśaṃ ṣoḍaśāśramathāpi vā| dvātriṃśadaśrasaṃyuktaṃ catuṣṣaṣṭyaśrasaṃyutam| napuṃsakaṃ bhavedbrahmā strīliṅgaṃ viṣṇubhāgakam| 
pulliṅgaṃ rudra...... (159b, pl1)
 not relavant portion. lohena nirmitaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ, dārujaṃ madhyamaṃ bhavet| dvijagṛhyādikaṃ vahni adhaṃ(dhamaṃ) ceti 
kīrtitam| sūryakāntenabhava śreṣṭhaḥ| kāṣṭhajo madhyamo bhavet| śrotriyākārajo vahniḥ kanyasa sviti kīrtitaḥ|
